  // শুধু যে কোডের প্রোভাইডার একটাই (নাম বদলালেও খুঁজে পায়)
function pkgNameHash_(s) { var h = 5381; for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = (((h << 5) + h) + s.charCodeAt(i)) | 0; return (h >>> 0).toString(36); }
function injectProviders_(arr) {
  if (!arr || !arr.length) return arr;
  for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
    var p = arr[i];
    if (!p || p.provider || p.code == null) continue;
    var c = String(p.code), ix = PRIV_PKG_PROV_[c + "|" + pkgNameHash_(String(p.name || ""))];
    if (ix === undefined) ix = PRIV_CODE_PROV_[c];
    if (ix !== undefined) p.provider = PRIV_PROVIDERS_[ix];
  }
  return arr;
}
/** packages-full.json এর টেক্সট পার্স করে প্রাইভেট ম্যাপ থেকে provider বসায়। */
function parsePkgs_(text) { return injectProviders_(JSON.parse(text)); }
/** পাবলিক service_meta তে custom সার্ভিসের provider যাবে না। */
function publicCustom_(list) {
  return (list || []).map(function (c) { var o = {}; for (var k in c) { if (k !== "provider") o[k] = c[k]; } return o; });
}

var SMS_AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT = 2; // a little looser — SMS text/OCR can round differently

// ---------- Secrets for the two new features below ----------
// SECURITY (hardened): these no longer live in this source file — a value
// hardcoded here leaks the moment this .gs/.js file is ever shared, zipped,
// emailed, or pushed to a repo. They're now stored in this script's private
// Script Properties (Apps Script's built-in secret storage, never exported
// with the code) and read via getAdminToken_() / getSmsToken_() below.
//
// ONE-TIME SETUP: in the Apps Script editor, pick "setupSecrets_" from the
// function dropdown at the top and click ▶ Run once. It creates both tokens
// as random values in Script Properties (nothing secret is stored in this
// file). To replace leaked tokens, run "rotateSecrets_" instead.
// ADMIN_TOKEN        -> used by admin.html (the mini admin panel) — this is
//                       what you type into the "Admin token" login box.
// SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN  -> used by the SMS-forwarding app on your phone.
// Treat both like passwords: don't forward the approve/reject email, don't
// leave admin.html logged in on a shared device.
//
// AUTO-ORDER-FORWARDING: a third one-time function, "setupProviderKeys_",
// stores your ~21 SMM panel API keys the same secure way (Script
// Properties, never in this file). Run it once from the editor. After
// that, approving an order (admin panel, email button, or SMS
// auto-verify) automatically places it on the correct provider panel.
// See the comment above setupProviderKeys_() further down for details.
function randomToken_(len) {
  // Utilities.getUuid() is backed by a cryptographically secure RNG (Math.random is not).
  var chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnpqrstuvwxyz23456789";
  var out = "";
  while (out.length < len) {
    var hex = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
    for (var i = 0; i + 2 <= hex.length && out.length < len; i += 2) {
      out += chars.charAt(parseInt(hex.substr(i, 2), 16) % chars.length);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Cryptographically secure N-digit number (no leading-zero loss). */
function secureDigits_(n) {
  var out = "";
  while (out.length < n) {
    var hex = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
    for (var i = 0; i + 2 <= hex.length && out.length < n; i += 2) {
      var b = parseInt(hex.substr(i, 2), 16);
      if (b < 250) out += String(b % 10); // reject 250-255 to avoid modulo bias
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Returns a Script Property, creating it with a random value if missing (security-hardening step 4). */
function getOrCreateSecret_(name, len) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var v = props.getProperty(name);
  if (v) return v;
  props.setProperty(name, randomToken_(len || 40));
  return props.getProperty(name);
}

/** Run once from the editor. Creates ADMIN_TOKEN and SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN in Script Properties
 *  ONLY if they are missing (existing values are never overwritten). No secret is stored in this file.
 *  The generated values are shown in View -> Logs / Execution log. */
function setupSecrets_() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var made = [];
  if (!props.getProperty("ADMIN_TOKEN")) { props.setProperty("ADMIN_TOKEN", randomToken_(28)); made.push("ADMIN_TOKEN"); }
  if (!props.getProperty("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN")) { props.setProperty("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN", randomToken_(28)); made.push("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN"); }
  // Step 4 (security): separate secrets so a leaked ADMIN_TOKEN can no longer forge customer sessions.
  // NEVER rotate PIN_PEPPER once users exist — every customer PIN would stop working.
  if (!props.getProperty("SESSION_SECRET")) { props.setProperty("SESSION_SECRET", randomToken_(48)); made.push("SESSION_SECRET"); }
  if (!props.getProperty("PIN_PEPPER")) { props.setProperty("PIN_PEPPER", randomToken_(48)); made.push("PIN_PEPPER"); }
  Logger.log(made.length
    ? ("Created: " + made.join(", ") + ". Read the values in Project Settings -> Script Properties.")
    : "Both secrets already exist in Script Properties — nothing changed.");
}

/** Run once from the editor to REPLACE both secrets with new random values (use if a token leaked).
 *  Afterwards: log into admin.html with the new ADMIN_TOKEN and update the phone SMS-forwarding app
 *  with the new SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN. Read the values in Project Settings -> Script Properties. */
function rotateSecrets_() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  props.setProperty("ADMIN_TOKEN", randomToken_(28));
  props.setProperty("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN", randomToken_(28));
  Logger.log("Both secrets replaced. Read the new values in Project Settings -> Script Properties.");
}

function getAdminToken_() {
  var v = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("ADMIN_TOKEN");
  if (!v) throw new Error("ADMIN_TOKEN not set — run setupSecrets once from the Apps Script editor first.");
  return v;
}
function getSmsToken_() {
  var v = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN");
  if (!v) throw new Error("SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN not set — run setupSecrets once from the Apps Script editor first.");
  return v;
}


// ---------- Telegram order/ticket notifications ----------
// ONE-TIME: set TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN + TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID in Script Properties
// or run setupTelegram_() after editing the placeholders below.
// Create bot via @BotFather → token. Chat ID: message the bot, then open
// https://api.telegram.org/bot<TOKEN>/getUpdates
function setupTelegram_() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  // Bot: @prime_boost_bd_bot — token set; set CHAT_ID after you /start the bot
  props.setProperty("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN", "PASTE_BOT_TOKEN_HERE"); // সিক্রেট কোডে রাখবেন না — Script Properties-এ সেট করুন
  // IMPORTANT: replace with your numeric chat id from getUpdates after messaging the bot
  props.setProperty("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID", "PASTE_CHAT_ID_HERE");
  Logger.log("Telegram bot token saved. Set TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID then run testTelegram_().");
}

function getTelegramBotToken_() {
  return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN") || "";
}
function getTelegramChatId_() {
  return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID") || "";
}

/** Send text to Telegram. Silent no-op if not configured. */
function notifyTelegram_(text, buttons) {
  var token = getTelegramBotToken_();
  var chatId = getTelegramChatId_();
  if (!token || !chatId || token.indexOf("PASTE_") === 0 || chatId.indexOf("PASTE_") === 0) {
    return { ok: false, error: "not_configured" };
  }
  try {
    var url = "https://api.telegram.org/bot" + token + "/sendMessage";
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
      method: "post",
      contentType: "application/json",
      payload: JSON.stringify((function () {
        var p = { chat_id: chatId, text: String(text || "").substring(0, 3500), disable_web_page_preview: true };
        // Optional inline buttons (only when the Telegram admin panel is set up)
        if (buttons && buttons.length && PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("TG_WEBHOOK_SECRET")) p.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: buttons };
        return p;
      })()),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    var code = resp.getResponseCode();
    var body = resp.getContentText();
    if (code >= 200 && code < 300) {
      var mid = null;
      try { var jj = JSON.parse(body); mid = jj && jj.result && jj.result.message_id; } catch (eM) {}
      return { ok: true, message_id: mid };
    }
    Logger.log("Telegram fail " + code + ": " + body.substring(0, 200));
    return { ok: false, error: body.substring(0, 150) };
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("Telegram error: " + err.message);
    return { ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

function testTelegram_() {
  var r = notifyTelegram_("✅ boostplus — Telegram নোটিফ ঠিক আছে।\nTime: " + new Date().toISOString());
  Logger.log(JSON.stringify(r));
}

function notifyOwnerTelegramOrder_(orderId, data, pricing, txn, method, mobile) {
  var lines = [
    "🛒 নতুন অর্ডার",
    "ID: " + orderId,
    "৳" + (pricing && pricing.amount != null ? pricing.amount : ""),
    (data.service_name || data.service_code || ""),
    "Qty: " + (data.quantity || ""),
    "Pay: " + (method || "") + (txn ? (" · " + txn) : ""),
    "Mobile: " + (mobile || ""),
    "Status: " + (data.status || "Pending")
  ];
  if (data.link) lines.push("Link: " + String(data.link).substring(0, 120));
  notifyTelegram_(lines.join("\n"), [[{ text: "🔎 খুলুন / অ্যাপ্রুভ", callback_data: "o:" + orderId }]]);
  try { warnDuplicateOrder_(sheet_(), orderId, mobile, data.service_code, data.link); } catch (eDup) {}
}

function notifyOwnerTelegramTicket_(ticketId, category, sub, orderId, mobile, message) {
  var lines = [
    "🎫 নতুন টিকিট",
    "ID: " + ticketId,
    (category || "") + (sub ? (" / " + sub) : ""),
    orderId ? ("Order: " + orderId) : "",
    "Mobile: " + (mobile || ""),
    String(message || "").substring(0, 400)
  ];
  notifyTelegram_(lines.filter(Boolean).join("\n"), [[{ text: "🎫 খুলুন / রিপ্লাই", callback_data: "t:" + ticketId }]]);
}


// ---------- Brute-force protection for the admin token ----------
// After ADMIN_LOCKOUT_MAX wrong attempts within the window, admin actions
// are refused for everyone (this script is single-admin scale, so a
// shared cooldown is an acceptable, simple trade-off) until the window
// expires — makes guessing a short token impractical.
var ADMIN_LOCKOUT_KEY = "admin_fail_count";
var ADMIN_LOCKOUT_MAX = 6;
var ADMIN_LOCKOUT_WINDOW_SECONDS = 900; // 15 minutes

function isAdminLockedOut_() {
  try {
    var n = Number(CacheService.getScriptCache().get(ADMIN_LOCKOUT_KEY) || 0);
    return n >= ADMIN_LOCKOUT_MAX;
  } catch (e) {
    return false; // cache is best-effort; never let it block legit admins
  }
}
function registerAdminFail_() {
  try {
    var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var n = Number(cache.get(ADMIN_LOCKOUT_KEY) || 0) + 1;
    cache.put(ADMIN_LOCKOUT_KEY, String(n), ADMIN_LOCKOUT_WINDOW_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) { /* best-effort */ }
}
function clearAdminFails_() {
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(ADMIN_LOCKOUT_KEY); } catch (e) { /* best-effort */ }
}
/** Central admin-token check used by every admin-only action below.
 *  Returns true only if not locked out AND the token matches. */
function checkAdminToken_(token) {
  if (isAdminLockedOut_()) return false;
  var tk = String(token || "");
  var ok = tk === getAdminToken_() || adminSessValid_(tk);
  if (ok) { clearAdminFails_(); } else { registerAdminFail_(); }
  return ok;
}
/** Same check, but distinguishes "locked out" from "wrong token" so the
 *  admin panel can show an accurate message instead of "wrong token" when
 *  the real cause is the temporary lockout. */
function adminAuthError_(token) {
  if (isAdminLockedOut_()) return "locked_out";
  return checkAdminToken_(token) ? null : "unauthorized";
}

/** Emails the site owner whenever a new order is submitted.
 *  Uses MailApp — sends from the Gmail account this script is running
 *  under (the "Execute as: Me" account), no extra setup needed.
 *  Free Gmail accounts get ~100 emails/day from Apps Script, which is
 *  far more than enough for order notifications at this scale.
 *
 *  The email includes one-click Approve/Reject buttons (plain links to this
 *  same web app's doGet, carrying ADMIN_TOKEN) so routine orders can be
 *  handled straight from the inbox without opening admin.html. Because the
 *  link embeds the admin token, treat this email like you'd treat the admin
 *  panel itself — don't forward it or leave the inbox logged in on a shared
 *  device. */
/** Diagnostic only — not called by the site. Select "sendTestEmail_" in the
 *  function dropdown at the top of this editor and click ▶ Run once.
 *  - If a permission popup appears, click Allow (this grants MailApp the
 *    first time, or after certain redeploys).
 *  - Then check the Execution log at the bottom (or the ⏱ Executions tab)
 *    for either "TEST EMAIL SENT OK" or the exact error it hit.
 *  - Also check the OWNER_EMAIL inbox (and Spam folder) for a message
 *    titled "boostplus test email". */
function sendTestEmail_() {
  try {
    MailApp.sendEmail(OWNER_EMAIL, "boostplus test email", "যদি এই ইমেইলটা পেয়ে থাকেন, MailApp ঠিকভাবে কাজ করছে।");
    Logger.log("TEST EMAIL SENT OK to " + OWNER_EMAIL);
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("TEST EMAIL FAILED: " + e.message);
  }
}

function notifyOwnerByEmail_(orderId, data, pricing, txn, method, mobile) {
  if (!OWNER_EMAIL || OWNER_EMAIL.indexOf("@") === -1) return;
  try {
    var subject = "🛒 New Order: " + orderId + " — ৳" + pricing.amount;
    var textBody =
      "একটা নতুন অর্ডার এসেছে:\n\n" +
      "Order ID: " + orderId + "\n" +
      "Service Code: " + (data.service_code || "") + "\n" +
      "Service Name: " + (data.service_name || "") + "\n" +
      "Platform: " + (data.platform || "") + "\n" +
      "Link: " + (data.link || "") + "\n" +
      "Quantity: " + (data.quantity || "") + "\n" +
      (data.comments ? "Comments:\n" + String(data.comments).substring(0, 3000) + "\n" : "") +
      "Amount: ৳" + pricing.amount + "\n" +
      "Payment Method: " + (method || "") + "\n" +
      "Transaction ID: " + (txn || "") + "\n" +
      "Sender Number: " + (mobile || "") + "\n" +
      "Status: " + (data.status || "Pending") + "\n" +
      "Time: " + new Date().toLocaleString() + "\n\n" +
      "Orders শিটে গিয়ে যাচাই করে অর্ডার শুরু করে দিন।";

    var baseUrl = "";
    try { baseUrl = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (e2) { baseUrl = ""; }

    var htmlBody = "<div style='font-family:sans-serif;font-size:14px;color:#111;'>" +
      "<h2 style='margin:0 0 12px;'>🛒 নতুন অর্ডার এসেছে</h2>" +
      "<table style='border-collapse:collapse;'>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Order ID</td><td><b>" + esc_(orderId) + "</b></td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Service</td><td>" + esc_(data.service_name || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Platform</td><td>" + esc_(data.platform || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Link</td><td>" + esc_(data.link || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Quantity</td><td>" + esc_(data.quantity || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      (data.comments ? "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;vertical-align:top;'>Comments</td><td style='white-space:pre-wrap;'>" + esc_(String(data.comments).substring(0, 3000)) + "</td></tr>" : "") +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Amount</td><td><b>৳" + esc_(pricing.amount) + "</b></td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Method</td><td>" + esc_(method || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>TrxID</td><td>" + esc_(txn || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "<tr><td style='padding:3px 10px 3px 0;color:#555;'>Sender</td><td>" + esc_(mobile || "") + "</td></tr>" +
      "</table>";

    if (baseUrl) {
      var approveUrl = baseUrl + "?action=admin_update&token=" + encodeURIComponent(getAdminToken_()) + "&order_id=" + encodeURIComponent(orderId) + "&status=Processing";
      var rejectUrl = baseUrl + "?action=admin_update&token=" + encodeURIComponent(getAdminToken_()) + "&order_id=" + encodeURIComponent(orderId) + "&status=Rejected";
      htmlBody += "<div style='margin-top:18px;'>" +
        "<a href='" + approveUrl + "' style='background:#22c55e;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:bold;margin-right:10px;'>✅ Approve (Processing)</a>" +
        "<a href='" + rejectUrl + "' style='background:#ef4444;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:bold;'>❌ Reject</a>" +
        "</div>" +
        "<p style='color:#888;font-size:12px;margin-top:14px;'>এই লিংক দুটো শুধু নিজের জন্য — কাউকে ফরওয়ার্ড করবেন না, এতে admin অ্যাক্সেস থাকে।</p>";
    } else {
      htmlBody += "<p style='color:#888;'>(One-click বাটন দেখাতে ব্যর্থ — Orders শিটে গিয়ে ম্যানুয়ালি যাচাই করুন।)</p>";
    }
    htmlBody += "</div>";

    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: OWNER_EMAIL,
      subject: subject,
      body: textBody,
      htmlBody: htmlBody
    });
  } catch (e) {
    // Email failure should never block the order itself — the order is
    // already safely saved in the sheet regardless of this.
  }
}

// ---------- Price / quantity validation (server is the source of truth) ----------

/** Loads the full code -> {price_usd, min, max} map from the "Prices" tab. */
// ---------- Price index caching ----------
// The Prices sheet has 15,000+ rows. Re-reading and re-indexing all of it
// on every single order request (as the original code did) is the main
// reason verification felt slow — this was measurably the biggest cost in
// the whole request. CacheService fixes that, but a single cache value is
// capped at 100KB, far smaller than the ~600KB+ this JSON needs, so the
// index is split across several chunks and reassembled on read.
var PRICE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 1800; // 30 min — a price update in the sheet
                                     // takes at most this long to take effect
var PRICE_CACHE_CHUNK_SIZE = 90000; // stays safely under the 100KB/key cap
var PRICE_CACHE_META_KEY = "price_idx_meta_v1";

function loadPriceIndex_() {
  var cached = readPriceIndexFromCache_();
  if (cached) return cached;

  // 1) Primary: live packages-full.json from the site (always matches catalog)
  var map = loadPriceIndexFromPackagesJson_();
  if (map && Object.keys(map).length) {
    writePriceIndexToCache_(map);
    return map;
  }

  // 2) Fallback: "Prices" sheet tab (manual import) if JSON fetch fails
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(PRICE_SHEET_NAME);
  if (!sh) {
    throw new Error(
      'Could not load prices from packages-full.json and no Prices sheet found. Upload packages-full.json to the site or import prices_for_sheet.csv as tab "Prices".'
    );
  }
  var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  map = {};
  for (var i = 1; i < values.length; i++) {
    var code = String(values[i][0]).trim();
    if (!code) continue;
    map[code] = {
      price_usd: Number(values[i][1]) || 0,
      min: Number(values[i][2]) || 0,
      max: Number(values[i][3]) || 0
    };
  }

  writePriceIndexToCache_(map);
  return map;
}

/** Builds price index from PACKAGES_JSON_URL. Returns null on failure. */
function loadPriceIndexFromPackagesJson_() {
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, {
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      followRedirects: true
    });
    if (resp.getResponseCode() < 200 || resp.getResponseCode() >= 300) return null;
    var arr = parsePkgs_(resp.getContentText());
    if (!arr || !arr.length) return null;
    var map = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      var p = arr[i];
      if (!p || p.code == null) continue;
      var code = String(p.code).trim();
      if (!code) continue;
      var usd = 0;
      if (typeof p.pricePerK === "number") {
        usd = p.pricePerK;
      } else {
        usd = Number(String(p.pricePerK || "").replace("$", "").replace(",", "").trim()) || 0;
      }
      map[code] = {
        price_usd: usd,
        min: Number(p.min) || 0,
        max: Number(p.max) || 0
      };
    }
    // custom services (admin / search-app added) — never override packages-full.json
    customServices_().forEach(function (c) {
      if (!c || c.code == null) return;
      var cc = String(c.code).trim();
      if (!cc || map[cc]) return;
      map[cc] = {
        price_usd: Number(String(c.pricePerK == null ? "" : c.pricePerK).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, "")) || 0,
        min: Number(c.min) || 0,
        max: Number(c.max) || 0
      };
    });
    return map;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

function readPriceIndexFromCache_() {
  try {
    var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var metaRaw = cache.get(PRICE_CACHE_META_KEY);
    if (!metaRaw) return null;
    var meta = JSON.parse(metaRaw);
    var keys = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < meta.count; i++) keys.push("price_idx_c" + i);
    var chunks = cache.getAll(keys);
    var joined = "";
    for (var j = 0; j < meta.count; j++) {
      var part = chunks["price_idx_c" + j];
      if (!part) return null; // a chunk expired/evicted — treat as full miss
      joined += part;
    }
    return JSON.parse(joined);
  } catch (e) {
    return null; // any cache problem just falls back to reading the sheet
  }
}

function writePriceIndexToCache_(map) {
  try {
    var json = JSON.stringify(map);
    var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var chunks = {};
    var count = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < json.length; i += PRICE_CACHE_CHUNK_SIZE) {
      chunks["price_idx_c" + count] = json.substring(i, i + PRICE_CACHE_CHUNK_SIZE);
      count++;
    }
    cache.putAll(chunks, PRICE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS);
    cache.put(PRICE_CACHE_META_KEY, JSON.stringify({ count: count }), PRICE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {
    // Caching is an optimization, not a requirement — if it fails for any
    // reason (rare), the order still gets validated correctly, just slower.
  }
}

function toBdtPrice_(usd) {
  return (Number(usd) || 0) * USD_TO_BDT_RATE;
}

/** Same markup tiers as app-core.js getSellingPrice() — keep both in sync. */
function getSellingPrice_(usd) {
  var p = toBdtPrice_(usd);
  var markup = 7;
  if (p > 200) markup = 30;
  else if (p > 100) markup = 25;
  else if (p > 70) markup = 21;
  else if (p > 40) markup = 16;
  else if (p > 20) markup = 12;
  return p + markup;
}

/**
 * Validates a service_code + quantity + client-claimed amount against the
 * server-side Prices sheet. Returns { ok:true, amount } with the
 * SERVER-COMPUTED amount (never trust the client's number), or
 * { ok:false, error, message }.
 */
function validateOrderPricing_(code, quantity, claimedAmount) {
  var dq = getDisqualifiedMap_()[String(code).trim()];
  if (dq) {
    return { ok: false, error: "service_disqualified", message: "এই সার্ভিসটি ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড খারাপ হওয়ায় বর্তমানে বন্ধ আছে। অন্য সার্ভিস বেছে নিন।" };
  }
  var priceIndex = loadPriceIndex_();
  var pkg = priceIndex[String(code).trim()];
  if (!pkg) {
    return { ok: false, error: "invalid_service", message: "Unknown service code." };
  }

  var qty = Number(quantity);
  if (!qty || qty <= 0 || isNaN(qty)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "invalid_quantity", message: "Invalid quantity." };
  }
  if (qty < pkg.min || qty > pkg.max) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "quantity_out_of_range",
      message: "Quantity must be between " + pkg.min + " and " + pkg.max + "."
    };
  }

  var unitPrice = getFinalUnitPrice_(code, pkg.price_usd);   // includes active admin discount
  var expectedAmount = Math.round(((unitPrice * qty) / 1000) * 100) / 100;
  var claimed = Number(claimedAmount) || 0;

  if (Math.abs(expectedAmount - claimed) > AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "amount_mismatch",
      message: "Price mismatch detected. Please reload the page and try again."
    };
  }

  return { ok: true, amount: expectedAmount };
}

function sheet_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_NAME);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.getSheets()[0];
  }
  return sh;
}

function ensureHeader_(sh) {
  if (sh.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sh.appendRow([
      "order_id", "service_code", "service_name", "platform", "link",
      "quantity", "amount", "txn_id", "paid_from", "whatsapp",
      "status", "created_at", "note", "proof_url", "comments", "proof_public_url"
    ]);
    return;
  }
  // Existing sheets: add proof_url column if missing
  // Existing sheets: add "comments" column (O) if its header is empty
  if (String(sh.getRange(1, 15).getValue() || "").trim() === "") {
    sh.getRange(1, 15).setValue("comments");
  }
  // Existing sheets: add "proof_public_url" column (P) if its header is empty
  if (String(sh.getRange(1, 16).getValue() || "").trim() === "") {
    sh.getRange(1, 16).setValue("proof_public_url");
  }
  // Existing sheets: add "provider_order_id" column (Q) if its header is empty
  if (String(sh.getRange(1, 17).getValue() || "").trim() === "") {
    sh.getRange(1, 17).setValue("provider_order_id");
  }
  if (String(sh.getRange(1, 18).getValue() || "").trim() === "") {
    sh.getRange(1, 18).setValue("forwarded_at");
  }
  if (String(sh.getRange(1, 19).getValue() || "").trim() === "") {
    sh.getRange(1, 19).setValue("completed_at");
  }
  var lastCol = sh.getLastColumn();
  if (lastCol < 14) {
    sh.getRange(1, 14).setValue("proof_url");
  } else {
    var h = String(sh.getRange(1, 14).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (h !== "proof_url") {
      // Only set if empty to avoid overwriting a custom header
      if (!h) sh.getRange(1, 14).setValue("proof_url");
    }
  }
}

/** Folder in the script owner's Drive that stores payment screenshots. */
function proofFolder_() {
  var FOLDER_NAME = "PrimeBoost Payment Proofs";
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(FOLDER_NAME);
  if (it.hasNext()) return it.next();
  return DriveApp.createFolder(FOLDER_NAME);
}

/**
 * Saves a base64-encoded JPEG/PNG as a Drive file and returns a
 * publicly viewable (anyone-with-link) URL suitable for <img src>.
 * Returns "" on failure so order creation is never blocked by Drive issues.
 */
/**
 * Returns { url: string, error: string }
 * url empty on failure — order creation must not depend on Drive succeeding.
 */
function saveProofToDrive_(orderId, base64, mime) {
  try {
    if (!base64 || String(base64).length < 50) {
      return { url: "", error: "empty_base64" };
    }
    var clean = String(base64).replace(/\s/g, "");
    var comma = clean.indexOf(",");
    if (comma !== -1) clean = clean.substring(comma + 1);

    // Guard against runaway payloads (Apps Script practical limit)
    if (clean.length > 3.5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return { url: "", error: "base64_too_large" };
    }

    var contentType = (mime && String(mime).indexOf("png") !== -1) ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg";
    var ext = contentType === "image/png" ? "png" : "jpg";
    var bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(clean);
    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(bytes, contentType, "proof_" + orderId + "." + ext);
    var folder = proofFolder_();
    var file = folder.createFile(blob);
    // Anyone-with-link VIEW so admin panel <img> and public gallery can load it
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    var url = "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" + file.getId() + "=w1200";
    return { url: url, error: "" };
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("saveProofToDrive_ error: " + err);
    // Most common: script not re-authorized after adding DriveApp (missing scope)
    return { url: "", error: String(err) };
  }
}

function normalizeTxn_(t) {
  return String(t || "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

// (normalizeMobile_ এর একটাই সংজ্ঞা নিচে আছে — এখানকার ডুপ্লিকেট সরানো হয়েছে)

/** For the public social-proof feed only — turns "1834706871" into
 *  "018***871": never expose a full customer number to every site visitor. */
function maskMobile_(m) {
  var digits = normalizeMobile_(m);
  if (!digits) return "";
  if (digits.length === 10) digits = "0" + digits; // stored without the leading 0
  if (digits.length < 7) return digits.replace(/./g, "*");
  return digits.substring(0, 3) + "***" + digits.substring(digits.length - 3);
}

function isValidTxnFormat_(txn, method) {
  var t = String(txn || "").trim();
  // Auto-wait placeholder until SMS attaches real TrxID
  if (/^WAIT[A-Za-z0-9]{6,14}$/i.test(t)) return true;
  if (!t || t.length < 6 || t.length > 20) {
    return false;
  }
  if (/\s/.test(t)) {
    return false;
  }
  if (/^(test|null|undefined|n\/a|none|0+)$/i.test(t)) {
    return false;
  }

  var m = String(method || "").toLowerCase();
  if (m === "rocket") {
    return /^\d{8,12}$/.test(t);
  }
  if (m === "nagad") {
    return /^[A-Za-z0-9]{6,12}$/.test(t);
  }
  if (m === "bkash") {
    return /^[A-Za-z0-9]{8,14}$/.test(t);
  }
  return /^[A-Za-z0-9]{6,20}$/.test(t);
}

function isValidMobile_(mobile) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  return /^01\d{9}$/.test(m);
}

// ---------- Fast txn/order lookup (incremental cache index) ----------
//
// Old approach: two separate full-sheet scans (getDataRange().getValues())
// on EVERY request — cost grows linearly (O(n)) with total orders and gets
// slower every day as the sheet grows.
//
// New approach: a Set of known txn_ids and order_ids is cached
// (CacheService, 6h TTL) alongside the row count it was built from. On each
// request we only read+scan the NEW rows appended since the cache was last
// built (sh.getLastRow() is a cheap metadata call), so cost stays ~O(1) in
// the common case instead of O(n). Falls back to a full scan automatically
// if the cache is empty/expired or exceeds the cache size limit.

var ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY = "primeboost_order_index_v1";
var ORDER_INDEX_TTL_SECONDS = 21600; // 6 hours (CacheService max)

function loadOrderIndex_(sh) {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  var cached = null;
  try {
    var raw = cache.get(ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY);
    if (raw) cached = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (ignore) {}

  var txns, orderIds, fromRow;
  if (cached && cached.rowCount <= lastRow) {
    txns = cached.txns;
    orderIds = cached.orderIds;
    fromRow = cached.rowCount + 1; // scan only rows added since cache was built
  } else {
    // No usable cache — rebuild from scratch (only happens on cold start / expiry).
    txns = {};
    orderIds = {};
    fromRow = 2; // row 1 is header
  }

  if (fromRow <= lastRow) {
    var numRows = lastRow - fromRow + 1;
    var newValues = sh.getRange(fromRow, 1, numRows, 8).getValues(); // cols A(order_id)..H(txn_id)
    for (var i = 0; i < newValues.length; i++) {
      var oid = String(newValues[i][0] || "").trim();
      var t = normalizeTxn_(newValues[i][7]);
      if (oid) orderIds[oid] = true;
      if (t) txns[t] = true;
    }
  }

  var index = { rowCount: lastRow, txns: txns, orderIds: orderIds };
  try {
    cache.put(ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(index), ORDER_INDEX_TTL_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {
    // Index grew past the ~100KB cache-value limit — safe to ignore, the
    // next request will simply rebuild from a full scan instead of a delta.
  }
  return index;
}

/** Call right after a successful appendRow() so the very next request
 *  (even within the same minute) doesn't need to rescan anything. */
function updateOrderIndexAfterInsert_(sh, orderId, txn) {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  try {
    var raw = cache.get(ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY);
    var index = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : { rowCount: sh.getLastRow() - 1, txns: {}, orderIds: {} };
    index.rowCount = sh.getLastRow();
    var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
    if (orderId) index.orderIds[String(orderId).trim()] = true;
    if (t) index.txns[t] = true;
    cache.put(ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(index), ORDER_INDEX_TTL_SECONDS);
  } catch (ignore) {
    // Cache write failing is harmless — worst case the next request rebuilds it.
  }
}

function txnExists_(sh, txn) {
  var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
  if (!t || t === "N/A" || t === "-") {
    return false;
  }
  var index = loadOrderIndex_(sh);
  return !!index.txns[t];
}

function orderIdExists_(sh, orderId) {
  var id = String(orderId || "").trim();
  if (!id) {
    return false;
  }
  var index = loadOrderIndex_(sh);
  return !!index.orderIds[id];
}

/** Auto-generate a unique numeric order ID (e.g. 63284).
 *  5 digits (10000-99999) first; if that range is nearly full it moves to 6 digits, then 7.
 *  Reads the live Order ID column once, so it never depends on a stale cache. */
function generateOrderId_(sh) {
  var used = {};
  try {
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last >= 2) {
      sh.getRange(2, COL_ORDERID, last - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
        var v = String(r[0] == null ? "" : r[0]).trim();
        if (v) used[v] = true;
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  for (var digits = 5; digits <= 7; digits++) {
    var min = Math.pow(10, digits - 1), span = 9 * min;
    for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 60; attempt++) {
      var id = String(min + Math.floor(Math.random() * span));
      if (!used[id]) return id;
    }
  }
  return String(Date.now()).slice(-8); // last-resort fallback, practically never reached
}

// ---------- Row lookup / status update helpers (used by admin panel + SMS webhook) ----------
// Column layout (1-indexed): A order_id, B service_code, C service_name, D platform,
// E link, F quantity, G amount, H txn_id, I paid_from, J whatsapp, K status, L created_at, M note, N proof_url

var COL_TXN = 8, COL_AMOUNT = 7, COL_STATUS = 11, COL_NOTE = 13, COL_ORDERID = 1, COL_METHOD = 9, COL_PROOF = 14;
var COL_SERVICE_CODE = 2, COL_LINK = 5, COL_QTY = 6, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID = 17;
var COL_FORWARDED_AT = 18, COL_COMPLETED_AT = 19;
var COL_COMMENTS = 15;   // Custom Comments সার্ভিসে কাস্টমারের কমেন্ট (column O)

/** Finds the sheet row number (1-indexed, header is row 1) for a given txn_id.
 *  Scans newest-first since recent orders are far more likely to be the match. */
function findRowByTxn_(sh, txn) {
  var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
  if (!t) return -1;
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return -1;
  var values = sh.getRange(2, COL_TXN, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (normalizeTxn_(values[i][0]) === t) return i + 2;
  }
  return -1;
}

/** কাস্টমার সাইটে প্রোভাইডারের অর্ডার আইডি দেখে; সে সেটা লিখলে তার নিজের অর্ডারগুলোর মধ্যে খুঁজে ভেতরের order_id বের করে।
 *  না মিললে (বা মোবাইল না থাকলে) যা লেখা ছিল তাই ফেরত — আগের আচরণ বদলায় না। অন্যের অর্ডারে মিলে যাওয়ার ঝুঁকি নেই (শুধু নিজের মোবাইলের সারি)। */
function resolveOrderIdForMobile_(mobile, typed) {
  var t = String(typed || "").trim();
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  if (!t || !m) return t;
  try {
    var sh = sheet_();
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last < 2) return t;
    var w = Math.max(COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID, 10);
    var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, w).getValues();
    var up = t.toUpperCase();
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (normalizeMobile_(vals[i][9]) !== m) continue;
      if (String(vals[i][COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID - 1] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === up) return String(vals[i][COL_ORDERID - 1]).trim();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return t;
}

function findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId) {
  var id = String(orderId || "").trim();
  if (!id) return -1;
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return -1;
  var values = sh.getRange(2, COL_ORDERID, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (String(values[i][0]).trim() === id) return i + 2;
  }
  return -1;
}

function rowToObject_(sh, row) {
  var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  var values = sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  var obj = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) obj[String(headers[i])] = values[i];
  return obj;
}

/** Appends to the note cell instead of overwriting, so manual notes and
 *  auto-verification notes never clobber each other. */
function appendNote_(sh, row, text) {
  var current = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_NOTE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var stamped = "[" + new Date().toLocaleString() + "] " + text;
  sh.getRange(row, COL_NOTE).setValue(current ? current + " | " + stamped : stamped);
}


function nowIsoDhaka_() {
  try {
    return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX");
  } catch (e) {
    return new Date().toISOString();
  }
}

/** Mark provider-forward start time (only once). */
function markForwardedAt_(sh, row) {
  var cur = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_FORWARDED_AT).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (cur) return cur;
  var iso = nowIsoDhaka_();
  sh.getRange(row, COL_FORWARDED_AT).setValue(iso);
  return iso;
}

/** Mark completion time (only once). Also writes the delivery-speed record
 *  (SpeedLog) — but only for orders that were really forwarded to a provider
 *  (provider order added + live tracking enabled). Wallet top-ups etc. never
 *  have a provider order id, so they are never counted. */
function markCompletedAt_(sh, row, status) {
  var cur = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_COMPLETED_AT).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (cur) {
    // আগে Partial হয়ে সময় বসে গেছে, এখন Completed হলে মেসেজ যাবে (ডুপ্লিকেট নিজে ঠেকায়)
    if (String(status || "").toLowerCase() === "completed") notifyOrderCompletedTelegram_(sh, row);
    return cur;
  }
  var iso = nowIsoDhaka_();
  sh.getRange(row, COL_COMPLETED_AT).setValue(iso);
  var st = String(status || "completed").toLowerCase();
  try {
    logDelivery_(sh, row, iso, st === "partial" ? "partial" : "completed");
  } catch (e) {}
  // সম্পন্ন হলে টেলিগ্রামে কার্ড (WhatsApp গ্রুপে ফরোয়ার্ড করার জন্য)। শুধু প্রথমবার যায়।
  if (st === "completed") notifyOrderCompletedTelegram_(sh, row);
  else if (st === "partial") notifyOrderPartialTelegram_(sh, row);
  return iso;
}

/** অর্ডার Partial হলে কার্ড + প্রোভাইডারের বাকি পরিমাণ + "খুলুন" বোতাম (সেখান থেকে রি-অর্ডার/রিফান্ডের সিদ্ধান্ত)। শুধু প্রথমবার, কখনো throw করে না। */
function notifyOrderPartialTelegram_(sh, row) {
  try {
    if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) return;
    var o = rowToObject_(sh, row);
    var oid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "");
    var mobile = o.whatsapp || "";
    var total = 0;
    try { total = getUserOrderStats_(mobile).total_orders; } catch (eCt) {}
    var card = buildOrderCard_(mobile, o.service_name, o.service_code, o.platform, o.quantity, o.amount, total, "⚠️ অর্ডার Partial", String(o.provider_order_id || oid || "").trim());
    var extra = "";
    try {
      var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
      var live = poid ? getLiveProviderStatus_(String(o.service_code || ""), poid) : null;
      if (live && live.remains !== undefined && live.remains !== null && live.remains !== "") extra = "\n🔻 বাকি (provider remains): " + live.remains + " / " + o.quantity;
    } catch (eLv) {}
    notifyTelegram_(card + extra + "\n🆔 " + oid, [[{ text: "🔎 খুলুন / সিদ্ধান্ত নিন", callback_data: "o:" + oid }]]);
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("partial-notify error: " + e.message);
  }
}

/** সম্পন্ন-মেসেজ আগে গেছে কিনা। না গিয়ে থাকলে এখনই "গেছে" লিখে রাখে (সর্বশেষ ২০০টি অর্ডার মনে রাখে)। */
function tgDoneSeen_(orderId) {
  try {
    var pr = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var arr = [];
    try { arr = JSON.parse(pr.getProperty("TG_DONE_IDS") || "[]") || []; } catch (e) {}
    var k = String(orderId);
    if (arr.indexOf(k) !== -1) return true;
    arr.push(k);
    if (arr.length > 200) arr = arr.slice(-200);
    pr.setProperty("TG_DONE_IDS", JSON.stringify(arr));
  } catch (e2) {}
  return false;
}

/** অর্ডার Completed হলে নতুন-অর্ডারের মতো একই কার্ড (শিরোনাম "✅ অর্ডার সম্পন্ন") +
 *  WhatsApp ফরোয়ার্ড ও খুলুন বাটনসহ টেলিগ্রামে পাঠায়। ওয়ালেট টপ-আপে যায় না।
 *  কখনো throw করে না। */
function notifyOrderCompletedTelegram_(sh, row) {
  try {
    if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) return;
    if (tgDoneSeen_(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue())) return;   // একই অর্ডারের জন্য একবারই
    var o = rowToObject_(sh, row);
    var mobile = o.whatsapp || "";
    var total = 0;
    try { total = getUserOrderStats_(mobile).total_orders; } catch (eCt) {}
    var card = buildOrderCard_(mobile, o.service_name, o.service_code, o.platform, o.quantity, o.amount, total, "✅ অর্ডার সম্পন্ন", String(o.provider_order_id || o.order_id || "").trim());
    notifyCardWa_(card, String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || ""));
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("completed-notify error: " + e.message);
  }
}

/** Parse sheet datetime / ISO to ms. */
function parseTimeMs_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return 0;
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]" && !isNaN(v.getTime())) return v.getTime();
  var s = String(v).trim();
  if (!s) return 0;
  var d = new Date(s);
  if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d.getTime();
  return 0;
}

function formatDurationBn_(ms) {
  if (!ms || ms < 0) ms = 0;
  var sec = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
  var h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
  var m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  var s = sec % 60;
  if (h > 0) return h + " ঘণ্টা " + m + " মিনিট " + s + " সেকেন্ড";
  if (m > 0) return m + " মিনিট " + s + " সেকেন্ড";
  return s + " সেকেন্ড";
}

/** Attach timer fields for admin / track responses. */
function attachTimerFields_(obj, status) {
  if (!obj) return obj;
  var fwd = obj.forwarded_at;
  var done = obj.completed_at;
  var fwdMs = parseTimeMs_(fwd);
  var doneMs = parseTimeMs_(done);
  var st = String(status || obj.status || "").toLowerCase();
  obj.forwarded_at = fwd ? String(fwd) : "";
  obj.completed_at = done ? String(done) : "";
  if (fwdMs) {
    obj.forwarded_at_ms = fwdMs;
    if (st === "completed" || st === "partial") {
      var endMs = doneMs || Date.now();
      obj.elapsed_ms = Math.max(0, endMs - fwdMs);
      obj.elapsed_label = formatDurationBn_(obj.elapsed_ms);
      obj.timer_running = false;
    } else if (st === "processing") {
      obj.elapsed_ms = Math.max(0, Date.now() - fwdMs);
      obj.elapsed_label = formatDurationBn_(obj.elapsed_ms);
      obj.timer_running = true;
    } else {
      obj.timer_running = false;
    }
  } else {
    obj.timer_running = false;
  }
  return obj;
}


// ---------- DELIVERY SPEED RECORD ----------
// প্রতিটি অর্ডার প্রোভাইডারে যোগ হওয়ার (provider order added + live tracking enabled)
// মুহূর্ত থেকে সম্পন্ন হওয়া পর্যন্ত কত সময় লাগল তা "SpeedLog" শিটে জমা হয়।
// সার্ভিসভিত্তিক সাম্প্রতিক রেকর্ড থেকে (১) সাইটে "পূর্বে এত সময়ে সম্পন্ন" ট্যাগ,
// (২) রেকর্ড খারাপ হলে সার্ভিস অটো-ডিসকোয়ালিফাই (সাইট থেকে হাইড + অর্ডার বন্ধ)।
// অ্যাডমিন চাইলে কোনো সার্ভিসকে exempt করতে বা আলাদা সময়-সীমা দিতে পারে।
var SPEEDLOG_SHEET = "SpeedLog";
var SPEED_WINDOW = 20;       // প্রতি সার্ভিসের সর্বশেষ কয়টি রেকর্ড বিবেচনা হবে
var SPEED_SCAN_ROWS = 4000;  // লগের শেষ কত সারি স্ক্যান হবে

function getSpeedConfig_() {
  var pr = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  return {
    min_samples: Number(pr.getProperty("SPEED_MIN_SAMPLES")) || 5,     // কমপক্ষে এতগুলো রেকর্ড না হলে বিচার হবে না
    max_hours: Number(pr.getProperty("SPEED_MAX_HOURS")) || 24,        // মিডিয়ান এর বেশি হলে খারাপ
    max_fail_pct: Number(pr.getProperty("SPEED_MAX_FAIL_PCT")) || 40   // ফেইল/রিজেক্ট % এর বেশি হলে খারাপ
  };
}

function speedLogSheet_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SPEEDLOG_SHEET);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(SPEEDLOG_SHEET);
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 8).setValues([["logged_at", "order_id", "service_code", "provider", "quantity", "secs", "result", "provider_order_id"]]);
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}

/** Appends one record. result: completed | partial | failed. Needs a provider
 *  order id AND forwarded_at, otherwise the order is not part of tracking. */
function logDelivery_(sh, row, iso, result) {
  var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var fwdMs = parseTimeMs_(sh.getRange(row, COL_FORWARDED_AT).getValue());
  if (!poid || !fwdMs) return;
  var orderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var code = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var qty = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
  var log = speedLogSheet_();
  var last = log.getLastRow();
  if (last >= 2) { // একই অর্ডার+ফলাফল দুইবার নয়
    var from = Math.max(2, last - 300);
    var vals = log.getRange(from, 2, last - from + 1, 6).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
      if (String(vals[i][0]) === orderId && String(vals[i][5]) === result) return;
    }
  }
  var endMs = parseTimeMs_(iso) || Date.now();
  var secs = Math.max(0, Math.round((endMs - fwdMs) / 1000));
  var provider = "";
  try { provider = providerForOrder_(code, poid); } catch (e) {}
  log.appendRow([iso, orderId, code, provider, qty, result === "failed" ? "" : secs, result, poid]);
  CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("SPEED_STATS_V1");
  CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(SVC_HIST_CACHE_KEY);
}

function medianOf_(arr) {
  if (!arr.length) return 0;
  var a = arr.slice().sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; });
  var m = Math.floor(a.length / 2);
  return a.length % 2 ? a[m] : Math.round((a[m - 1] + a[m]) / 2);
}

/** { code: {n, fail, partial, avg_sec, med_sec, last_sec} } — সর্বশেষ SPEED_WINDOW টি রেকর্ডের ভিত্তিতে। */
function getSpeedStats_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var cached = cache.get("SPEED_STATS_V1");
  if (cached) { try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {} }
  var out = {};
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var log = ss.getSheetByName(SPEEDLOG_SHEET);
    if (log && log.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var last = log.getLastRow();
      var cnt = Math.min(last - 1, SPEED_SCAN_ROWS);
      var vals = log.getRange(last - cnt + 1, 1, cnt, 7).getValues();
      var per = {};
      for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { // নতুন থেকে পুরনো
        var code = String(vals[i][2] || "").trim();
        if (!code) continue;
        var res = String(vals[i][6] || "");
        if (!per[code]) per[code] = [];
        if (per[code].length >= SPEED_WINDOW) continue;
        per[code].push({ secs: Number(vals[i][5]) || 0, res: res });
      }
      Object.keys(per).forEach(function (code) {
        var rec = per[code], ok = [], fail = 0, partial = 0;
        rec.forEach(function (r) {
          if (r.res === "completed" && r.secs > 0) ok.push(r.secs);
          else if (r.res === "failed") fail++;
          else if (r.res === "partial") partial++;
        });
        var sum = 0; ok.forEach(function (x) { sum += x; });
        out[code] = {
          n: ok.length, fail: fail, partial: partial, total: rec.length,
          avg_sec: ok.length ? Math.round(sum / ok.length) : 0,
          med_sec: medianOf_(ok),
          last_sec: ok.length ? ok[0] : 0
        };
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try { cache.put("SPEED_STATS_V1", JSON.stringify(out), 600); } catch (e) {}
  return out;
}

/** সাইটে দেখানোর জন্য: শুধু সম্পন্ন রেকর্ড আছে এমন সার্ভিস। */
function getPublicSpeedStats_() {
  var st = getSpeedStats_(), out = {};
  Object.keys(st).forEach(function (c) {
    var x = st[c];
    if (!x.n) return;
    out[c] = { n: x.n, med_sec: x.med_sec, avg_sec: x.avg_sec, label: formatDurationShortBn_(x.med_sec * 1000) };
  });
  return out;
}

/** ছোট বাংলা সময়: ৩ ঘণ্টা ১২ মিনিট / ২৫ মিনিট / ৪০ সেকেন্ড */
function formatDurationShortBn_(ms) {
  var sec = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  var d = Math.floor(sec / 86400), h = Math.floor((sec % 86400) / 3600), m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  if (d > 0) return d + " দিন" + (h ? " " + h + " ঘণ্টা" : "");
  if (h > 0) return h + " ঘণ্টা" + (m ? " " + m + " মিনিট" : "");
  if (m > 0) return m + " মিনিট";
  return sec + " সেকেন্ড";
}

/** { code: "কারণ" } — রেকর্ড খারাপ হিসেবে চিহ্নিত সার্ভিস (exempt বাদে)।
 *  এগুলো অটো-বন্ধ হয় না; অ্যাডমিন প্যানেলের "রেকর্ড খারাপ" তালিকায় যায়, অ্যাডমিন যাচাই করবে। */
function getBadRecordMap_() {
  var out = {};
  try {
    var cfg = getSpeedConfig_();
    var meta = getServiceMeta_();
    var st = getSpeedStats_();
    Object.keys(st).forEach(function (c) {
      if (meta.exempt.indexOf(c) !== -1) return;
      var x = st[c];
      if (x.total < cfg.min_samples) return;
      var limitH = Number(meta.limits[c]) || cfg.max_hours;
      var failPct = x.total ? Math.round((x.fail / x.total) * 100) : 0;
      if (x.n >= cfg.min_samples && x.med_sec > limitH * 3600) {
        out[c] = "slow: মিডিয়ান " + formatDurationShortBn_(x.med_sec * 1000) + " > সীমা " + limitH + " ঘণ্টা";
      } else if (failPct > cfg.max_fail_pct) {
        out[c] = "fail: " + x.fail + "/" + x.total + " অর্ডার ব্যর্থ (" + failPct + "%)";
      }
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  return out;
}

/** { code: "কারণ" } — অ্যাডমিন যাচাই করে নিশ্চিত করেছে এমন সার্ভিস: সাইট থেকে হাইড + অর্ডার বন্ধ। */
function getDisqualifiedMap_() {
  var out = {};
  try {
    var meta = getServiceMeta_();
    var bad = getBadRecordMap_();
    meta.confirmed.forEach(function (c) {
      if (meta.exempt.indexOf(c) !== -1) return;
      out[c] = "অ্যাডমিন নিশ্চিত" + (bad[c] ? " (" + bad[c] + ")" : "");
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  return out;
}

/** অ্যাডমিন রিপোর্ট: সব ট্র্যাক করা সার্ভিস, ধীর → দ্রুত। */
function buildSpeedReport_() {
  var st = getSpeedStats_(), dq = getBadRecordMap_(), cf = getDisqualifiedMap_(), rows = [];
  Object.keys(st).forEach(function (c) {
    var x = st[c];
    rows.push({ code: c, n: x.n, fail: x.fail, partial: x.partial, total: x.total,
      med_sec: x.med_sec, avg_sec: x.avg_sec, last_sec: x.last_sec,
      med_label: x.med_sec ? formatDurationShortBn_(x.med_sec * 1000) : "—",
      disqualified: !!dq[c], confirmed: !!cf[c], reason: dq[c] || cf[c] || "" });
  });
  rows.sort(function (a, b) { return b.med_sec - a.med_sec; });
  return rows;
}

/** টাইম-ট্রিগারে চলে: Processing অর্ডারের লাইভ স্ট্যাটাস চেক করে Completed/Partial
 *  হলে সঙ্গে সঙ্গে সময় রেকর্ড করে (কাস্টমার ট্র্যাক পেজ না খুললেও)। */

function sweepProcessingOrders() {
  var sh = sheet_();
  ensureHeader_(sh);
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last < 2) return 0;
  var cnt = Math.min(last - 1, 600);
  var start = last - cnt + 1;
  var vals = sh.getRange(start, 1, cnt, COL_COMPLETED_AT).getValues();
  var checked = 0, changed = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < vals.length && checked < 50; i++) {
    var r = vals[i];
    var st = String(r[COL_STATUS - 1] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var poid = String(r[COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID - 1] || "").trim();
    if (st !== "processing" || !poid) continue;
    checked++;
    var live = getLiveProviderStatus_(String(r[COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || ""), poid);
    if (!live) continue;
    // প্রোভাইডার remains=0 দেখালে লিংক যাচাই করে সঙ্গে সঙ্গে সম্পন্ন ধরা (টাইম কাউন্ট বন্ধ)
    var pre = checkRemainsZeroComplete_(sh, String(r[COL_ORDERID - 1] || ""), String(r[COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || ""), live, "Processing");
    if (String(pre).toLowerCase() === "completed") { changed++; continue; }
    var after = syncStatusFromLive_(sh, String(r[COL_ORDERID - 1] || ""), live.status, "Processing");
    if (String(after).toLowerCase() !== "processing") changed++;
    var mappedRej = mapProviderStatus_(live.status, "Processing");
    // প্রোভাইডার নিজে cancel করলে সাইটের স্ট্যাটাস বদলায় না (অ্যাডমিন সিদ্ধান্ত নেয়), কিন্তু ব্যর্থতা রেকর্ডে যায়
    if (String(mappedRej).toLowerCase() === "rejected") {
      try { logDelivery_(sh, start + i, nowIsoDhaka_(), "failed"); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  return changed;
}

/** এডিটরের Run ড্রপডাউনে দেখা যাবে: প্রতি ৫ মিনিটে sweepProcessingOrders চালু করে। */
function setupSpeedSweepTrigger() {
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) {
    if (t.getHandlerFunction() === "sweepProcessingOrders") ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t);
  });
  ScriptApp.newTrigger("sweepProcessingOrders").timeBased().everyMinutes(5).create();
  return "ok";
}

function setStatus_(sh, row, status, noteText) {
  sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).setValue(status);
  var st = String(status || "").toLowerCase();
  if (st === "completed" || st === "partial") {
    markCompletedAt_(sh, row, st);
  }
  if (st === "rejected") {
    try { logDelivery_(sh, row, nowIsoDhaka_(), "failed"); } catch (e) {}
    // ওয়ালেট-পেমেন্ট নয় এমন অর্ডার Reject হলে রিফান্ড মেসেজ আসে না — তাই এখানে আলাদা বিস্তারিত জানানো
    try {
      var rjMethod = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_METHOD).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      var rjTxn = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_TXN).getValue() || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      var rjCode = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
      if (rjMethod !== "wallet" && rjTxn.indexOf("WALLET") !== 0 && rjCode.toUpperCase() !== "TOPUP") {
        var rjLink = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_LINK).getValue() || "").trim();
        if (rjLink.length > 60) rjLink = rjLink.substring(0, 57) + "...";
        notifyTelegram_("❌ অর্ডার Reject হয়েছে (ওয়ালেট পেমেন্ট নয়, অটো-রিফান্ড নেই)\n" +
          String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim() +
          "\nমোবাইল: " + normalizeMobile_(sh.getRange(row, 10).getValue()) +
          "\n৳" + (Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_AMOUNT).getValue()) || 0) +
          "\nকারণ: " + (noteText || "উল্লেখ নেই") +
          (rjCode ? "\nসার্ভিস কোড: " + rjCode : "") +
          "\nপরিমাণ: " + String(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue() || "") +
          (rjLink ? "\nলিংক: " + rjLink : "") +
          "\nপেমেন্ট: " + (rjMethod || "—") + (rjTxn ? " / " + rjTxn : ""));
      }
    } catch (eRj) {}
  }
  if (st === "processing") {
    // If already has provider order id, ensure timer start exists
    var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
    if (poid) markForwardedAt_(sh, row);
  }
  if (noteText) appendNote_(sh, row, noteText);
}

// ---------- Editor-run buttons ----------
// The Apps Script "Run" dropdown hides every function whose name ends with an
// underscore (those are treated as private). The one-time setup functions above
// end with "_", so these public wrappers (no underscore) are what appear in the
// dropdown. They just call the originals — nothing else changed.
function setupProviderKeys() { setupProviderKeys_(); }
function setupSecrets() { setupSecrets_(); }
function setupTelegram() { setupTelegram_(); }
/** CAUTION: replaces ADMIN_TOKEN and SMS_WEBHOOK_TOKEN — only run if a token leaked. */
function rotateSecrets() { rotateSecrets_(); }

/** { "provider.com": {api_url, api_key}, ... } — set once via setupProviderKeys_(). */
function getProviderConfig_() {
  var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("PROVIDER_CONFIG");
  if (!raw) return {};
  try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { return {}; }
}


/** Fetch one panel balance via action=balance. Returns {provider, balance, currency, ok, error}. */
function fetchOneBalance_(provider, cfg) {
  var out = { provider: provider, balance: null, currency: "USD", ok: false, error: "" };
  if (!cfg || !cfg.api_url || !cfg.api_key) {
    out.error = "no_key";
    return out;
  }
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, {
      method: "post",
      payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "balance" },
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      followRedirects: true
    });
    var body = resp.getContentText();
    var json = null;
    try { json = JSON.parse(body); } catch (e) {}
    if (json && json.error) {
      out.error = String(json.error);
      return out;
    }
    if (json && (json.balance !== undefined && json.balance !== null)) {
      out.balance = Number(json.balance);
      out.currency = json.currency || "USD";
      out.ok = true;
      return out;
    }
    out.error = "unexpected";
    out.raw = body.substring(0, 120);
  } catch (err) {
    out.error = err.message || "fetch_failed";
  }
  return out;
}

/** Admin: list balances for all configured providers (or one if data.provider set). */
function handleAdminBalances_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  }
  var cfgAll = getProviderConfig_();
  var only = data.provider ? String(data.provider).trim() : "";
  var names = [];
  if (only) {
    if (cfgAll[only]) names = [only];
    else return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unknown_provider", provider: only });
  } else {
    names = Object.keys(cfgAll);
  }
  // Parallel fetch — much faster than sequential for ~20 panels
  var requests = [];
  var mapIdx = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    var c = cfgAll[names[i]];
    if (!c || !c.api_url || !c.api_key) continue;
    mapIdx.push(names[i]);
    requests.push({
      url: c.api_url,
      method: "post",
      payload: { key: c.api_key, action: "balance" },
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      followRedirects: true
    });
  }
  var balances = [];
  try {
    var responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(requests);
    for (var j = 0; j < responses.length; j++) {
      var prov = mapIdx[j];
      var out = { provider: prov, balance: null, currency: "USD", ok: false, error: "" };
      try {
        var body = responses[j].getContentText();
        var json = null;
        try { json = JSON.parse(body); } catch (e2) {}
        if (json && json.error) {
          out.error = String(json.error);
        } else if (json && json.balance !== undefined && json.balance !== null) {
          out.balance = Number(json.balance);
          out.currency = json.currency || "USD";
          out.ok = true;
        } else {
          out.error = "unexpected";
        }
      } catch (e3) {
        out.error = e3.message || "parse_fail";
      }
      balances.push(out);
    }
  } catch (eAll) {
    // fallback sequential
    for (var k = 0; k < names.length; k++) {
      balances.push(fetchOneBalance_(names[k], cfgAll[names[k]]));
    }
  }
  balances.sort(function (a, b) {
    if (a.ok && b.ok) return (b.balance || 0) - (a.balance || 0);
    if (a.ok) return -1;
    if (b.ok) return 1;
    return String(a.provider).localeCompare(String(b.provider));
  });

  // After top-up: any provider with balance > 0 → retry all held (funds-low) orders
  var autoRetry = data.auto_retry !== false && data.auto_retry !== "false";
  var retrySummary = null;
  if (autoRetry) {
    try {
      var anyFunds = false;
      for (var bi = 0; bi < balances.length; bi++) {
        if (balances[bi].ok && Number(balances[bi].balance) > 0.001) {
          anyFunds = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (anyFunds) {
        retrySummary = retryHeldProviderOrders_(null);
      }
    } catch (eRetry) {
      retrySummary = { error: String(eRetry) };
    }
  }

  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, balances: balances, held_retry: retrySummary });
}



/** code -> { provider, refill } from packages-full.json (tags include Refill). */
function loadServiceMetaIndex_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var cached = cache.get("SERVICE_META_IDX");
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {}
  }
  var idx = {};
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var arr = parsePkgs_(resp.getContentText());
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      var p = arr[i];
      if (!p || p.code == null) continue;
      var tags = p.tags || [];
      var refill = false;
      for (var t = 0; t < tags.length; t++) {
        if (String(tags[t]).toLowerCase().indexOf("refill") !== -1) { refill = true; break; }
      }
      // name hint
      if (!refill && /refill/i.test(String(p.name || ""))) refill = true;
      idx[String(p.code)] = { provider: p.provider || "", refill: refill };
    }
    customServices_().forEach(function (c) {
      if (!c || c.code == null || idx[String(c.code)]) return;
      var ctags = c.tags || [], crefill = /refill/i.test(String(c.name || "")) || String(c.refill || "").toLowerCase() === "yes";
      for (var ct = 0; ct < ctags.length; ct++) { if (String(ctags[ct]).toLowerCase().indexOf("refill") !== -1) { crefill = true; break; } }
      idx[String(c.code)] = { provider: c.provider || "", refill: crefill };
    });
    cache.put("SERVICE_META_IDX", JSON.stringify(idx), PRICE_CACHE_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {}
  return idx;
}

/** Ask the provider API whether this service_id has refill enabled. */
function providerServiceAllowsRefill_(cfg, serviceCode) {
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, {
      method: "post",
      payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "services" },
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    var arr = JSON.parse(resp.getContentText());
    if (!arr || !arr.length) return null; // unknown
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      if (String(arr[i].service) === String(serviceCode)) {
        var r = arr[i].refill;
        if (r === true || r === 1 || r === "1" || r === "true" || r === "yes") return true;
        if (r === false || r === 0 || r === "0" || r === "false" || r === "no") return false;
        // missing field — check name
        if (/refill/i.test(String(arr[i].name || ""))) return true;
        return false;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}

// ---------- Provider service capabilities (Refill / Cancel flags from the provider "services" API) ----------
// The provider's own service list says which services have refill / cancel. We cache one compact map per provider
// (code -> bit1 refill, bit2 cancel) for 15 minutes, so building the Order History page never calls the provider per order.
var _providerFlagsMemo_ = {};
function flagTrue_(v) { return v === true || v === 1 || v === "1" || v === "true" || v === "yes" || v === "Yes"; }
function cacheBigGet_(key) {
  var c = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var n = Number(c.get(key + "_n") || 0);
  if (!n) return null;
  var parts = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { var p = c.get(key + "_" + i); if (p == null) return null; parts.push(p); }
  return parts.join("");
}
function cacheBigPut_(key, str, sec) {
  var CH = 90000, n = Math.ceil(str.length / CH) || 1, o = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) o[key + "_" + i] = str.substr(i * CH, CH);
  o[key + "_n"] = String(n);
  CacheService.getScriptCache().putAll(o, sec);
}
function providerFlags_(provider) {
  if (_providerFlagsMemo_[provider] !== undefined) return _providerFlagsMemo_[provider];
  var out = null, key = "PSVCFL_" + String(provider).replace(/[^\w.-]/g, "_").substring(0, 60);
  try {
    var raw = cacheBigGet_(key);
    if (raw != null) {
      out = raw === "-" ? null : JSON.parse(raw);
    } else {
      var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
      if (cfg && cfg.api_key && cfg.api_url) {
        var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, { method: "post", payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "services" }, muteHttpExceptions: true });
        var arr = JSON.parse(resp.getContentText());
        if (arr && arr.length) {
          out = {};
          arr.forEach(function (sv) {
            var refill = sv.refill !== undefined ? flagTrue_(sv.refill) : /refill/i.test(String(sv.name || ""));
            out[String(sv.service)] = (refill ? 1 : 0) | (flagTrue_(sv.cancel) ? 2 : 0);
          });
        }
      }
      cacheBigPut_(key, out ? JSON.stringify(out) : "-", out ? 900 : 120);
    }
  } catch (e) { out = null; }
  _providerFlagsMemo_[provider] = out;
  return out;
}
/** { refill: true|false|null, cancel: true|false|null }  (null = provider list unavailable / service unknown) */
function serviceCaps_(serviceCode) {
  var code = String(serviceCode == null ? "" : serviceCode).trim();
  var provider = code ? loadProviderIndex_()[code] : "";
  if (!provider) return { refill: null, cancel: null };
  var m = providerFlags_(provider);
  if (!m || m[code] === undefined) return { refill: null, cancel: null };
  return { refill: !!(m[code] & 1), cancel: !!(m[code] & 2) };
}

/** Cancel on the provider panel via API v2: action=cancel&orders=<provider_order_id>.
 *  Only runs for services the provider lists with cancel enabled. Returns {ok, ...}; never throws. */
function providerCancelCore_(sh, row, orderId, who) {
  var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var code = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var provider = providerForOrder_(code, poid);
  if (!poid || !provider) return { ok: false, error: "no_provider_order" };
  if (getPinByPoid_(code, poid)) {   // ব্যাকআপ প্রোভাইডারের অর্ডার — cancel-ক্ষমতা অজানা, তাই নিরাপদে ব্লক
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel blocked: order is on a backup provider (" + provider + ") — cancel from the provider panel.");
    return { ok: false, error: "cancel_not_supported" };
  }
  var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
  if (!cfg || !cfg.api_key || !cfg.api_url) return { ok: false, error: "no_api_key" };
  if (serviceCaps_(code).cancel !== true) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel blocked: service " + code + " has no cancel option on " + provider);
    return { ok: false, error: "cancel_not_supported" };
  }
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, { method: "post", payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "cancel", orders: poid }, muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var body = resp.getContentText(), json = null;
    try { json = JSON.parse(body); } catch (eJ) {}
    if (json && !Array.isArray(json) && json.error) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel FAILED (" + provider + "): " + json.error);
      return { ok: false, error: String(json.error) };
    }
    var item = Array.isArray(json) ? (json.filter(function (x) { return String(x && x.order) === poid; })[0] || json[0]) : json;
    var c = item && item.cancel;
    if (c === 1 || c === "1" || c === true || c === "true" || String(c).toLowerCase() === "ok") {
      try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("LIVESTATUS_" + poid); } catch (eC) {}
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel sent via " + who + " → " + provider + " order " + poid + " (API).");
      return { ok: true, provider: provider, provider_order_id: poid };
    }
    var why = (c && typeof c === "object") ? (c.error || JSON.stringify(c)) : ((item && item.error) || body.substring(0, 120));
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel FAILED (" + provider + "): " + String(why).substring(0, 150));
    return { ok: false, error: String(why).substring(0, 150) };
  } catch (err) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Cancel error: " + err.message);
    return { ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

/** { service_code: "provider.com", ... } — read live from the same
 *  packages-full.json used for price validation, cached the same way. */
function loadProviderIndex_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var cached = cache.get("PROVIDER_INDEX");
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {}
  }
  var idx = {};
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var arr = parsePkgs_(resp.getContentText());
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      if (arr[i] && arr[i].provider) idx[String(arr[i].code)] = arr[i].provider;
    }
    customServices_().forEach(function (c) {
      if (c && c.code != null && c.provider && !idx[String(c.code)]) idx[String(c.code)] = c.provider;
    });
    cache.put("PROVIDER_INDEX", JSON.stringify(idx), PRICE_CACHE_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) { /* leave idx empty — forwarding will just be skipped */ }
  return idx;
}

/** Calls the provider panel's "status" action for one order and returns
 *  {charge, start_count, remains, status} (provider's own field names),
 *  or null if anything about it fails — the caller falls back to our own
 *  stored status in that case. Cached briefly so a customer refreshing
 *  Track Order repeatedly doesn't hammer the provider's API. */
function getLiveProviderStatus_(serviceCode, providerOrderId) {
  if (!providerOrderId) return null;
  var cacheKey = "LIVESTATUS_" + providerOrderId;
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {}
  }
  var provider = providerForOrder_(serviceCode, providerOrderId);
  if (!provider) return null;
  var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
  if (!cfg || !cfg.api_key || !cfg.api_url) return null;
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, {
      method: "post",
      payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "status", order: providerOrderId },
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    var json = JSON.parse(resp.getContentText());
    if (json && !json.error) {
      cache.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(json), 60); // 60s — enough to stop refresh-spam, still "real-time" to a person
      return json;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}

/** Maps a provider panel's status wording onto the ones our own tracking
 *  UI already knows how to color/label (see buildOrderDetailHTML). Only
 *  used for *display* — never overwrites our own sheet's status column,
 *  so admin logic (auto-forward, re-approval, etc.) is unaffected. */
function mapProviderStatus_(providerStatus, ourStatus) {
  var s = String(providerStatus || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (s === "completed") return "Completed";
  if (s === "in progress" || s === "processing") return "Processing";
  if (s === "partial") return "Partial";
  if (s === "canceled" || s === "cancelled") return "Rejected";
  if (s === "pending") return "Processing"; // provider accepted, still queued
  return ourStatus; // unknown wording — trust our own status instead
}

/** Places the order on its provider panel once it's approved (status ->
 *  "Processing"). Called from handleAdminUpdate_ and the SMS auto-verify
 *  path. Any problem is written to the Note column, never thrown — a
 *  failed auto-forward should never undo the approval itself. */

/** Sync sheet status from provider live status (Completed / Partial).
 *  Only upgrades Processing/Partial → Partial or Completed. Never touches
 *  Rejected or already-Completed. Returns the status string to show. */
function syncStatusFromLive_(sh, orderId, liveStatus, currentStatus) {
  var mapped = mapProviderStatus_(liveStatus, currentStatus);
  var cur = String(currentStatus || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var mapL = String(mapped || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cur === "rejected" || cur === "completed") return currentStatus;
  if (mapL !== "completed" && mapL !== "partial") return currentStatus;
  if (cur === mapL) return mapped;
  // Only move forward: processing→partial/completed, partial→completed
  if (cur === "processing" || cur === "partial" || cur === "approved") {
    try {
      var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, String(orderId || ""));
      if (row !== -1) {
        var sheetSt = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (sheetSt === "rejected" || sheetSt === "completed") return currentStatus;
        // ওয়ালেট অর্ডারে প্রোভাইডার Partial দিলে এখনই Partial/রিফান্ড নয় — অটো-রিট্রাই ফ্লো (runAutoRefundSweep) আগে বাকিটা আবার পাঠাবে
        if (mapL === "partial" && sheetSt !== "partial" && partialAutoEligible_(sh, row)) return currentStatus;
        if (sheetSt !== mapL) {
          sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).setValue(mapped);
          markCompletedAt_(sh, row, mapL);
          appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-marked " + mapped + " from provider live status.");
        }
        return mapped;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return currentStatus;
}



// ---------- REMAINS = 0 → লিংক যাচাই → সম্পন্ন (রিয়েল-টাইম ডেলিভারি টাইম) ----------
// প্রোভাইডার "Completed" দেরিতে দেখায়। তাই প্রোভাইডারের status API-তে remains = 0 দেখালে
// অর্ডারের লিংক খুলে বর্তমান সংখ্যা পড়া হয়। বর্তমান সংখ্যা >= start_count + অর্ডার পরিমাণ হলে
// অর্ডার "Completed" ধরা হয় ও টাইম কাউন্ট বন্ধ হয়। লিংক পড়া না গেলে অর্ডার Processing-ই থাকে
// এবং নোটে [R0-UNVERIFIED] লেখা হয় (অ্যাডমিন হাতে যাচাই করবে)।

/** "1,234" / "1 234" / "1.2K" / "3.4M" → { n, exact } । পড়া না গেলে null। */
function parseCountText_(t) {
  var x = String(t || "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, "");
  if (!x) return null;
  var m = x.match(/^([\d.,]+)([KkMmBb]?)$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  var num = m[1], suf = m[2].toLowerCase();
  if (suf) {
    var v = parseFloat(num.replace(/,/g, ""));
    if (isNaN(v)) return null;
    return { n: Math.round(v * ({ k: 1e3, m: 1e6, b: 1e9 })[suf]), exact: false };
  }
  if (/^\d{1,3}(\.\d{3})+$/.test(num)) num = num.replace(/\./g, ""); // 1.234 = ১২৩৪
  num = num.replace(/,/g, "");
  var n = Number(num);
  if (isNaN(n)) return null;
  return { n: n, exact: true };
}

/** সার্ভিসের নাম দেখে কোন সংখ্যা মেলাতে হবে। */
function metricKind_(serviceName) {
  var s = String(serviceName || "").toLowerCase();
  if (/follow|subscrib|member|সাবস্ক|ফলো/.test(s)) return "followers";
  if (/like|react|heart|love|লাইক|রিয়্যাক|রিয়াক/.test(s)) return "likes";
  if (/view|watch|play|ভিউ/.test(s)) return "views";
  if (/share|শেয়ার/.test(s)) return "shares";
  if (/comment|কমেন্ট/.test(s)) return "comments";
  return "";
}

function fetchLinkHtml_(link) {
  var l = String(link || "").trim();
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(l)) return { ok: false, reason: "invalid link" };
  var low = l.toLowerCase();
  var url = l;
  var tg = l.match(/^https?:\/\/(?:www\.)?(?:t|telegram)\.me\/(?:s\/)?([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\/(\d+)/i);
  if (tg) url = "https://t.me/" + tg[1] + "/" + tg[2] + "?embed=1&mode=tme"; // পোস্ট ভিউ
  var ua = (low.indexOf("facebook.com") !== -1 || low.indexOf("fb.com") !== -1 || low.indexOf("fb.watch") !== -1 || low.indexOf("instagram.com") !== -1)
    ? "facebookexternalhit/1.1 (+http://www.facebook.com/externalhit_uatext.php)"
    : "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36";
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
      method: "get", muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true,
      headers: { "User-Agent": ua, "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9" }
    });
    var code = resp.getResponseCode();
    if (code !== 200) return { ok: false, reason: "HTTP " + code };
    return { ok: true, html: resp.getContentText().substring(0, 800000), url: url };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "fetch error" };
  }
}

/** পেজের HTML থেকে নির্দিষ্ট ধরনের সংখ্যা বের করে। { count, exact, src } বা { count:null, reason } */
function readLinkCount_(link, kind) {
  if (!kind) return { count: null, reason: "metric unknown (service name)" };
  var f = fetchLinkHtml_(link);
  if (!f.ok) return { count: null, reason: f.reason };
  var html = f.html, low = String(link).toLowerCase(), raw = null, src = "";
  function pick(re) { var m = html.match(re); return m ? m[1] : null; }
  var og = pick(/<meta[^>]+(?:property|name)=["']og:description["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']*)["']/i) ||
           pick(/<meta[^>]+content=["']([^"']*)["'][^>]+(?:property|name)=["']og:description["']/i) || "";
  function ogPick(word) { var m = og.match(new RegExp("([\\d.,]+\\s?[KkMmBb]?)\\s*" + word, "i")); return m ? m[1] : null; }

  if (/(^|\/\/)(www\.)?(t|telegram)\.me\//.test(low)) {
    if (/\/\d+(\?|$|\/)/.test(low.replace(/^https?:\/\/[^\/]+/, "")) && kind === "views") {
      raw = pick(/tgme_widget_message_views">([\d.,KkMm\s]+)</); src = "telegram post views";
    } else if (kind === "followers") {
      raw = pick(/tgme_page_extra[^>]*>\s*([\d\s,.]+[KkMm]?)\s*(?:subscribers|members)/i); src = "telegram members";
    }
  } else if (low.indexOf("tiktok.com") !== -1) {
    var key = { views: "playCount", likes: "diggCount", shares: "shareCount", comments: "commentCount", followers: "followerCount" }[kind];
    if (key) { raw = pick(new RegExp('"' + key + '":\\s*"?(\\d+)')); src = "tiktok " + kind; }
  } else if (low.indexOf("youtube.com") !== -1 || low.indexOf("youtu.be") !== -1) {
    if (kind === "views") { raw = pick(/"viewCount":"(\d+)"/) || pick(/itemprop="interactionCount"\s+content="(\d+)"/); src = "youtube views"; }
    else if (kind === "likes") { raw = pick(/"likeCount":"?(\d+)/) || pick(/"label":"([\d,]+) likes"/); src = "youtube likes"; }
    else if (kind === "followers") { raw = pick(/"subscriberCountText":\{"simpleText":"([\d.,]+[KMB]?) subscribers?"/); src = "youtube subscribers"; }
    else if (kind === "comments") { raw = pick(/"commentCount":"?(\d+)/); src = "youtube comments"; }
  }
  if (raw === null) { // সাধারণ ফলব্যাক: og:description (Instagram/Facebook ইত্যাদি)
    var word = { followers: "(?:followers|subscribers)", likes: "likes", comments: "comments", views: "views", shares: "shares" }[kind];
    if (word) { raw = ogPick(word); if (raw) src = "og:description " + kind; }
  }
  if (raw === null) return { count: null, reason: "count not found on page (login wall / JS page?)" };
  var pc = parseCountText_(raw);
  if (!pc) return { count: null, reason: "unparseable: " + String(raw).substring(0, 20) };
  return { count: pc.n, exact: pc.exact, src: src };
}

/** Processing অর্ডারে প্রোভাইডার remains=0 দেখালে লিংক যাচাই করে Completed করে।
 *  Completed করলে "Completed" ফেরত দেয়, নইলে currentStatus। কখনো throw করে না। */
function checkRemainsZeroComplete_(sh, orderId, serviceCode, live, currentStatus) {
  try {
    if (!live || String(currentStatus || "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "processing") return currentStatus;
    var ps = String(live.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (ps === "completed" || ps === "partial" || ps === "canceled" || ps === "cancelled") return currentStatus;
    if (live.remains === undefined || live.remains === null || String(live.remains).trim() === "" || Number(live.remains) !== 0) return currentStatus;
    if (live.start_count === undefined || live.start_count === null || String(live.start_count).trim() === "") return currentStatus;
    var start = Number(live.start_count);
    if (isNaN(start)) return currentStatus;

    var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(), ck = "R0CHK_" + orderId;
    if (cache.get(ck)) return currentStatus;            // একই অর্ডারে ৪ মিনিটে একবারের বেশি পেজ খুলব না
    cache.put(ck, "1", 240);

    var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
    if (row === -1) return currentStatus;
    var rv = sh.getRange(row, 1, 1, COL_COMPLETED_AT).getValues()[0];
    if (String(rv[COL_STATUS - 1] || "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "processing") return currentStatus;
    var link = String(rv[COL_LINK - 1] || "").trim();
    var qty = Number(rv[COL_QTY - 1]) || 0;
    var kind = metricKind_(String(rv[2] || ""));
    var note = String(rv[COL_NOTE - 1] || "");
    var target = start + qty;

    var res = readLinkCount_(link, kind);
    if (res && res.count !== null && res.count !== undefined) {
      // সংখ্যা সংক্ষেপে (1.2M) পাওয়া গেলে স্পষ্টভাবে লক্ষ্যের ওপরে না থাকলে নিশ্চিত ধরা হয় না
      var ok = res.exact ? (res.count >= target) : (res.count >= target * 1.01);
      if (ok) {
        sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).setValue("Completed");
        markCompletedAt_(sh, row, "completed");
        appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-completed: provider remains 0 + link verified (" + kind + " " + res.count + " >= start " + start + " + qty " + qty + " = " + target + "; " + res.src + ").");
        return "Completed";
      }
      if (note.indexOf("R0-WAIT") === -1) {
        appendNote_(sh, row, "[R0-WAIT] Provider remains 0 but link shows " + res.count + " < target " + target + " (start " + start + " + qty " + qty + ") — still checking.");
      }
      return currentStatus;
    }
    if (note.indexOf("R0-UNVERIFIED") === -1) {
