      appendNote_(sh, row, "[R0-UNVERIFIED] Provider remains 0 but link count could not be read (" + (res && res.reason || "unknown") + ") — verify manually.");
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return currentStatus;
}


/** True if provider API error means their panel balance is too low. */
function isProviderFundsError_(err) {
  var s = String(err || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return false;
  var keys = [
    "not enough funds",
    "not enough balance",
    "insufficient funds",
    "insufficient balance",
    "low balance",
    "no funds",
    "balance is low",
    "out of funds",
    "insufficient credit",
    "not enough money",
    "balance too low",
    "funds are not enough"
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    if (s.indexOf(keys[i]) !== -1) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Queue admin alert (shown in admin panel + Telegram). */
/**
 * টপ-আপ হিসাব: এই অর্ডারের খরচ (প্রভাইডার রেট x পরিমাণ / 1000) + ওই প্রভাইডারে আটকে থাকা সব অর্ডারের মোট খরচ,
 * লাইভ ব্যালেন্স বাদ দিয়ে কত টপ-আপ করতে হবে (USD ও BDT)। কোনো ধাপ ব্যর্থ হলে অবশিষ্ট ফিল্ড ফাঁকা থাকে — অর্ডার ফ্লো আটকায় না।
 */
function calcProviderTopup_(provider, code, qty) {
  var info = { rate_usd: 0, qty: Number(qty) || 0, need_usd: 0, need_bdt: 0, held_count: 0, held_need_usd: 0, balance_usd: null, topup_usd: 0, topup_bdt: 0 };
  try {
    var rateIdx = loadServiceRateIndex_();
    var rate = Number(rateIdx[String(code)]) || 0;
    info.rate_usd = rate;
    info.need_usd = rate > 0 ? rate * info.qty / 1000 : 0;
    info.need_bdt = Math.ceil(info.need_usd * USD_TO_BDT_RATE);
    // একই প্রভাইডারে আটকে থাকা সব অর্ডার (এই অর্ডারসহ)
    var total = 0, count = 0;
    try {
      var sh = sheet_(), last = sh.getLastRow();
      if (last >= 2) {
        var provIdx = loadProviderIndex_();
        var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, Math.max(COL_NOTE, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID, COL_STATUS, COL_QTY, COL_SERVICE_CODE)).getValues();
        for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
          var v = vals[i];
          var st = String(v[COL_STATUS - 1] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
          if (st !== "processing" && st !== "pending") continue;
          if (String(v[COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID - 1] || "").trim()) continue;
          var nt = String(v[COL_NOTE - 1] || "");
          if (nt.indexOf("PROVIDER_BALANCE_LOW") === -1 && nt.indexOf("Waiting provider balance") === -1) continue;
          var sc = String(v[COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || "").trim();
          if (String(provIdx[sc] || "") !== String(provider)) continue;
          var r2 = Number(rateIdx[sc]) || 0, q2 = Number(v[COL_QTY - 1]) || 0;
          if (r2 > 0 && q2 > 0) { total += r2 * q2 / 1000; count++; }
        }
      }
    } catch (eH) {}
    if (!(total > 0)) { total = info.need_usd; count = info.need_usd > 0 ? 1 : 0; }
    info.held_count = count;
    info.held_need_usd = total;
    try {
      var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
      var bal = fetchOneBalance_(provider, cfg);
      if (bal && bal.ok && isFinite(bal.balance)) info.balance_usd = Number(bal.balance);
    } catch (eB) {}
    var have = info.balance_usd == null ? 0 : Math.max(0, info.balance_usd);
    info.topup_usd = Math.max(0, total - have);
    info.topup_bdt = Math.ceil(info.topup_usd * USD_TO_BDT_RATE);
  } catch (e) {}
  return info;
}

function pushAdminAlert_(obj) {
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var raw = props.getProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS") || "[]";
    var arr = [];
    try { arr = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { arr = []; }
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) arr = [];
    obj.created_at = nowIsoDhaka_();
    obj.id = "AL-" + Date.now();
    arr.unshift(obj);
    if (arr.length > 50) arr = arr.slice(0, 50);
    // Script Properties values max out near 9KB: drop the oldest alerts instead of silently losing the new one
    while (arr.length > 1 && JSON.stringify(arr).length > 8500) arr.pop();
    props.setProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS", JSON.stringify(arr));
  } catch (e) {}
}

function listAdminAlerts_() {
  try {
    var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS") || "[]";
    var arr = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function clearAdminAlert_(id) {
  var arr = listAdminAlerts_();
  arr = arr.filter(function (a) { return String(a.id) !== String(id); });
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS", JSON.stringify(arr));
}


/** code -> provider cost per 1000 (number, from packages-full.json pricePerK). */
function loadServiceRateIndex_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var cached = cache.get("SERVICE_RATE_IDX");
  if (cached) {
    try { return JSON.parse(cached); } catch (e) {}
  }
  var idx = {};
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var arr = parsePkgs_(resp.getContentText());
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      var p = arr[i];
      if (!p || p.code == null) continue;
      var r = Number(String(p.pricePerK == null ? "" : p.pricePerK).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ""));
      if (r > 0) idx[String(p.code)] = r;
    }
    customServices_().forEach(function (c) {
      if (!c || c.code == null || idx[String(c.code)]) return;
      var cr = Number(String(c.pricePerK == null ? "" : c.pricePerK).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ""));
      if (cr > 0) idx[String(c.code)] = cr;
    });
    cache.put("SERVICE_RATE_IDX", JSON.stringify(idx), PRICE_CACHE_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {}
  return idx;
}

/**
 * BALANCE-GATED AUTO-FORWARD (runs silently in the backend, nothing shown in the UI).
 * Orders that could not be placed because the provider panel had too little
 * balance (no provider_order_id yet) are held. This function checks each
 * provider's live balance and calls the provider "add" API ONLY when
 * balance >= the order's cost (pricePerK x quantity / 1000). Otherwise the
 * order just keeps waiting — no API call, no note, no alert on each scan.
 * optionalProvider: only that panel; null = all.
 * Returns { tried, ok, failed, skipped_low_balance, results[] }
 */
/**
 * Hold থেকে অটো-ফরোয়ার্ড হলে নতুন অর্ডারের মতো পূর্ণ কার্ড পাঠায়
 * (মোবাইল, মোট অর্ডার, সার্ভিস, কোড ও প্ল্যাটফর্ম, পরিমাণ, টাকা + WhatsApp ও খুলুন বাটন)।
 * শেষে ছোট Auto-forward লাইন। কার্ড বানাতে ব্যর্থ হলে আগের ছোট মেসেজে ফিরে যায়।
 */
function notifyHeldForwarded_(sh, row, orderId, provider, poid, label) {
  label = label || "✅ Auto-forward (provider balance OK)";
  var tail = "\n\n" + label + "\nProvider: " + provider + " · PO: " + poid;
  try {
    var o = rowToObject_(sh, row);
    var mobile = o.whatsapp || "";
    var total = 0;
    try { total = getUserOrderStats_(mobile).total_orders; } catch (eCt) {}
    var card = buildOrderCard_(mobile, o.service_name, o.service_code, o.platform, o.quantity, o.amount, total, undefined, String(poid || o.provider_order_id || orderId || "").trim());
    notifyCardWa_(card, orderId, card + tail);
  } catch (e) {
    notifyTelegram_(label + "\nOrder: " + orderId + "\nProvider: " + provider + "\nPO: " + poid);
  }
}

/** প্রোভাইডারে অর্ডার সফলভাবে গেলে (ম্যানুয়াল রিট্রাই / অ্যাডমিন অ্যাপ্রুভ / SMS অটো / ব্যাকআপ)
 *  টেলিগ্রামে পূর্ণ অর্ডার কার্ড পাঠায়। fwd = forwardOrderToProvider_ এর ফল। কখনো throw করে না। */
function notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, fwd, label) {
  try {
    if (!fwd || !fwd.ok) return;
    var oid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "");
    notifyHeldForwarded_(sh, row, oid, fwd.provider || "", fwd.provider_order_id || "", label);
  } catch (e) {}
}

function retryHeldProviderOrders_(optionalProvider) {
  var sh = sheet_();
  ensureHeader_(sh);
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  var out = { tried: 0, ok: 0, failed: 0, skipped_low_balance: 0, results: [] };
  if (last < 2) return out;

  var only = optionalProvider ? String(optionalProvider).trim().toLowerCase() : "";
  var provIdx = loadProviderIndex_();
  var rateIdx = loadServiceRateIndex_();
  var cfgAll = getProviderConfig_();
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var balances = {};          // provider -> remaining balance (null = unknown)
  var maxTry = 15;            // per run safety

  // Oldest held order first -> first come, first served when funds are limited
  for (var row = 2; row <= last && out.tried < maxTry; row++) {
    var status = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (status !== "processing" && status !== "pending") continue;
    var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
    if (poid) continue;
    var note = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_NOTE).getValue() || "");
    // Only orders held for provider balance (never blind-retry other failures)
    if (note.indexOf("PROVIDER_BALANCE_LOW") === -1 &&
        note.indexOf("Waiting provider balance") === -1 &&
        !isProviderFundsError_(note)) continue;

    var serviceCode = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
    var provider = provIdx[serviceCode] || "";
    if (!provider) continue;
    if (only && String(provider).toLowerCase() !== only) continue;
    var cfg = cfgAll[provider];
    if (!cfg || !cfg.api_key || !cfg.api_url) continue;

    var orderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "");
    // cool-down after a surprising funds error even though balance looked enough
    if (cache.get("HELDCOOL_" + orderId)) continue;

    // 1) live provider balance (fetched once per provider per run)
    if (!(provider in balances)) {
      var bal = fetchOneBalance_(provider, cfg);
      balances[provider] = (bal && bal.ok && isFinite(bal.balance)) ? Number(bal.balance) : null;
    }
    var have = balances[provider];
    if (have === null) continue;                       // balance unknown -> wait for next scan

    // 2) order cost on the provider
    var qty = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
    var rate = rateIdx[serviceCode];
    var need = (rate && qty > 0) ? (rate * qty / 1000) : 0;
    if (have <= 0.0001 || (need > 0 && have + 1e-9 < need)) {
      out.skipped_low_balance++;                       // not enough yet -> silent wait
      continue;
    }

    // 3) balance is enough -> create the order on the provider
    out.tried++;
    var fwd = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row) || { ok: false, error: "unknown" };
    if (fwd.ok) {
      out.ok++;
      if (need > 0) balances[provider] = have - need;  // keep local balance in step within this run
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Provider balance sufficient — order forwarded automatically.");
      setStatus_(sh, row, "Processing", "Provider balance OK — order forwarded");
      try {
        var alerts = listAdminAlerts_();
        alerts.forEach(function (a) {
          if (a && String(a.order_id) === orderId) clearAdminAlert_(a.id);
        });
      } catch (eClr) {}
      try {
        notifyHeldForwarded_(sh, row, orderId, fwd.provider || provider, fwd.provider_order_id || "");
      } catch (eTg) {}
      out.results.push({ order_id: orderId, ok: true, provider: fwd.provider || provider });
    } else {
      out.failed++;
      try { cache.put("HELDCOOL_" + orderId, "1", 1800); } catch (eC) {}   // wait 30 min before touching it again
      // ব্যালেন্সের অপেক্ষা স্বাভাবিক (নীরব)। অন্য কারণে ব্যর্থ হলে টেলিগ্রামে সতর্কতা — একই অর্ডারে ৬ ঘণ্টায় একবার
      try {
        if (!isProviderFundsError_(fwd.error) && !cache.get("HELDFAILTG_" + orderId)) {
          cache.put("HELDFAILTG_" + orderId, "1", 21600);
          notifyTelegram_("⚠️ প্রোভাইডারে পাঠানো যায়নি\nOrder: " + orderId + "\nProvider: " + provider + "\nকারণ: " + String(fwd.error || "unknown").substring(0, 140) + "\nআবার চেষ্টা হবে ৩০ মিনিট পরে।",
            [[{ text: "🔎 খুলুন", callback_data: "o:" + orderId }]]);
        }
      } catch (eFt) {}
      out.results.push({ order_id: orderId, ok: false, error: fwd.error, provider: provider });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Time-driven trigger (every 5 min): scans provider balances in the backend and
 * forwards held orders whose cost is covered. Install once with setupBalanceScanTrigger().
 */
function retryHeldOrdersTrigger_() {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) return;
  try {
    var r = retryHeldProviderOrders_(null);
    if (r.tried > 0) {
      Logger.log("balanceScan: forwarded=" + r.ok + " failed=" + r.failed + " waiting_low_balance=" + r.skipped_low_balance);
    }
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/** Run ONCE from the Apps Script editor: removes the old timed-retry triggers and
 *  installs the 5-minute provider-balance scan. */
function setupBalanceScanTrigger() {
  var ts = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();
  var removed = 0, have = false;
  for (var i = 0; i < ts.length; i++) {
    var h = ts[i].getHandlerFunction();
    if (h === "autoRetryCanceledTrigger_") { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(ts[i]); removed++; }
    else if (h === "retryHeldOrdersTrigger_") {
      if (have) { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(ts[i]); removed++; } else { have = true; }
    }
  }
  if (!have) ScriptApp.newTrigger("retryHeldOrdersTrigger_").timeBased().everyMinutes(5).create();
  Logger.log("Balance-scan trigger ready; old retry triggers removed: " + removed);
  return "ok (removed " + removed + ")";
}

function forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row, qtyOverride, ov) {
  try {
    var serviceCode = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
    var link = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_LINK).getValue() || "").trim();
    var qty = (qtyOverride !== undefined && qtyOverride !== null && qtyOverride !== "")
      ? Number(qtyOverride)
      : Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
    if (!serviceCode || !link || !qty || qty <= 0) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward skipped (missing service code/link/quantity) — place manually.");
      return { ok: false, error: "missing_fields" };
    }

    var provider, svcId = serviceCode, onBackup = false;
    if (ov && ov.provider) { provider = ov.provider; svcId = String(ov.service_id || serviceCode); onBackup = true; }   // ধাপ 7: ব্যাকআপে ফেইলওভার
    else {
      var pinO = null;
      try { pinO = getPinByOrder_(String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim()); } catch (ePo) {}
      if (pinO && pinO.p) { provider = pinO.p; svcId = String(pinO.s || serviceCode); onBackup = true; }   // আগে ব্যাকআপে সরানো অর্ডার — পুনঃ-অর্ডারও ব্যাকআপেই
      else provider = loadProviderIndex_()[serviceCode];
    }
    if (!provider) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward skipped: no provider mapped for service " + serviceCode + " — place manually.");
      return { ok: false, error: "no_provider_map", service_code: serviceCode };
    }

    var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
    if (!cfg || !cfg.api_key || !cfg.api_url) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward skipped: no API key set for " + provider + " — place manually.");
      return { ok: false, error: "no_api_key", provider: provider };
    }

    // Custom Comments: কাস্টমারের লেখা কমেন্ট (column O, প্রতি লাইনে ১টা) প্রোভাইডারে পাঠানো হয়
    var payload = { key: cfg.api_key, action: "add", service: svcId, link: link, quantity: qty };
    var cmLines = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_COMMENTS).getValue() || "").replace(/\r/g, "")
      .split("\n").map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(function (x) { return x; });
    if (cmLines.length) {
      if (cmLines.length < qty) {
        appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward skipped: comments (" + cmLines.length + ") < quantity (" + qty + ") — place manually.");
        return { ok: false, error: "comments_mismatch" };
      }
      if (cmLines.length > qty) cmLines = cmLines.slice(0, qty);   // qty কমানো হলে প্রথম qty-টা কমেন্ট যাবে
      payload.comments = cmLines.join("\n");
      payload.quantity = cmLines.length;
    }

    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, {
      method: "post",
      payload: payload,
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    var body = resp.getContentText();
    var json = null;
    try { json = JSON.parse(body); } catch (e) {}

    if (json && json.order) {
      if (onBackup) {   // পিন আগে (ট্র্যাকিং যেন ভুল প্রোভাইডারে না যায়), তারপর অর্ডার-আইডি
        addPin_(serviceCode, String(json.order), String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim(), provider, svcId,
          ov ? (ov.old_provider || "") : "", ov ? (ov.old_poid || "") : "", ov ? (ov.actor || "") : "");
      }
      sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).setValue(String(json.order));
      markForwardedAt_(sh, row);
      try { stRecordForward_(provider, serviceCode, String(json.order), String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || ""), Number(payload.quantity) || qty); } catch (e) {}
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forwarded — provider order added, live tracking enabled." + (cmLines.length ? " (" + cmLines.length + " custom comments sent)" : ""));
      return { ok: true, provider: provider, provider_order_id: String(json.order) };
    } else if (json && json.error) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward FAILED (" + provider + "): " + json.error + " — place manually.");
      return { ok: false, error: String(json.error), provider: provider };
    } else {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward (" + provider + ") — unexpected response, check manually: " + body.substring(0, 200));
      return { ok: false, error: "unexpected_response", provider: provider, body: body.substring(0, 200) };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Auto-forward error: " + err.message + " — place manually.");
    return { ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

/** Admin "Retry API" — re-call provider add after balance top-up or previous FAIL.
 *  Skips if provider_order_id already set, unless force=true. */
function handleAdminRetryForward_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  }
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  }
  var existing = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var force = data.force === true || data.force === "true" || data.force === 1;
  if (existing && !force) {
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: false,
      error: "already_forwarded",
      message: "Provider order already exists: " + existing + ". Force retry only if you are sure.",
      provider_order_id: existing
    });
  }
  var qtyOverride = null;
  if (data.quantity !== undefined && data.quantity !== null && data.quantity !== "") {
    qtyOverride = Number(data.quantity);
  }
  appendNote_(sh, row, "Manual retry/re-order via admin" + (qtyOverride ? (" (qty " + qtyOverride + ")") : "") + ".");
  var result = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row, qtyOverride) || { ok: false, error: "unknown" };
  result.order_id = data.order_id;
  notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, result, "✅ ম্যানুয়াল রিট্রাই সফল — প্রভাইডারে গেছে");
  return jsonOut_(result);
}

// ---------- Canceled-order re-order (provider cancelled before finishing) ----------
// Provider status "Canceled" -> place a NEW provider order for the Remains amount.
//  * Manual only: admin panel "Retry Order" button  (action: admin_retry_canceled).
//    (The old 15-minute timed auto re-order was removed.)
var CANCEL_STATE_KEY = "CANCEL_RETRY_STATE";

function isCanceledProviderStatus_(s) {
  s = String(s || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return s === "canceled" || s === "cancelled";
}

function loadCancelState_() {
  try {
    var o = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(CANCEL_STATE_KEY) || "{}");
    return (o && typeof o === "object") ? o : {};
  } catch (e) { return {}; }
}

function saveCancelState_(st) {
  // keep it small: drop entries with nothing pending and no tries
  var keys = Object.keys(st);
  if (keys.length > 300) {
    keys.sort(function (a, b) { return (st[a].t || 0) - (st[b].t || 0); });
    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length - 300; i++) delete st[keys[i]];
  }
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(CANCEL_STATE_KEY, JSON.stringify(st));
}

/** Fresh (uncached) provider status for the row's current provider order. */
function freshLiveStatus_(serviceCode, poid) {
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("LIVESTATUS_" + poid); } catch (e) {}
  return getLiveProviderStatus_(serviceCode, poid);
}

/** Quantity to re-order = provider Remains, never more than the original quantity. */
function canceledReorderQty_(sh, row, live) {
  var origQty = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
  var remains = Number(live && live.remains);
  if (!remains || remains <= 0) return 0;
  if (origQty > 0 && remains > origQty) remains = origQty;
  return remains;
}

/** Re-orders the Remains of a canceled provider order. Returns {ok, ...}. */
function reorderCanceledRow_(sh, row, live, who) {
  var orderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "");
  var oldPoid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var qty = canceledReorderQty_(sh, row, live);
  if (qty <= 0) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Canceled re-order skipped (" + who + "): provider remains is 0/unknown — check manually.");
    return { ok: false, error: "no_remains" };
  }
  appendNote_(sh, row, "Provider order " + oldPoid + " canceled (remains " + live.remains + ") — re-ordering qty " + qty + " (" + who + ").");
  try { logDelivery_(sh, row, nowIsoDhaka_(), "failed"); } catch (eLg) {} // পুরনো প্রোভাইডার অর্ডার cancel = ব্যর্থ রেকর্ড
  var fwd = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row, qty) || { ok: false, error: "unknown" };
  if (fwd.ok) {
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("LIVESTATUS_" + oldPoid); } catch (e) {}
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Canceled order re-placed: " + oldPoid + " → " + fwd.provider_order_id + " (qty " + qty + ").");
    try {
      notifyTelegram_("🔁 Canceled order re-placed (" + who + ")\nOrder: " + orderId + "\nQty: " + qty + "\nNew PO: " + fwd.provider_order_id + " (old " + oldPoid + ")");
    } catch (eTg) {}
  }
  fwd.qty = qty;
  fwd.old_provider_order_id = oldPoid;
  return fwd;
}

/** Admin button: body { action:"admin_retry_canceled", token, order_id }.
 *  Server re-checks the provider status itself, so a double click / stale card
 *  can never create a duplicate order (only works while status is Canceled). */
function handleAdminRetryCanceled_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  var status = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (status !== "processing" && status !== "partial") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_status", message: "Order status is " + status + " — only Processing/Partial can be re-ordered." });
  }
  var force = data.force === true || data.force === "true" || data.force === 1 || data.force === "1";
  var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var serviceCode = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var key = String(data.order_id);

  function resetState_() {
    var st = loadCancelState_();
    st[key] = { since: 0, tries: 0, t: Date.now() }; // manual retry gives auto-retry a fresh start
    saveCancelState_(st);
  }

  // No provider order yet (never forwarded / forward failed) -> plain forward, full quantity.
  if (!poid) {
    var f0 = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row) || { ok: false, error: "unknown" };
    if (f0.ok) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Manual Retry Order (admin): forwarded → " + f0.provider_order_id + ".");
      resetState_();
      notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, f0, "✅ ম্যানুয়াল রিট্রাই সফল — প্রভাইডারে গেছে");
    }
    f0.order_id = data.order_id;
    return jsonOut_(f0);
  }

  var live = freshLiveStatus_(serviceCode, poid);
  if (!live) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "status_unavailable", message: "Provider status could not be fetched. Try again." });

  if (!isCanceledProviderStatus_(live.status)) {
    var pst = String(live.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!force) {
      // Not canceled: let the admin decide (client shows a confirm and re-sends with force:true).
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_canceled", needs_confirm: true,
        message: "Provider status is \"" + live.status + "\" (remains " + live.remains + ").",
        provider_status: live.status, remains: live.remains });
    }
    if (pst === "completed") {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "already_completed", message: "Provider says this order is Completed — nothing to re-order." });
    }
    // forced: use remains if known, else full quantity
    var origQ = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
    if (!(Number(live.remains) > 0)) live.remains = origQ;
  }

  var res = reorderCanceledRow_(sh, row, live, "admin");
  res.order_id = data.order_id;
  if (res.ok) resetState_();
  return jsonOut_(res);
}


// ---------- SMS parsing (best-effort — bKash/Nagad/Rocket confirmation SMS text) ----------
// Real SMS wording varies by gateway and changes over time, so this pulls out just the
// two things we actually need — amount and TrxID — with several fallback patterns rather
// than trying to fully parse the sentence.

function extractTrxFromSms_(text) {
  var s = String(text || "");
  var patterns = [
    /Trx\s*ID\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{6,20})/i,
    /Txn\s*ID\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{6,20})/i,
    /Transaction\s*ID\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{6,20})/i,
    /ট্রানজেকশন\s*আইডি\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{6,20})/i,
    /Ref(?:erence)?\s*(?:No\.?)?\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]{6,20})/i
  ];
  for (var i = 0; i < patterns.length; i++) {
    var m = s.match(patterns[i]);
    if (m && m[1]) return m[1];
  }
  return "";
}

function extractAmountFromSms_(text) {
  var s = String(text || "");
  var m = s.match(/(?:Tk|BDT|৳)\s*\.?\s*([0-9][0-9,]*\.?[0-9]*)/i);
  if (m && m[1]) return Number(String(m[1]).replace(/,/g, "")) || 0;
  return 0;
}

function jsonOut_(obj) {
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(obj))
    .setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

/** Minimal HTML-escaping for text interpolated into the one-click-link
 *  confirmation page (order IDs/status are short and safe already, this is
 *  just a precaution). */
function esc_(v) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
    return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c];
  });
}

/** SMS-forwarding webhook. Your phone's SMS-forwarder app posts the raw text
 *  of every incoming bKash/Nagad/Rocket SMS here. We pull out the TrxID,
 *  find the matching Pending order (the customer typed that same TrxID when
 *  placing the order), cross-check the amount, and auto-approve on a match.
 *  Never auto-approves on TrxID match alone if the amount clearly disagrees —
 *  that gets flagged for manual review instead, safer than a wrong auto-approve. */
// ---------- SMS/order race-condition fix ----------
// A payment SMS often arrives BEFORE the customer finishes typing their
// TrxID into the site and submitting the order — MacroDroid can forward the
// SMS within seconds, well before the human gets back to the browser. So
// when handleSmsVerify_ finds no matching row yet, it parks the SMS's
// (txn, amount) here for a while; the order-creation code below checks this
// same cache so whichever side (SMS or order) shows up first still gets
// matched to the other, instead of the SMS being silently discarded.
var SMS_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 7200; // 2 hours — plenty for someone to submit the form

function cacheUnmatchedSms_(txn, amount, fromMobile, provider, dtMs) {
  var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
  if (!t) return;
  try {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().put("sms_" + t, JSON.stringify({ amount: amount, from: String(fromMobile || ""), provider: String(provider || ""), dt: Number(dtMs) || 0, at: Date.now() }), SMS_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {
    // Cache is best-effort; if it fails we simply lose the early-SMS
    // optimization for this one TrxID, nothing else breaks.
  }
}

/** Looks up (and consumes) a cached SMS for this TrxID. Returns the cached
 *  amount, or null if nothing was cached (SMS hasn't arrived yet, or the
 *  cache entry already expired / was already consumed). */
function consumeCachedSmsAmount_(txn) {
  var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
  if (!t) return null;
  try {
    var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var raw = cache.get("sms_" + t);
    if (!raw) return null;
    cache.remove("sms_" + t);
    var parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    SMS_LAST_CACHED_FROM_ = String((parsed && parsed.from) || "");
    SMS_LAST_CACHED_PROVIDER_ = String((parsed && parsed.provider) || "");
    SMS_LAST_CACHED_DT_ = Number((parsed && parsed.dt) || 0);
    return (parsed && typeof parsed.amount === "number") ? parsed.amount : null;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}


/** Find a single Pending order matching SMS amount (and optional mobile).
 *  Returns row number or -1. If multiple amount matches and no mobile match, returns -1 (safe). */
function extractMobileFromSms_(text) {
  var s = String(text || "");
  var m = s.match(/01[0-9]{9}/);
  return m ? m[0] : "";
}
function findPendingRowByAmount_(sh, amount, mobileHint) {
  if (!(amount > 0)) return -1;
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return -1;
  // G amount, H txn, I paid_from, J whatsapp, K status
  var vals = sh.getRange(2, 7, lastRow - 1, 11).getValues();
  var svcCodes = sh.getRange(2, COL_SERVICE_CODE, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  var matches = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
    var st = String(vals[i][4] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (st !== "pending") continue;
    // ওয়ালেট টপ-আপ শুধু TrxID মিলে ক্রেডিট হবে — শুধু অ্যামাউন্ট মিলিয়ে অন্যের পেমেন্ট দিয়ে ব্যালেন্স যোগ ঠেকাতে
    if (String(svcCodes[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue;
    var amt = Number(vals[i][0]) || 0;
    if (Math.abs(amt - amount) > SMS_AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT) continue;
    matches.push({ row: i + 2, mobile: normalizeMobile_(vals[i][3] || "") });
  }
  if (!matches.length) return -1;
  var hint = String(mobileHint || "");
  if (hint) {
    var mobileHits = matches.filter(function (x) { return x.mobile && smsFromMatches_(hint, x.mobile); });
    if (mobileHits.length === 1) return mobileHits[0].row;
    if (mobileHits.length > 1) return -1;
  }
  // v21: amount-only matching is unsafe (another customer's payment could approve this order).
  // The SMS is cached instead and matches by TrxID when the order form arrives.
  if (matches.length === 1 && String(smsProp_("SMS_ALLOW_AMOUNT_ONLY_MATCH", "0")) === "1") return matches[0].row;
  return -1;
}

// ---------- SMS security hardening (v21) ----------
// Script Properties (all optional):
//   SMS_AUTO_APPROVE_MAX_BDT   default 5000  -> bigger payments are never auto-approved (manual review)
//   SMS_BALANCE_CHECK          alert (default) | hold | off  -> compares SMS "Balance" with the previous one
//   SMS_SENDER_MATCH           hold (default) | off -> SMS "from" number must equal the order's mobile
//   SMS_ALLOW_AMOUNT_ONLY_MATCH 0 (default) | 1 -> approve by amount alone when no TrxID matches (unsafe)
var SMS_LAST_CACHED_FROM_ = "", SMS_LAST_CACHED_PROVIDER_ = "", SMS_LAST_CACHED_DT_ = 0;
var SMS_BALANCE_TOLERANCE_BDT = 1;

function smsProp_(k, d) {
  try {
    var v = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(k);
    return (v === null || v === "") ? d : v;
  } catch (e) { return d; }
}
function smsCap_() { return Number(smsProp_("SMS_AUTO_APPROVE_MAX_BDT", 5000)) || 5000; }

function safeEq_(a, b) {
  a = String(a); b = String(b);
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
  var d = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) d |= (a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i));
  return d === 0;
}

function smsBadTokenAlert_() {
  try {
    var c = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    var n = Number(c.get("sms_bad_token") || 0) + 1;
    c.put("sms_bad_token", String(n), 900);
    if (n === 5) notifyTelegram_("🚨 SMS webhook-এ ১৫ মিনিটে ৫+ ভুল টোকেন — কেউ ইউআরএল ধরে চেষ্টা করছে। সন্দেহ হলে rotateSecrets চালান ও অ্যাপে নতুন টোকেন দিন।");
  } catch (e) {}
}

function extractBalanceFromSms_(text) {
  var m = String(text || "").match(/Balance\s*[:\-]?\s*(?:Tk|BDT|৳)?\s*\.?\s*([0-9][0-9,]*\.?[0-9]*)/i);
  return m ? (Number(String(m[1]).replace(/,/g, "")) || 0) : 0;
}

function smsWalletKey_(data, text) {
  if (data && data.__provider) return String(data.__provider);
  if (data && data.__provider) return data.__provider;
  var s = String((data && data.sender) || "") + " " + String(text || "");
  if (/bkash/i.test(s)) return "bkash";
  if (/nagad/i.test(s)) return "nagad";
  if (/rocket|16216|dbbl/i.test(s)) return "rocket";
  return "other";
}

/** "" = fine to auto-approve; otherwise a reason -> keep the order Pending for manual review. */
function smsHoldReason_(data, smsText, smsAmount) {
  var cap = smsCap_();
  if (smsAmount > cap) return "বড় অ্যামাউন্ট (৳" + smsAmount + " > ৳" + cap + ") — ম্যানুয়াল চেক দরকার";
  var mode = String(smsProp_("SMS_BALANCE_CHECK", "alert")).toLowerCase();
  var bal = Number(data && data.__balance) || extractBalanceFromSms_(smsText);
  if (bal > 0 && mode !== "off") {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    var key = "SMS_LASTBAL_" + smsWalletKey_(data, smsText);
    var last = Number(props.getProperty(key) || 0);
    props.setProperty(key, String(bal));   // always move the baseline so one odd SMS can't block the next
    if (last > 0 && Math.abs(bal - (last + smsAmount)) > SMS_BALANCE_TOLERANCE_BDT) {
      var why = "ব্যালেন্স মিলছে না (আগের ৳" + last + " + ৳" + smsAmount + " ≠ ৳" + bal + ") — ভুয়া SMS হতে পারে, অথবা মাঝে অন্য লেনদেন হয়েছে";
      if (mode === "hold") return why;
      try { notifyTelegram_("⚠️ SMS ব্যালেন্স অসঙ্গতি (অর্ডার অটো-অ্যাপ্রুভ হয়েছে)\n" + why); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  return "";
}

// ---------- SMS parser + validator (v22) ----------
// Reads bKash / Nagad / Rocket "money received" SMS into fields and checks them before anything is approved.
// Script Properties (all optional): SMS_MAX_SMS_AGE_MIN (360), SMS_FUTURE_SKEW_MIN (5), SMS_DEVICE_SKEW_MIN (15),
//   SMS_MAX_PAY_BEFORE_ORDER_MIN (180), SMS_MAX_PAY_AFTER_ORDER_MIN (1440), SMS_SENDER_MATCH (hold|off)
var MFS_MONTHS_ = { JAN: 0, FEB: 1, MAR: 2, APR: 3, MAY: 4, JUN: 5, JUL: 6, AUG: 7, SEP: 8, OCT: 9, NOV: 10, DEC: 11 };

function mfsNum_(s) { var n = Number(String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/,/g, "")); return isFinite(n) ? n : 0; }

/** Dhaka wall-clock (UTC+6) -> epoch ms. Returns 0 if the date is not a real calendar date/time. */
function mfsDt_(y, mo, d, h, mi, s) {
  if (y < 100) y += 2000;
  if (mo < 0 || mo > 11 || d < 1 || d > 31 || h < 0 || h > 23 || mi < 0 || mi > 59 || s < 0 || s > 59) return 0;
  var t = Date.UTC(y, mo, d, h, mi, s);
  var chk = new Date(t);
  if (chk.getUTCMonth() !== mo || chk.getUTCDate() !== d) return 0;
  return t - 6 * 3600 * 1000;
}

function mfsFmt_(ms) {
  try { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ms), "Asia/Dhaka", "dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm"); } catch (e) { return new Date(ms).toISOString(); }
}

/** Real bKash/Nagad/Rocket SMS have ONE fixed layout. Matching the whole text (start to end) stops anyone from hiding
 *  fake fields inside the free-text "Ref" that the sender types (Nagad/bKash show it in the SMS). Returns fields or null. */
function mfsStrict_(prov, s) {
  var m, o = { amount: 0, fee: 0, balance: 0, from: "", trx: "", dt: 0, link: "" };
  var N = "([\\d,]+(?:\\.\\d+)?)";
  if (prov === "bkash") {
    m = s.match(new RegExp("^You have received(?: payment)? Tk ?" + N + " from (01\\d{9})\\.?(?: Ref[: ]+.{0,60}?)?\\.? Fee ?Tk ?" + N + "\\.? Balance ?Tk ?" + N + "\\.? TrxID:? ?([A-Za-z0-9]+) at (\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4}) (\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))?\\.?$", "i"));
    if (!m) return null;
    o.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]); o.from = m[2]; o.fee = mfsNum_(m[3]); o.balance = mfsNum_(m[4]); o.trx = m[5].toUpperCase();
    o.dt = mfsDt_(+m[8], +m[7] - 1, +m[6], +m[9], +m[10], +(m[11] || 0));
    return o;
  }
  if (prov === "nagad") {
    m = s.match(new RegExp("^Money Received\\.? Amount ?: ?Tk ?" + N + " Sender ?: ?(01\\d{9}) Ref ?: ?(.{0,80}?) ?Txn ?ID ?: ?([A-Za-z0-9]+) Balance ?: ?Tk ?" + N + " (\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{1,2})\\/(\\d{4}) (\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))?\\.?$", "i"));
    if (!m) return null;
    o.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]); o.from = m[2]; o.trx = m[4].toUpperCase(); o.balance = mfsNum_(m[5]);
    o.dt = mfsDt_(+m[8], +m[7] - 1, +m[6], +m[9], +m[10], +(m[11] || 0));
    return o;
  }
  if (prov === "rocket") {
    m = s.match(new RegExp("^Tk ?" + N + " received from A\\/C ?: ?(\\*+\\d+|\\d{5,}) Fee ?: ?Tk ?" + N + ",? Your A\\/C Balance ?: ?Tk ?-?" + N + " Txn ?Id ?: ?(\\d+) Date ?: ?(\\d{1,2})-([A-Za-z]{3})-(\\d{2,4}) (\\d{1,2}):(\\d{2})(?::(\\d{2}))? ?(am|pm)\\.?(?: Download (\\S+))?$", "i"));
    if (!m) return null;
    o.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]); o.from = m[2]; o.fee = mfsNum_(m[3]); o.balance = mfsNum_(m[4]); o.trx = m[5]; o.link = m[13] || "";
    var hh = +m[9], ap = m[12].toLowerCase();
    if (ap === "pm" && hh < 12) hh += 12;
    if (ap === "am" && hh === 12) hh = 0;
    var mon = MFS_MONTHS_[String(m[7]).toUpperCase()];
    o.dt = (mon === undefined) ? 0 : mfsDt_(+m[8], mon, +m[6], hh, +m[10], +(m[11] || 0));
    return o;
  }
  return null;
}

/** TrxID length per wallet (from real SMS): bKash 10 letters/digits, Nagad 8, Rocket 10 digits. SMS_TRX_LEN_STRICT=0 relaxes it. */
function mfsTrxOk_(prov, trx) {
  var strict = String(smsProp_("SMS_TRX_LEN_STRICT", "1")) !== "0";
  if (prov === "bkash") return strict ? /^[A-Z0-9]{10}$/.test(trx) : /^[A-Z0-9]{8,12}$/.test(trx);
  if (prov === "nagad") return strict ? /^[A-Z0-9]{8}$/.test(trx) : /^[A-Z0-9]{6,12}$/.test(trx);
  if (prov === "rocket") return strict ? /^\d{10}$/.test(trx) : /^\d{8,12}$/.test(trx);
  return false;
}

function parseMfsSms_(text) {
  var s = String(text || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  var r = { provider: "", amount: 0, fee: 0, balance: 0, from: "", trx: "", dt: 0, ids: 0, problems: [] };
  var m;
  r.ids = (s.match(/(?:Trx|Txn)\s*ID/gi) || []).length;
  if (s.length > 400) r.problems.push("SMS অস্বাভাবিক লম্বা");

  if (/You have received\b/i.test(s) && /TrxID/i.test(s)) {
    r.provider = "bkash";
    m = s.match(/received(?: payment)?\s+Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s+from\s+(01\d{9})/i);
    if (m) { r.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]); r.from = m[2]; }
    m = s.match(/Fee\s*Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.fee = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/Balance\s*Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.balance = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/TrxID\s*[:\-]?\s*([A-Za-z0-9]+)/i); if (m) r.trx = m[1].toUpperCase();
    m = s.match(/\bat\s+(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/i);
    if (m) r.dt = mfsDt_(+m[3], +m[2] - 1, +m[1], +m[4], +m[5], +(m[6] || 0));
    if (!mfsTrxOk_("bkash", r.trx)) r.problems.push("bKash TrxID-র ফরম্যাট ঠিক নয় (" + r.trx + ")");
    if (!r.from) r.problems.push("প্রেরকের নম্বর পাওয়া যায়নি");
  } else if (/Money Received/i.test(s) && /Txn\s*ID/i.test(s)) {
    r.provider = "nagad";
    m = s.match(/Amount\s*:\s*Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/Sender\s*:\s*(01\d{9})/i); if (m) r.from = m[1];
    m = s.match(/Balance\s*:\s*Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.balance = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/Txn\s*ID\s*:\s*([A-Za-z0-9]+)/i); if (m) r.trx = m[1].toUpperCase();
    m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
    if (m) r.dt = mfsDt_(+m[3], +m[2] - 1, +m[1], +m[4], +m[5], +(m[6] || 0));
    if (!mfsTrxOk_("nagad", r.trx)) r.problems.push("Nagad TxnID-র ফরম্যাট ঠিক নয় (" + r.trx + ")");
    if (!r.from) r.problems.push("প্রেরকের নম্বর পাওয়া যায়নি");
  } else if (/received from A\/C/i.test(s) && /Txn\s*Id/i.test(s)) {
    r.provider = "rocket";
    m = s.match(/Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*received from/i); if (m) r.amount = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/A\/C\s*:\s*(\*+\d{2,}|\d{5,})/i); if (m) r.from = m[1];
    m = s.match(/Fee\s*:\s*Tk\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.fee = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/Balance\s*:\s*Tk\s*-?\s*([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)/i); if (m) r.balance = mfsNum_(m[1]);
    m = s.match(/Txn\s*Id\s*:\s*(\d+)/i); if (m) r.trx = m[1];
    m = s.match(/Date\s*:\s*(\d{1,2})-([A-Za-z]{3})-(\d{2,4})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*(am|pm)/i);
    if (m) {
      var hh = +m[4], ap = m[7].toLowerCase();
      if (ap === "pm" && hh < 12) hh += 12;
      if (ap === "am" && hh === 12) hh = 0;
      var mon = MFS_MONTHS_[String(m[2]).toUpperCase()];
      if (mon !== undefined) r.dt = mfsDt_(+m[3], mon, +m[1], hh, +m[5], +(m[6] || 0));
    }
    if (!mfsTrxOk_("rocket", r.trx)) r.problems.push("Rocket TxnId-র ফরম্যাট ঠিক নয় (" + r.trx + ")");
    if (!r.from) r.problems.push("প্রেরকের A/C পাওয়া যায়নি");
  }
  if (r.provider && String(smsProp_("SMS_STRICT_STRUCTURE", "on")).toLowerCase() !== "off") {
    var sm = mfsStrict_(r.provider, s);
    if (!sm) {
      r.problems.push("SMS-এর গঠন আসল " + r.provider + " SMS-এর সাথে মেলেনি (নকল বা বদলানো হতে পারে)");
    } else {
      r.amount = sm.amount; r.from = sm.from; r.fee = sm.fee; r.balance = sm.balance; r.trx = sm.trx; r.dt = sm.dt;
      if (r.problems.length && mfsTrxOk_(r.provider, r.trx)) {
        r.problems = r.problems.filter(function (p) { return p.indexOf("ফরম্যাট ঠিক নয়") === -1 && p.indexOf("পাওয়া যায়নি") === -1; });
      }
    }
  }
  if (r.provider) {
    var links = s.match(/(?:https?:\/\/|\b(?:bit\.ly|tinyurl\.com|t\.me|wa\.me|goo\.gl)\/)\S+/gi) || [];
    for (var li = 0; li < links.length; li++) {
      var okLink = r.provider === "rocket" && links[li].toLowerCase().replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").replace(/[.,]+$/, "") === String(smsProp_("SMS_ROCKET_LINK", "bit.ly/nexuspay")).toLowerCase();
      if (!okLink) { r.problems.push("SMS-এ অচেনা লিংক আছে (" + links[li].substring(0, 40) + ") — আসল SMS-এ থাকে না"); break; }
    }
    if (r.fee > 0 && String(smsProp_("SMS_ALLOW_FEE", "0")) !== "1") r.problems.push("SMS-এ ফি আছে (৳" + r.fee + ") — সাধারণ Send Money-তে ফি ০ থাকে, ম্যানুয়াল চেক দরকার");
    if (!(r.amount > 0)) r.problems.push("অ্যামাউন্ট পড়া যায়নি");
    if (!r.dt) r.problems.push("SMS-এর তারিখ/সময় পড়া যায়নি");
    if (r.ids !== 1) r.problems.push("SMS-এ TrxID লেখা " + r.ids + "বার আছে (১ বার হওয়ার কথা)");
  }
  return r;
}

function smsSenderProvider_(sender) {
  var s = String(sender || "");
  if (/bkash/i.test(s)) return "bkash";
  if (/nagad/i.test(s)) return "nagad";
  if (/rocket|16216|dbbl/i.test(s)) return "rocket";
  return "";
}

function methodProvider_(method) {
  var m = String(method || "").toLowerCase();
  if (m.indexOf("bkash") !== -1) return "bkash";
  if (m.indexOf("nagad") !== -1) return "nagad";
  if (m.indexOf("rocket") !== -1) return "rocket";
  return "";
}

/** SMS sender number vs a stored number. Rocket masks the account ("***622"): then only the last digits are compared. */
function smsFromMatches_(from, mobile) {
  var f = String(from || ""), m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  if (!f) return true;
  if (f.indexOf("*") === -1 && /^01\d{9}$/.test(normalizeMobile_(f))) return normalizeMobile_(f) === m;
  var tail = f.replace(/\D/g, "");
  return tail.length >= 2 && m.length >= tail.length && m.slice(-tail.length) === tail;
}

/** "" = SMS itself looks sane. Otherwise a reason; the SMS is then NOT approved and NOT cached. */
function smsValidateParsed_(P, data, nowMs) {
  if (!P.provider) return "অচেনা SMS ফরম্যাট (bKash/Nagad/Rocket-এর 'টাকা পেয়েছেন' SMS নয়)";
  if (P.problems.length) return P.problems.join("; ");
  var future = Number(smsProp_("SMS_FUTURE_SKEW_MIN", 5)) * 60000;
  var maxAge = Number(smsProp_("SMS_MAX_SMS_AGE_MIN", 360)) * 60000;
  if (P.dt > nowMs + future) return "SMS-এর তারিখ ভবিষ্যতের (" + mfsFmt_(P.dt) + ")";
  if (nowMs - P.dt > maxAge) return "SMS অনেক পুরনো (" + mfsFmt_(P.dt) + ") — পুরনো পেমেন্ট আবার এসেছে?";
  var rcv = Number(data && data.received_at) || 0;
  if (rcv > 0) {
    var tol = Number(smsProp_("SMS_DEVICE_SKEW_MIN", 15)) * 60000;
    if (Math.abs(rcv - P.dt) > tol) return "SMS-এর লেখা তারিখ (" + mfsFmt_(P.dt) + ") ফোনে আসার সময়ের (" + mfsFmt_(rcv) + ") সাথে মেলেনি";
  }
  var sp = smsSenderProvider_(data && data.sender);
  if (sp && sp !== P.provider) return "প্রেরক আইডি (" + data.sender + ") আর SMS-এর ধরন (" + P.provider + ") মেলেনি";
  return "";
}

function orderCreatedMs_(sh, row) {
  try {
    var v = sh.getRange(row, 12).getValue();
    if (v instanceof Date) return v.getTime();
    var t = Date.parse(String(v));
    return isNaN(t) ? 0 : t;
  } catch (e) { return 0; }
}

/** Compare the SMS with the order row it matched. "" = consistent, otherwise a reason to hold for manual review. */
function smsOrderChecks_(sh, row, P, data, nowMs) {
  var ordProv = methodProvider_(sh.getRange(row, COL_METHOD).getValue());
  if (ordProv && ordProv !== P.provider) return "পেমেন্ট মেথড মেলেনি (অর্ডার: " + ordProv + ", SMS: " + P.provider + ")";
  if (String(smsProp_("SMS_SENDER_MATCH", "hold")).toLowerCase() === "hold") {
    var ordMobile = normalizeMobile_(sh.getRange(row, 10).getValue());
    if (/^01\d{9}$/.test(ordMobile) && !smsFromMatches_(P.from, ordMobile)) {
      return "SMS-এর প্রেরক (" + P.from + ") অর্ডারের নম্বরের (" + ordMobile + ") সাথে মেলেনি";
    }
  }
  var created = orderCreatedMs_(sh, row);
  if (created) {
    var pre = Number(smsProp_("SMS_MAX_PAY_BEFORE_ORDER_MIN", 180)) * 60000;
    var post = Number(smsProp_("SMS_MAX_PAY_AFTER_ORDER_MIN", 1440)) * 60000;
    if (P.dt < created - pre) return "পেমেন্ট (" + mfsFmt_(P.dt) + ") অর্ডারের (" + mfsFmt_(created) + ") অনেক আগের — পুরনো TrxID ব্যবহার?";
    if (P.dt > created + post) return "পেমেন্ট (" + mfsFmt_(P.dt) + ") অর্ডারের (" + mfsFmt_(created) + ") অনেক পরের";
  }
  return "";
}

/** Early-SMS path (SMS arrived before the order form): same checks against the order being created right now. */
function smsEarlyHoldReason_(amount, fromMobile, orderMobile, provider, dtMs, orderMethod) {
  var cap = smsCap_();
  if (amount > cap) return "বড় অ্যামাউন্ট (৳" + amount + " > ৳" + cap + ") — ম্যানুয়াল চেক দরকার";
  var ordProv = methodProvider_(orderMethod);
  if (provider && ordProv && provider !== ordProv) return "পেমেন্ট মেথড মেলেনি (অর্ডার: " + ordProv + ", SMS: " + provider + ")";
  if (String(smsProp_("SMS_SENDER_MATCH", "hold")).toLowerCase() === "hold") {
    var om = normalizeMobile_(orderMobile || "");
    if (/^01\d{9}$/.test(om) && !smsFromMatches_(fromMobile, om)) {
      return "SMS-এর প্রেরক নম্বর (" + fromMobile + ") অর্ডারের নম্বরের (" + om + ") সাথে মেলেনি";
    }
  }
  if (dtMs) {
    var pre = Number(smsProp_("SMS_MAX_PAY_BEFORE_ORDER_MIN", 180)) * 60000;
    if (Date.now() - dtMs > pre) return "পেমেন্ট (" + mfsFmt_(dtMs) + ") অর্ডার দেওয়ার অনেক আগের — পুরনো TrxID ব্যবহার?";
  }
  return "";
}

function smsAlertOnce_(key, msg) {
  try {
    var c = CacheService.getScriptCache(), k = "sms_alert_" + key;
    if (c.get(k)) return;
    c.put(k, "1", 600);
    notifyTelegram_(msg);
  } catch (e) {}
}

function smsLog_(P, data, orderId, result, reason) {
  try {
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName("SMS_Log");
    if (!sh) {
      sh = ss.insertSheet("SMS_Log");
      sh.appendRow(["time", "provider", "trxid", "amount", "from", "sms_time", "phone_received", "order_id", "result", "reason"]);
      sh.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
    var rcv = Number(data && data.received_at) || 0;
    sh.appendRow([nowIsoDhaka_(), (P && P.provider) || "", (P && P.trx) || "", (P && P.amount) || "", (P && P.from) || "",
      (P && P.dt) ? mfsFmt_(P.dt) : "", rcv ? mfsFmt_(rcv) : "", String(orderId || ""), result, String(reason || "").substring(0, 250)]);
  } catch (e) {}
}

function handleSmsVerify_(sh, data) {
  if (!safeEq_(String(data.token || ""), getSmsToken_())) {
    smsBadTokenAlert_();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unauthorized" });
  }

  var smsText = String(data.sms_text || "");
  var trx = extractTrxFromSms_(smsText);
  if (!trx) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_txn_found", message: "Could not find a TrxID in the SMS text." });
  }

  // v22: read the SMS into fields and sanity-check it (format, date, TrxID shape, phone receive time, sender id)
  var P = parseMfsSms_(smsText);
  var nowMs = Date.now();
  var badSms = smsValidateParsed_(P, data, nowMs);
  if (badSms) {
    smsLog_(P, data, "", "rejected", badSms);
    smsAlertOnce_("rej_" + normalizeTxn_(trx), "⚠️ SMS অটো-অ্যাপ্রুভ হয়নি\n" + badSms + "\nTrxID: " + trx);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "rejected_sms", reason: badSms, txn_id: trx });
  }
  data.__provider = P.provider;
  data.__balance = P.balance;
  trx = P.trx;

  var row = findRowByTxn_(sh, trx);
  if (row === -1) {
    var smsAmountForCache = P.amount;
    // Paymently-style: match unique Pending order by amount (no TrxID on order yet)
    var byAmt = findPendingRowByAmount_(sh, smsAmountForCache, P.from);
    if (byAmt !== -1) {
      row = byAmt;
      // attach real TrxID from SMS onto the order
      try { sh.getRange(row, COL_TXN).setValue(trx); } catch (eSet) {}
      try { updateOrderIndexAfterInsert_(sh, sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue(), trx); } catch (eIdx) {}
    } else {
      cacheUnmatchedSms_(trx, smsAmountForCache, P.from, P.provider, P.dt);
      smsLog_(P, data, "", "cached", "অর্ডার এখনো আসেনি");
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "no_matching_order_yet_cached", txn_id: trx });
    }
  }

  var currentStatus = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (currentStatus.toLowerCase() !== "pending") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "already_resolved", order_id: sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue(), status: currentStatus });
  }

  var smsAmount = P.amount;
  var orderAmount = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_AMOUNT).getValue()) || 0;

  if (smsAmount > 0 && !isTopupRow_(sh, row) && Math.abs(smsAmount - orderAmount) > SMS_AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT) {
    setStatus_(sh, row, "Pending", "⚠️ SMS matched TrxID but amount differs (SMS ৳" + smsAmount + " vs order ৳" + orderAmount + ") — needs manual check.");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "flagged_amount_mismatch", order_id: sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() });
  }

  // v21: security gate (cap, balance continuity) — hold instead of auto-approving
  var holdWhy = smsOrderChecks_(sh, row, P, data, nowMs) || smsHoldReason_(data, smsText, smsAmount);
  if (holdWhy) {
    var heldId = sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue();
    smsLog_(P, data, heldId, "held", holdWhy);
    setStatus_(sh, row, "Pending", "⚠️ " + holdWhy);
    try { notifyTelegram_("⚠️ SMS অটো-অ্যাপ্রুভ আটকানো হয়েছে\nOrder: " + heldId + "\n" + holdWhy); } catch (eHold) {}
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "held_for_review", order_id: heldId });
  }

  // "Processing" (not "Approved") because the customer-facing tracking page
  // (app.js buildOrderDetailHTML) only has special messaging for Pending /
  // Processing / Completed / Rejected — "Approved" would silently fall back
  // to the Pending message and look like nothing happened.
  if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) {
    if (!(smsAmount > 0)) {
      setStatus_(sh, row, "Pending", "⚠️ SMS-এ অ্যামাউন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি — টপ-আপ ম্যানুয়াল চেক দরকার।");
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "topup_needs_manual_check", order_id: sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() });
    }
    var topSms = applyTopupCredit_(sh, row, smsAmount);
    var orderIdTop = sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue();
    if (topSms && topSms.error === "amount_mismatch") {
      smsLog_(P, data, orderIdTop, "held", topSms.reconcile ? topSms.reconcile.reason : "topup amount mismatch");
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "flagged_amount_mismatch", order_id: orderIdTop });
    }
    smsLog_(P, data, orderIdTop, "topup_credited", "");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: !!topSms.ok, action: "topup_credited", order_id: orderIdTop, topup: topSms });
  }
  setStatus_(sh, row, "Processing", "✅ Auto-verified via SMS — now processing.");
  var fwdSms = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row);
  if (fwdSms && fwdSms.ok) {
    notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, fwdSms, "✅ SMS অটো-অ্যাপ্রুভ — প্রভাইডারে গেছে");
  } else {
    try {
      notifyTelegram_("✅ SMS অটো-অ্যাপ্রুভ\nOrder: " + String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "") + "\nএখন Processing (প্রভাইডারে এখনো যায়নি)");
    } catch (eSmsTg) {}
  }
  var orderId = sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue();
  smsLog_(P, data, orderId, "approved", "");
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, action: "approved", order_id: orderId });
}

/** Admin panel action: list orders, newest first, optionally filtered by
 *  status. Shared by the doPost "admin_list" branch (what admin.html
 *  actually calls) and the doGet "admin_list" branch (kept only for
 *  backward-compat / manual testing via a pasted URL). */

/** Fast dashboard counts — no provider API, minimal payload. */
/**
 * হিসাব আলাদা আলাদা রাখা হয়েছে:
 *  - অর্ডার (সার্ভিস বিক্রি)  → counts / today / daily / all_time   (TOPUP সারি এখানে ধরা হয় না)
 *  - টপ-আপ (ওয়ালেটে টাকা ঢোকা) → topup_counts / topup_today / topup_all   (আলাদা)
 *  - প্রতিটি অর্ডারের জন্য তিনটা সংখ্যা আলাদা:
 *      customer = কাস্টমারের বিল (sheet-এর amount)
 *      cost     = প্রোভাইডারের খরচ (প্রোভাইডার দাম $/1K × qty ÷ 1000 × USD_TO_BDT_RATE)
 *      profit   = customer − cost
 *    শুধু "accepted" অর্ডার (Processing / Partial / Approved / Completed) টাকার হিসাবে ধরা হয়।
 *    Pending (এখনো যাচাই হয়নি) ও Rejected আলাদা দেখানো হয়, বিক্রিতে ধরা হয় না।
 *  - দিন ঠিক হয় Asia/Dhaka টাইমজোন দিয়ে (UTC নয়)।
 */
function dhakaDayKey_(v) {
  try {
    if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v.getTime())) {
      return Utilities.formatDate(v, "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    }
    var s = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim();
    if (!s) return "";
    // ISO string with explicit zone (…Z or …+06:00) → convert to Dhaka day
    if (/[zZ]$|[+-]\d\d:?\d\d$/.test(s) && s.indexOf("T") > 0) {
      var d = new Date(s);
      if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return Utilities.formatDate(d, "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd");
    }
    return s.substring(0, 10);
  } catch (e) {
    return String(v || "").substring(0, 10);
  }
}

function round2_(n) { return Math.round((Number(n) || 0) * 100) / 100; }

function newOrderAgg_() {
  return {
    orders: 0, pending: 0, accepted: 0, completed: 0, rejected: 0,
    customer: 0, cost: 0, profit: 0,                       // accepted অর্ডার
    completed_customer: 0, completed_cost: 0, completed_profit: 0, // শুধু Completed
    pending_amount: 0, rejected_amount: 0,
    cost_missing: 0,                                        // দাম খুঁজে না পাওয়া অর্ডার
    _cust_known: 0, _done_cust_known: 0
  };
}

function finishOrderAgg_(a) {
  var out = {
    orders: a.orders, pending: a.pending, accepted: a.accepted, completed: a.completed, rejected: a.rejected,
    customer: round2_(a.customer), cost: round2_(a.cost), profit: round2_(a._cust_known - a.cost),
    completed_customer: round2_(a.completed_customer), completed_cost: round2_(a.completed_cost),
    completed_profit: round2_(a._done_cust_known - a.completed_cost),
    pending_amount: round2_(a.pending_amount), rejected_amount: round2_(a.rejected_amount),
    cost_missing: a.cost_missing
  };
  return out;
}

// ---------- ধাপ 1: দীর্ঘ চলমান (আটকে থাকা) সার্ভিস প্যানেল ----------
// চলমান = অর্ডারের status "Processing" (প্রোভাইডারের in progress/pending ও এখানে Processing হয়)।
// শুরুর সময় = forwarded_at (কলাম 18), না থাকলে created_at (কলাম 12)। completed_at থাকলে চলমান নয়।
// শুধু-পড়া: শীটে কিছু লেখে না। থ্রেশহোল্ড: Script Property STUCK_HOURS (ডিফল্ট 4)।
var STUCK_CACHE_KEY = "STUCK_GROUPS_V1";
var STUCK_CACHE_SECONDS = 30;
var STUCK_MAX_ORDERS_PER_SERVICE = 40;

function getStuckHours_() {
  var n = Number(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("STUCK_HOURS"));
  return (n > 0 && n <= 720) ? n : 4;
}

function stuckToMs_(v) {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") return NaN;
  if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]") return v.getTime();
  var t = Date.parse(String(v).trim());
  return isNaN(t) ? NaN : t;
}

function stuckBuildGroups_(sh) {
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  var groups = {};
  if (lastRow < 2) return groups;
  var nRows = lastRow - 1;
  var stCol = sh.getRange(2, COL_STATUS, nRows, 1).getValues();
  var minIdx = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < nRows; i++) {
    if (String(stCol[i][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "processing") { minIdx = i; break; }
  }
  if (minIdx === -1) return groups;
  var vals = sh.getRange(2 + minIdx, 1, nRows - minIdx, COL_COMPLETED_AT).getValues();
  var nowMs = Date.now();
  for (var r = 0; r < vals.length; r++) {
    var row = vals[r];
    if (String(row[COL_STATUS - 1] || "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "processing") continue;
    var code = String(row[COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || "").trim();
    if (!code || code.toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue;
    if (String(row[COL_COMPLETED_AT - 1] || "").trim()) continue;
    var fwd = row[COL_FORWARDED_AT - 1];
    var startMs = stuckToMs_(fwd);
    var startRaw = fwd;
    if (isNaN(startMs)) { startRaw = row[11]; startMs = stuckToMs_(startRaw); }
    var hours = isNaN(startMs) ? null : Math.max(0, (nowMs - startMs) / 3600000);
    var g = groups[code];
    if (!g) {
      g = groups[code] = { service_code: code, service_name: String(row[2] || ""), platform: String(row[3] || ""), running: 0, orders: [] };
    }
    g.running++;
    g.orders.push({
      order_id: String(row[COL_ORDERID - 1] || ""),
      link: String(row[COL_LINK - 1] || ""),
      quantity: Number(row[COL_QTY - 1]) || 0,
      status: "Processing",
      started_at: startMs ? Utilities.formatDate(new Date(startMs), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX") : "",
      start_ms: isNaN(startMs) ? 0 : startMs,
      provider_order_id: String(row[COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID - 1] || "")
    });
  }
  return groups;
}

function handleAdminStuck_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });

  // UI থেকে থ্রেশহোল্ড বদলানো (ঐচ্ছিক)
  if (data.set_threshold !== undefined && data.set_threshold !== null && data.set_threshold !== "") {
    var nt = Number(data.set_threshold);
    if (!(nt > 0 && nt <= 720)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_threshold" });
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("STUCK_HOURS", String(nt));
  }
  var threshold = getStuckHours_();
  var force = data.force === true || data.force === "true" || data.force === 1 || data.set_threshold !== undefined;

  var groups = null, cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  if (!force) {
    try { var c = cache.get(STUCK_CACHE_KEY); if (c) groups = JSON.parse(c); } catch (e1) { groups = null; }
  }
  if (!groups) {
    groups = stuckBuildGroups_(sh);
    try { var js = JSON.stringify(groups); if (js.length < 90000) cache.put(STUCK_CACHE_KEY, js, STUCK_CACHE_SECONDS); } catch (e2) {}
  }

  var hiddenSet = {};
  try { getServiceMeta_().hidden.forEach(function (h) { hiddenSet[String(h)] = 1; }); } catch (e3) {}
  var nowMs = Date.now();
  var services = [], totRunning = 0, totOver = 0;
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(function (code) {
    var g = groups[code];
    var over = 0, oldest = 0;
    var orders = g.orders.map(function (o) {
      var h = o.start_ms ? Math.max(0, (nowMs - o.start_ms) / 3600000) : null;
      if (h !== null) { if (h >= threshold) over++; if (h > oldest) oldest = h; }
      return { order_id: o.order_id, link: o.link, quantity: o.quantity, status: o.status,
               started_at: o.started_at, hours: h === null ? null : Math.round(h * 10) / 10,
               provider_order_id: o.provider_order_id, over: h !== null && h >= threshold };
    });
    orders.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.hours || 0) - (a.hours || 0); });
    services.push({ service_code: g.service_code, service_name: g.service_name, platform: g.platform,
      hidden: !!hiddenSet[code], running: g.running, over: over, oldest_hours: Math.round(oldest * 10) / 10,
      truncated: orders.length > STUCK_MAX_ORDERS_PER_SERVICE,
      orders: orders.slice(0, STUCK_MAX_ORDERS_PER_SERVICE) });
    totRunning += g.running; totOver += over;
  });
  // চলমান সার্ভিস ওপরে; তাদের মধ্যে বেশি পুরনো আগে
  services.sort(function (a, b) {
    if ((b.running > 0) !== (a.running > 0)) return b.running > 0 ? 1 : -1;
    return b.oldest_hours - a.oldest_hours;
  });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, threshold_hours: threshold, now: nowIsoDhaka_(),
    totals: { running: totRunning, over: totOver, services: services.length }, services: services });
}

// ---------- ধাপ 2: হোম ড্যাশবোর্ড (শুধু সংখ্যা; আয়/লাভ ইচ্ছাকৃতভাবে নেই) ----------
// শুধু-পড়া। ৩০ সেকেন্ড ক্যাশ। প্রোভাইডার ব্যালেন্স এখানে নেই (ধীর) — অ্যাডমিন আলাদা বাটনে admin_balances ডাকে।
var DASH_CACHE_KEY = "DASH_V1";
function handleAdminDashboard_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var force = data.force === true || data.force === "true" || data.force === 1;
  var todayKey = dhakaDayKey_(new Date());
  if (!force) {
    try {
      var c = cache.get(DASH_CACHE_KEY);
      if (c) { var cj = JSON.parse(c); if (cj && cj.date === todayKey) return jsonOut_(cj); }
    } catch (e0) {}
  }
  var out = {
    ok: true, date: todayKey, now: nowIsoDhaka_(),
    today: { orders: 0, pending: 0, processing: 0, partial: 0, completed: 0, rejected: 0, other: 0 },
    live: { pending: 0, processing: 0, partial: 0 },
    topup: { pending: 0 },
    stuck: { over: 0, running: 0, threshold_hours: getStuckHours_() },
    tickets: { open: 0, in_progress: 0 }
  };
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var n = lastRow - 1;
    var codes = sh.getRange(2, COL_SERVICE_CODE, n, 1).getValues();
    var sts = sh.getRange(2, COL_STATUS, n, 1).getValues();
    var crs = sh.getRange(2, 12, n, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var code = String(codes[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      var st = String(sts[i][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (code === "TOPUP") { if (st === "pending") out.topup.pending++; continue; }
      if (st === "pending") out.live.pending++;
      else if (st === "processing") out.live.processing++;
      else if (st === "partial") out.live.partial++;
      if (dhakaDayKey_(crs[i][0]) === todayKey) {
        out.today.orders++;
        if (out.today.hasOwnProperty(st)) out.today[st]++; else out.today.other++;
      }
    }
  }
  // আটকে থাকা (ধাপ 1-এর একই গ্রুপ/ক্যাশ)
  try {
    var groups = null;
    try { var gc = cache.get(STUCK_CACHE_KEY); if (gc) groups = JSON.parse(gc); } catch (e1) {}
    if (!groups) {
      groups = stuckBuildGroups_(sh);
      try { var js = JSON.stringify(groups); if (js.length < 90000) cache.put(STUCK_CACHE_KEY, js, STUCK_CACHE_SECONDS); } catch (e2) {}
    }
    var nowMs = Date.now(), th = out.stuck.threshold_hours;
    Object.keys(groups).forEach(function (k) {
      groups[k].orders.forEach(function (o) {
        out.stuck.running++;
        if (o.start_ms && (nowMs - o.start_ms) / 3600000 >= th) out.stuck.over++;
      });
    });
  } catch (e3) {}
  // টিকেট
  try {
    var tsh = ticketSheet_(), tl = tsh.getLastRow();
    if (tl >= 2) {
      var tv = tsh.getRange(2, 9, tl - 1, 1).getValues();
      for (var t = 0; t < tv.length; t++) {
        var ts = String(tv[t][0] || "Open").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (ts === "open") out.tickets.open++; else if (ts === "in progress") out.tickets.in_progress++;
      }
    }
  } catch (e4) {}
  try { cache.put(DASH_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(out), STUCK_CACHE_SECONDS); } catch (e5) {}
  return jsonOut_(out);
}

// ---------- ধাপ 3: বাল্ক অ্যাকশন (admin_bulk) ----------
// প্রতিটি অর্ডার বিদ্যমান একক handler দিয়েই চলে (নতুন রিফান্ড/টাকার লজিক নেই)।
// সর্বোচ্চ ২৫টি; গ্লোবাল লকের ভেতরে (doPost); batch_id দিয়ে ডবল-সাবমিট নিরাপদ; TOPUP সারি বাদ।
var BULK_MAX = 25;
var BULK_TIME_GUARD_MS = 150000;
var BULK_ELIGIBLE = {
  complete: { processing: 1, partial: 1, approved: 1 },
  cancel:   { pending: 1, approved: 1, processing: 1, partial: 1 },
  retry:    { pending: 1, approved: 1, processing: 1, partial: 1 },
  refill:   { completed: 1 }
};
function bulkUnwrap_(o) {
  try { if (o && typeof o.getContent === "function") return JSON.parse(o.getContent()); } catch (e) {}
  return o || {};
}
function handleAdminBulk_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var op = String(data.op || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!BULK_ELIGIBLE[op]) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_op" });
  var batchId = String(data.batch_id || "").trim();
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,64}$/.test(batchId)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_batch_id" });
  var ids = [], seen = {};
  (Array.isArray(data.order_ids) ? data.order_ids : []).forEach(function (x) {
    var id = String(x || "").trim();
    if (id && !seen[id]) { seen[id] = 1; ids.push(id); }
  });
  if (!ids.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_orders" });
  if (ids.length > BULK_MAX) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "too_many", message: "একবারে সর্বোচ্চ " + BULK_MAX + "টি।" });

  // ডবল-সাবমিট: একই batch_id আবার এলে আগের ফলই ফেরত
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(), ckey = "BULK_" + batchId;
  try {
    var prev = cache.get(ckey);
    if (prev) {
      var pj = JSON.parse(prev);
      if (pj && pj.state === "done") { pj.result.duplicate = true; return jsonOut_(pj.result); }
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "in_progress", message: "এই ব্যাচ চলছে বা আধাআধি থেমেছে — নতুন করে চালাবেন না, অর্ডার তালিকা দেখুন।" });
    }
    cache.put(ckey, JSON.stringify({ state: "running" }), 600);
  } catch (e0) {}

  var t0 = Date.now();
  var success = [], failed = [], skipped = [], refunded = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
    var oid = ids[i];
    if (Date.now() - t0 > BULK_TIME_GUARD_MS) { skipped.push({ order_id: oid, reason: "time_limit" }); continue; }
    try {
      var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, oid);
      if (row === -1) { failed.push({ order_id: oid, error: "order_not_found" }); continue; }
      if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) { skipped.push({ order_id: oid, reason: "topup_use_single" }); continue; }
      var st = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!BULK_ELIGIBLE[op][st]) { skipped.push({ order_id: oid, reason: "status_" + (st || "empty") }); continue; }
      var res;
      if (op === "complete") res = bulkUnwrap_(handleAdminUpdate_(sh, { token: data.token, order_id: oid, status: "Completed", note: "Bulk complete" }));
      else if (op === "cancel") res = bulkUnwrap_(handleAdminUpdate_(sh, { token: data.token, order_id: oid, status: "Rejected", note: "Bulk cancel" }));
      else if (op === "retry") res = bulkUnwrap_(handleAdminRetryForward_(sh, { token: data.token, order_id: oid }));
      else res = bulkUnwrap_(handleAdminRefill_(sh, { token: data.token, order_id: oid }));
      if (res && res.ok !== false && !res.error) {
        success.push({ order_id: oid, status: res.status || "", refunded: res.refunded || 0 });
        if (res.refunded) refunded += Number(res.refunded) || 0;
        if (res.refund_error) failed.push({ order_id: oid, error: "refund_error:" + res.refund_error });
      } else {
        failed.push({ order_id: oid, error: String((res && (res.error || res.message)) || "unknown") });
      }
      if (op === "retry" || op === "refill") Utilities.sleep(300); // প্রোভাইডার রেট-লিমিট
    } catch (eI) {
      failed.push({ order_id: oid, error: "exception:" + String(eI && eI.message || eI).slice(0, 120) });
    }
  }
  var result = { ok: true, batch_id: batchId, op: op, total: ids.length,
    success: success, failed: failed, skipped: skipped, refunded_total: refunded,
    partial: failed.length > 0 || skipped.length > 0 };
  try { cache.put(ckey, JSON.stringify({ state: "done", result: result }), 600); } catch (e1) {}
  return jsonOut_(result);
}

// ---------- ধাপ 5: সার্ভিস হেলথ স্কোর (admin_health) ----------
// শুধু-পড়া। ভিত্তি: SpeedLog-এর সর্বশেষ SPEED_WINDOW রেকর্ড (getSpeedStats_)। কিছু লুকায়/বন্ধ করে না —
// খারাপ হলে শুধু "লুকানোর প্রস্তাব" দেয়; সিদ্ধান্ত অ্যাডমিনের। ধাপ 1-এর "৪ঘণ্টা+" আটকে-থাকা ধারণার সঙ্গে আলাদা।
// স্কোর (০–১০০) = সম্পন্ন-হার ৫০ + ব্যর্থতা ২৫ + গতি ২৫। (রিফিল-হার বাদ: OrderActions-এ সার্ভিস-কোড নেই।)
function healthClamp_(x, lo, hi) { return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, x)); }
function healthScoreOne_(x, cfg, limitH) {
  var total = x.total || 0;
  var completion = total ? (x.n / total) : 0;
  var failPct = total ? (x.fail / total) * 100 : 0;
  var partialPct = total ? (x.partial / total) * 100 : 0;
  var sComp = 50 * completion;
  var sFail = 25 * healthClamp_(1 - failPct / (cfg.max_fail_pct * 1.5), 0, 1);
  var sSpeed = 0;
  if (x.n > 0 && x.med_sec > 0) {
    var L = limitH * 3600;
    sSpeed = 25 * healthClamp_((1.5 * L - x.med_sec) / (1.25 * L), 0, 1);
  }
  var reasons = [];
  if (failPct > cfg.max_fail_pct) reasons.push("ব্যর্থ " + Math.round(failPct) + "% (" + x.fail + "/" + total + ")");
  if (x.n >= 1 && x.med_sec > limitH * 3600) reasons.push("ধীর: মিডিয়ান " + formatDurationShortBn_(x.med_sec * 1000) + " > সীমা " + limitH + " ঘণ্টা");
  if (partialPct >= 30) reasons.push("পার্শিয়াল " + Math.round(partialPct) + "%");
  if (completion < 0.5) reasons.push("সম্পন্ন মাত্র " + Math.round(completion * 100) + "%");
  return { score: Math.round(sComp + sFail + sSpeed), fail_pct: Math.round(failPct), partial_pct: Math.round(partialPct), reasons: reasons };
}
function handleAdminHealth_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var cfg = getSpeedConfig_(), meta = getServiceMeta_(), st = getSpeedStats_();
  var hiddenSet = {}; (meta.hidden || []).forEach(function (c) { hiddenSet[String(c)] = 1; });
  var exemptSet = {}; (meta.exempt || []).forEach(function (c) { exemptSet[String(c)] = 1; });
  var list = [], counts = { good: 0, ok: 0, bad: 0, nodata: 0 }, suggest = 0;
  Object.keys(st).forEach(function (code) {
    var x = st[code];
    var limitH = Number(meta.limits && meta.limits[code]) || cfg.max_hours;
    var row = { code: code, total: x.total, n: x.n, fail: x.fail, partial: x.partial,
      med_sec: x.med_sec, med_label: x.med_sec ? formatDurationShortBn_(x.med_sec * 1000) : "—",
      hidden: !!hiddenSet[code], exempt: !!exemptSet[code] };
    if (x.total < cfg.min_samples) {
      row.score = null; row.grade = "nodata"; row.reasons = []; row.suggest_hide = false; counts.nodata++;
    } else {
      var h = healthScoreOne_(x, cfg, limitH);
      row.score = h.score; row.fail_pct = h.fail_pct; row.partial_pct = h.partial_pct; row.reasons = h.reasons;
      row.grade = h.score >= 80 ? "good" : (h.score >= 60 ? "ok" : "bad");
      row.suggest_hide = h.score < 50 && !row.hidden && !row.exempt;
      counts[row.grade]++; if (row.suggest_hide) suggest++;
    }
    list.push(row);
  });
  list.sort(function (a, b) {
    var sa = a.score === null ? 999 : a.score, sb = b.score === null ? 999 : b.score;
    return sa - sb;
  });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, config: cfg, counts: counts, suggest_hide: suggest, services: list });
}

// ---------- ধাপ 6: খরচ-বাড়ার পরীক্ষা (admin_cost_watch) — শুধু-পড়া ----------
// মার্জিন-রুলে দাম বসানোর সময় রাখা প্রোভাইডার-খরচের ছবি (cost_snap, USD/1K) এর সাথে এখনকার খরচ মেলায়।
// খরচ বাড়লে (বা এখনকার ছাড়-পরবর্তী মার্জিন ন্যূনতমের নিচে নামলে) তালিকায় আনে। কিছু নিজে বদলায় না।
function handleAdminCostWatch_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var minM = Number(data.min_margin); if (!(minM >= 0 && minM < 90)) minM = 10;
  var meta = getServiceMeta_(), idx = loadServiceRateIndex_(), items = [];
  Object.keys(meta.cost_snap || {}).forEach(function (code) {
    var price = Number(meta.prices[code]);
    if (!(price > 0)) return;                         // কাস্টম দাম আর নেই — নজরদারি বন্ধ
    var snap = Number(meta.cost_snap[code]), now = Number(idx[code]);
    if (!(snap > 0) || !(now > 0)) return;
    var cost = now * USD_TO_BDT_RATE, fin = price, d = meta.discounts[code];
    if (discountActive_(d)) {
      var val = Number(d.value) || 0;
      var off = d.type === "fixed" ? val : price * Math.min(val, 90) / 100;
      fin = price - Math.max(0, Math.min(off, price * 0.9));
    }
    var mg = fin > 0 ? ((fin - cost) / fin) * 100 : -100;
    var rose = now > snap * 1.0001, low = mg < minM;
    if (!rose && !low) return;
    items.push({ code: code, snap_usd: snap, now_usd: now, rise_pct: Math.round(((now - snap) / snap) * 1000) / 10,
      price: price, final_price: Math.round(fin * 100) / 100, margin_now: Math.round(mg * 10) / 10,
      rose: rose, below_min: low, hidden: (meta.hidden || []).indexOf(code) !== -1 });
  });
  items.sort(function (a, b) { return a.margin_now - b.margin_now; });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, min_margin: minM, count: items.length, items: items.slice(0, 100) });
}

// ---------- ধাপ 7: ব্যাকআপ প্রোভাইডার (ম্যানুয়াল ফেইলওভার) ----------
// নকশা: সাধারণ অর্ডারে কিছুই বদলায় না (প্রোভাইডার = সার্ভিস-কোডের প্রাইমারি)। শুধু ফেইলওভার-করা অর্ডার "পিন" শীটে
// (ProviderPins) লেখা হয়; স্ট্যাটাস/রিফিল/ক্যানসেল-পথ ওই পিন দেখে সঠিক প্রোভাইডারে যায়। স্বয়ংক্রিয় নয় — অ্যাডমিন চাপলে।
// ডুপ্লিকেট-সুরক্ষা: প্রাইমারিতে অর্ডার চলছে/অজানা হলে অ্যাডমিনের স্পষ্ট নিশ্চিতকরণ ছাড়া যায় না; একটি অর্ডার একবারই।
var PINS_SHEET = "ProviderPins", PINS_CACHE_KEY = "PINS_V1";
function pinsSheet_(create) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(PINS_SHEET);
  if (!sh && create) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(PINS_SHEET);
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 8).setValues([["key", "provider", "service_id", "order_id", "old_provider", "old_poid", "ts", "actor"]]);
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}
function loadPins_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  try { var c = cache.get(PINS_CACHE_KEY); if (c) return JSON.parse(c); } catch (e) {}
  var out = { byPoid: {}, byOrder: {} };
  try {
    var sh = pinsSheet_(false);
    if (sh && sh.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var from = Math.max(2, sh.getLastRow() - 3000 + 1);
      sh.getRange(from, 1, sh.getLastRow() - from + 1, 4).getValues().forEach(function (r) {
        var k = String(r[0] || ""); if (!k) return;
        var pin = { p: String(r[1] || ""), s: String(r[2] || ""), o: String(r[3] || "") };
        out.byPoid[k] = pin; if (pin.o) out.byOrder[pin.o] = pin;
      });
    }
  } catch (e1) {}
  try { var js = JSON.stringify(out); if (js.length < 90000) cache.put(PINS_CACHE_KEY, js, 300); } catch (e2) {}
  return out;
}
function getPinByPoid_(code, poid) { if (!code || !poid) return null; return loadPins_().byPoid[String(code) + "|" + String(poid)] || null; }
function getPinByOrder_(oid) { if (!oid) return null; return loadPins_().byOrder[String(oid)] || null; }
function providerForOrder_(code, poid) {
  var pin = getPinByPoid_(code, poid);
  if (pin && pin.p) return pin.p;
  return loadProviderIndex_()[code] || "";
}
function addPin_(code, poid, orderId, provider, serviceId, oldProv, oldPoid, actor) {
  var sh = pinsSheet_(true);
  sh.appendRow([String(code) + "|" + String(poid), provider, "'" + String(serviceId), orderId, oldProv, oldPoid, nowIsoDhaka_(), actor || ""]);
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(PINS_CACHE_KEY); } catch (e) {}
}

/** ব্যাকআপ প্রোভাইডারের "services" তালিকায় এই আইডি আছে কিনা। {name, rate, min, max} | {notfound:true} | null (আনা যায়নি) */
function backupServiceLookup_(cfg, serviceId) {
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, { method: "post", payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "services" }, muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var arr = JSON.parse(resp.getContentText());
    if (!arr || !arr.length) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      if (String(arr[i].service) === String(serviceId)) {
        return { name: String(arr[i].name || "").slice(0, 160), rate: arr[i].rate, min: arr[i].min, max: arr[i].max, category: String(arr[i].category || "").slice(0, 80) };
      }
    }
    return { notfound: true };
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}

var FAILOVER_ELIGIBLE = { pending: 1, approved: 1, processing: 1 };
/** ফেইলওভারের যোগ্যতা ও ঝুঁকি-পরিকল্পনা (শুধু-পড়া; প্রাইমারির লাইভ স্ট্যাটাস আনে)। */
function failoverPlan_(sh, row) {
  var oid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim();
  var code = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var st = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) return { ok: false, error: "topup" };
  if (!FAILOVER_ELIGIBLE[st]) return { ok: false, error: "status_" + (st || "empty") };
  var bk = (getServiceMeta_().backup || {})[code];
  if (!bk || !bk.provider || !bk.service_id) return { ok: false, error: "no_backup" };
  if (getPinByOrder_(oid)) return { ok: false, error: "already_failed_over" };
  var primary = loadProviderIndex_()[code] || "";
  if (!primary) return { ok: false, error: "no_primary" };
  if (primary === bk.provider) return { ok: false, error: "same_provider" };
  var cfgB = getProviderConfig_()[bk.provider];
  if (!cfgB || !cfgB.api_key || !cfgB.api_url) return { ok: false, error: "no_backup_key" };
  var origQty = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue()) || 0;
  var plan = { ok: true, order_id: oid, service_code: code, status: st, primary: primary, backup: bk.provider, backup_service_id: bk.service_id,
    provider_order_id: poid, qty: origQty, orig_qty: origQty, primary_status: "", dead: false, needs_confirm: false, reason: "" };
  if (!poid) { plan.reason = "not_forwarded"; return plan; }   // প্রাইমারিতে কখনো যায়নি — সরাসরি ব্যাকআপে
  var lv = freshLiveStatus_(code, poid);
  var ps = lv && lv.status ? String(lv.status).trim().toLowerCase() : "";
  plan.primary_status = ps;
  if (ps === "completed") return { ok: false, error: "primary_completed", primary_status: ps };
  if (isCanceledProviderStatus_(ps) || ps === "failed" || ps === "refunded") {
    plan.dead = true; plan.reason = "primary_dead";
    var rq = canceledReorderQty_(sh, row, lv);
    if (rq > 0) plan.qty = rq;                       // বাকি (remains) পরিমাণই ব্যাকআপে
  } else {
    plan.needs_confirm = true;                       // চলছে/অজানা — ডুপ্লিকেট ডেলিভারির ঝুঁকি
    plan.reason = ps ? "primary_active" : "primary_unknown";
    if (lv && Number(lv.start_count) >= 0 && ps) plan.progress = { start_count: lv.start_count, remains: lv.remains };
  }
  return plan;
}
function handleAdminFailoverCheck_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  return jsonOut_(failoverPlan_(sh, row));
}
function handleAdminFailover_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  var plan = failoverPlan_(sh, row);
  if (!plan.ok) return jsonOut_(plan);
  var confirmed = data.confirm_primary_dead === true || data.confirm_primary_dead === "true";
