  if (plan.needs_confirm && !confirmed) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "confirm_required", message: "প্রাইমারিতে অর্ডার চলছে/অজানা — আগে নিশ্চিত করুন।", plan: plan });
  var oid = plan.order_id;
  appendNote_(sh, row, "Failover (admin): " + plan.primary + (plan.provider_order_id ? " order " + plan.provider_order_id : " (never forwarded)") + " → backup " + plan.backup +
    (plan.qty !== plan.orig_qty ? " (qty " + plan.qty + " of " + plan.orig_qty + ")" : "") + (plan.needs_confirm ? " [admin confirmed primary not delivering]" : "") + ".");
  if (plan.dead && plan.provider_order_id) { try { logDelivery_(sh, row, nowIsoDhaka_(), "failed"); } catch (eLg) {} }   // প্রাইমারির ব্যর্থ-রেকর্ড (হেলথ স্কোরের জন্য)
  var r = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row, plan.qty, { provider: plan.backup, service_id: plan.backup_service_id,
    old_provider: plan.primary, old_poid: plan.provider_order_id, actor: String(data.actor || "").slice(0, 40) }) || { ok: false, error: "unknown" };
  if (r.ok) {
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("LIVESTATUS_" + plan.provider_order_id); } catch (e) {}
    notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, r, "🔁 ব্যাকআপ প্রভাইডারে গেছে (" + plan.backup + ")");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, order_id: oid, backup: plan.backup, provider_order_id: r.provider_order_id, qty: plan.qty });
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: r.error || "forward_failed", message: "ব্যাকআপে পাঠানো যায়নি; অর্ডার আগের অবস্থায় আছে।", order_id: oid });
}

function handleAdminStats_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });

  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();

  // অর্ডার স্ট্যাটাস কাউন্ট — শুধু আসল অর্ডার (TOPUP বাদ)
  var counts = { Pending: 0, Processing: 0, Partial: 0, Completed: 0, Rejected: 0, Other: 0, total: 0 };
  // টপ-আপ আলাদা
  var topCounts = { Pending: 0, Completed: 0, Rejected: 0, Other: 0, total: 0 };

  var todayStr = dhakaDayKey_(new Date());
  var dailyAgg = {};   // শুরু থেকে আজ পর্যন্ত — যে দিনে অর্ডার আছে সেই দিনগুলো
  dailyAgg[todayStr] = newOrderAgg_();

  var todayAgg = newOrderAgg_();
  var allAgg = newOrderAgg_();

  var topToday = { requests: 0, credited: 0, credited_amount: 0, pending: 0, pending_amount: 0, rejected: 0, rejected_amount: 0 };
  var topAll = { credited: 0, credited_amount: 0 };

  var priceIndex = null, costOk = true;
  try { priceIndex = loadPriceIndex_(); } catch (ePI) { priceIndex = {}; costOk = false; }

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    // A..L : B=service_code(2), F=quantity(6), G=amount(7), K=status(11), L=created_at(12)
    var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 12).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
      var code = String(vals[i][1] || "").trim();
      var qty = Number(vals[i][5]) || 0;
      var amount = Number(vals[i][6]) || 0;
      var st = String(vals[i][10] || "").trim();
      var stL = st.toLowerCase();
      var dstr = dhakaDayKey_(vals[i][11]);
      var isTop = code.toUpperCase() === "TOPUP";

      if (isTop) {
        topCounts.total++;
        if (topCounts.hasOwnProperty(st)) topCounts[st]++; else topCounts.Other++;
        if (stL === "completed") { topAll.credited++; topAll.credited_amount += amount; }
        if (dstr === todayStr) {
          topToday.requests++;
          if (stL === "completed") { topToday.credited++; topToday.credited_amount += amount; }
          else if (stL === "pending") { topToday.pending++; topToday.pending_amount += amount; }
          else if (stL === "rejected") { topToday.rejected++; topToday.rejected_amount += amount; }
        }
        continue; // টপ-আপ অর্ডারের হিসাবে যাবে না
      }

      // ---- আসল অর্ডার ----
      counts.total++;
      if (counts.hasOwnProperty(st)) counts[st]++; else counts.Other++;

      var aggs = [allAgg];
      if (dstr === todayStr) aggs.push(todayAgg);
      if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dstr)) {
        if (!dailyAgg[dstr]) dailyAgg[dstr] = newOrderAgg_();
        aggs.push(dailyAgg[dstr]);
      }

      var accepted = (stL === "processing" || stL === "partial" || stL === "approved" || stL === "completed");
      var cost = 0, costKnown = false;
      if (accepted) {
        var pkg = priceIndex && priceIndex[code];
        if (pkg && costOk) {
          cost = (Number(pkg.price_usd) || 0) * USD_TO_BDT_RATE * qty / 1000;
          costKnown = true;
        }
      }

      for (var ai = 0; ai < aggs.length; ai++) {
        var a = aggs[ai];
        a.orders++;
        if (stL === "pending") { a.pending++; a.pending_amount += amount; }
        else if (stL === "rejected") { a.rejected++; a.rejected_amount += amount; }
        else if (accepted) {
          a.accepted++;
          a.customer += amount;
          if (costKnown) { a.cost += cost; a._cust_known += amount; } else { a.cost_missing++; }
          if (stL === "completed") {
            a.completed++;
            a.completed_customer += amount;
            if (costKnown) { a.completed_cost += cost; a._done_cust_known += amount; }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  var today = finishOrderAgg_(todayAgg);
  var daily = Object.keys(dailyAgg).sort().reverse().map(function (k) { var o = finishOrderAgg_(dailyAgg[k]); o.date = k; return o; });
  var firstDate = daily.length ? daily[daily.length - 1].date : todayStr;

  // পুরোনো admin পেজ/টেলিগ্রামের জন্য আগের ফিল্ডের নাম বজায় রাখা হলো (এখন শুধু অর্ডারের হিসাব)
  today.date = todayStr;
  today.amount = today.customer;
  today.completed_amount = today.completed_customer;

  return jsonOut_({
    ok: true,
    counts: counts,
    today: today,
    daily: daily,
    all_time: finishOrderAgg_(allAgg),
    first_date: firstDate,
    topup_counts: topCounts,
    topup_today: {
      requests: topToday.requests,
      credited: topToday.credited, credited_amount: round2_(topToday.credited_amount),
      pending: topToday.pending, pending_amount: round2_(topToday.pending_amount),
      rejected: topToday.rejected, rejected_amount: round2_(topToday.rejected_amount)
    },
    topup_all: { credited: topAll.credited, credited_amount: round2_(topAll.credited_amount) },
    cost_ok: costOk,
    usd_rate: USD_TO_BDT_RATE
  });
}

function handleAdminList_(sh, token, status, opts) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(token);
  if (authErr) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  }
  opts = opts || {};
  // live=true → check provider status (slow). Default OFF for instant list.
  var wantLive = opts.live === true || opts.live === "true" || opts.live === 1 || opts.live === "1";
  var maxLive = wantLive ? 5 : 0;
  var maxOrders = 80;

  var filterStatus = String(status || "Pending").trim();
  var q = String((opts && opts.q) || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  var orders = [];
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var lastCol = Math.min(sh.getLastColumn(), 20);
    var headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0];
    var nRows = lastRow - 1;
    var allValues;
    var dFrom = String((opts && opts.date_from) || "").trim(), dTo = String((opts && opts.date_to) || "").trim();
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dFrom)) dFrom = "";
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dTo)) dTo = "";
    if (q || dFrom || dTo) {
      allValues = sh.getRange(2, 1, nRows, lastCol).getValues(); // search/date filter needs every row
    } else {
      // Fast path: read only the status column, find the newest <= maxOrders matches,
      // then read just the block from the oldest match to the end (not the whole sheet).
      var stCol = sh.getRange(2, COL_STATUS, nRows, 1).getValues();
      var wantAllSt = filterStatus === "All";
      var fl = filterStatus.toLowerCase();
      var picked = 0, minIdx = -1;
      for (var s0 = nRows - 1; s0 >= 0 && picked < maxOrders; s0--) {
        if (wantAllSt || String(stCol[s0][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === fl) { picked++; minIdx = s0; }
      }
      allValues = (minIdx === -1) ? [] : sh.getRange(2 + minIdx, 1, nRows - minIdx, lastCol).getValues();
    }

    // Lightweight: only load provider index if live sync needed; else client uses provider_map.json
    var provIdx = {}, pinsAL = null;
    var metaIdx = {};
    if (wantLive) {
      try { provIdx = loadProviderIndex_() || {}; } catch (eProv) { provIdx = {}; }
      try { metaIdx = loadServiceMetaIndex_() || {}; } catch (eM) { metaIdx = {}; }
    } else {
      // try cache only (no network) — CacheService may already have index
      try {
        var c = CacheService.getScriptCache().get("PROVIDER_INDEX");
        if (c) provIdx = JSON.parse(c);
      } catch (eC) {}
      try {
        var c2 = CacheService.getScriptCache().get("SERVICE_META_IDX");
        if (c2) metaIdx = JSON.parse(c2);
      } catch (eC2) {}
    }

    var liveLookups = 0;
    for (var i = allValues.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var st = String(allValues[i][COL_STATUS - 1] || "").trim();
      if (filterStatus !== "All" && st.toLowerCase() !== filterStatus.toLowerCase()) continue;
      var obj = {};
      for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) obj[String(headers[j])] = allValues[i][j];
      if (dFrom || dTo) {
        var dk = dhakaDayKey_(obj.created_at);
        if (!dk || (dFrom && dk < dFrom) || (dTo && dk > dTo)) continue;
      }
      // Search: order_id, mobile, txn, service_code, name, provider_order_id
      if (q) {
        var hay = (
          String(obj.order_id || "") + " " +
          String(obj.whatsapp || "") + " " +
          String(obj.txn_id || "") + " " +
          String(obj.service_code || "") + " " +
          String(obj.service_name || "") + " " +
          String(obj.provider_order_id || "") + " " +
          String(obj.link || "")
        ).toLowerCase();
        if (hay.indexOf(q) === -1) continue;
      }

      var scode = String(obj.service_code || "").trim();
      if (scode && provIdx[scode]) obj.provider = provIdx[scode];
      try {   // ব্যাকআপে-সরানো অর্ডারে আসল (ব্যাকআপ) প্রোভাইডার দেখাই
        var pnL = obj.provider_order_id && (pinsAL || (pinsAL = loadPins_().byPoid))[scode + "|" + obj.provider_order_id];
        if (pnL && pnL.p) { obj.provider = pnL.p; obj.failover = true; }
      } catch (ePn) {}
      if (scode && metaIdx[scode]) obj.refill_allowed = !!metaIdx[scode].refill;
      else obj.refill_allowed = true; // show refill btn; server still gates on submit

      var stLower = st.toLowerCase();
      if (maxLive > 0 && obj.provider_order_id && (stLower === "processing" || stLower === "partial") && liveLookups < maxLive) {
        liveLookups++;
        try {
          var live = getLiveProviderStatus_(String(obj.service_code || ""), String(obj.provider_order_id));
          if (live) {
            if (live.start_count !== undefined) obj.live_start_count = live.start_count;
            if (live.remains !== undefined) obj.live_remains = live.remains;
            if (live.status !== undefined) obj.live_provider_status = live.status;
            if (stLower === "processing") {
              var pre2 = checkRemainsZeroComplete_(sh, obj.order_id, String(obj.service_code || ""), live, obj.status || st);
              if (String(pre2).toLowerCase() === "completed") { obj.status = pre2; try { obj.completed_at = String(sh.getRange(findRowByOrderId_(sh, obj.order_id), COL_COMPLETED_AT).getValue() || ""); } catch (eC) {} }
            }
            var synced = syncStatusFromLive_(sh, obj.order_id, live.status, obj.status || st);
            if (synced) obj.status = synced;
          }
        } catch (eLive) {}
      }

      attachTimerFields_(obj, obj.status || st);
      orders.push(obj);
      if (orders.length >= maxOrders) break;
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, orders: orders, live: wantLive });
}


// ---------- Public proof redaction (admin panel) ----------
// Original screenshots (col N) stay private for verification. The admin covers the
// number / TrxID in admin.html, and only that redacted copy (col P) goes to the gallery.
var COL_PUBLIC = 16;
var PUBLIC_PROOF_FOLDER = "PrimeBoost Public Proofs";
var TEMPLATES_KEY = "REDACT_TEMPLATES";

function publicProofFolder_() {
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(PUBLIC_PROOF_FOLDER);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(PUBLIC_PROOF_FOLDER);
}

function fileIdFromUrl_(url) {
  var m = String(url || "").match(/[?&]id=([\w-]+)/) || String(url || "").match(/\/d\/([\w-]+)/);
  return m ? m[1] : "";
}

function trashPublicFile_(url) {
  try {
    var id = fileIdFromUrl_(url);
    if (id) DriveApp.getFileById(id).setTrashed(true);
  } catch (ignore) {}
}

/** Returns the ORIGINAL proof image (base64) so the admin editor can draw on it
 *  without cross-origin canvas problems. Admin token required. */
function handleAdminGetProof_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found" });
  var obj = rowToObject_(sh, row);
  var id = fileIdFromUrl_(obj.proof_url);
  if (!id) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_proof" });
  try {
    var blob = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob();
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: true,
      mime: blob.getContentType(),
      base64: Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes()),
      method: String(obj.paid_from || ""),
      public_url: String(obj.proof_public_url || "")
    });
  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "drive_read_failed", message: String(err) });
  }
}

/** Saves the REDACTED image as the public version (or removes it when image_base64 is empty). */
function handleAdminSavePublic_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found" });
  var cell = sh.getRange(row, COL_PUBLIC);
  var oldUrl = String(cell.getValue() || "");

  if (!data.image_base64) { // unpublish
    trashPublicFile_(oldUrl);
    cell.setValue("");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, url: "" });
  }
  var clean = String(data.image_base64).replace(/\s/g, "");
  var comma = clean.indexOf(",");
  if (comma !== -1) clean = clean.substring(comma + 1);
  if (clean.length < 100) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "empty_image" });
  if (clean.length > 3.5 * 1024 * 1024) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "image_too_large" });
  try {
    var bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(clean);
    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(bytes, "image/jpeg", "public_proof_" + String(data.order_id) + ".jpg");
    var file = publicProofFolder_().createFile(blob);
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    var url = "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" + file.getId() + "=w1200";
    trashPublicFile_(oldUrl);
    cell.setValue(url);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, url: url });
  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "save_failed", message: String(err) });
  }
}

function loadTemplates_() {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(TEMPLATES_KEY) || "{}") || {};
  } catch (e) {
    return {};
  }
}

function handleAdminGetTemplates_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, templates: loadTemplates_() });
}

/** Remembers where the number/TrxID sit for a payment app (bkash / nagad / rocket …)
 *  as fractions of the image (x, y, w, h in 0..1) plus its width/height ratio,
 *  so the next screenshot of the same app opens with the boxes already placed. */
function handleAdminSaveTemplate_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var method = String(data.method || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "").substring(0, 20);
  var ratio = Number(data.ratio);
  var boxes = data.boxes;
  if (!method || !(ratio > 0.1 && ratio < 5) || !Array.isArray(boxes) || !boxes.length || boxes.length > 12) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_template" });
  }
  var clean = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < boxes.length; i++) {
    var b = boxes[i] || {};
    var x = Number(b.x), y = Number(b.y), w = Number(b.w), h = Number(b.h);
    if (isNaN(x) || isNaN(y) || isNaN(w) || isNaN(h) || w <= 0 || h <= 0) continue;
    x = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); y = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, y));
    w = Math.min(1 - x, w); h = Math.min(1 - y, h);
    clean.push({ x: +x.toFixed(4), y: +y.toFixed(4), w: +w.toFixed(4), h: +h.toFixed(4) });
  }
  if (!clean.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_template" });

  var all = loadTemplates_();
  var list = all[method] || [];
  var replaced = false;
  for (var k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
    if (Math.abs(list[k].r - ratio) < 0.03) { list[k] = { r: +ratio.toFixed(4), boxes: clean }; replaced = true; break; }
  }
  if (!replaced) list.push({ r: +ratio.toFixed(4), boxes: clean });
  if (list.length > 8) list = list.slice(list.length - 8);
  all[method] = list;
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(TEMPLATES_KEY, JSON.stringify(all));
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, templates: all });
}


/** Place a refill on the provider panel for one order row. Returns a PLAIN object (not an HTTP response),
 *  so both the admin panel and the customer "Refill" button share exactly the same checks.
 *  `who` is only used in the order note ("admin" | "customer"). */
function refillCore_(sh, row, orderId, who) {
  var providerOrderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (!providerOrderId) {
    return ({
      ok: false,
      error: "no_provider_order",
      message: "এই অর্ডারে provider order ID নেই — আগে auto-forward সফল হতে হবে।"
    });
  }

  var serviceCode = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
  var provider = providerForOrder_(serviceCode, providerOrderId);
  if (!provider) {
    return ({ ok: false, error: "no_provider_map", message: "Service code-এর provider ম্যাপ নেই।" });
  }
  var cfg = getProviderConfig_()[provider];
  if (!cfg || !cfg.api_key || !cfg.api_url) {
    return ({ ok: false, error: "no_api_key", message: "Provider API key নেই: " + provider });
  }

  // Only allow refill when the service has refill enabled on the provider
  var pinR = getPinByPoid_(serviceCode, providerOrderId);
  var allows = providerServiceAllowsRefill_(cfg, pinR && pinR.s ? pinR.s : serviceCode);
  if (allows === false) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill blocked: service " + serviceCode + " has refill OFF on " + provider);
    return ({
      ok: false,
      error: "refill_not_enabled",
      message: "এই সার্ভিসে রিফিল অপশন অফ আছে — রিফিল পাঠানো যাবে না।"
    });
  }
  if (allows === null) {
    // API unknown — fall back to packages tags
    var meta = loadServiceMetaIndex_()[serviceCode];
    if (!meta || !meta.refill) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill blocked: service " + serviceCode + " has no Refill tag / unknown API flag");
      return ({
        ok: false,
        error: "refill_not_enabled",
        message: "এই সার্ভিসে রিফিল অন নেই (packages/API) — রিফিল যাবে না।"
      });
    }
  }

  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(cfg.api_url, {
      method: "post",
      payload: { key: cfg.api_key, action: "refill", order: providerOrderId },
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    var body = resp.getContentText();
    var json = null;
    try { json = JSON.parse(body); } catch (e) {}

    if (json && (json.ok === true || json.status === "ok" || json.refill === "ok" || (!json.error && resp.getResponseCode() < 300))) {
      // Some panels return {} or {status:"ok"} on success
      if (json.error) {
        appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill FAILED (" + provider + "): " + json.error);
        return ({ ok: false, error: String(json.error), provider: provider, provider_order_id: providerOrderId });
      }
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill requested via " + who + " → " + provider + " order " + providerOrderId);
      return ({
        ok: true,
        provider: provider,
        provider_order_id: providerOrderId,
        site_order_id: orderId,
        message: "Refill request sent to " + provider
      });
    }
    if (json && json.error) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill FAILED (" + provider + "): " + json.error);
      return ({ ok: false, error: String(json.error), provider: provider, provider_order_id: providerOrderId });
    }
    // Empty body / unknown — treat non-error HTTP as success for panels that return little
    if (resp.getResponseCode() >= 200 && resp.getResponseCode() < 300 && (!json || !json.error)) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill requested via " + who + " → " + provider + " order " + providerOrderId + " (response: " + body.substring(0, 80) + ")");
      return ({ ok: true, provider: provider, provider_order_id: providerOrderId, site_order_id: orderId, raw: body.substring(0, 120) });
    }
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill unexpected (" + provider + "): " + body.substring(0, 150));
    return ({ ok: false, error: "unexpected_response", body: body.substring(0, 200), provider: provider });
  } catch (err) {
    appendNote_(sh, row, "Refill error: " + err.message);
    return ({ ok: false, error: err.message });
  }
}

/** Place a refill on the provider panel for a site order_id.
 *  Uses SMM API v2: action=refill&order=<provider_order_id>
 *  Body: { action:"admin_refill", token, order_id } */
function handleAdminRefill_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });

  var orderId = String(data.order_id || "").trim();
  if (!orderId) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_id_required" });

  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found", message: "Site order not found: " + orderId });

  return jsonOut_(refillCore_(sh, row, orderId, "admin"));
}

/** True if the ledger already has a refund for this order (prevents double refunds). */
function hasLedgerTopup_(orderId) {
  try {
    var lsh = ledgerSheet_();
    var last = lsh.getLastRow();
    if (last < 2) return false;
    var vals = lsh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 8).getValues();
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (String(vals[i][2]).toLowerCase() === "topup" && String(vals[i][5]) === String(orderId)) return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function hasLedgerRefund_(orderId) {
  try {
    var lsh = ledgerSheet_();
    var last = lsh.getLastRow();
    if (last < 2) return false;
    var vals = lsh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 8).getValues();
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (String(vals[i][2]).toLowerCase() === "refund" && String(vals[i][5]) === String(orderId)) return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

/** When a WALLET-paid order becomes Rejected (admin panel / email button), give the money back
 *  to the customer's wallet. Safe to call repeatedly: it refunds only once per order. */
function refundWalletOrderOnReject_(sh, row, reason) {
  try {
    var method = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_METHOD).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var txn = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_TXN).getValue() || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    if (method !== "wallet" && txn.indexOf("WALLET") !== 0) return { ok: true, skipped: "not_wallet" };
    if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) return { ok: true, skipped: "topup" };
    var orderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "").trim();
    var mobile = normalizeMobile_(sh.getRange(row, 10).getValue());
    var amount = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_AMOUNT).getValue()) || 0;
    if (!orderId || !mobile || amount <= 0) return { ok: false, error: "missing_order_data" };
    if (hasLedgerRefund_(orderId)) return { ok: true, skipped: "already_refunded" };
    var ref = adjustUserBalance_(mobile, amount, "refund", orderId, "Refund: order rejected " + orderId, false);
    if (!ref.ok) {
      appendNote_(sh, row, "⚠️ Refund FAILED ৳" + amount + " (" + (ref.error || "error") + ") — manual refund needed");
      return ref;
    }
    appendNote_(sh, row, "💰 Refunded ৳" + amount + " to wallet " + mobile + " (new balance ৳" + ref.balance + ")");
    try {
      var tgSvc = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim();
      var tgQty = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_QTY).getValue() || "").trim();
      var tgLink = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_LINK).getValue() || "").trim();
      if (tgLink.length > 60) tgLink = tgLink.substring(0, 57) + "...";
      var tgPoid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
      var tgNote = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_NOTE).getValue() || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
      if (tgNote.length > 220) tgNote = "…" + tgNote.substring(tgNote.length - 217);
      notifyTelegram_("↩️ Wallet রিফান্ড\n" + mobile + "\n৳" + amount + "\n" + orderId +
        "\nঅর্ডার Reject হয়েছে" +
        "\n━━━━━━━━" +
        "\nকারণ: " + (reason || "উল্লেখ নেই") +
        (tgSvc ? "\nসার্ভিস কোড: " + tgSvc : "") +
        (tgQty ? "\nপরিমাণ: " + tgQty : "") +
        (tgLink ? "\nলিংক: " + tgLink : "") +
        (tgPoid ? "\nProvider Order: " + tgPoid : "") +
        (tgNote ? "\nনোট: " + tgNote : "") +
        "\nনতুন ব্যালেন্স: ৳" + ref.balance);
    } catch (eTg) {}
    return ref;
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(err) };
  }
}

/** Admin panel action: approve / reject / change status on one order. */
function handleAdminUpdate_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  }
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  }
  var newStatus = String(data.status || "").trim();
  var allowed = { "Pending": 1, "Approved": 1, "Rejected": 1, "Processing": 1, "Partial": 1, "Completed": 1 };
  if (!allowed[newStatus]) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_status" });
  }
  // Wallet top-up: Processing/Completed → credit balance, no provider forward
  if (isTopupRow_(sh, row) && (newStatus === "Processing" || newStatus === "Completed" || newStatus === "Approved")) {
    var top = applyTopupCredit_(sh, row);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: !!top.ok, order_id: data.order_id, status: top.ok ? "Completed" : "Pending", topup: top, error: top.error });
  }
  var prevStatus = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  setStatus_(sh, row, newStatus, data.note ? ("Admin: " + data.note) : ("Manually set to " + newStatus + " via admin panel."));
  if (newStatus === "Completed" || newStatus === "Rejected") speedupClear_(data.order_id);
  // Wallet-paid order rejected -> money goes back to the customer's wallet
  var refundInfo = null;
  if (newStatus === "Rejected" && prevStatus !== "rejected") {
    refundInfo = refundWalletOrderOnReject_(sh, row, "অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল থেকে Reject" + (data.note ? " — " + data.note : ""));
  } else if (newStatus === "Rejected") {
    // already Rejected earlier (e.g. by a version without refund) -> still make sure refund exists
    refundInfo = refundWalletOrderOnReject_(sh, row, "অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল থেকে Reject" + (data.note ? " — " + data.note : ""));
  }
  // skip_forward: after Partial re-order we already placed the provider order
  if (newStatus === "Processing" && !(data.skip_forward === true || data.skip_forward === "true" || data.skip_forward === 1)) {
    if (isTopupRow_(sh, row)) {
      applyTopupCredit_(sh, row);
    } else {
      var fwdAdm = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row);
      notifyForwardedOk_(sh, row, fwdAdm, "✅ অ্যাডমিন অ্যাপ্রুভ — প্রভাইডারে গেছে");
    }
  }
  var out = { ok: true, order_id: data.order_id, status: newStatus };
  if (refundInfo && refundInfo.ok && !refundInfo.skipped) { out.refunded = refundInfo.delta; out.balance = refundInfo.balance; }
  if (refundInfo && !refundInfo.ok) { out.refund_error = refundInfo.error || "refund_failed"; }
  return jsonOut_(out);
}

/** Deletes an order row entirely — but only once it's already Rejected.
 *  This is enforced here on the server, not just hidden/disabled in the
 *  admin.html UI, since the UI check alone could be bypassed by anyone who
 *  can see the network request. Rejecting first is the safety pause before
 *  an irreversible delete. */
function handleAdminDelete_(sh, data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  }
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, data.order_id);
  if (row === -1) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_not_found" });
  }
  var currentStatus = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (currentStatus !== "rejected") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_rejected", message: "Order must be Rejected before it can be deleted." });
  }
  // Safety: never delete a wallet-paid order whose money was not returned yet
  var delRef = refundWalletOrderOnReject_(sh, row, "ডিলিটের আগে বাকি রিফান্ড");
  if (delRef && delRef.ok === false) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "refund_failed", message: "রিফান্ড দেওয়া যায়নি, তাই ডিলিট বন্ধ: " + (delRef.error || "") });
  }
  sh.deleteRow(row);
  // Row numbers below this point shifted and the order index cache's
  // txn/orderId maps no longer line up with real row positions — safest is
  // to drop it and let the next request rebuild a fresh, correct one.
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(ORDER_INDEX_CACHE_KEY); } catch (e) { /* best-effort */ }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, order_id: data.order_id, deleted: true });
}

/** Basic anti-spam: counts this mobile number's Pending (i.e. still
 *  unverified) orders placed in the last `hours` hours. Used to stop one
 *  number from flooding the sheet with many unverified orders — it does NOT
 *  try to detect fake TrxIDs (that's not possible without a merchant API),
 *  it just limits the volume of noise a single number can generate. */
function countRecentPendingByMobile_(sh, mobile, hours) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  if (!m) return 0;
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return 0;
  var cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - hours * 3600 * 1000);
  var values = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues();
  var count = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var row = values[i];
    var rowMobile = normalizeMobile_(row[9]);  // column J (0-based index 9) = whatsapp/mobile
    var rowStatus = String(row[10] || "").trim().toLowerCase(); // column K = status
    var rowCreated = row[11]; // column L = created_at
    if (rowMobile !== m || rowStatus !== "pending") continue;
    var createdDate = rowCreated instanceof Date ? rowCreated : new Date(rowCreated);
    if (!isNaN(createdDate.getTime()) && createdDate >= cutoff) count++;
  }
  return count;
}

/** Flags (does not block) a new TrxID that closely resembles a TrxID already
 *  on file — e.g. someone tweaking the last character of a real/previous
 *  TrxID to get a "new" one past the duplicate check. This can't tell real
 *  from fake, it just gives the admin a visible reason to double-check
 *  against the actual bKash/Nagad transaction history before approving. */
function findSimilarTxnNote_(sh, txn) {
  var t = normalizeTxn_(txn);
  if (!t || t.length < 6) return "";
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return "";
  var scanRows = Math.min(lastRow - 1, 300); // recent orders only, plenty for this heuristic
  var startRow = lastRow - scanRows + 1;
  var values = sh.getRange(startRow, COL_TXN, scanRows, 1).getValues();
  var prefixLen = Math.max(6, t.length - 2);
  var prefix = t.substring(0, prefixLen);
  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var other = normalizeTxn_(values[i][0]);
    if (!other || other === t) continue;
    if (other.length >= prefixLen && other.substring(0, prefixLen) === prefix) {
      return "⚠️ TrxID similar to an existing one (" + other + ") — verify against real bKash/Nagad history before approving.";
    }
  }
  return "";
}

// ==========================================================
// SUPPORT TICKETS (Tickets page on the site + admin.html "Tickets" tab)
// Stored in a separate "Tickets" sheet tab (auto-created on first use).
// Columns: ticket_id | created_at | category | subcategory | order_id |
//          mobile | message | attachments | status | admin_reply |
//          replied_at | updated_at
// Public endpoints NEVER return mobile numbers or attachment links.
// ==========================================================
var TICKET_SHEET_NAME = "Tickets";
var TICKET_HEADERS = [
  "ticket_id", "created_at", "category", "subcategory", "order_id",
  "mobile", "message", "attachments", "status", "admin_reply",
  "replied_at", "updated_at"
];
var TICKET_CATEGORIES = { "Orders": 1, "Payments": 1, "Other": 1, "Services": 1 };
var TICKET_SUBCATEGORIES = { "Refill": 1, "Partial / Cancel": 1, "Speed up": 1, "Other": 1 };
var TICKET_STATUSES = { "Open": 1, "In Progress": 1, "Resolved": 1, "Closed": 1 };
var TICKET_MAX_PER_HOUR = 5;      // per mobile number
var TICKET_MAX_FILES = 3;
var TICKET_MAX_MESSAGE = 1500;
var TICKET_FOLDER_NAME = "PrimeBoost Ticket Files";

function ticketSheet_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(TICKET_SHEET_NAME);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(TICKET_SHEET_NAME);
  }
  if (sh.getLastRow() === 0) {
    sh.appendRow(TICKET_HEADERS);
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}

function nowDhaka_() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
}

/** Stops spreadsheet formula injection (=, +, -, @ at the start of a cell). */
function safeCell_(v) {
  var s = String(v == null ? "" : v);
  return /^[=+\-@]/.test(s) ? "'" + s : s;
}

function ticketFolder_() {
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(TICKET_FOLDER_NAME);
  return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(TICKET_FOLDER_NAME);
}

/** Saves one base64 image into the (private) ticket folder. Returns a
 *  Drive link only the script owner can open, or "" on failure. */
function saveTicketFile_(ticketId, idx, base64, mime) {
  try {
    var clean = String(base64 || "").replace(/\s/g, "");
    var comma = clean.indexOf(",");
    if (comma !== -1) clean = clean.substring(comma + 1);
    if (clean.length < 50 || clean.length > 1.6 * 1024 * 1024) return "";
    var isPng = String(mime || "").indexOf("png") !== -1;
    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(
      Utilities.base64Decode(clean),
      isPng ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg",
      ticketId + "_" + (idx + 1) + (isPng ? ".png" : ".jpg")
    );
    return ticketFolder_().createFile(blob).getUrl();
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("saveTicketFile_ error: " + err);
    return "";
  }
}

/** Sheets can drop the leading 0 of a number-looking cell — restore it. */
function ticketMobile_(v) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(v);
  return (m.length === 10 && m.charAt(0) === "1") ? "0" + m : m;
}

function countRecentTicketsByMobile_(sh, mobile, hours) {
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return 0;
  var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 6).getValues(); // A..F
  var cutoff = Date.now() - hours * 3600 * 1000;
  var n = 0;
  for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var created = new Date(String(vals[i][1]).replace(" ", "T") + "+06:00").getTime();
    if (!isNaN(created) && created < cutoff) break; // rows are chronological
    if (ticketMobile_(vals[i][5]) === mobile) n++;
  }
  return n;
}

function findTicketRow_(sh, ticketId) {
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return -1;
  var ids = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (String(ids[i][0]) === ticketId) return i + 2;
  }
  return -1;
}

function handleTicketCreate_(data) {
  var ticketId = String(data.ticket_id || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^TK-[A-Z0-9]{8}$/.test(ticketId)) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_ticket_id", message: "Invalid ticket ID." });
  }
  var category = String(data.category || "").trim();
  if (!TICKET_CATEGORIES[category]) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_category", message: "Invalid category." });
  }
  var sub = "";
  if (category === "Orders") {
    sub = String(data.subcategory || "").trim();
    if (!TICKET_SUBCATEGORIES[sub]) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_subcategory", message: "Invalid subcategory." });
    }
  }
  var orderId = String(data.order_id || "").trim().toUpperCase().substring(0, 30);
  if (category === "Orders" && !orderId) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "order_id_required", message: "Order ID is required." });
  }
  var mobile = normalizeMobile_(data.mobile);
  if (category === "Orders") orderId = String(resolveOrderIdForMobile_(mobile, orderId)).toUpperCase().substring(0, 30);   // কাস্টমারের দেখা প্রোভাইডার আইডি → ভেতরের আইডি
  if (!isValidMobile_(mobile)) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_mobile", message: "Invalid mobile number." });
  }
  var message = String(data.message || "").trim();
  if (message.length < 5) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "message_required", message: "Message is too short." });
  }
  message = message.substring(0, TICKET_MAX_MESSAGE);

  var sh = ticketSheet_();

  // Same ticket_id sent twice (retry after a flaky network) → treat as success.
  if (findTicketRow_(sh, ticketId) !== -1) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, ticket_id: ticketId, duplicate: true });
  }
  if (countRecentTicketsByMobile_(sh, mobile, 1) >= TICKET_MAX_PER_HOUR) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "too_many", message: "Too many tickets. Please wait a while." });
  }

  var links = [];
  var files = (data.files && data.files.length) ? data.files : [];
  for (var i = 0; i < files.length && i < TICKET_MAX_FILES; i++) {
    var url = saveTicketFile_(ticketId, i, files[i].base64, files[i].mime);
    if (url) links.push(url);
  }

  var now = nowDhaka_();
  sh.appendRow([
    ticketId, now, category, sub, safeCell_(orderId), "'" + mobile,
    safeCell_(message), links.join("\n"), "Open", "", "", now
  ]);

  try {
    MailApp.sendEmail({
      to: OWNER_EMAIL,
      subject: "🎫 New ticket " + ticketId + " — " + category + (sub ? " / " + sub : ""),
      body: "Ticket: " + ticketId + "\nCategory: " + category + (sub ? " / " + sub : "") +
        "\nOrder ID: " + (orderId || "-") + "\nMobile: " + mobile +
        "\nFiles: " + links.length + "\n\n" + message +
        "\n\nReply from admin.html → Tickets tab."
    });
  } catch (mailErr) {
    Logger.log("ticket mail failed: " + mailErr);
  }
  try {
    notifyOwnerTelegramTicket_(ticketId, category, sub, orderId, mobile, message);
  } catch (eTg2) {
    Logger.log("tg ticket: " + eTg2);
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, ticket_id: ticketId });
}

/** Public: returns only the caller's own tickets (they must know the random
 *  ticket IDs). No mobile number, no attachment links. */
function handleTicketList_(idsCsv) {
  var want = {};
  String(idsCsv || "").split(",").slice(0, 30).forEach(function (s) {
    s = String(s).trim().toUpperCase();
    if (/^TK-[A-Z0-9]{8}$/.test(s)) want[s] = true;
  });
  var out = [];
  var sh = ticketSheet_();
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, TICKET_HEADERS.length).getValues();
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var id = String(vals[i][0]);
      if (!want[id]) continue;
      out.push({
        ticket_id: id,
        created_at: String(vals[i][1]),
        category: vals[i][2],
        subcategory: vals[i][3],
        order_id: vals[i][4],
        message: vals[i][6],
        status: vals[i][8] || "Open",
        admin_reply: vals[i][9] || "",
        replied_at: String(vals[i][10] || "")
      });
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, tickets: out });
}

function handleAdminTicketList_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var filter = String(data.status || "Open").trim();
  var sh = ticketSheet_();
  var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  var out = [];
  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, TICKET_HEADERS.length).getValues();
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var st = String(vals[i][8] || "Open");
      if (filter !== "All" && st.toLowerCase() !== filter.toLowerCase()) continue;
      var o = {};
      for (var j = 0; j < TICKET_HEADERS.length; j++) o[TICKET_HEADERS[j]] = String(vals[i][j]);
      o.mobile = ticketMobile_(o.mobile);
      o.attachments = o.attachments ? o.attachments.split("\n") : [];
      out.push(o);
      if (out.length >= 200) break;
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, tickets: out });
}

/** অ্যাডমিন: কাস্টমারের Refill/Cancel লগ (OrderActions শিট) — শুধু পড়া, সর্বশেষ ২০০০ সারি স্ক্যান, সর্বোচ্চ ২০০ ফেরত।
 *  ফিল্টার: type (refill|cancel|cancel_request|All), result (ok|fail|All), q (অর্ডার আইডি বা মোবাইলের অংশ)। */
function handleAdminOrderActions_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var fType = String(data.type || "All").trim().toLowerCase();
  var fRes = String(data.result || "All").trim().toLowerCase();
  var q = String(data.q || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var out = [], counts = {}, scanned = 0;
  var sh = refillLogSheet_();
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last >= 2) {
    var from = Math.max(2, last - 1999);
    var vals = sh.getRange(from, 1, last - from + 1, 7).getValues();
    scanned = vals.length;
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var r = {
        time_ms: Number(vals[i][0]) || 0, created_at: String(vals[i][1]), order_id: String(vals[i][2]),
        mobile: String(vals[i][3]), type: String(vals[i][4]), result: String(vals[i][5]), detail: String(vals[i][6])
      };
      var k = r.type + "|" + r.result;
      counts[k] = (counts[k] || 0) + 1;
      if (out.length >= 200) continue;
      if (fType !== "all" && r.type.toLowerCase() !== fType) continue;
      if (fRes !== "all" && r.result.toLowerCase() !== fRes) continue;
      if (q && (r.order_id + " " + r.mobile).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) === -1) continue;
      out.push(r);
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, actions: out, counts: counts, scanned: scanned });
}

function handleAdminTicketReply_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var sh = ticketSheet_();
  var row = findTicketRow_(sh, String(data.ticket_id || "").trim().toUpperCase());
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "ticket_not_found" });
  var status = String(data.status || "").trim();
  if (status && !TICKET_STATUSES[status]) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_status" });
  var now = nowDhaka_();
  var reply = String(data.reply || "").trim().substring(0, 1500);
  if (reply) {
    sh.getRange(row, 10).setValue(safeCell_(reply)); // admin_reply
    sh.getRange(row, 11).setValue(now);              // replied_at
  }
  if (status) sh.getRange(row, 9).setValue(status);  // status
  sh.getRange(row, 12).setValue(now);                // updated_at
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true });
}


// ---------- Service catalog overrides (hide / speed / custom add) ----------
function getServiceMeta_() {
  var raw = readServiceMetaRaw_();
  var empty = { hidden: [], speed: {}, custom: [], exempt: [], limits: {}, confirmed: [], discounts: {}, prices: {}, cost_snap: {}, backup: {}, catcfg: {}, catadd: [], catmove: {}, catorder: {}, removed: {}, top: {} };
  if (!raw) return empty;
  try {
    var o = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!o || typeof o !== "object") return empty;
    if (!Array.isArray(o.hidden)) o.hidden = [];
    if (!o.speed || typeof o.speed !== "object") o.speed = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(o.custom)) o.custom = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(o.exempt)) o.exempt = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(o.confirmed)) o.confirmed = [];
    if (!o.limits || typeof o.limits !== "object") o.limits = {};
    if (!o.discounts || typeof o.discounts !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.discounts)) o.discounts = {};
    if (!o.prices || typeof o.prices !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.prices)) o.prices = {};
    if (!o.cost_snap || typeof o.cost_snap !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.cost_snap)) o.cost_snap = {};
    if (!o.backup || typeof o.backup !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.backup)) o.backup = {};
    if (!o.catcfg || typeof o.catcfg !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.catcfg)) o.catcfg = {};
    if (!o.removed || typeof o.removed !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.removed)) o.removed = {};
    if (!o.top || typeof o.top !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.top)) o.top = {};
    if (!Array.isArray(o.catadd)) o.catadd = [];
    if (!o.catmove || typeof o.catmove !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.catmove)) o.catmove = {};
    if (!o.catorder || typeof o.catorder !== "object" || Array.isArray(o.catorder)) o.catorder = {};
    return o;
  } catch (e) {
    return empty;
  }
}
// SERVICE_META আগে একটাই Script Property-তে থাকত — Google-এর সীমা প্রতি property ৯ KB। সার্ভিস/ক্যাটাগরি/বাদ-তালিকা
// বাড়লে সেভ ফেল করত (ক্যাটাগরি নাম বদল, কাস্টম কমেন্ট/সার্ভিস যোগ ব্যর্থ)। এখন ছোট ছোট টুকরোয় (SERVICE_META_0..N) ভাগ করে রাখা হয়।
var SM_CHUNK_ = 2800;   // অক্ষর; বাংলা ৩ বাইট ধরলেও ৯ KB-এর নিচে থাকে
function readServiceMetaRaw_() {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var all = p.getProperties();
  var n = Number(all["SERVICE_META_N"] || 0);
  if (n > 0) {
    var s = "";
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var part = all["SERVICE_META_" + i];
      if (part == null) { s = null; break; }
      s += part;
    }
    if (s !== null) return s;
  }
  return all["SERVICE_META"] || null;   // পুরোনো একক-property ফরম্যাট
}
function saveServiceMeta_(meta) {
  var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var json = JSON.stringify(meta), parts = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < json.length; i += SM_CHUNK_) parts.push(json.substring(i, i + SM_CHUNK_));
  var oldN = Number(p.getProperty("SERVICE_META_N") || 0), obj = {};
  parts.forEach(function (c, j) { obj["SERVICE_META_" + j] = c; });
  obj["SERVICE_META_N"] = String(parts.length);
  p.setProperties(obj, false);   // এক কলে — মাঝপথে অর্ধেক সেভ হয় না
  for (var k = parts.length; k < oldN; k++) p.deleteProperty("SERVICE_META_" + k);
  if (p.getProperty("SERVICE_META") !== null) p.deleteProperty("SERVICE_META");
}

/** Services added from the admin panel / SMM search app (stored in SERVICE_META.custom). */
function customServices_() {
  try { return getServiceMeta_().custom || []; } catch (e) { return []; }
}
/** Drop the cached code->provider/price/rate indexes so a just-added/removed custom service works immediately. */
function clearServiceIndexCaches_() {
  try {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().removeAll(["SERVICE_META_IDX", "PROVIDER_INDEX", "SERVICE_RATE_IDX", "LEGACY_IDX", "CAT_IDX", PRICE_CACHE_META_KEY]);
  } catch (e) {}
}

/** { code: provider } of the HIDDEN "legacy" services in packages-full.json (kept only so old orders still resolve).
 *  They are not on the storefront, so the search app may add the same code again (same provider) as a custom service. */
function legacyProviderIndex_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var hit = cache.get("LEGACY_IDX");
  if (hit) { try { return JSON.parse(hit); } catch (e) {} }
  var idx = {};
  try {
    var arr = parsePkgs_(UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true }).getContentText());
    for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) if (arr[i] && arr[i].legacy && arr[i].provider) idx[String(arr[i].code)] = arr[i].provider;
    cache.put("LEGACY_IDX", JSON.stringify(idx), PRICE_CACHE_SECONDS);
  } catch (e) {}
  return idx;
}

/** Public: site merges this after loading packages-full.json */
// ---------- সম্পন্ন অর্ডারের পাবলিক তালিকা (সাইটের "সম্পন্ন অর্ডার" ট্যাব) ----------
// শুধু: সার্ভিস কোড, সার্ভিসের নাম, পরিমাণ, সম্পন্নের সময়, আর (দ্রুত হলে) কত সময়ে হয়েছে।
// অর্ডার আইডি, মোবাইল, লিংক, টাকা — কিছুই বাইরে যায় না।
var PUBLIC_DONE_MAX = 200;       // সর্বোচ্চ কয়টা অর্ডার দেখাবে (সাম্প্রতিক ২০০টা; ২৫০ চাইলে 250 দিন)
var PUBLIC_DONE_SCAN = 2500;     // শিটের শেষ কত সারি স্ক্যান করবে
var PUBLIC_DONE_FAST_MIN = 60;   // এই মিনিটের মধ্যে সম্পন্ন হলেই "কত সময়ে হয়েছে" দেখাবে

function handleDonePublic_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var hit = cache.get("PUBLIC_DONE_V2");
  if (hit) { try { return jsonOut_(JSON.parse(hit)); } catch (e0) {} }
  var out = { ok: true, items: [], fast_min: PUBLIC_DONE_FAST_MIN, total_done: 0 };
  try {
    var sh = sheet_();
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last >= 2) {
      var from = Math.max(2, last - PUBLIC_DONE_SCAN + 1);
      var vals = sh.getRange(from, 1, last - from + 1, 19).getValues();
      var rows = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
        var r = vals[i];
        if (String(r[10] || "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "completed") continue;   // Partial বাদ — শুধু পূর্ণ সম্পন্ন
        var code = String(r[1] || "").trim();
        if (!code || code.toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue;
        var doneMs = parseTimeMs_(r[18]);
        if (!doneMs) continue;
        var startMs = parseTimeMs_(r[17]) || parseTimeMs_(r[11]);
        if (!(startMs && doneMs >= startMs && (doneMs - startMs) <= PUBLIC_DONE_FAST_MIN * 60000)) continue;   // শুধু দ্রুত সম্পন্ন অর্ডার
        rows.push({ t: doneMs, s: startMs, code: code, name: String(r[2] || "").trim(), qty: Number(r[5]) || 0 });
      }
      rows.sort(function (a, b) { return b.t - a.t; });
      rows = rows.slice(0, PUBLIC_DONE_MAX);
      for (var k = 0; k < rows.length; k++) {
        var x = rows[k], item = { code: x.code, name: x.name, qty: x.qty, at: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(x.t), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX") };
        if (x.s && x.t >= x.s && (x.t - x.s) <= PUBLIC_DONE_FAST_MIN * 60000) {
          item.took_sec = Math.round((x.t - x.s) / 1000);
          item.took = formatDurationShortBn_(x.t - x.s);
        }
        out.items.push(item);
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { out.ok = false; out.error = "load_failed"; }
  if (out.ok) { try { cache.put("PUBLIC_DONE_V2", JSON.stringify(out), 120); } catch (e1) {} }
  return jsonOut_(out);
}

function handleServiceMetaPublic_() {
  var meta = getServiceMeta_();
  var dq = getDisqualifiedMap_();
  var hidden = meta.hidden.slice();
  Object.keys(meta.removed || {}).forEach(function (c) { if (hidden.indexOf(c) === -1) hidden.push(c); });
  Object.keys(dq).forEach(function (c) { if (hidden.indexOf(c) === -1) hidden.push(c); });
  return jsonOut_({
    ok: true,
    hidden: hidden,
    speed: meta.speed,
    custom: publicCustom_(meta.custom),   // ধাপ ৯: provider ছাড়া
    speed_stats: getPublicSpeedStats_(),
    discounts: activeDiscounts_(meta),
    prices: meta.prices || {},
    catcfg: meta.catcfg || {},
    catadd: meta.catadd || [],
    catmove: meta.catmove || {},
    catorder: meta.catorder || {},
    top: meta.top || {},
    topup_bonus: (function () { var c = getTopupBonusCfg_(); return c.active && c.tiers.length ? c : { active: false, tiers: [] }; })()
  });
}

function handleAdminServiceMeta_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var meta = getServiceMeta_();
  var sub = String(data.sub || data.op || "").trim();
  if (sub === "audit_add" || sub === "audit_list") return jsonOut_(auditHandle_(sub, data));
  if (sub.indexOf("cat_") === 0) return handleCatMeta_(meta, sub, data);
  if (sub.indexOf("top_") === 0) return handleTopMeta_(meta, sub, data);

  // ধাপ ৯: অ্যাডমিন পেজের জন্য code -> provider (টোকেন-সুরক্ষিত; আগে পাবলিক provider_map.json ছিল)
  if (sub === "provider_map") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, map: loadProviderIndex_() || {} });
  }

  if (sub === "list" || !sub) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, hidden: meta.hidden, speed: meta.speed, custom: meta.custom,
      disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_(), bad_record: getBadRecordMap_(), exempt: meta.exempt, limits: meta.limits,
      speed_stats: getPublicSpeedStats_(), speed_config: getSpeedConfig_(), discounts: meta.discounts, prices: meta.prices, backup: meta.backup,
      catcfg: meta.catcfg, catadd: meta.catadd, catmove: meta.catmove, catorder: meta.catorder, removed: meta.removed, top: meta.top,
      history: getServiceHistoryCompact_() });
  }

  if (sub === "history") {   // বিস্তারিত: একটি কোডের (code) বা সব সার্ভিসের ইতিহাস
    var hAll = getServiceHistory_(), hc = String(data.code || "").trim();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, history: hc ? (hAll[hc] || null) : hAll });
  }

  if (sub === "speed_report") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, report: buildSpeedReport_(), config: getSpeedConfig_(),
      disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_(), exempt: meta.exempt, limits: meta.limits });
  }

  if (sub === "dq_confirm" || sub === "dq_clear") {
    var codeD = String(data.code || "").trim();
    if (!codeD) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    meta.confirmed = meta.confirmed.filter(function (c) { return c !== codeD; });
    meta.exempt = meta.exempt.filter(function (c) { return c !== codeD; });
    if (sub === "dq_confirm") meta.confirmed.push(codeD);   // যাচাই শেষ → বন্ধ
    else meta.exempt.push(codeD);                            // ক্লিয়ার → চালু থাকবে, আর ফ্ল্যাগ হবে না
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_(), bad_record: getBadRecordMap_(), exempt: meta.exempt });
  }

  if (sub === "exempt" || sub === "unexempt") {
    var codeE = String(data.code || "").trim();
    if (!codeE) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    meta.exempt = meta.exempt.filter(function (c) { return c !== codeE; });
    if (sub === "exempt") meta.exempt.push(codeE);
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, exempt: meta.exempt, disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_() });
  }

  if (sub === "set_limit") {
    var codeL = String(data.code || "").trim();
    var hrs = Number(data.hours);
    if (!codeL) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    if (!hrs || hrs <= 0) delete meta.limits[codeL]; else meta.limits[codeL] = hrs;
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, limits: meta.limits, disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_() });
  }

  if (sub === "set_config") {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    if (data.min_samples != null && Number(data.min_samples) >= 1) props.setProperty("SPEED_MIN_SAMPLES", String(Math.floor(Number(data.min_samples))));
    if (data.max_hours != null && Number(data.max_hours) > 0) props.setProperty("SPEED_MAX_HOURS", String(Number(data.max_hours)));
    if (data.max_fail_pct != null && Number(data.max_fail_pct) > 0) props.setProperty("SPEED_MAX_FAIL_PCT", String(Number(data.max_fail_pct)));
    CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("SPEED_STATS_V1");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, config: getSpeedConfig_(), disqualified: getDisqualifiedMap_() });
  }

  if (sub === "hide") {
    var code = String(data.code || "").trim();
    if (!code) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    if (meta.hidden.indexOf(code) === -1) meta.hidden.push(code);
    markServiceRemoved_(meta, code, "hide");
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, hidden: meta.hidden, removed: meta.removed });
  }

  if (sub === "unhide") {
    var codeU = String(data.code || "").trim();
    meta.hidden = meta.hidden.filter(function (c) { return c !== codeU; });
    delete meta.removed[codeU];
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    logServiceEvent_(codeU, "unhide", {}, "admin");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, hidden: meta.hidden, removed: meta.removed });
  }

  if (sub === "list_removed") return jsonOut_({ ok: true, removed: meta.removed });
  if (sub === "mark_removed") {   // SMM সার্চ অ্যাপ থেকে: items = [{code, name, provider, at}]
    (data.items || []).forEach(function (it) {
      var c = String((it && it.code) || "").trim();
      if (!c) return;
      var old = meta.removed[c] || {};
      meta.removed[c] = { name: String(it.name || old.name || "").substring(0, 120), platform: old.platform || "", category: old.category || "",
        provider: String(it.provider || old.provider || ""), at: it.at || old.at || new Date().toISOString(), via: old.via || "search_app" };
      if (!old.at) logServiceEvent_(c, "remove", meta.removed[c], "search_app");
    });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, hidden: meta.hidden, custom: meta.custom, removed: meta.removed });
  }
  if (sub === "unmark_removed") {
    delete meta.removed[String(data.code || "").trim()];
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    logServiceEvent_(String(data.code || "").trim(), "unmark", {}, "admin");
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, hidden: meta.hidden, custom: meta.custom, removed: meta.removed });
  }
  if (sub === "restore_removed") {   // সরানো তালিকা থেকে ফেরানো — আবার সাইটে দেখাবে (কাস্টম হলে আবার add করতে হবে)
    var codeV = String(data.code || "").trim();
    var had = !!meta.removed[codeV];
    delete meta.removed[codeV];
    meta.hidden = meta.hidden.filter(function (c) { return c !== codeV; });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    if (had) logServiceEvent_(codeV, "restore", {}, "admin");
    clearServiceIndexCaches_();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, restored: had, removed: meta.removed });
  }

  if (sub === "set_speed") {
    var codeS = String(data.code || "").trim();
    var speed = String(data.speed || "normal").toLowerCase(); // instant | slow | normal
    if (!codeS) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    if (speed === "normal" || speed === "") {
      delete meta.speed[codeS];
    } else {
      meta.speed[codeS] = speed;
    }
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, speed: meta.speed });
  }

  if (sub === "add") {
    var pkg = data.service || {};
    var c = String(pkg.code || data.code || "").trim();
    if (!c) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_required" });
    var entry = {
      code: c,
      name: String(pkg.name || data.name || ("Service " + c)).trim(),
      platform: String(pkg.platform || data.platform || "other").toLowerCase().trim(),
      category: String(pkg.category || data.category || "Custom").trim(),
      tags: Array.isArray(pkg.tags) ? pkg.tags : (String(data.tags || "").split(",").map(function (t) { return t.trim(); }).filter(Boolean)),
      pricePerK: String(pkg.pricePerK || data.pricePerK || "$0").trim(),
      min: Number(pkg.min || data.min || 10) || 10,
      max: Number(pkg.max || data.max || 10000) || 10000,
      popular: !!(pkg.popular || data.popular),
      provider: String(pkg.provider || data.provider || "").trim()
    };
    if (entry.pricePerK && entry.pricePerK.charAt(0) !== "$" && !isNaN(Number(entry.pricePerK))) {
      entry.pricePerK = "$" + entry.pricePerK;
    }
    if (data.refill || pkg.refill) entry.refill = String(pkg.refill || data.refill);
    // Service IDs repeat across providers: refuse a code already used by a DIFFERENT provider (it would mis-route orders).
    var rmPrev = meta.removed[c];
    if (rmPrev && rmPrev.provider && entry.provider && rmPrev.provider !== entry.provider) rmPrev = null;   // অন্য প্রোভাইডারের একই আইডি = আলাদা সার্ভিস
    if (rmPrev && !data.force) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "previously_removed", removed_info: rmPrev, removed: meta.removed,
        message: "এই সার্ভিস (" + c + ") আগে সাইট থেকে বাদ দেওয়া হয়েছে" + (rmPrev.name ? " — " + rmPrev.name : "") + "। আবার যোগ করতে force দিন।" });
    }
    var prevCustom = null;
    meta.custom.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.code) === c) prevCustom = x; });
    var strictAdd = !!data.strict;   // set by the SMM search app; the admin form keeps its old (override) behaviour
    if (strictAdd && prevCustom && entry.provider && prevCustom.provider && prevCustom.provider !== entry.provider) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_conflict", existing_provider: prevCustom.provider });
    }
    if (strictAdd && !prevCustom) {
      clearServiceIndexCaches_();
      var onSite = loadProviderIndex_()[c];
      if (onSite) {
        var legacyP = legacyProviderIndex_()[c];   // hidden legacy service of the SAME provider = fine to add again
        if (!(legacyP && legacyP === onSite && onSite === entry.provider)) {
          return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: onSite === entry.provider ? "already_on_site" : "code_conflict", existing_provider: onSite });
        }
      }
    }
    meta.custom = meta.custom.filter(function (x) { return String(x.code) !== c; });
    meta.custom.push(entry);
    // unhide if was hidden (and clear the "removed" mark — admin chose to add it again)
    meta.hidden = meta.hidden.filter(function (h) { return h !== c; });
    delete meta.removed[c];
    if (data.speed) meta.speed[c] = String(data.speed).toLowerCase();
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    logServiceEvent_(c, "add", entry, data.strict ? "search_app" : (data.force ? "force" : "admin"));
    clearServiceIndexCaches_();
    var provCfgOk = true;
    try { provCfgOk = !!(entry.provider && getProviderConfig_()[entry.provider]); } catch (e) {}
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, service: entry, custom: meta.custom, provider_configured: provCfgOk });
  }

  // ---------- Discounts (per service code) ----------
  if (sub === "set_discount") {
    var codeDs = String(data.code || "").trim();
    var dsc = cleanDiscount_(data);
    if (!codeDs || !dsc) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_discount" });
    meta.discounts[codeDs] = dsc;
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, discounts: meta.discounts });
  }
  if (sub === "set_discount_bulk") {
    var dscB = cleanDiscount_(data);
    var codesB = Array.isArray(data.codes) ? data.codes.slice(0, 200) : [];
    if (!dscB || !codesB.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_discount" });
    codesB.forEach(function (cb) { meta.discounts[String(cb).trim()] = dscB; });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, discounts: meta.discounts });
  }
  if (sub === "remove_discount") {
    delete meta.discounts[String(data.code || "").trim()];
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, discounts: meta.discounts });
  }

  // ---------- Custom selling price (per service code, BDT per 1K) ----------
  if (sub === "set_price") {
    var codeP = String(data.code || "").trim();
    var priceP = cleanCustomPrice_(data.price);
    if (!codeP || priceP == null) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_price" });
    meta.prices[codeP] = priceP;
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, prices: meta.prices });
  }
  if (sub === "set_price_bulk") {
    var priceB = cleanCustomPrice_(data.price);
    var codesPB = Array.isArray(data.codes) ? data.codes.slice(0, 200) : [];
    if (priceB == null || !codesPB.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_price" });
    codesPB.forEach(function (cp) { meta.prices[String(cp).trim()] = priceB; });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, prices: meta.prices });
  }
  // ধাপ 6: সার্ভিসভিত্তিক আলাদা দাম একসাথে (মার্জিন-রুল)। সার্ভার-পক্ষের সুরক্ষা: খরচ জানা না থাকলে বা
  // ছাড়-পরবর্তী মার্জিন min_margin-এর নিচে হলে ওই এন্ট্রি বাদ (বাকিগুলো বসে)। খরচের ছবি cost_snap-এ রাখা হয়।
  if (sub === "set_price_map") {
    var mapPM = (data.map && typeof data.map === "object" && !Array.isArray(data.map)) ? data.map : null;
    var minM = Number(data.min_margin);
    if (!mapPM || !(minM >= 0 && minM < 90)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_input" });
    var keysPM = Object.keys(mapPM);
    if (!keysPM.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_items" });
    if (keysPM.length > 200) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "too_many", message: "একবারে সর্বোচ্চ ২০০টি।" });
    var rateIdx = loadServiceRateIndex_();
    var applied = [], rejected = [];
    keysPM.forEach(function (k) {
      var code = String(k).trim(), np = cleanCustomPrice_(mapPM[k]);
      if (!code || np == null) { rejected.push({ code: code, reason: "invalid_price" }); return; }
      var usd = Number(rateIdx[code]);
      if (!(usd > 0)) { rejected.push({ code: code, reason: "no_cost" }); return; }
      var cost = usd * USD_TO_BDT_RATE;
      var d = meta.discounts[code], fin = np;
      if (discountActive_(d)) {
        var val = Number(d.value) || 0;
        var off = d.type === "fixed" ? val : np * Math.min(val, 90) / 100;
        fin = np - Math.max(0, Math.min(off, np * 0.9));
      }
      var mg = fin > 0 ? ((fin - cost) / fin) * 100 : -100;
      if (mg < minM) { rejected.push({ code: code, reason: "below_min_margin", margin: Math.round(mg * 10) / 10 }); return; }
      meta.prices[code] = np;
      meta.cost_snap[code] = usd;
      applied.push(code);
    });
    if (applied.length) saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, applied: applied, rejected: rejected });
  }
  // ধাপ 7: ব্যাকআপ-ম্যাপ (সার্ভিস কোড → ব্যাকআপ প্রোভাইডার + তার সার্ভিস-আইডি)। হাতে বসানো; ভুল আইডি ঠেকাতে প্রোভাইডারের তালিকায় আছে কিনা যাচাই।
  if (sub === "backup_info") {
    var cfgBI = getProviderConfig_();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, backup: meta.backup, providers: Object.keys(cfgBI).filter(function (k) { return cfgBI[k] && cfgBI[k].api_key && cfgBI[k].api_url; }) });
  }
  if (sub === "set_backup") {
    var cB = String(data.code || "").trim(), pB = String(data.provider || "").trim(), sB = String(data.service_id || "").trim();
    if (!cB || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}$/.test(sB)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "bad_input" });
    var cfgsB = getProviderConfig_();
    if (!cfgsB[pB] || !cfgsB[pB].api_key || !cfgsB[pB].api_url) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unknown_provider" });
    var primB = loadProviderIndex_()[cB];
    if (!primB) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_primary", message: "এই সার্ভিসের প্রাইমারি প্রোভাইডার জানা নেই।" });
    if (primB === pB) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "same_provider", message: "ব্যাকআপ প্রাইমারির মতো একই প্রোভাইডার হতে পারে না।" });
    var lkB = backupServiceLookup_(cfgsB[pB], sB);
    if (lkB && lkB.notfound) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "service_not_found", message: "এই সার্ভিস-আইডি ওই প্রোভাইডারের তালিকায় নেই।" });
    if (!lkB && !(data.skip_check === true || data.skip_check === "true")) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "check_failed", message: "প্রোভাইডারের তালিকা আনা যায়নি — আইডি যাচাই করা গেল না।" });
    if (data.dry_run === true || data.dry_run === "true") return jsonOut_({ ok: true, dry_run: true, check: lkB || null });   // শুধু যাচাই — সেভ নয়
    meta.backup[cB] = { provider: pB, service_id: sB };
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, check: lkB || null });
  }
  if (sub === "remove_backup") {
    delete meta.backup[String(data.code || "").trim()];
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true });
  }

  if (sub === "remove_price") {
    delete meta.prices[String(data.code || "").trim()];
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, prices: meta.prices });
  }

  // ---------- ব্যাচ অপারেশন (দ্রুত): একটা অনুরোধে অনেক সার্ভিস — একবার লোড, একবার সেভ, একবার লগ ----------
  if (sub === "bulk_remove") {   // items=[{sub:"remove_custom"|"hide", code}], cats=[{sub:"cat_remove"|"cat_reset", key}]
    var bItems = Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items.slice(0, 400) : [];
    var pkgSnap = null;   // packages-full.json মাত্র একবার নামাই (আগে প্রতি সার্ভিসে একবার নামত)
    var bSnap = function (code) {
      var cs = meta.custom || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < cs.length; i++) if (String(cs[i].code) === code) return { name: cs[i].name, platform: cs[i].platform, category: cs[i].category, provider: cs[i].provider || "" };
      if (pkgSnap === null) {
        pkgSnap = {};
        try {
          var arr = parsePkgs_(UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true }).getContentText());
          for (var j = 0; j < arr.length; j++) if (arr[j] && arr[j].code != null) pkgSnap[String(arr[j].code)] = { name: arr[j].name, platform: arr[j].platform, category: arr[j].category, provider: arr[j].provider || "" };
        } catch (eP) {}
      }
      return pkgSnap[code] || { name: "", platform: "", category: "", provider: "" };
};
    var bLog = [], bDone = 0, bNow = nowIsoDhaka_();
    bItems.forEach(function (it) {
      var cd = String((it && it.code) || "").trim(); if (!cd) return;
      var kind = (it.sub === "hide") ? "hide" : "remove_custom";
      var old = meta.removed[cd], snap = (old && old.name) ? old : bSnap(cd);
      if (kind === "hide" && meta.hidden.indexOf(cd) === -1) meta.hidden.push(cd);
      meta.removed[cd] = { name: String(snap.name || "").substring(0, 120), platform: snap.platform || "", category: snap.category || "", provider: snap.provider || "", at: new Date().toISOString(), via: kind };
      if (kind === "remove_custom") meta.custom = meta.custom.filter(function (x) { return String(x.code) !== cd; });
      bLog.push([bNow, cd, "remove", meta.removed[cd].name, meta.removed[cd].provider, kind]);
      bDone++;
    });
    (Array.isArray(data.cats) ? data.cats : []).forEach(function (cc) {
      var key = String((cc && cc.key) || "").trim(); if (!key || key.indexOf("|") < 1) return;
      if (cc.sub === "cat_remove") {
        var isAdded = meta.catadd.some(function (a) { return a.platform + "|" + a.name === key; });
        if (isAdded) {
          meta.catadd = meta.catadd.filter(function (a) { return a.platform + "|" + a.name !== key; });
          delete meta.catcfg[key];
          Object.keys(meta.catmove).forEach(function (cd) { if (meta.catmove[cd] === key) delete meta.catmove[cd]; });
          var pl = key.split("|")[0];
          if (meta.catorder[pl]) meta.catorder[pl] = meta.catorder[pl].filter(function (x) { return x !== key; });
        } else { var c2 = meta.catcfg[key] || {}; c2.hide = true; meta.catcfg[key] = c2; }
      } else if (cc.sub === "cat_reset") { delete meta.catcfg[key]; }
    });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    clearServiceIndexCaches_();
    bulkLogEvents_(bLog);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, removed_n: bDone, hidden: meta.hidden, custom: meta.custom, removed: meta.removed,
      catcfg: meta.catcfg, catadd: meta.catadd, catmove: meta.catmove, catorder: meta.catorder });
  }

  if (sub === "bulk_add") {   // items=[{code,name,platform,category,pricePerK,min,max,provider,tags,refill,force}] — SMM সার্চ অ্যাপের strict মোড
    var aList = Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items.slice(0, 80) : [];
    var aRes = [], aLog = [], aNow = nowIsoDhaka_(), aIdx = null, aLegacy = null;
    aList.forEach(function (pkgA) {
      var c = String((pkgA && pkgA.code) || "").trim();
      if (!c) { aRes.push({ code: "", ok: false, error: "code_required" }); return; }
      var entry = {
        code: c, name: String(pkgA.name || ("Service " + c)).trim(),
        platform: String(pkgA.platform || "other").toLowerCase().trim(), category: String(pkgA.category || "Custom").trim(),
        tags: Array.isArray(pkgA.tags) ? pkgA.tags : [], pricePerK: String(pkgA.pricePerK || "$0").trim(),
        min: Number(pkgA.min || 10) || 10, max: Number(pkgA.max || 10000) || 10000, popular: false, provider: String(pkgA.provider || "").trim()
      };
      if (entry.pricePerK && entry.pricePerK.charAt(0) !== "$" && !isNaN(Number(entry.pricePerK))) entry.pricePerK = "$" + entry.pricePerK;
      if (pkgA.refill) entry.refill = String(pkgA.refill);
      var rmPrev = meta.removed[c];
      if (rmPrev && rmPrev.provider && entry.provider && rmPrev.provider !== entry.provider) rmPrev = null;
      if (rmPrev && !pkgA.force) { aRes.push({ code: c, ok: false, error: "previously_removed", removed_info: rmPrev }); return; }
      var prevCustom = null;
      meta.custom.forEach(function (x) { if (String(x.code) === c) prevCustom = x; });
      if (prevCustom && entry.provider && prevCustom.provider && prevCustom.provider !== entry.provider) { aRes.push({ code: c, ok: false, error: "code_conflict", existing_provider: prevCustom.provider }); return; }
      if (!prevCustom) {
        if (aIdx === null) { clearServiceIndexCaches_(); aIdx = loadProviderIndex_() || {}; aLegacy = legacyProviderIndex_() || {}; }   // একবারই
        var onSite = aIdx[c];
        if (onSite) {
          var legacyP = aLegacy[c];
          if (!(legacyP && legacyP === onSite && onSite === entry.provider)) { aRes.push({ code: c, ok: false, error: onSite === entry.provider ? "already_on_site" : "code_conflict", existing_provider: onSite }); return; }
        }
      }
      meta.custom = meta.custom.filter(function (x) { return String(x.code) !== c; });
      meta.custom.push(entry);
      meta.hidden = meta.hidden.filter(function (h) { return h !== c; });
      delete meta.removed[c];
      aLog.push([aNow, c, "add", String(entry.name).substring(0, 120), String(entry.provider || ""), "search_app"]);
      aRes.push({ code: c, ok: true });
    });
    if (aLog.length) { saveServiceMeta_(meta); clearServiceIndexCaches_(); bulkLogEvents_(aLog); }
    var aProvOk = true;
    try { var pc = getProviderConfig_(); aProvOk = aList.every(function (x) { return !x.provider || !!pc[String(x.provider).trim()]; }); } catch (eC) {}
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, results: aRes, custom: meta.custom, hidden: meta.hidden, removed: meta.removed, provider_configured: aProvOk });
  }

  if (sub === "remove_custom") {
    var codeR = String(data.code || "").trim();
    markServiceRemoved_(meta, codeR, "remove_custom");
    meta.custom = meta.custom.filter(function (x) { return String(x.code) !== codeR; });
    saveServiceMeta_(meta);
    clearServiceIndexCaches_();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, custom: meta.custom, removed: meta.removed });
  }

  return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unknown_sub" });
}







// ============================================================
// Telegram: প্রোভাইডারের সার্ভিস সার্চ করে সরাসরি সাইটে যোগ / রিমুভ
//   /addsvc facebook followers non drop     → সব প্রোভাইডারে খোঁজে (লাইভ API)
//   /addsvc @smmrx instagram likes          → শুধু একটা প্রোভাইডারে (দ্রুত)
//   /addsvc 7469                            → সার্ভিস আইডি ধরে
// ============================================================
var SVC_USD_BDT_ = 131;
function svcSellBdt_(rateUsd) {   // সাইটের ডিফল্ট markup (admin.html এর সাথে একই)
  var base = (Number(rateUsd) || 0) * SVC_USD_BDT_, m = 7;
  if (base > 200) m = 30; else if (base > 100) m = 25; else if (base > 70) m = 21; else if (base > 40) m = 16; else if (base > 20) m = 12;
  return base + m;
}
function svcPlatGuess_(name, cat) {
  var t = (String(name) + " " + String(cat)).toLowerCase();
  if (/facebook|\bfb\b/.test(t)) return "facebook";
  if (/instagram|\big\b|\binsta\b/.test(t)) return "instagram";
  if (/tiktok|tik tok/.test(t)) return "tiktok";
  if (/telegram/.test(t)) return "telegram";
  if (/youtube|\byt\b/.test(t)) return "youtube";
  if (/twitter|\bx\b|\bx\s*\//.test(t)) return "twitter";
  return "other";
}
var SVC_TYPES_ = [[/live/, "live"], [/monetiz/, "monetiz"], [/spam|report/, "spam"], [/story/, "stor"], [/retweet|share/, "share"], [/comment/, "comment"],
  [/react/, "react"], [/member|subscri/, "member"], [/follower/, "follower"], [/view|reel|watch/, "view"], [/like/, "like"], [/engage/, "engage"]];
/** নাম দেখে সাইটের বিদ্যমান ক্যাটাগরির মধ্যে সবচেয়ে মানানসই একটা বেছে নেয় (orig নাম ফেরত দেয়)। */
function svcCatGuess_(plat, name) {
  var list = catEffective_(plat), t = String(name).toLowerCase(), type = "";
  for (var i = 0; i < SVC_TYPES_.length; i++) if (SVC_TYPES_[i][0].test(t)) { type = SVC_TYPES_[i][1]; break; }
  var best = null, bs = -1;
  list.forEach(function (c) {
    var cl = c.orig.toLowerCase(), sc = 0;
    if (type && cl.indexOf(type) >= 0) sc += 4;
    if (/non.?drop|low.?drop/.test(t) && /low drop|non drop/.test(cl)) sc += 2;
    if (/cheap/.test(t) && /cheap/.test(cl)) sc += 1;
    if (/fast|instant/.test(t) && /fast/.test(cl)) sc += 1;
    if (/bangladesh|\bbd\b/.test(t) && /bangladesh|\bbd\b/.test(cl)) sc += 2;
    if (/^other/.test(cl)) sc += 0.1;
    if (sc > bs) { bs = sc; best = c; }
  });
  return best ? best.orig : "Custom";
}

/** সব (বা একটা) কনফিগার করা প্রোভাইডারে "services" API ডেকে ফিল্টার করে। ৫টা করে ব্যাচে — মেমোরি বাঁচাতে। */
function svcSearch_(query) {
  var cfgAll = getProviderConfig_(), names = Object.keys(cfgAll).filter(function (n) { return cfgAll[n] && cfgAll[n].api_url && cfgAll[n].api_key; });
  var toks = String(query || "").toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(String), provF = [], words = [];
  toks.forEach(function (x) { if (x.charAt(0) === "@" && x.length > 1) provF.push(x.substring(1)); else words.push(x); });
  if (provF.length) names = names.filter(function (n) { return provF.some(function (f) { return n.toLowerCase().indexOf(f) >= 0; }); });
  var idMode = words.length === 1 && /^\d+$/.test(words[0]);
  var out = [], failed = [], PER = 40;
  for (var b = 0; b < names.length; b += 5) {
    var batch = names.slice(b, b + 5), reqs = batch.map(function (n) {
      return { url: cfgAll[n].api_url, method: "post", payload: { key: cfgAll[n].api_key, action: "services" }, muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true };
    });
    var resps;
    try { resps = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(reqs); } catch (e) { batch.forEach(function (n) { failed.push(n); }); continue; }
    for (var i = 0; i < resps.length; i++) {
      var arr = null;
      try { arr = JSON.parse(resps[i].getContentText()); } catch (e) {}
      if (!Array.isArray(arr)) { failed.push(batch[i]); continue; }
      var got = 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < arr.length && got < PER; j++) {
        var x = arr[j];
        if (!x || x.service == null) continue;
        var id = String(x.service), nm = String(x.name || ""), cat = String(x.category || "");
        if (idMode) { if (id !== words[0]) continue; }
        else {
          var hay = (nm + " " + cat + " " + svcPlatGuess_(nm, cat)).toLowerCase(), ok = true;
          for (var k = 0; k < words.length; k++) if (hay.indexOf(words[k]) < 0) { ok = false; break; }
          if (!ok || !words.length) continue;
        }
        var rf = x.refill === true || /^(true|yes|1)$/i.test(String(x.refill));
        out.push({ p: batch[i], id: id, n: nm.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().substring(0, 110), r: Number(x.rate) || 0, mn: Number(x.min) || 0, mx: Number(x.max) || 0,
          rf: rf, c: cat.substring(0, 40), t: String(x.type || "Default") });
        got++;
      }
      resps[i] = null;
    }
  }
  out.sort(function (a, b) { return a.r - b.r; });
  return { items: out.slice(0, 60), failed: failed, searched: names.length, total: out.length };
}

function svcStatus_(r, meta, provIdx) {
  var rm = meta.removed[r.id];
  if (rm && (!rm.provider || rm.provider === r.p)) return "removed";
  var cust = null;
  (meta.custom || []).forEach(function (c) { if (String(c.code) === r.id) cust = c; });
  if (cust) return (cust.provider && cust.provider !== r.p) ? "conflict" : "custom";
  var sp = provIdx[r.id];
  if (sp) return sp === r.p ? "static" : "conflict";
  return "off";
}
var SVC_ST_ICON_ = { off: "➕", custom: "✅", static: "✅", removed: "🚫", conflict: "⚠️" };

function svcGet_(chat, key) { var v = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(key + chat); if (!v) return null; try { return JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { return null; } }
function svcPut_(chat, key, val) { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(key + chat, JSON.stringify(val), 3600); }

function tgaSvcPrompt_(ctx) {
  tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "svq" });
