      created_at: vals[i][7]
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function listUsers_(limit) {
  var sh = usersSheet_();
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  limit = Math.min(Number(limit) || 50, 200);
  if (last < 2) return [];
  var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 5).getValues();
  var out = [];
  for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0 && out.length < limit; i--) {
    var mob = normalizeMobile_(vals[i][0]);
    if (!mob) continue;
    out.push({
      mobile: mob,
      balance: Number(vals[i][1]) || 0,
      note: String(vals[i][2] || ""),
      created_at: vals[i][3],
      updated_at: vals[i][4]
    });
  }
  return out;
}


/** Legacy (v1) PIN hash: one static salt for everyone. Kept ONLY to verify old PINs and upgrade them on login. */
function hashPinLegacy_(pin) {
  var s = String(pin || "");
  var raw = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, s + "|primeboost-wallet-v1");
  return raw.map(function (b) {
    var v = (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16);
    return v.length === 1 ? "0" + v : v;
  }).join("");
}

/** v2 PIN hash: HMAC-SHA256 keyed with a secret pepper (Script Properties, never in the sheet),
 *  salted per user by mobile number. Output looks like "v2$<hex>". */
function hashPin_(pin, mobile) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  if (!m) return hashPinLegacy_(pin); // callers must always pass the mobile; legacy only as a safe fallback
  var raw = Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature("pin|" + m + "|" + String(pin || ""), getOrCreateSecret_("PIN_PEPPER", 48));
  return "v2$" + raw.map(function (b) {
    var v = (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16);
    return v.length === 1 ? "0" + v : v;
  }).join("");
}

/** Constant-time string compare. */
function safeEqual_(a, b) {
  a = String(a || ""); b = String(b || "");
  var diff = a.length ^ b.length;
  var n = Math.max(a.length, b.length);
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) diff |= (a.charCodeAt(i) || 0) ^ (b.charCodeAt(i) || 0);
  return diff === 0;
}

/** true if `pin` matches the stored hash (v2 or legacy v1). */
function pinMatches_(stored, pin, mobile) {
  stored = String(stored || "");
  if (!stored) return false;
  if (stored.indexOf("v2$") === 0) return safeEqual_(stored, hashPin_(pin, mobile));
  return safeEqual_(stored, hashPinLegacy_(pin));
}

/** Session-signing secret. Separate from ADMIN_TOKEN and has NO hard-coded fallback:
 *  if the secret cannot be read, signing fails closed instead of using a guessable string. */
function sessionSecret_() {
  return getOrCreateSecret_("SESSION_SECRET", 48);
}

function hmacHex_(msg) {
  var raw = Utilities.computeHmacSha256Signature(String(msg), sessionSecret_());
  return raw.map(function (b) {
    var v = (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16);
    return v.length === 1 ? "0" + v : v;
  }).join("");
}

function makeSessionToken_(mobile) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  var exp = Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days
  var payload = m + "." + exp;
  return payload + "." + hmacHex_(payload);
}

function parseSessionToken_(token) {
  var parts = String(token || "").split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
  var mobile = parts[0];
  var exp = Number(parts[1]);
  var sig = parts[2];
  if (!mobile || !exp || !sig) return null;
  if (Date.now() > exp) return null;
  if (!safeEqual_(hmacHex_(mobile + "." + exp), sig)) return null;
  return { mobile: normalizeMobile_(mobile), exp: exp };
}

function isValidPin_(pin) {   // login / old password: lenient (old 4–6 digit PINs must still work)
  var s = String(pin || "");
  return s.length >= 4 && s.length <= 32;
}
function isValidNewPassword_(pw) {   // new password (register / reset / change): 6–32 characters
  var s = String(pw || "");
  return s.length >= 6 && s.length <= 32;
}
/** Account ID check for NEW accounts: a User name (3–30 chars: a-z 0-9 _ - or Bengali) or a full 11-digit mobile number. */
function isValidAcctId_(key) {
  key = String(key || "");
  if (/^[0-9]+$/.test(key)) return /^01[0-9]{9}$/.test(key);
  return /^[a-z0-9_\-\u0980-\u09FF]{3,30}$/.test(key);
}
function reqAcct_(data) { return normalizeMobile_(data.username || data.mobile); }



// ---------- Order card (Telegram / WhatsApp) + top-customer badge ----------
var BADGE_MIN_ORDERS = 5;   // এর কম অর্ডারে ব্যাজ পাওয়া যাবে না
var BADGE_TOP_N = 3;        // সবচেয়ে বেশি অর্ডারকারী টপ ৩ জন
var BADGE_DEFS = [
  { icon: "👑", bn: "টপ কাস্টমার #১", en: "Top Customer #1" },
  { icon: "🥈", bn: "টপ কাস্টমার #২", en: "Top Customer #2" },
  { icon: "🥉", bn: "টপ কাস্টমার #৩", en: "Top Customer #3" }
];

/** Top customers by number of real orders (no TOPUP, no Rejected). Cached 5 min. */
function getTopCustomers_() {
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  try {
    var hit = cache.get("top_customers_v1");
    if (hit) return JSON.parse(hit);
  } catch (e0) {}
  var list = [];
  try {
    var sh = sheet_();
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last >= 2) {
      var vals = sh.getRange(2, 2, last - 1, 10).getValues(); // B..K
      var counts = {};
      for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
        if (String(vals[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue;
        if (String(vals[i][9] || "").toLowerCase() === "rejected") continue;
        var m = normalizeMobile_(vals[i][8]);
        if (!m) continue;
        if (m.length === 10) m = "0" + m;
        counts[m] = (counts[m] || 0) + 1;
      }
      var arr = Object.keys(counts).map(function (k) { return { mobile: k, orders: counts[k] }; });
      arr.sort(function (a, b) { return b.orders - a.orders || (a.mobile < b.mobile ? -1 : 1); });
      for (var r = 0; r < arr.length && list.length < BADGE_TOP_N; r++) {
        if (arr[r].orders < BADGE_MIN_ORDERS) break;
        list.push({ mobile: arr[r].mobile, orders: arr[r].orders, rank: list.length + 1 });
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try { cache.put("top_customers_v1", JSON.stringify(list), 300); } catch (e2) {}
  return list;
}

/** Honorary badge for a customer, or null. */
function getCustomerBadge_(mobile) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  if (m.length === 10) m = "0" + m;
  var top = getTopCustomers_();
  for (var i = 0; i < top.length; i++) {
    if (top[i].mobile === m) {
      var d = BADGE_DEFS[top[i].rank - 1] || BADGE_DEFS[0];
      return { rank: top[i].rank, icon: d.icon, label_bn: d.bn, label_en: d.en, orders: top[i].orders };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/** 01710965555 -> 0171096**** (shown to admin + shared on WhatsApp) */
function maskMobileCard_(m) {
  var d = normalizeMobile_(m);
  if (d.length === 10) d = "0" + d;
  if (d.length < 8) return d.replace(/./g, "*");
  return d.substring(0, 7) + d.substring(7).replace(/./g, "*");
}

function bnDigits_(n) {
  return String(n).replace(/[0-9]/g, function (c) { return "০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯".charAt(Number(c)); });
}

function cardPlatformName_(p) {
  p = String(p || "").trim();
  if (!p) return "";
  var low = p.toLowerCase();
  if (low === "twitter" || low === "x") return "X";
  if (low === "tiktok") return "TikTok";
  if (low === "youtube") return "YouTube";
  return p.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.slice(1);
}


// ---------- ডুপ্লিকেট অর্ডার সতর্কতা (ধাপ 2.3) ----------
// একই কাস্টমার + একই সার্ভিস + একই লিংকে ১৫ মিনিটের মধ্যে আবার অর্ডার এলে অ্যাডমিনকে টেলিগ্রামে সতর্কতা।
// অর্ডার আটকানো হয় না (সাইট বদলানো ছাড়া কাস্টমারকে "নিশ্চিত করুন" জিজ্ঞেস করা যায় না) — শুধু জানানো।
var DUP_WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

function dupLinkNorm_(l) {
  return String(l || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.|m\.|web\.|mbasic\.)?/, "").replace(/[?#].*$/, "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
}
function warnDuplicateOrder_(sh, orderId, mobile, code, link) {
  try {
    if (String(code || "").toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") return;
    var nl = dupLinkNorm_(link);
    if (!nl) return;
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last < 3) return;
    var start = Math.max(2, last - 149);
    var vals = sh.getRange(start, 1, last - start + 1, 12).getValues();
    var m10 = String(mobile || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10);
    var now = Date.now(), prev = [];
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var r = vals[i];
      if (String(r[0]) === String(orderId)) continue;
      var t = parseTimeMs_(r[11]);
      if (t && now - t > DUP_WINDOW_MS) continue;
      if (String(r[1]) !== String(code)) continue;
      if (String(r[9] || "").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(-10) !== m10) continue;
      if (String(r[10] || "").toLowerCase() === "rejected") continue;
      if (dupLinkNorm_(r[4]) !== nl) continue;
      prev.push(String(r[0]));
      if (prev.length >= 3) break;
    }
    if (!prev.length) return;
    var btn = [[{ text: "🔎 নতুন " + orderId, callback_data: "o:" + orderId }]];
    prev.forEach(function (p) { btn.push([{ text: "🔎 আগের " + p, callback_data: "o:" + p }]); });
    notifyTelegram_("⚠️ সম্ভাব্য ডুপ্লিকেট অর্ডার\n👤 " + maskMobileCard_(mobile) + "\n🏷 কোড: " + code + "\n🔗 " + String(link).substring(0, 100) +
      "\nনতুন: " + orderId + "\nআগের (১৫ মিনিটের মধ্যে): " + prev.join(", ") + "\nদরকার হলে একটা Reject করুন।", btn);
  } catch (e) { Logger.log("dup-warn error: " + e.message); }
}

/** The shared order card text. */
function buildOrderCard_(mobile, serviceName, code, platform, qty, amount, totalOrders, title, shownId) {
  var badge = getCustomerBadge_(mobile);
  var plat = cardPlatformName_(platform);
  var lines = [
    title || "🛒 অর্ডার",
    "👤 " + maskMobileCard_(mobile) + (totalOrders ? " (মোট অর্ডার: " + bnDigits_(totalOrders) + "টি)" : ""),
  ];
  if (badge) lines.push(badge.icon + " " + badge.label_bn);
  lines.push(
    "📦 সার্ভিস: " + (serviceName || code),
    "🏷 কোড: " + code + (plat ? " · " + plat : ""),
    "🔢 পরিমান: " + qty,
    "💰 ৳" + amount
  );
  if (shownId) lines.push("🆔 অর্ডার আইডি: #" + shownId);   // সাইটে কাস্টমার যে আইডি দেখছে
  return lines.join("\n");
}

/** টপ-আপ পেমেন্ট মাধ্যমের সুন্দর নাম */
function topupMethodLabel_(m) {
  var k = String(m || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var map = { bkash: "bKash", nagad: "Nagad", rocket: "Rocket", npsb: "NPSB / ব্যাংক", binance: "Binance" };
  return map[k] || (m ? String(m) : "—");
}

/** গ্রুপে ফরোয়ার্ড করার মতো টপ-আপ কার্ড (ব্যালেন্স/TrxID নেই, নম্বর মাস্ক করা) */
function buildTopupCard_(mobile, amount, bonusAmt, bonusPct, method) {
  var badge = getCustomerBadge_(mobile);
  var lines = [
    "✅ ফান্ড অ্যাড সম্পন্ন",
    "━━━━━━━━━━━━━━",
    "👤 " + maskMobileCard_(mobile)
  ];
  if (badge) lines.push(badge.icon + " " + badge.label_bn);
  lines.push("💳 মাধ্যম: " + topupMethodLabel_(method), "💰 পরিমাণ: ৳" + amount);
  if (bonusAmt) {
    lines.push("🎁 বোনাস: +৳" + bonusAmt + " (" + bonusPct + "%)", "✨ মোট যোগ হয়েছে: ৳" + (Number(amount) + Number(bonusAmt)));
  }
  lines.push("🕒 " + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm a"));
  return lines.join("\n");
}

/** Telegram URL button that opens WhatsApp's share picker with the card text. */
function waShareButton_(text) {
  return { text: "📲 WhatsApp এ ফরোয়ার্ড", url: "https://wa.me/?text=" + encodeURIComponent(text) };
}
/** কার্ডের নিচে "☐ বাছুন" টগল বাটন — একসাথে অনেক কার্ড বেছে /wa দিয়ে WhatsApp-এ পাঠানোর জন্য। */
function waSelBtn_(on) {
  return { text: on ? "☑ বাছা হয়েছে" : "☐ বাছুন", callback_data: "ws:t" };
}
/** কার্ডের কিবোর্ড। state: "sel" = বাছা (অটো-সিলেক্টেড, পাঠানো বাকি) · "off" = বাছা নেই · "sent" = WhatsApp-এ পাঠানো হয়ে গেছে (✔ চিহ্ন)। */
function waKb_(text, oid, state) {
  var b = waSelBtn_(state === "sel");
  if (state === "sent") b.text = "✔ পাঠানো হয়েছে";
  var r2 = [];
  if (oid) r2.push({ text: "🔎 খুলুন", callback_data: "o:" + oid });
  r2.push(b);
  var kb = [[waShareButton_(text)], r2];
  if (state === "sel") kb.push([{ text: "📲 বাছা সবগুলো WhatsApp-এ পাঠান", callback_data: "ws:g" }]);
  return kb;
}
/** নতুন কার্ড (🛒 অর্ডার / ✅ অর্ডার সম্পন্ন / ✅ ফান্ড অ্যাড সম্পন্ন) পাঠায় এবং নিজে থেকে "বাছা" তালিকায় ঢুকিয়ে দেয়।
 *  যেগুলো পাঠানো হয়নি সেগুলো একসাথে /wa বা "বাছা সবগুলো" বাটনে যাবে; পাঠানো হলে কার্ডে ✔ চিহ্ন বসে। tgText: টেলিগ্রামে দেখানোর লেখা (WhatsApp-এ যাবে শুধু card)। */
function notifyCardWa_(card, oid, tgText) {
  var chat = "", sel = [], auto = false;
  try { chat = String(getTelegramChatId_() || ""); sel = chat ? waSelGet_(chat) : []; auto = !!chat && sel.length < WA_SEL_MAX_; } catch (e0) {}
  var res = notifyTelegram_(tgText || card, waKb_(card, oid, auto ? "sel" : "off"));
  try {
    if (auto && res && res.ok && res.message_id) {
      // একসাথে কয়েকটা কার্ড এলে একটা আরেকটার বাছাই যেন মুছে না ফেলে — লক নিয়ে তালিকা আবার পড়ে যোগ করি
      var lk = null;
      try { lk = LockService.getScriptLock(); lk.waitLock(5000); } catch (eL) { lk = null; }
      try { sel = waSelGet_(chat); sel.push({ m: res.message_id, t: card, o: String(oid || "") }); waSelPut_(chat, sel); }
      finally { try { if (lk) lk.releaseLock(); } catch (eR) {} }
    }
  } catch (e1) {}
  return res;
}
/** মেসেজের কিবোর্ড থেকে "o:<অর্ডার আইডি>" বের করে। */
function waOidFromMsg_(msg) {
  try {
    var kb = (msg && msg.reply_markup && msg.reply_markup.inline_keyboard) || [];
    for (var r = 0; r < kb.length; r++) for (var c = 0; c < kb[r].length; c++) {
      var d = kb[r][c].callback_data;
      if (d && d.indexOf("o:") === 0) return d.substring(2);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return "";
}

/** Place SMM order paid from wallet balance — no Trx, auto Processing + forward. */
function handleWalletOrder_(sh, data) {
  var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
  if (!sess) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন করুন (Wallet)" });
  }

  var code = String(data.service_code || "").trim();
  var link = String(data.link || "").trim();
  var qty = Number(data.quantity);
  var comments = String(data.comments || "").trim();
  var serviceName = String(data.service_name || "").trim();
  var platform = String(data.platform || "").trim();

  if (!code) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "missing_service", message: "সার্ভিস কোড নেই" });
  if (!link) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "missing_link", message: "লিংক দিন" });
  if (!qty || qty <= 0) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_quantity", message: "পরিমাণ ঠিক করুন" });

  // Server-side price (ignore client amount for charging)
  var pricing = validateOrderPricing_(code, qty, data.amount);
  // If amount_mismatch, still compute expected from index for wallet
  var amount = 0;
  if (pricing && pricing.ok) {
    amount = Number(pricing.amount);
  } else if (pricing && pricing.error === "amount_mismatch") {
    // Recompute without claimed check
    var priceIndex = loadPriceIndex_();
    var pkg = priceIndex[code];
    if (!pkg) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_service", message: "Unknown service" });
    if (qty < pkg.min || qty > pkg.max) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "quantity_out_of_range", message: "Quantity range error" });
    }
    var unitPrice = getFinalUnitPrice_(code, pkg.price_usd);
    amount = Math.round(((unitPrice * qty) / 1000) * 100) / 100;
  } else {
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: false,
      error: (pricing && pricing.error) || "price_error",
      message: (pricing && pricing.message) || "দাম যাচাই ব্যর্থ"
    });
  }

  if (amount <= 0) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_amount", message: "অবৈধ দাম" });
  }

  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(30000);
  } catch (eLock) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "সার্ভার ব্যস্ত, আবার চেষ্টা করুন" });
  }

  try {
    // Deduct first (atomic with lock)
    var adj = adjustUserBalance_(sess.mobile, -amount, "order", "pending", "Wallet order debit", false);
    if (!adj.ok) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: adj.error || "insufficient_balance",
        balance: adj.balance,
        need: amount,
        message: "ব্যালেন্স কম। দরকার ৳" + amount + (adj.balance != null ? (" · আছে ৳" + adj.balance) : "")
      });
    }

    ensureHeader_(sh);
    var orderId = generateOrderId_(sh);
    var now = nowIsoDhaka_();
    var note = "Paid from wallet ৳" + amount;
    if (comments) note += " | comments stored";

    sh.appendRow([
      orderId,
      code,
      serviceName || code,
      platform,
      link,
      qty,
      amount,
      "WALLET",
      "Wallet",
      sess.mobile,
      "Processing",
      now,
      note,
      "",
      comments,
      ""
    ]);
    updateOrderIndexAfterInsert_(sh, orderId, "WALLET-" + orderId);
    warnDuplicateOrder_(sh, orderId, sess.mobile, code, link);

    var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
    var fwd = { ok: false };
    var refunded = false;
    var finalBalance = adj.balance;
    if (row !== -1) {
      fwd = forwardOrderToProvider_(sh, row) || { ok: false };
      if (fwd.ok) {
        markForwardedAt_(sh, row);
      } else {
        // Forward failed — no provider order id yet
        var poid = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValue() || "").trim();
        if (!poid) {
          var fundsErr = isProviderFundsError_(fwd.error);
          if (fundsErr) {
            // Provider panel balance low → DO NOT refund customer; hold order + alert admin
            var provName = fwd.provider || loadProviderIndex_()[code] || "provider";
            appendNote_(sh, row, "⚠️ PROVIDER_BALANCE_LOW (" + provName + "): " + String(fwd.error || "") + " — hold order, top-up provider, then Retry API.");
            setStatus_(sh, row, "Processing", "Waiting provider balance top-up (" + provName + ")");
            var tp = calcProviderTopup_(provName, code, qty);
            var alUserName = "";
            try { var alUsh = usersSheet_(); var alUrow = findUserRow_(alUsh, sess.mobile); if (alUrow !== -1) alUserName = userNameAt_(alUsh, alUrow); } catch (eAlU) {}
            var alBalNow = Number(finalBalance);
            var alBalBefore = Math.round((alBalNow + Number(amount)) * 100) / 100;
            var alSvcName = cleanName_(serviceName || "").substring(0, 60);
            var alPlatform = cleanName_(platform || "").substring(0, 24);
            var alLink = String(link || "").substring(0, 80);
            pushAdminAlert_({
              type: "provider_topup",
              severity: "high",
              order_id: orderId,
              user_mobile: String(sess.mobile || ""),
              user_name: alUserName,
              user_balance: alBalNow,
              user_balance_before: alBalBefore,
              service_name: alSvcName,
              platform: alPlatform,
              link: alLink,
              provider: provName,
              service_code: code,
              qty: qty,
              amount_bdt: amount,
              rate_usd: tp.rate_usd,
              need_usd: tp.need_usd,
              need_bdt: tp.need_bdt,
              held_count: tp.held_count,
              held_need_usd: tp.held_need_usd,
              balance_usd: tp.balance_usd,
              topup_usd: tp.topup_usd,
              topup_bdt: tp.topup_bdt,
              error: String(fwd.error || "not enough funds"),
              message: "Provider ব্যালেন্স কম — " + provName + " টপ-আপ করুন (≈ $" + tp.topup_usd.toFixed(4) + " / ৳" + tp.topup_bdt + "), তারপর অর্ডার " + orderId + " Retry API"
            });
            try {
              notifyTelegram_(
                "🚨 PROVIDER টপ-আপ লাগবে\n" +
                "Panel: " + provName + "\n" +
                "Error: " + String(fwd.error || "not enough funds") + "\n" +
                "Order: " + orderId + "\n" +
                "👤 User: " + (alUserName ? alUserName + " · " : "") + String(sess.mobile || "") + "\n" +
                "💰 User ব্যালেন্স: ৳" + alBalNow + " (অর্ডারের আগে ৳" + alBalBefore + ")\n" +
                "📦 Service: " + (alSvcName || "—") + " (ID " + code + ")\n" +
                (alPlatform ? ("📱 Platform: " + alPlatform + "\n") : "") +
                "🔢 পরিমাণ: " + qty + "\n" +
                "🔗 Link: " + alLink + "\n" +
                (tp.rate_usd > 0 ? ("Rate: $" + tp.rate_usd + "/1K → এই অর্ডার $" + tp.need_usd.toFixed(4) + " (৳" + tp.need_bdt + ")\n") : "") +
                (tp.held_count > 1 ? ("আটকে আছে " + tp.held_count + "টি অর্ডার, মোট $" + tp.held_need_usd.toFixed(4) + "\n") : "") +
                (tp.balance_usd != null ? ("প্যানেল ব্যালেন্স: $" + tp.balance_usd.toFixed(4) + "\n") : "") +
                (tp.topup_usd > 0 ? ("💳 টপ-আপ করুন: $" + tp.topup_usd.toFixed(4) + " ≈ ৳" + tp.topup_bdt + "\n") : "") +
                "Customer paid ৳" + amount + " (NO refund — hold)\n" +
                "→ Panel টপ-আপ করে Admin → Retry API"
              );
            } catch (eFundTg) {}
          } else {
            // Other forward errors → auto-refund customer
            var ref = adjustUserBalance_(sess.mobile, amount, "refund", orderId, "Auto-refund: provider forward failed " + orderId, false);
            if (ref.ok) {
              refunded = true;
              finalBalance = ref.balance;
              setStatus_(sh, row, "Rejected", "Auto-refunded ৳" + amount + " — provider forward failed: " + String(fwd.error || ""));
            } else {
              setStatus_(sh, row, "Pending", "Forward failed — manual refund needed: " + String(fwd.error || ""));
            }
          }
        } else {
          markForwardedAt_(sh, row);
        }
      }
    }

    try {
      if (fwd.ok) {
        var cardTotal = 0;
        try { cardTotal = getUserOrderStats_(sess.mobile).total_orders; } catch (eCt) {}
        var card = buildOrderCard_(sess.mobile, serviceName, code, platform, qty, amount, cardTotal, undefined, String(fwd.provider_order_id || orderId || "").trim());
        notifyCardWa_(card, orderId);
      } else if (refunded) {
        notifyTelegram_("🛒 Wallet অর্ডার\n" + sess.mobile + "\n৳" + amount + "\n" + orderId + "\n↩️ Auto-refunded");
      }
      // funds-low already notified above
    } catch (eTg) {}

    var holdFunds = !fwd.ok && !refunded && isProviderFundsError_(fwd.error);
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: true,
      order_id: orderId,
      status: refunded ? "Rejected" : "Processing",
      amount: amount,
      balance: finalBalance,
      forward_ok: !!fwd.ok,
      refunded: refunded,
      provider_funds_low: holdFunds,
      provider_order_id: fwd.provider_order_id || "",
      message: refunded
        ? ("প্রভাইডার ফরওয়ার্ড ব্যর্থ — ৳" + amount + " ওয়ালেটে ফেরত দেওয়া হয়েছে")
        : (holdFunds
          ? "অর্ডার গ্রহণ হয়েছে। প্রভাইডার ব্যালেন্স আপডেটের পর শুরু হবে।"
          : "ওয়ালেট থেকে কেটে অর্ডার Processing এ আছে")
    });
  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: String(err) });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}


// ---------- PIN change / forgot-PIN helpers ----------
var PIN_RESET_TTL_SECONDS = 600;      // recovery code valid for 10 minutes
var PIN_RESET_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 60;  // min gap between code requests
var PIN_RESET_MAX_SENDS_PER_HOUR = 5;
var PIN_RESET_MAX_TRIES = 5;          // wrong-code attempts before the code is burned
var PIN_CHANGE_MAX_FAILS = 5;         // wrong old-PIN attempts before a 15 min lock
var PIN_CHANGE_LOCK_SECONDS = 900;
var WALLET_LOGIN_MAX_FAILS = 5;       // wrong PINs at login before a 15 min lock (per mobile)
var WALLET_LOGIN_LOCK_SECONDS = 900;

function cacheGetNum_(key) {
  try { return Number(CacheService.getScriptCache().get(key) || 0); } catch (e) { return 0; }
}
function cachePut_(key, val, ttl) {
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(key, String(val), ttl); } catch (e) { /* best-effort */ }
}
function cacheDel_(key) {
  try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(key); } catch (e) { /* best-effort */ }
}

/** Sends an SMS through a generic HTTP gateway configured in Script Properties:
 *    SMS_GATEWAY_URL  e.g. https://example.com/api?api_key={key}&senderid={sender}&number={to}&message={msg}
 *    SMS_GATEWAY_KEY    (optional, replaces {key})
 *    SMS_GATEWAY_SENDER (optional, replaces {sender})
 *  {to} = 8801XXXXXXXXX, {msg} = URL-encoded text. Returns {ok, error?}. */
function sendSms_(mobile, text) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var tpl = props.getProperty("SMS_GATEWAY_URL") || "";
  if (!tpl) return { ok: false, error: "not_configured" };
  var to = "88" + normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  var url = tpl
    .replace("{to}", encodeURIComponent(to))
    .replace("{msg}", encodeURIComponent(text))
    .replace("{key}", encodeURIComponent(props.getProperty("SMS_GATEWAY_KEY") || ""))
    .replace("{sender}", encodeURIComponent(props.getProperty("SMS_GATEWAY_SENDER") || ""));
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { method: "get", muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var c = resp.getResponseCode();
    if (c >= 200 && c < 300) return { ok: true };
    Logger.log("SMS gateway fail " + c + ": " + resp.getContentText().substring(0, 200));
    return { ok: false, error: "gateway_" + c };
  } catch (err) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(err) };
  }
}

function hashResetCode_(mobile, code) {
  return hmacHex_("pinreset|" + normalizeMobile_(mobile) + "|" + String(code));
}

/** Logged-in user changes PIN using the old PIN. */
function handleChangePin_(data) {
  var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
  if (!sess) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন মেয়াদ শেষ। আবার লগইন করুন।" });
  var oldPin = String(data.old_pin || "");
  var newPin = String(data.new_pin || "");
  if (!isValidPin_(oldPin)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_old_pin", message: "পুরনো পাসওয়ার্ড/পিন ঠিক করে দিন" });
  if (!isValidNewPassword_(newPin)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_pin", message: "নতুন পাসওয়ার্ড কমপক্ষে ৬ অক্ষরের হতে হবে" });
  if (oldPin === newPin) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "same_pin", message: "নতুন পিন পুরনো পিনের মতো হতে পারবে না" });

  var failKey = "pinfail_" + sess.mobile;
  if (cacheGetNum_(failKey) >= PIN_CHANGE_MAX_FAILS) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "locked", message: "অনেকবার ভুল পিন দেওয়া হয়েছে। ১৫ মিনিট পর চেষ্টা করুন।" });
  }
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(15000); } catch (eL) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "সার্ভার ব্যস্ত, আবার চেষ্টা করুন" });
  }
  try {
    var sh = usersSheet_();
    var row = findUserRow_(sh, sess.mobile);
    if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found", message: "অ্যাকাউন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি" });
    var stored = String(sh.getRange(row, 6).getValue() || "");
    if (!stored || !pinMatches_(stored, oldPin, sess.mobile)) {
      cachePut_(failKey, cacheGetNum_(failKey) + 1, PIN_CHANGE_LOCK_SECONDS);
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "wrong_pin", message: "পুরনো পিন ভুল" });
    }
    sh.getRange(row, 6).setValue(hashPin_(newPin, sess.mobile));
    sh.getRange(row, 5).setValue(nowIsoDhaka_());
    cacheDel_(failKey);
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, message: "পিন সফলভাবে পরিবর্তন হয়েছে" });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

// ---------- ধাপ ২২: অ্যাকাউন্ট পাতা (প্রোফাইল + ইমেইল) ----------
// Users শিটের H কলাম = email (প্রথম সেভে হেডার বসে)। account_get: name/mobile/email ফেরত। account_save: শুধু email বদলায় (খালি = মুছে ফেলা)।
// নাম এখানে বদলানো যায় না (নামের অনন্যতা রেজিস্টার/লগইনে নিয়ন্ত্রিত)।
var ACCOUNT_EMAIL_COL = 8;
var ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL = 9;   // I কলাম: "m" (ছেলে) / "f" (মেয়ে) / খালি
function isValidEmail_(e) { return e.length <= 100 && /^[^\s@<>"']+@[^\s@<>"']+\.[^\s@<>"']{2,}$/.test(e); }
function handleAccount_(data, sub) {
  var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
  if (!sess) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "সেশন শেষ হয়েছে, আবার লগইন করুন" });
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(15000); } catch (eL) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "সার্ভার ব্যস্ত, একটু পরে চেষ্টা করুন" });
  }
  try {
    var sh = usersSheet_();
    var row = findUserRow_(sh, sess.mobile);
    if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found", message: "অ্যাকাউন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি" });
    try { if (String(sh.getRange(1, ACCOUNT_EMAIL_COL).getValue() || "").trim() === "") sh.getRange(1, ACCOUNT_EMAIL_COL).setValue("email"); } catch (eH) {}
    try { if (String(sh.getRange(1, ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL).getValue() || "").trim() === "") sh.getRange(1, ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL).setValue("gender"); } catch (eH2) {}
    var changed = "";
    if (sub === "account_save") {
      if (data.email !== undefined) {
        var email = String(data.email == null ? "" : data.email).trim().toLowerCase();
        if (email && !isValidEmail_(email)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_email", message: "ইমেইল ঠিক নয়" });
        sh.getRange(row, ACCOUNT_EMAIL_COL).setValue(email);
        changed = email ? "ইমেইল সেভ হয়েছে" : "ইমেইল মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে";
      }
      if (data.gender !== undefined) {
        var gIn = String(data.gender || "");
        if (gIn !== "" && gIn !== "m" && gIn !== "f") return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_gender", message: "ছেলে বা মেয়ে বেছে নিন" });
        sh.getRange(row, ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL).setValue(gIn);
        changed = gIn === "m" ? "ছেলে বেছে নেওয়া হয়েছে" : (gIn === "f" ? "মেয়ে বেছে নেওয়া হয়েছে" : "জেন্ডার মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে");
      }
      sh.getRange(row, 5).setValue(nowIsoDhaka_());
    }
    var saved = String(sh.getRange(row, ACCOUNT_EMAIL_COL).getValue() || "").trim();
    var gSaved = String(sh.getRange(row, ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL).getValue() || "").trim();
    if (gSaved !== "m" && gSaved !== "f") gSaved = "";
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, mobile: sess.mobile, name: userNameAt_(sh, row), email: saved, gender: gSaved,
      message: sub === "account_save" ? (changed || "সেভ হয়েছে") : "" });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

// ---------- Forgot-PIN requests -> ADMIN PANEL (no SMS to the customer) ----------
// The 6-digit code is NOT sent to the customer's number. It is stored in Script Properties
// (PIN_RESET_REQUESTS) and shown in the admin panel (+ Telegram). The admin gives the code to
// the customer (WhatsApp/support) after checking who they are.
function pinReqLoad_() {
  try {
    var arr = JSON.parse(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("PIN_RESET_REQUESTS") || "[]");
    if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return [];
    var now = Date.now();
    return arr.filter(function (r) { return r && r.exp > now; });
  } catch (e) { return []; }
}
function pinReqSave_(arr) {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("PIN_RESET_REQUESTS", JSON.stringify((arr || []).slice(0, 30)));
}
function pinReqFind_(arr, mobile) {
  for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) if (arr[i].mobile === mobile) return i;
  return -1;
}

/** Forgot PIN step 1: customer asks for a code -> code goes to the ADMIN panel, not the number. */
function handleForgotPinRequest_(data) {
  var mobile = reqAcct_(data);
  if (!mobile || mobile.length > 40) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_mobile", message: "আপনার User name দিন" });
  }
  if (cacheGetNum_("pinreset_cd_" + mobile)) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "cooldown", message: "একটু অপেক্ষা করুন, ১ মিনিট পর আবার অনুরোধ করতে পারবেন।" });
  }
  var sendsKey = "pinreset_cnt_" + mobile;
  if (cacheGetNum_(sendsKey) >= PIN_RESET_MAX_SENDS_PER_HOUR) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "too_many", message: "অনেকবার অনুরোধ করা হয়েছে। ১ ঘণ্টা পর চেষ্টা করুন।" });
  }
  cachePut_("pinreset_cd_" + mobile, 1, PIN_RESET_COOLDOWN_SECONDS);
  cachePut_(sendsKey, cacheGetNum_(sendsKey) + 1, 3600);

  // Same reply whether or not the account exists (no number enumeration).
  var generic = { ok: true, message: "অনুরোধ এডমিনের কাছে পাঠানো হয়েছে। এডমিন যাচাই করে আপনাকে ৬ ডিজিটের কোড জানাবেন (WhatsApp/সাপোর্ট)। কোডের মেয়াদ ১০ মিনিট।" };
  var row = findUserRow_(usersSheet_(), mobile);
  if (row === -1) return jsonOut_(generic);

  var code = secureDigits_(6);
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(10000); } catch (eL) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "সার্ভার ব্যস্ত, আবার চেষ্টা করুন" });
  }
  try {
    var arr = pinReqLoad_();
    var idx = pinReqFind_(arr, mobile);
    if (idx !== -1) arr.splice(idx, 1);
    arr.unshift({ mobile: mobile, code: code, tries: 0, created_at: nowIsoDhaka_(), exp: Date.now() + PIN_RESET_TTL_SECONDS * 1000 });
    pinReqSave_(arr);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
  try { notifyTelegram_("🔑 PIN রিকভারি অনুরোধ\n" + mobile + "\nকোড: " + code + "\n(এডমিন প্যানেলেও আছে — কাস্টমারকে জানান, ১০ মিনিট বৈধ)"); } catch (eTg) {}
  return jsonOut_(generic);
}

/** Forgot PIN step 2: verify code and set the new PIN. */
function handleForgotPinReset_(data) {
  var mobile = reqAcct_(data);
  var code = String(data.code || "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  var newPin = String(data.new_pin || "");
  if (!mobile || mobile.length > 40) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_mobile", message: "আপনার User name দিন" });
  if (!/^[0-9]{6}$/.test(code)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_code", message: "৬ ডিজিটের কোড দিন" });
  if (!isValidNewPassword_(newPin)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_pin", message: "নতুন পাসওয়ার্ড কমপক্ষে ৬ অক্ষরের হতে হবে" });

  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(15000); } catch (eL) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "সার্ভার ব্যস্ত, আবার চেষ্টা করুন" });
  }
  try {
    var reqs = pinReqLoad_();
    var ri = pinReqFind_(reqs, mobile);
    if (ri === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_expired", message: "কোডের মেয়াদ শেষ বা অনুরোধ করা হয়নি। আবার অনুরোধ করুন।" });
    var st = reqs[ri];
    if (String(st.code) !== code) {
      st.tries = (st.tries || 0) + 1;
      if (st.tries >= PIN_RESET_MAX_TRIES) {
        reqs.splice(ri, 1);
        pinReqSave_(reqs);
        return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "code_burned", message: "অনেকবার ভুল কোড। নতুন করে অনুরোধ করুন।" });
      }
      reqs[ri] = st;
      pinReqSave_(reqs);
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "wrong_code", message: "কোড ভুল (" + (PIN_RESET_MAX_TRIES - st.tries) + " বার চেষ্টা বাকি)" });
    }
    var sh = usersSheet_();
    var row = findUserRow_(sh, mobile);
    if (row === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found", message: "অ্যাকাউন্ট পাওয়া যায়নি" });
    sh.getRange(row, 6).setValue(hashPin_(newPin, mobile));
    sh.getRange(row, 5).setValue(nowIsoDhaka_());
    reqs.splice(ri, 1);
    pinReqSave_(reqs);
    cacheDel_("pinfail_" + mobile);
    var bal = Number(sh.getRange(row, 2).getValue()) || 0;
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, token: makeSessionToken_(mobile), mobile: mobile, balance: bal, message: "নতুন পিন সেট হয়েছে" });
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

function handleWalletAuth_(data) {
  var sub = String(data.sub || "").trim();

  if (sub === "register") {
    var mobile = reqAcct_(data);
    var pin = String(data.pin || "");
    var regName = cleanName_(data.username || data.mobile);
    if (!isValidAcctId_(mobile)) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_username", message: "User name ৩–৩০ অক্ষরের হতে হবে (ইংরেজি অক্ষর, সংখ্যা, _ বা -)। শুধু সংখ্যা দিলে সেটি ১১ ডিজিটের মোবাইল নম্বর হতে হবে।" });
    }
    if (!isValidNewPassword_(pin)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_pin", message: "পাসওয়ার্ড কমপক্ষে ৬ অক্ষরের হতে হবে" });
    var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    try {
      lock.waitLock(15000);
    } catch (eL) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "busy", message: "একটু পর আবার চেষ্টা করুন" });
    }
    try {
      var sh = usersSheet_();
      if (findUserRow_(sh, mobile) !== -1) {
        return jsonOut_({
          ok: false,
          error: "exists",
          message: "এই User name দিয়ে ইতিমধ্যে অ্যাকাউন্ট আছে। অন্য User name দিন বা লগইন করুন।"
        });
      }
      var now = nowIsoDhaka_();
      var regGender = (data.gender === "m" || data.gender === "f") ? data.gender : "";
      sh.appendRow([mobile, 0, "", now, now, hashPin_(pin, mobile), regName, "", regGender]);   // H = email, I = gender
      var tok = makeSessionToken_(mobile);
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: true,
        token: tok,
        mobile: mobile,
        name: regName,
        gender: regGender,
        balance: 0,
        message: "আপনার অ্যাকাউন্ট তৈরি হয়েছে"
      });
    } finally {
      try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e2) {}
    }
  }

  if (sub === "login") {
    var mobile2 = reqAcct_(data);
    var pin2 = String(data.pin || "");
    if (!mobile2 || !isValidPin_(pin2)) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_login", message: "User name ও পাসওয়ার্ড দিন" });
    }
    // Brute-force protection: a 4-6 digit PIN has few combinations, so lock the number after repeated misses.
    var loginFailKey = "pinfail_" + mobile2;
    if (cacheGetNum_(loginFailKey) >= WALLET_LOGIN_MAX_FAILS) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "locked", message: "অনেকবার ভুল পিন দেওয়া হয়েছে। ১৫ মিনিট পর আবার চেষ্টা করুন।" });
    }
    var sh2 = usersSheet_();
    var row = findUserRow_(sh2, mobile2);
    if (row === -1) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found", message: "এই User name-এ অ্যাকাউন্ট নেই। আগে রেজিস্টার করুন।" });
    }
    var stored = String(sh2.getRange(row, 6).getValue() || "");
    if (!stored) {
      // Legacy user without pin — set pin on first login attempt if they pass new pin as set
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "no_pin", message: "এই অ্যাকাউন্টে পাসওয়ার্ড সেট নেই। নতুন পাসওয়ার্ড দিয়ে 'পিন সেট' করুন।" });
    }
    var storedName = userNameAt_(sh2, row);
    if (!pinMatches_(stored, pin2, mobile2)) {
      var nFail = cacheGetNum_(loginFailKey) + 1;
      cachePut_(loginFailKey, nFail, WALLET_LOGIN_LOCK_SECONDS);
      var left = WALLET_LOGIN_MAX_FAILS - nFail;
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: left > 0 ? "wrong_login" : "locked",
        message: left > 0 ? ("User name বা পাসওয়ার্ড ভুল (" + left + " বার চেষ্টা বাকি)") : "অনেকবার ভুল হয়েছে। কিছুক্ষণ পর আবার চেষ্টা করুন।" });
    }
    cacheDel_(loginFailKey);
    if (stored.indexOf("v2$") !== 0) {
      // Silent upgrade of an old static-salt hash to the per-user v2 hash.
      try { sh2.getRange(row, 6).setValue(hashPin_(pin2, mobile2)); } catch (eUp) {}
    }
    var bal = Number(sh2.getRange(row, 2).getValue()) || 0;
    var gen2 = ""; try { gen2 = String(sh2.getRange(row, ACCOUNT_GENDER_COL).getValue() || "").trim(); } catch (eG) {}
    if (gen2 !== "m" && gen2 !== "f") gen2 = "";
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, token: makeSessionToken_(mobile2), mobile: mobile2, name: storedName, gender: gen2, balance: bal });
  }

  if (sub === "set_pin") {
    // For users created via admin adjust without pin
    var mobile3 = reqAcct_(data);
    var pin3 = String(data.pin || "");
    if (!mobile3 || !isValidNewPassword_(pin3)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid", message: "User name ও পাসওয়ার্ড (কমপক্ষে ৬ অক্ষর) ঠিক করুন" });
    var sh3 = usersSheet_();
    var row3 = findUserRow_(sh3, mobile3);
    if (row3 === -1) {
      var now3 = nowIsoDhaka_();
      sh3.appendRow([mobile3, 0, "", now3, now3, hashPin_(pin3, mobile3)]);
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, token: makeSessionToken_(mobile3), mobile: mobile3, balance: 0, message: "অ্যাকাউন্ট+পিন তৈরি" });
    }
    var existingPin = String(sh3.getRange(row3, 6).getValue() || "");
    if (existingPin) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "pin_exists", message: "পিন আগেই আছে। লগইন করুন।" });
    }
    sh3.getRange(row3, 6).setValue(hashPin_(pin3, mobile3));
    var bal3 = Number(sh3.getRange(row3, 2).getValue()) || 0;
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, token: makeSessionToken_(mobile3), mobile: mobile3, balance: bal3, message: "পিন সেট হয়েছে" });
  }

  if (sub === "change_pin") return handleChangePin_(data);
  if (sub === "account_get" || sub === "account_save") return handleAccount_(data, sub);   // ধাপ ২২: অ্যাকাউন্ট পাতা
  if (sub === "forgot_pin_request") return handleForgotPinRequest_(data);
  if (sub === "forgot_pin_reset") return handleForgotPinReset_(data);

  if (sub === "me") {
    var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
    if (!sess) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন মেয়াদ শেষ। আবার লগইন করুন।" });
    var u = getUserBalance_(sess.mobile);
    u.ok = true;
    u.token_ok = true;
    try { var shMe = usersSheet_(), rowMe = findUserRow_(shMe, sess.mobile); if (rowMe !== -1) u.name = userNameAt_(shMe, rowMe); } catch (eMe) {}
    var st = getUserOrderStats_(sess.mobile);
    u.total_spend = st.total_spend;
    u.total_orders = st.total_orders;
    u.completed_orders = st.completed_orders;
    try { u.badge = getCustomerBadge_(sess.mobile); } catch (eB) { u.badge = null; }
    return jsonOut_(u);
  }

  if (sub === "orders") {
    // Order History page: only this customer's real orders (no wallet top-ups), newest first.
    var sessO = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
    if (!sessO) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন করুন" });
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: true,
      mobile: sessO.mobile,
      orders: listUserOrders_(sessO.mobile, data.limit || 200, { cap: 300, skipTopup: true, actions: true })
    });
  }

  if (sub === "history") {
    var sessH = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
    if (!sessH) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন করুন" });
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: true,
      mobile: sessH.mobile,
      orders: listUserOrders_(sessH.mobile, data.limit || 20, { skipTopup: true }),
      ledger: listLedger_(sessH.mobile, data.ledger_limit || 15)
    });
  }

  if (sub === "live") return handleOrdersLive_(data);
  if (sub === "speedup") return handleSpeedupRequest_(data);
  if (sub === "refill" || sub === "cancel") return handleCustomerOrderAction_(data, sub);

  return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unknown_sub" });
}

/** Order History: live Start count / Remaining straight from the provider panel.
 *  Body: { action:"wallet_auth", sub:"live", token, order_ids:[...] }  (max 8 per call)
 *  Only the logged-in customer's own orders are answered. Provider identity is never returned.
 *  getLiveProviderStatus_ caches each lookup for 60s, so repeated refreshes stay cheap. */
function handleOrdersLive_(data) {
  var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
  if (!sess) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth" });
  var m = normalizeMobile_(sess.mobile);
  var want = {}, list = [];
  (Array.isArray(data.order_ids) ? data.order_ids : []).forEach(function (x) {
    var id = String(x == null ? "" : x).trim();
    if (id && !want[id] && list.length < 8) { want[id] = true; list.push(id); }
  });
  var items = {};
  if (!m || !list.length) return jsonOut_({ ok: true, items: items });
  try {
    var sh = sheet_();
    var last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last >= 2) {
      var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID).getValues();
      for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var oid = String(vals[i][COL_ORDERID - 1] || "").trim();
        if (!want[oid] || items[oid]) continue;
        if (normalizeMobile_(vals[i][9]) !== m) continue;          // col J = customer's number
        var poid = String(vals[i][COL_PROVIDER_ORDER_ID - 1] || "").trim();
        if (!poid) { items[oid] = { live: false }; continue; }     // not forwarded to a provider yet
        var live = getLiveProviderStatus_(String(vals[i][COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || ""), poid);
        if (!live) { items[oid] = { live: false }; continue; }
        var it = { live: true, status: mapProviderStatus_(live.status, String(vals[i][COL_STATUS - 1] || "")) };
        if (live.start_count !== undefined && live.start_count !== "") it.start_count = Number(live.start_count);
        if (live.remains !== undefined && live.remains !== "") it.remains = Number(live.remains);
        items[oid] = it;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, items: items });
}

/** Customer creates a wallet top-up "order" — paid like normal, then credited on approve. */
function handleWalletTopup_(sh, data) {
  var sess = parseSessionToken_(data.token);
  if (!sess) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "auth", message: "লগইন করুন" });

  var amount = Number(data.amount);
  if (!amount || isNaN(amount) || amount < WALLET_MIN_TOPUP) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "min_amount", message: "সর্বনিম্ন " + WALLET_MIN_TOPUP + " টাকা টপ-আপ করুন" });
  }

  var method = String(data.paid_from || data.method || "").trim();
  var txn = String(data.txn_id || data.trx || "").trim();
  if (!method) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "method_required", message: "পেমেন্ট মেথড দিন" });
  if (!txn || txn.length < 6) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "txn_required", message: "TrxID দিন" });

  if (txnExists_(sh, txn)) {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "duplicate_txn", message: "এই TrxID আগেই ব্যবহার হয়েছে" });
  }

  // ফি সার্ভারে হিসাব হয় (ক্লায়েন্টের ফি বিশ্বাস করা হয় না): শিটে মোট পাঠানো অ্যামাউন্ট থাকে, ওয়ালেটে ক্রেডিট হয় মোট − ফি
  var topupCredit = amount;
  var topupFee = topupFee_(method, topupCredit);
  amount = Math.round((topupCredit + topupFee) * 100) / 100;

  ensureHeader_(sh);
  var orderId = "WT-" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyyMMdd-HHmmss") + "-" + String(Math.floor(Math.random() * 900 + 100));
  var now = nowIsoDhaka_();
  // A-P columns aligned with order header
  sh.appendRow([
    orderId, "TOPUP", "Wallet Top-up", "Wallet", "",
    1, amount, txn, method, sess.mobile,
    "Pending", now, "Wallet top-up request", "", topupFee > 0 ? "TOPUP_FEE=" + topupFee : "", ""
  ]);
  updateOrderIndexAfterInsert_(sh, orderId, txn);

  try {
    notifyTelegram_("💰 ফান্ড অ্যাড অনুরোধ\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n👤 " + sess.mobile + "\n💵 পরিমাণ: ৳" + topupCredit + (topupFee > 0 ? " + ফি ৳" + topupFee + " = মোট ৳" + amount : "") + "\n💳 মাধ্যম: " + topupMethodLabel_(method) + "\n🔖 TrxID: " + txn + "\n🧾 ID: " + orderId, [[{ text: "🔎 খুলুন / ক্রেডিট", callback_data: "o:" + orderId }]]);
  } catch (eTg) {}

  // If SMS already cached this txn, auto-credit
  try {
    var cached = getUnmatchedSmsAmount_(txn);
    if (cached && cached > 0) {
      var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
      if (row !== -1) {
        var apc = applyTopupCredit_(sh, row, cached);   // ভেতরে হিসাব মেলানো হয়: ক্লেম + ফি = SMS-এর মোট; না মিললে Pending + অ্যালার্ট
        if (apc && apc.ok) {
          return jsonOut_({ ok: true, order_id: orderId, status: "Completed", balance: getUserBalance_(sess.mobile).balance, message: "টপ-আপ অটো ভেরিফাই হয়েছে" });
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (eCache) {}

  return jsonOut_({
    ok: true,
    order_id: orderId,
    status: "Pending",
    message: "টপ-আপ জমা হয়েছে। পেমেন্ট ভেরিফাই হলে ব্যালেন্স যোগ হবে।"
  });
}

function isTopupRow_(sh, row) {
  return String(sh.getRange(row, COL_SERVICE_CODE).getValue() || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "TOPUP";
}

/** Credit wallet when TOPUP order is approved; mark Completed. */
function applyTopupCredit_(sh, row, verifiedAmount) {
  if (!isTopupRow_(sh, row)) return { ok: false, error: "not_topup" };
  var st = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue() || "").toLowerCase();
  if (st === "completed") {
    return { ok: true, already: true };
  }
  var mobile = String(sh.getRange(row, 10).getValue() || ""); // whatsapp col
  var orderId = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_ORDERID).getValue() || "");
  // SMS দিয়ে যাচাই হওয়া সত্যিকারের অ্যামাউন্ট থাকলে সেটাই ক্রেডিট হবে — কাস্টমারের লেখা অ্যামাউন্ট নয়।
  var rcOv = null;
  if (verifiedAmount != null && Number(verifiedAmount) > 0) {
    var rc = topupReconcile_(sh, row, verifiedAmount);
    if (!rc.ok) { topupFlagMismatch_(sh, row, rc, orderId, mobile); return { ok: false, error: "amount_mismatch", reconcile: rc }; }
    rcOv = rc;
    try {
      sh.getRange(row, COL_AMOUNT).setValue(rc.sms);                                   // আসলে যা এসেছে সেটাই শিটে
      if (rc.fee > 0) sh.getRange(row, COL_COMMENTS).setValue("TOPUP_FEE=" + rc.fee);   // পুরনো সারিতেও ফি-মার্কার বসে
    } catch (eV) {}
  }
  var amount = Number(sh.getRange(row, COL_AMOUNT).getValue()) || 0;
  // ফি-সহ নতুন টপ-আপ: শিটের অ্যামাউন্ট = কাস্টমারের পাঠানো মোট; ওয়ালেটে যাবে মোট − ফি (পুরনো সারিতে মার্কার নেই → ফি ০)
  try {
    var feeMark = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_COMMENTS).getValue() || "").match(/^TOPUP_FEE=([\d.]+)$/);
    if (feeMark) amount = Math.round((amount - Number(feeMark[1])) * 100) / 100;
  } catch (eFee) {}
  if (rcOv) amount = rcOv.credit;   // SMS-যাচাইয়ে ওয়ালেটে সবসময় ক্লেমের টাকাই যোগ হয় (সহনসীমার ছোট পার্থক্যেও)
  // একই টপ-আপ ২ বার ক্রেডিট ঠেকাতে: লেজারে আগেই ক্রেডিট থাকলে আর যোগ হবে না (রেস/ডাবল-SMS সুরক্ষা)
  if (hasLedgerTopup_(orderId)) {
    setStatus_(sh, row, "Completed", "Wallet already credited (duplicate blocked)");
    return { ok: true, already: true };
  }
  if (amount < WALLET_MIN_TOPUP) {
    setStatus_(sh, row, "Rejected", "Top-up amount below minimum");
    return { ok: false, error: "min_amount" };
  }
  var adj = adjustUserBalance_(mobile, amount, "topup", orderId, "Top-up verified " + orderId, false);
  if (!adj.ok) {
    setStatus_(sh, row, "Pending", "Wallet credit failed: " + (adj.error || ""));
    return adj;
  }
  var finalBalance = adj.balance;
  var bonusAmt = 0, bonusPct = 0;
  try {
    var bi = topupBonusFor_(amount);
    if (bi.bonus > 0) {
      var badj = adjustUserBalance_(mobile, bi.bonus, "adjust", orderId + "-BONUS", "Top-up bonus " + bi.percent + "% (" + orderId + ")", false);
      if (badj.ok) { finalBalance = badj.balance; bonusAmt = bi.bonus; bonusPct = bi.percent; }
      else { try { notifyTelegram_("WARNING bonus credit failed\n" + normalizeMobile_(mobile) + "\n" + orderId + " bonus " + bi.bonus); } catch (eN) {} }
    }
  } catch (eB) {}
  setStatus_(sh, row, "Completed", "Wallet credited +" + amount + (bonusAmt ? " (+bonus " + bonusAmt + ")" : "") + " → balance " + finalBalance);
  try {
    var tkMethod = String(sh.getRange(row, COL_METHOD).getValue() || "").trim();
    var tkCard = buildTopupCard_(mobile, amount, bonusAmt, bonusPct, tkMethod);
    notifyCardWa_(tkCard, orderId);   // ব্যালেন্স/ID ইচ্ছে করে বাদ — গ্রুপে ফরোয়ার্ড করলে ফাঁস হবে না
  } catch (e) {}
  return { ok: true, balance: finalBalance, order_id: orderId, bonus: bonusAmt };
}

/** Optional: read unmatched SMS cache amount for txn (if helper exists). */
function getUnmatchedSmsAmount_(txn) {
  // FIX: handleSmsVerify_ SMS জমা রাখে CacheService-এ (cacheUnmatchedSms_), তাই এখান থেকেই পড়তে হবে।
  try {
    var amt = consumeCachedSmsAmount_(txn);
    return (amt !== null && amt > 0) ? amt : 0;
  } catch (e) {}
  return 0;
}

/**
 * রিফান্ড হিস্ট্রি + অর্ডার হিস্ট্রি (এডমিন, মোবাইল নম্বর দিয়ে)।
 * refunds: ওয়ালেট লেজারের type=refund এন্ট্রি (সাথে অর্ডারের সার্ভিস/পরিমাণ/স্ট্যাটাস)।
 * orders: ওই নম্বরের সব অর্ডার, প্রতিটায় refunded / refund_pending ফ্ল্যাগ।
 */
function customerHistory_(mobile, limit) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobile);
  limit = Math.min(Number(limit) || 200, 500);
  var out = { ok: true, mobile: m, balance: null, refunds: [], refund_total: 0, orders: [], order_count: 0, refund_pending_count: 0 };
  if (!m) return { ok: false, error: "mobile_required" };
  try { out.balance = getUserBalance_(m).balance; } catch (eB) {}

  // 1) লেজার থেকে রিফান্ড
  var refundRefs = {};
  try {
    var lsh = ledgerSheet_(), llast = lsh.getLastRow();
    if (llast >= 2) {
      var lv = lsh.getRange(2, 1, llast - 1, 8).getValues();
      for (var i = lv.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (normalizeMobile_(lv[i][1]) !== m) continue;
        if (String(lv[i][2]).toLowerCase() !== "refund") continue;
        var amt = Number(lv[i][3]) || 0;
        refundRefs[String(lv[i][5])] = true;
        if (out.refunds.length < limit) {
          out.refunds.push({ id: lv[i][0], amount: amt, balance_after: lv[i][4], ref: String(lv[i][5] || ""), reason: String(lv[i][6] || ""), created_at: lv[i][7] });
        }
        out.refund_total += amt;
      }
    }
  } catch (eL) {}

  // 2) অর্ডার হিস্ট্রি
  var byId = {};
  try {
    var sh = sheet_(), last = sh.getLastRow();
    if (last >= 2) {
      var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 13).getValues();
      for (var j = vals.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        var v = vals[j];
        if (normalizeMobile_(v[9]) !== m) continue;
        var code = String(v[COL_SERVICE_CODE - 1] || "").trim();
        if (code.toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue;
        var oid = String(v[0] || "");
        var status = String(v[COL_STATUS - 1] || "");
        var paidFrom = String(v[8] || "");
        var refunded = !!refundRefs[oid];
        var pending = !refunded && status.toLowerCase() === "rejected" && paidFrom.toLowerCase() === "wallet";
        if (pending) out.refund_pending_count++;
        var o = {
          order_id: oid, service_code: code, service_name: String(v[2] || ""), platform: String(v[3] || ""),
          link: String(v[4] || ""), quantity: v[5], amount: Number(v[6]) || 0, paid_from: paidFrom,
          status: status, created_at: v[11], note: String(v[COL_NOTE - 1] || ""),
          refunded: refunded, refund_pending: pending
        };
        byId[oid] = o;
        out.order_count++;
        if (out.orders.length < limit) out.orders.push(o);
      }
    }
  } catch (eO) {}

  out.refunds.forEach(function (r) {
    var o = byId[r.ref];
    if (o) { r.service_name = o.service_name; r.quantity = o.quantity; r.order_amount = o.amount; r.order_status = o.status; r.link = o.link; }
  });
  out.refund_total = Math.round(out.refund_total * 100) / 100;
  return out;
}

function handleAdminWallet_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });

  var sub = String(data.sub || data.op || "get").trim();

  if (sub === "get" || sub === "lookup") {
    var mobile = String(data.mobile || "").trim();
    if (!mobile) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "mobile_required" });
    var u = getUserBalance_(mobile);
    u.ledger = listLedger_(mobile, 20);
    return jsonOut_(u);
  }

  if (sub === "list_users") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, users: listUsers_(data.limit || 50) });
  }

  // Pending forgot-PIN requests (the code the customer must be given by the admin).
  if (sub === "pin_requests") {
    var prs = pinReqLoad_().map(function (r) {
      return { mobile: r.mobile, code: r.code, created_at: r.created_at, expires_in: Math.max(0, Math.round((r.exp - Date.now()) / 1000)) };
    });
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, requests: prs });
  }
  if (sub === "pin_request_dismiss") {
    var dm = normalizeMobile_(data.mobile);
    var dl = pinReqLoad_(); var di = pinReqFind_(dl, dm);
    if (di !== -1) { dl.splice(di, 1); pinReqSave_(dl); }
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true });
  }

  if (sub === "customer_history" || sub === "refund_history" || sub === "order_history") {
    return jsonOut_(customerHistory_(data.mobile, data.limit));
  }

  if (sub === "list_ledger") {
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, ledger: listLedger_(data.mobile || "", data.limit || 30) });
  }

  if (sub === "adjust") {
    var mob = String(data.mobile || "").trim();
    var delta = Number(data.delta);
    var reason = String(data.reason || "").trim();
    if (!mob) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "mobile_required" });
    if (!delta || isNaN(delta)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_amount" });
    if (!reason) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "reason_required" });
    // Admin credit (যোগ): কোনো মিনিমাম/ম্যাক্সিমাম লিমিট নেই — শুধু এডমিন প্যানেল থেকে।
    // (কাস্টমারের নিজের টপ-আপে WALLET_MIN_TOPUP প্রযোজ্য থাকে।)
    var type = String(data.type || "adjust").trim();
    if (["adjust", "topup", "refund", "order"].indexOf(type) === -1) type = "adjust";
    var force = data.force === true || data.force === "true";
    var result = adjustUserBalance_(mob, delta, type, String(data.ref || "admin"), reason, force);
    if (result.ok) {
      result.ledger = listLedger_(mob, 10);
    }
    return jsonOut_(result);
  }

  // Admin sets a new PIN for a customer (forgot-PIN without SMS). Returns the PIN once so admin can tell the customer.
  if (sub === "reset_pin") {
    var rmob = normalizeMobile_(data.mobile);
    if (!rmob) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_mobile", message: "User name / মোবাইল দিন" });
    var rpin = String(data.new_pin || "").trim();
    if (!rpin) rpin = secureDigits_(6);
    if (!isValidNewPassword_(rpin)) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_pin", message: "পাসওয়ার্ড কমপক্ষে ৬ অক্ষরের হতে হবে" });
    var rsh = usersSheet_();
    var rrow = findUserRow_(rsh, rmob);
    if (rrow === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "not_found", message: "এই নম্বরে অ্যাকাউন্ট নেই" });
    rsh.getRange(rrow, 6).setValue(hashPin_(rpin, rmob));
    rsh.getRange(rrow, 5).setValue(nowIsoDhaka_());
    cacheDel_("pinfail_" + rmob);   // clear wrong-PIN lock
    cacheDel_("pinreset_" + rmob);  // burn any pending recovery code
    try { var rq = pinReqLoad_(); var rqi = pinReqFind_(rq, rmob); if (rqi !== -1) { rq.splice(rqi, 1); pinReqSave_(rq); } } catch (eRq) {}
    try { notifyTelegram_("🔑 এডমিন PIN রিসেট করেছেন\n" + rmob); } catch (eTg) {}
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, mobile: rmob, new_pin: rpin, message: "পিন রিসেট হয়েছে" });
  }

  // Ensure sheets exist (first open)
  if (sub === "init") {
    usersSheet_();
    ledgerSheet_();
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, message: "Users + WalletLedger sheets ready" });
  }

  return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unknown_sub" });
}



// =====================================================================
// SITE THEME (ধাপ ২) — অ্যাডমিন থেকে সাইটের থিম মোড বদলানো
// মোড: "premium" (ডার্ক প্রিমিয়াম) | "light" (লাইট ক্লিন) | "toggle" (ভিজিটর নিজে ডার্ক/লাইট বদলাবে)
// =====================================================================
var THEME_KEY = "SITE_THEME_MODE";
var THEME_MODES = ["premium", "light", "toggle"];
function getThemeMode_() {
  try {
    var m = String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(THEME_KEY) || "premium");
    return THEME_MODES.indexOf(m) === -1 ? "premium" : m;
  } catch (e) { return "premium"; }
}
function handleThemePublic_() {
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, mode: getThemeMode_() });
}
function handleAdminThemeGet_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, mode: getThemeMode_(), modes: THEME_MODES });
}
function handleAdminThemeSave_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var mode = String(data.mode || "").trim();
  if (THEME_MODES.indexOf(mode) === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_mode" });
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(THEME_KEY, mode);
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, mode: mode });
}

// =====================================================================
// SERVICE STATUS NOTICE (P৪) — অ্যাডমিন থেকে লেখা নোটিশ, কাস্টমার সাইটের ওপরে ব্যানার
// Script Property SITE_NOTICE = JSON {on, text, level, updated, by}। level: info | warn | bad
// পাবলিক: doGet ?action=notice_get (শুধু on + text + level + updated, কে লিখেছে তা নয়)
// অ্যাডমিন: admin_notice_get, admin_notice_save (লগে যায়)
// =====================================================================
var NOTICE_KEY = "SITE_NOTICE";
var NOTICE_LEVELS = ["info", "warn", "bad"];
var NOTICE_MAX = 200;
function noticeClean_(v) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, NOTICE_MAX);
}
function getNotice_() {
  var n = { on: false, text: "", level: "info", updated: "", by: "" };
  try {
    var raw = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(NOTICE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return n;
    var o = JSON.parse(raw);
    n.text = noticeClean_(o.text);
    n.on = !!o.on && !!n.text;
    n.level = NOTICE_LEVELS.indexOf(o.level) === -1 ? "info" : o.level;
    n.updated = String(o.updated || "");
    n.by = String(o.by || "").slice(0, 40);
  } catch (e) {}
  return n;
}
function handleNoticePublic_() {
  var n = getNotice_();
  if (!n.on) return jsonOut_({ ok: true, on: false });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, on: true, text: n.text, level: n.level, updated: n.updated });
}
function handleAdminNoticeGet_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, notice: getNotice_(), max: NOTICE_MAX, levels: NOTICE_LEVELS });
}
function handleAdminNoticeSave_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var on = data.on === true || data.on === 1 || data.on === "1" || data.on === "true";
  var text = noticeClean_(data.text);
  var level = String(data.level || "info");
  if (NOTICE_LEVELS.indexOf(level) === -1) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "invalid_level" });
  if (on && !text) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "empty_text" });
  var by = adminLogStr_(data.actor, 40) || "admin";
  var rec = { on: on, text: text, level: level, updated: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"), by: by };
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty(NOTICE_KEY, JSON.stringify(rec));
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, notice: getNotice_() });
}

/** ধাপ ৮: টোকেনহীন সরাসরি-অর্ডার পথ (action ছাড়া POST) ডিফল্টে বন্ধ।
 *  অর্ডার এখন শুধু wallet_order দিয়ে হয়। জরুরি রোলব্যাকে Script Property
 *  ALLOW_DIRECT_ORDER = 1 দিলে পুরনো পথ আবার খোলে (নতুন ডিপ্লয় ছাড়াই)। */
function directOrderAllowed_() {
  try { return String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("ALLOW_DIRECT_ORDER") || "") === "1"; }
  catch (e) { return false; }
}
function directOrderDisabledResponse_() {
  return jsonOut_({
    ok: false,
    error: "direct_order_disabled",
    message: "সরাসরি পেমেন্টে অর্ডার বন্ধ। ওয়ালেটে টপ-আপ করে অর্ডার করুন।"
  });
}

/** পাবলিক অর্ডার-ট্র্যাকিংয়ে TrxID আংশিক ঢেকে দেয় (ধাপ ৮)। */
function maskTxn_(t) {
  var s = String(t || "").trim();
  if (!s || /^wallet/i.test(s) || s === "N/A") return s;
  if (s.length < 6) return s.replace(/./g, "*");
  return s.substring(0, 2) + "****" + s.substring(s.length - 3);
}

// ---------- ধাপ 4: অ্যাডমিন অ্যাক্টিভিটি লগ ----------
// শীট AdminLog: [ts, actor, action, target, before, after, note]. শুধু লেখা-অ্যাকশন লগ হয়; পড়ার অ্যাকশন নয়।
// doPost এখন একটি র‍্যাপার: doPostCore_ চালায়, তারপর (সফল/ব্যর্থ, টোকেন-ভুল বাদে) লগ লেখে।
// গোপন তথ্য (টোকেন/PIN/পাসওয়ার্ড/নতুন পিন) কখনও লগে যায় না — শুধু নির্দিষ্ট ফিল্ড তোলা হয়।
var ADMINLOG_SHEET = "AdminLog";
var ADMINLOG_HEADERS = ["ts", "actor", "action", "target", "before", "after", "note"];
var ADMINLOG_KEEP = 5000;
var ADMINLOG_ARCHIVE_AT = 6000;
var ADMINLOG_ALWAYS = { admin_update: 1, admin_delete: 1, admin_refill: 1, admin_retry_forward: 1, admin_retry_canceled: 1,
  admin_retry_held: 1, admin_bulk: 1, admin_failover: 1, admin_ticket_reply: 1, admin_save_public: 1, admin_save_template: 1, admin_theme_save: 1, admin_notice_save: 1 };
var ADMINLOG_SUBS = {
  admin_wallet: { adjust: 1, reset_pin: 1, pin_request_dismiss: 1 },
  admin_cred: { set: 1, logout_all: 1 },
  admin_service_meta: { set_price: 1, set_price_bulk: 1, set_price_map: 1, remove_price: 1, set_discount: 1, set_discount_bulk: 1, remove_discount: 1, remove_custom: 1, add: 1, hide: 1, unhide: 1, exempt: 1, unexempt: 1, mark_removed: 1, unmark_removed: 1, restore_removed: 1,
    set_config: 1, set_limit: 1, set_speed: 1, dq_clear: 1, dq_confirm: 1, set_backup: 1, remove_backup: 1 },
  admin_speedup: { done: 1 }
};

function adminLogStr_(v, n) {
  var t = String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g, " ").trim();
  return t.length > n ? t.slice(0, n) : t;
}

function adminLogSheet_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(ADMINLOG_SHEET);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(ADMINLOG_SHEET);
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, ADMINLOG_HEADERS.length).setValues([ADMINLOG_HEADERS]);
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sh;
}

function adminLogPre_(e) {
  var raw = (e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || "";
  if (!raw || raw.indexOf("admin_") === -1) return null;
  var d = JSON.parse(raw);
  var action = String(d.action || "");
  var sub = String(d.sub || "");
  if (!ADMINLOG_ALWAYS[action] && !(ADMINLOG_SUBS[action] && ADMINLOG_SUBS[action][sub])) return null;
  var ctx = { action: action + (ADMINLOG_SUBS[action] ? ":" + sub : ""), actor: adminLogStr_(d.actor, 40) || "admin", target: "", before: "", note: "" };
  var ids = Array.isArray(d.order_ids) ? d.order_ids : null;
  if (ids) ctx.target = adminLogStr_(ids.slice(0, 5).join(","), 120) + (ids.length > 5 ? " …(" + ids.length + "টি)" : "");
  else ctx.target = adminLogStr_(d.order_id || d.ticket_id || d.mobile || d.code || (Array.isArray(d.codes) ? d.codes.slice(0, 5).join(",") : "") || "", 120);
  // note: কেবল নিরাপদ ফিল্ড (টোকেন/পিন/মেসেজ-বডি নয়)
  var bits = [];
  if (d.op) bits.push("op=" + adminLogStr_(d.op, 20));
  if (d.status) bits.push("status=" + adminLogStr_(d.status, 20));
  if (d.delta !== undefined) bits.push("delta=" + adminLogStr_(d.delta, 20));
  if (d.price !== undefined) bits.push("price=" + adminLogStr_(d.price, 20));
  if (d.confirm_primary_dead) bits.push("confirm_primary_dead");
  if (d.service_id !== undefined) bits.push("backup_service_id=" + adminLogStr_(d.service_id, 30) + "; provider=" + adminLogStr_(d.provider, 40));
  if (d.map && typeof d.map === "object") bits.push("map=" + Object.keys(d.map).length + "টি; min_margin=" + adminLogStr_(d.min_margin, 10));
  if (d.type) bits.push("type=" + adminLogStr_(d.type, 20));
  if (d.reason) bits.push("reason=" + adminLogStr_(d.reason, 100));
  if (d.note) bits.push("note=" + adminLogStr_(d.note, 100));
  if (action === "admin_notice_save") { bits.push("on=" + (d.on === true || d.on === 1 || d.on === "1" || d.on === "true" ? 1 : 0)); bits.push("level=" + adminLogStr_(d.level, 10)); bits.push("text=" + adminLogStr_(d.text, 100)); }
  ctx.note = bits.join("; ");
  if (action === "admin_update" || action === "admin_delete") {
    try {
      var sh = sheet_(), row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, d.order_id);
      if (row !== -1) ctx.before = adminLogStr_(sh.getRange(row, COL_STATUS).getValue(), 30);
    } catch (eB) {}
  }
  return ctx;
}

function adminLogPost_(ctx, out) {
  var resp = {};
  try { if (out && typeof out.getContent === "function") resp = JSON.parse(out.getContent()); } catch (e0) {}
  var err = resp && resp.error ? String(resp.error) : "";
  if (err === "unauthorized" || err === "locked_out" || err === "busy" || err === "bad_op" || err === "bad_batch_id") return; // স্প্যাম/অ্যাক্সেস-ব্যর্থ লগ নয়
  var after = "";
  if (resp.status) after = adminLogStr_(resp.status, 30);
  if (resp.balance !== undefined && resp.balance !== null) after = (after ? after + "; " : "") + "bal=" + adminLogStr_(resp.balance, 20);
  if (resp.success && resp.failed && resp.skipped) after = "ok " + resp.success.length + "/fail " + resp.failed.length + "/skip " + resp.skipped.length;
  if (resp.refunded) after += (after ? "; " : "") + "refunded=" + adminLogStr_(resp.refunded, 20);
  if (resp.refunded_total) after += (after ? "; " : "") + "refunded=" + adminLogStr_(resp.refunded_total, 20);
  var result = (resp.ok === false || err) ? "FAIL:" + adminLogStr_(err || resp.message || "unknown", 60) : "ok";
  var note = result + (ctx.note ? " | " + ctx.note : "");
  var sh = adminLogSheet_();
  sh.appendRow([Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"), ctx.actor, ctx.action, ctx.target, ctx.before, after, adminLogStr_(note, 300)]);
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last > ADMINLOG_ARCHIVE_AT) adminLogArchive_(sh, last);
}

function adminLogArchive_(sh, last) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(3000)) return; // ব্যস্ত থাকলে পরের বার
  try {
    last = sh.getLastRow();
    var n = last - 1 - ADMINLOG_KEEP;
    if (n < 200) return;
    var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, n, ADMINLOG_HEADERS.length).getValues();
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var name = "AdminLogArchive_" + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM");
    var ar = ss.getSheetByName(name);
    if (!ar) { ar = ss.insertSheet(name); ar.getRange(1, 1, 1, ADMINLOG_HEADERS.length).setValues([ADMINLOG_HEADERS]); ar.setFrozenRows(1); }
    ar.getRange(ar.getLastRow() + 1, 1, vals.length, ADMINLOG_HEADERS.length).setValues(vals);
    sh.deleteRows(2, n);
  } finally { lock.releaseLock(); }
}

function doPost(e) {
  var ctx = null;
  try { ctx = adminLogPre_(e); } catch (e0) { ctx = null; }
  var out = doPostCore_(e);
  if (ctx) { try { adminLogPost_(ctx, out); } catch (e1) { Logger.log("adminlog: " + e1); } }
  return out;
}

function handleAdminLog_(data) {
  var authErr = adminAuthError_(data.token);
  if (authErr) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authErr });
  var limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(data.limit, 10) || 150, 1), 300);
  var fActor = adminLogStr_(data.actor_filter, 40).toLowerCase();
  var fAct = adminLogStr_(data.act, 40).toLowerCase();
  var q = adminLogStr_(data.q, 60).toLowerCase();
  var dFrom = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(data.date_from || "")) ? String(data.date_from) : "";
  var dTo = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(data.date_to || "")) ? String(data.date_to) : "";
  var sh = adminLogSheet_(), last = sh.getLastRow();
  var rows = [], actors = {}, acts = {}, scanned = 0;
  if (last >= 2) {
    var from = Math.max(2, last - 2999);
    var vals = sh.getRange(from, 1, last - from + 1, ADMINLOG_HEADERS.length).getValues();
    scanned = vals.length;
    for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var r = vals[i];
      var ts = String(r[0]);
      actors[String(r[1])] = 1; acts[String(r[2])] = 1;
      if (rows.length >= limit) continue;
      var day = ts.slice(0, 10);
      if ((dFrom && day < dFrom) || (dTo && day > dTo)) continue;
      if (fActor && String(r[1]).toLowerCase() !== fActor) continue;
      if (fAct && String(r[2]).toLowerCase().indexOf(fAct) === -1) continue;
      if (q && (String(r[3]) + " " + String(r[6])).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) === -1) continue;
      rows.push({ ts: ts, actor: String(r[1]), action: String(r[2]), target: String(r[3]), before: String(r[4]), after: String(r[5]), note: String(r[6]) });
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ ok: true, rows: rows, actors: Object.keys(actors).sort(), actions: Object.keys(acts).sort(), scanned: scanned });
}

function doPostCore_(e) {
  // ----- Telegram bot webhook (admin panel in Telegram) — handled before anything else -----
  try {
    var tgRaw = (e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || "";
    if (tgRaw.indexOf('"update_id"') !== -1) {
      var tgUpd = JSON.parse(tgRaw);
      if (tgUpd && tgUpd.update_id !== undefined) return tgaHandleUpdate_(e, tgUpd);
    }
  } catch (eTgUpd) { Logger.log("tg route: " + eTgUpd); }
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  var holdsLock = false;
  try {
    // Read-only admin actions must NOT queue behind the global lock (a slow balance
    // check used to make the order list answer "busy" => empty panel). They never
    // append/modify orders, so they can't race with a new order being written.
    var preAction = "", preLive = false;
    try {
      var preData = JSON.parse((e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || "{}");
      preAction = String(preData.action || "");
      preLive = preData.live === true || preData.live === "true" || preData.live === 1 || preData.live === "1";
    } catch (ePre) {}
    // ধাপ ৮: action ছাড়া অনুরোধ = পুরনো সরাসরি-অর্ডার পথ। লকের আগেই ফিরিয়ে দিই, যাতে স্প্যামে লক না আটকায়।
    if (!preAction && !directOrderAllowed_()) {
      return directOrderDisabledResponse_();
    }
    var lockFree = (preAction === "admin_balances" || preAction === "admin_stats" || preAction === "admin_profit" || preAction === "admin_login" || preAction === "admin_provider_log" ||
                    preAction === "admin_ticket_list" || preAction === "admin_order_actions" || preAction === "admin_speedup" || preAction === "admin_speedtest" || preAction === "admin_provider_ticket" || preAction === "admin_appstate" || preAction === "admin_stuck" || preAction === "admin_dashboard" || preAction === "admin_health" || preAction === "admin_cost_watch" || preAction === "admin_failover_check" || preAction === "admin_log" || (preAction === "admin_list" && !preLive));
    if (!lockFree) {
      if (!lock.tryLock(15000)) {
        return jsonOut_({
          ok: false,
          error: "busy",
          message: "Server busy, try again in a moment."
        });
      }
      holdsLock = true;
    }

    var raw = "{}";
    if (e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) {
      raw = e.postData.contents;
    }
    var data = JSON.parse(raw);
    // সার্ভিস স্টুডিও (PtServices.gs): svc_ দিয়ে শুরু সব অ্যাকশন এখানে যাবে
    if (String(data.action || "").indexOf("svc_") === 0) return jsonOut_(ptSvcRoute_(data));
    var sh = sheet_();
    ensureHeader_(sh);

    // ----- New order-review actions (SMS auto-verify webhook / admin panel) -----
    // Kept inside the same doPost + same lock so they can never race with a
    // brand-new order being appended at the same moment.
    // ----- Support tickets (no order-sheet access needed) -----
    if (data.action === "ticket_create") {
      return handleTicketCreate_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_ticket_list") {
      return handleAdminTicketList_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_order_actions") {
      return handleAdminOrderActions_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_ticket_reply") {
      return handleAdminTicketReply_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "sms_verify") {
      return handleSmsVerify_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_theme_get") {
      return handleAdminThemeGet_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_theme_save") {
      return handleAdminThemeSave_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_notice_get") {
      return handleAdminNoticeGet_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_notice_save") {
      return handleAdminNoticeSave_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_balances") {
      return handleAdminBalances_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_stats") {
      return handleAdminStats_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_profit") {
      return handleAdminProfit_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_login") {
      return handleAdminLogin_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_provider_log") {
      return handleAdminProviderLog_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_auto_refund_run") {
      return handleAdminAutoRefundRun_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_cred") {
      return handleAdminCred_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_stuck") {
      return handleAdminStuck_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_dashboard") {
      return handleAdminDashboard_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_bulk") {
      return handleAdminBulk_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_failover_check") {
      return handleAdminFailoverCheck_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_failover") {
      return handleAdminFailover_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_health") {
      return handleAdminHealth_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_cost_watch") {
      return handleAdminCostWatch_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_log") {
      return handleAdminLog_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_list") {
      return handleAdminList_(sh, data.token, data.status, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_retry_forward") {
      return handleAdminRetryForward_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_retry_canceled") {
      return handleAdminRetryCanceled_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_retry_held") {
      var authH = adminAuthError_(data.token);
      if (authH) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authH });
      var rr = retryHeldProviderOrders_(data.provider || null);
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, held_retry: rr });
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_service_meta") {
      return handleAdminServiceMeta_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_speedup") {
      return handleAdminSpeedup_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_speedtest") {
      return handleAdminSpeedTest_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_appstate") {
      return handleAdminAppState_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_wallet") {
      return handleAdminWallet_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_alerts") {
      var authA = adminAuthError_(data.token);
      if (authA) return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: authA });
      var subA = String(data.sub || "list");
      if (subA === "clear" && data.id) {
        clearAdminAlert_(data.id);
        return jsonOut_({ ok: true, alerts: listAdminAlerts_() });
      }
      if (subA === "clear_all") {
        PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS", "[]");
        return jsonOut_({ ok: true, alerts: [] });
      }
      return jsonOut_({ ok: true, alerts: listAdminAlerts_() });
    }
    if (data.action === "wallet_auth") {
      return handleWalletAuth_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "wallet_topup") {
      return handleWalletTopup_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "wallet_order") {
      return handleWalletOrder_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "service_meta") {
      return handleServiceMetaPublic_();
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_update") {
      return handleAdminUpdate_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_delete") {
      return handleAdminDelete_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_refill") {
      return handleAdminRefill_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_provider_ticket") {
      return handleAdminProviderTicket_(data);
    }
    // Public proof redaction (admin.html "Public Proof" editor)
    if (data.action === "admin_get_proof") {
      return handleAdminGetProof_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_save_public") {
      return handleAdminSavePublic_(sh, data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_get_templates") {
      return handleAdminGetTemplates_(data);
    }
    if (data.action === "admin_save_template") {
      return handleAdminSaveTemplate_(data);
    }

    // Any remaining "action" means an unknown/outdated route — never fall into order-create
    if (data.action) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: "unknown_action",
        message: "Unknown action: " + String(data.action) + ". Deploy latest google-apps-script.js (New version)."
      });
    }

    // ধাপ ৮: দ্বিতীয় প্রহরী (উপরের গার্ড কোনোভাবে এড়ালেও অর্ডার তৈরি হবে না)
    if (!directOrderAllowed_()) {
      return directOrderDisabledResponse_();
    }

    var orderId = String(data.order_id || "").trim();
    var txn = String(data.txn_id || "").trim();
    var method = String(data.paid_from || "").trim();
    var mobile = String(data.whatsapp || "").trim();
    if (!mobile && data.paid_from_mobile) {
      mobile = String(data.paid_from_mobile).trim();
    }

    // order_id from the browser is only a suggestion; the server assigns the final numeric ID below.

    if (method && method.toLowerCase() !== "binance") {
      if (!isValidMobile_(mobile)) {
        return jsonOut_({
          ok: false,
          error: "invalid_mobile",
          message: "Mobile number must be exactly 11 digits (01XXXXXXXXX)."
        });
      }
    }

    if (!isValidTxnFormat_(txn, method)) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: "invalid_txn",
        message: "Invalid Transaction ID format."
      });
    }

    if (txnExists_(sh, txn)) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: "txn_used",
        message: "This TrxID has already been used."
      });
    }

    // Same ID + same TrxID = a retried submit of the same order -> report it as already done.
