    // Same ID but a different order (numeric IDs are short, so collisions are possible) -> assign a fresh ID.
    if (orderId && orderIdExists_(sh, orderId)) {
      var exRow = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
      var exTxn = exRow > 0 ? String(sh.getRange(exRow, COL_TXN).getValue() || "").trim().toUpperCase() : "";
      if (exRow > 0 && exTxn && exTxn === txn.toUpperCase()) {
        return jsonOut_({
          ok: true,
          duplicate: true,
          order_id: orderId
        });
      }
      orderId = "";
    }
    if (!/^\d{5,8}$/.test(orderId)) {
      orderId = generateOrderId_(sh);
    }

    // Anti-spam: cap how many *unverified* (Pending) orders one mobile
    // number can rack up in a short window. Legit customers rarely place
    // many at once; this mainly slows down someone testing random TrxIDs.
    var RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_HOURS = 3;
    var RATE_LIMIT_MAX_PENDING = 5;
    if (method && method.toLowerCase() !== "binance") {
      var recentPending = countRecentPendingByMobile_(sh, mobile, RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_HOURS);
      if (recentPending >= RATE_LIMIT_MAX_PENDING) {
        return jsonOut_({
          ok: false,
          error: "rate_limited",
          message: "Too many unverified orders from this number recently. Please wait for an existing order to be verified, or contact support."
        });
      }
    }

    // Server-side price/quantity truth check — never trust the client's
    // amount. Recomputes the price from the "Prices" sheet and rejects the
    // order if the browser's numbers don't match (tampering, stale page, bug).
    // Custom Comments: কমেন্ট থাকলে লাইন সংখ্যা == quantity হতে হবে (সার্ভার-সাইড যাচাই)
    var commentsText = String(data.comments || "").replace(/\r/g, "").trim();
    if (commentsText) {
      var cLines = commentsText.split("\n").map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(function (x) { return x; });
      if (cLines.length !== Number(data.quantity)) {
        return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "comments_mismatch", message: "Comments সংখ্যা ও Quantity মিলছে না। পেজ রিফ্রেশ করে আবার চেষ্টা করুন।" });
      }
      commentsText = cLines.join("\n").substring(0, 45000); // Sheets cell limit safety
    }

    var pricing = validateOrderPricing_(data.service_code, data.quantity, data.amount);
    if (!pricing.ok) {
      return jsonOut_(pricing);
    }

    // Heuristic-only note (never blocks the order) so the admin panel shows
    // a visible warning on TrxIDs that look like a tweaked version of one
    // already on file — see findSimilarTxnNote_ for why this can't be a hard rule.
    var similarNote = findSimilarTxnNote_(sh, txn);
    var initialNote = data.note || "";
    if (similarNote) {
      initialNote = initialNote ? (initialNote + " | " + similarNote) : similarNote;
    }

    // Race-condition fix: the payment SMS sometimes arrives (and gets
    // forwarded by MacroDroid) before the customer submits this very form.
    // If that already happened, a cached (txn -> amount) entry is sitting
    // here waiting — consume it and start the order as already-verified
    // instead of Pending, rather than waiting on a second SMS that will
    // never come for this TrxID.
    var initialStatus = data.status || "Pending";
    var cachedSmsAmount = consumeCachedSmsAmount_(txn);
    if (cachedSmsAmount !== null) {
      var earlyHold = "";
      try { earlyHold = smsEarlyHoldReason_(cachedSmsAmount, SMS_LAST_CACHED_FROM_, (typeof mobile !== "undefined" ? mobile : ""), SMS_LAST_CACHED_PROVIDER_, SMS_LAST_CACHED_DT_, (typeof method !== "undefined" ? method : "")); } catch (eEh) {}
      if (Math.abs(cachedSmsAmount - pricing.amount) <= SMS_AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT && earlyHold) {
        initialNote = initialNote ? (initialNote + " | ⚠️ " + earlyHold) : ("⚠️ " + earlyHold);
      } else if (Math.abs(cachedSmsAmount - pricing.amount) <= SMS_AMOUNT_TOLERANCE_BDT) {
        initialStatus = "Processing";
        initialNote = initialNote
          ? (initialNote + " | ✅ Auto-verified via SMS (SMS arrived before this order was submitted).")
          : "✅ Auto-verified via SMS (SMS arrived before this order was submitted).";
      } else {
        initialNote = initialNote
          ? (initialNote + " | ⚠️ A matching-TrxID SMS arrived but amount differs (SMS ৳" + cachedSmsAmount + " vs order ৳" + pricing.amount + ") — needs manual check.")
          : "⚠️ A matching-TrxID SMS arrived but amount differs (SMS ৳" + cachedSmsAmount + " vs order ৳" + pricing.amount + ") — needs manual check.";
      }
    }

    // Final, direct, uncached re-check right before writing — this closes
    // the gap even if the fast cache-based txnExists_ check above ever had
    // a stale/edge-case miss (this is what let duplicate TrxIDs from
    // several near-simultaneous submissions slip through before). This
    // reads the live sheet directly, still inside the same script lock, so
    // it costs one extra small read but guarantees no duplicate TrxID is
    // ever actually written.
    if (findRowByTxn_(sh, txn) !== -1) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: "txn_used",
        message: "This TrxID has already been used."
      });
    }

    // Save payment screenshot to Drive (optional — never blocks the order)
    var proofUrl = "";
    var proofErr = "";
    if (data.proof_base64) {
      var proofResult = saveProofToDrive_(orderId, data.proof_base64, data.proof_mime || "image/jpeg");
      proofUrl = proofResult.url || "";
      proofErr = proofResult.error || "";
      if (!proofUrl && proofErr) {
        initialNote = initialNote
          ? (initialNote + " | ⚠️ Proof upload failed: " + proofErr)
          : ("⚠️ Proof upload failed: " + proofErr);
      }
    } else {
      initialNote = initialNote
        ? (initialNote + " | ⚠️ No proof_base64 in request")
        : "⚠️ No proof_base64 in request";
    }

    sh.appendRow([
      orderId,
      data.service_code || "",
      data.service_name || "",
      data.platform || "",
      data.link || "",
      data.quantity || "",
      pricing.amount, // server-computed amount — the client's value is discarded
      txn,
      method,
      normalizeMobile_(mobile) || mobile,
      initialStatus,
      data.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
      initialNote,
      proofUrl,
      commentsText
    ]);

    updateOrderIndexAfterInsert_(sh, orderId, txn);
    notifyOwnerByEmail_(orderId, data, pricing, txn, method, mobile);
    try { notifyOwnerTelegramOrder_(orderId, data, pricing, txn, method, mobile); } catch (eTg) { Logger.log("tg order: " + eTg); }

    return jsonOut_({
      ok: true,
      order_id: orderId,
      amount: pricing.amount,
      proof_url: proofUrl,
      proof_error: proofErr,
      message: "Order created successfully."
    });

  } catch (err) {
    return jsonOut_({
      ok: false,
      error: String(err)
    });
  } finally {
    try {
      if (holdsLock) lock.releaseLock();
    } catch (ignore) {}
  }
}

function doGet(e) {
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "ver") {   // deploy check: open <exec URL>?action=ver
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, version: BACKEND_VERSION, note: "boostplus backend", features: "username-login, gender, a0-foundation, s1-stuck-panel, s2-dashboard, s3-bulk, s4-adminlog, s5-health, s6-margin, s7-backup, p4-notice" });
  }
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "service_meta") {
    return handleServiceMetaPublic_();
  }
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "done_list") {
    return handleDonePublic_();
  }
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "theme_get") {
    return handleThemePublic_();
  }
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "notice_get") {
    return handleNoticePublic_();
  }
  var sh = sheet_();
  ensureHeader_(sh);

  // ----- One-click Approve/Reject links clicked from the order-notification
  // email. Uses GET (a plain link) instead of the admin panel's POST, and
  // returns a small human-readable HTML page instead of JSON since a person
  // is looking at this in a browser, not a script. -----
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "admin_update") {
    var emailActionData = {
      token: e.parameter.token,
      order_id: e.parameter.order_id,
      status: e.parameter.status,
      note: "via email button"
    };
    var result = handleAdminUpdate_(sh, emailActionData);
    var resultObj = JSON.parse(result.getContent());
    var pageTitle = resultObj.ok ? "✅ Done" : "❌ Failed";
    var pageMsg = resultObj.ok
      ? ("Order " + esc_(emailActionData.order_id) + " is now " + esc_(resultObj.status) + ".")
      : ("Could not update the order: " + esc_(resultObj.error || "unknown error"));
    return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(
      "<html><body style='font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;padding:40px 16px;'>" +
      "<h2>" + pageTitle + "</h2><p>" + pageMsg + "</p></body></html>"
    );
  }

  // ----- One-time Drive permission / folder self-test (admin only)
  // Open:  .../exec?action=test_drive&token=YOUR_ADMIN_TOKEN
  // If Drive scope is missing you will see ok:false and an error message.
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "test_drive") {
    if (!checkAdminToken_(e.parameter.token)) {
      return jsonOut_({ ok: false, error: "unauthorized" });
    }
    try {
      var folder = proofFolder_();
      var testBlob = Utilities.newBlob("proof-drive-ok", "text/plain", "drive_test.txt");
      var testFile = folder.createFile(testBlob);
      testFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: true,
        folder: folder.getName(),
        folder_id: folder.getId(),
        file_id: testFile.getId(),
        message: "Drive write OK. Check Google Drive for folder: PrimeBoost Payment Proofs"
      });
    } catch (err) {
      return jsonOut_({
        ok: false,
        error: String(err),
        message: "Drive failed. Re-deploy Web App and accept Drive permission when Google asks."
      });
    }
  }

  // ----- Public Payment Proof gallery — only screenshots from orders that
  // Admin has moved to Processing or Completed AND published a redacted copy.
  // No TrxID, no mobile, no note — and the image itself has them covered.
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "payment_proofs") {
    var shP = sheet_();
    ensureHeader_(shP);
    var lastRowP = shP.getLastRow();
    var proofs = [];
    if (lastRowP >= 2) {
      var scanP = Math.min(lastRowP - 1, 500);
      var startP = lastRowP - scanP + 1;
      var colsP = Math.max(shP.getLastColumn(), 16);
      var valsP = shP.getRange(startP, 1, scanP, colsP).getValues();
      for (var pi = valsP.length - 1; pi >= 0 && proofs.length < 60; pi--) {
        var rp = valsP[pi];
        var stP = String(rp[10] || "").trim().toLowerCase(); // K = status
        if (stP !== "processing" && stP !== "completed") continue;
        // P = proof_public_url (index 15) — the REDACTED copy made in admin.html.
        // The original screenshot (col N) is never exposed publicly: it shows
        // the customer's full number and TrxID.
        var urlP = String(rp[15] || "").trim();
        if (!urlP || urlP.indexOf("http") !== 0) continue;
        proofs.push({
          url: urlP,
          method: String(rp[8] || ""), // paid_from
          amount: rp[6] || "",
          service_name: String(rp[2] || ""),
          time: rp[11] || ""
        });
      }
    }
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, proofs: proofs });
  }

  // ----- Public "recent completed orders" feed for the homepage social-proof
  // widget. Deliberately requires NO token (it's meant for every visitor's
  // browser) — so it must never return anything sensitive: no TrxID, no
  // full mobile number, no order_id, no note. Just enough to show something
  // like "018***871 just completed an order for X". -----
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "recent_completed") {
    var sh0 = sheet_();
    ensureHeader_(sh0);
    var lastRow0 = sh0.getLastRow();
    var feed = [];
    if (lastRow0 >= 2) {
      var scanCount = Math.min(lastRow0 - 1, 400); // recent orders only
      var startRow0 = lastRow0 - scanCount + 1;
      var vals0 = sh0.getRange(startRow0, 1, scanCount, sh0.getLastColumn()).getValues();
      for (var vi = vals0.length - 1; vi >= 0 && feed.length < 15; vi--) {
        var r = vals0[vi];
        var st0 = String(r[10] || "").trim().toLowerCase(); // status column K
        if (st0 !== "completed") continue;
        feed.push({
          service_name: String(r[2] || ""),
          platform: String(r[3] || ""),
          quantity: r[5] || "",
          mobile_masked: maskMobile_(r[9]),
          time: r[11] || "",
          took: (r[16] && parseTimeMs_(r[17]) && parseTimeMs_(r[18]) && parseTimeMs_(r[18]) >= parseTimeMs_(r[17]))
            ? formatDurationBn_(parseTimeMs_(r[18]) - parseTimeMs_(r[17])) : ""
        });
      }
    }
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, orders: feed });
  }

  // ----- Public: a visitor's own tickets (by their random ticket IDs) -----
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "ticket_list") {
    return handleTicketList_(e.parameter.ids);
  }

  // ----- Admin panel: list orders, newest first, optionally filtered by status -----
  // Kept here (GET) only for backward compatibility / manual testing — the
  // admin panel itself now calls this same logic via doPost (see
  // handleAdminList_ + the "admin_list" branch in doPost) so the token
  // travels in the POST body instead of sitting in a URL/query string.
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.action === "admin_list") {
    return handleAdminList_(sh, e.parameter.token, e.parameter.status, e.parameter);
  }

  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.stats) {
    // Only count orders whose status is "Completed" — not every submitted
    // order (Pending/Processing/Cancelled don't count as a finished job).
    var lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
    var completedOrders = 0;
    if (lastRow >= 2) {
      var statusValues = sh.getRange(2, 2, lastRow - 1, 10).getValues(); // B..K
      for (var i = 0; i < statusValues.length; i++) {
        if (String(statusValues[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "TOPUP") continue; // টপ-আপ গণনা হবে না
        var st = String(statusValues[i][9] || "").trim().toLowerCase(); // K = status
        if (st === "completed") completedOrders++;
      }
    }
    return jsonOut_({ ok: true, total_orders: completedOrders });
  }

  var checkTxn = "";
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.check_txn) {
    checkTxn = String(e.parameter.check_txn).trim();
  }
  if (checkTxn) {
    var exists = txnExists_(sh, checkTxn);
    return jsonOut_({ exists: exists });
  }

  var checkMobile = "";
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.check_mobile) {
    checkMobile = String(e.parameter.check_mobile).trim();
  }
  if (checkMobile) {
    return jsonOut_({ valid: isValidMobile_(checkMobile) });
  }

  var orderId = "";
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.order_id) {
    orderId = String(e.parameter.order_id).trim();
    if (e.parameter.mobile) orderId = resolveOrderIdForMobile_(e.parameter.mobile, orderId);   // প্রোভাইডার আইডি লিখলেও কাস্টমারের নিজের অর্ডার মিলবে
  }
  if (!orderId) {
    return jsonOut_({ error: "order_id required" });
  }

  var values = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (values.length < 2) {
    return jsonOut_({ error: "not found" });
  }

  var headers = values[0].map(function (h) {
    return String(h);
  });

  for (var i = values.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(values[i][0]) === orderId) {
      var row = {};
      for (var j = 0; j < headers.length; j++) {
        row[headers[j]] = values[i][j];
      }

      // ----- Live status from the provider panel, if this order was
      // auto-forwarded — but the provider's identity itself (which panel,
      // the raw note text that may mention it) must never reach the
      // customer. Only clean, curated fields go out. -----
      var live = null;
      if (row.provider_order_id) {
        live = getLiveProviderStatus_(String(row.service_code || ""), String(row.provider_order_id));
      }
      if (live) {
        row.status = syncStatusFromLive_(sh, orderId, live.status, row.status);
        if (live.start_count !== undefined) row.start_count = live.start_count;
        if (live.remains !== undefined) row.remains = live.remains;
      }
      // Strip anything that could reveal or hint at the supplier panel.
      delete row.provider_order_id;
      delete row.note;
      attachTimerFields_(row, row.status);

      // ধাপ ৮: এই পাবলিক লুকআপ টোকেনহীন, অর্ডার আইডি ৫ ডিজিটের সংখ্যা (যেমন 63284), তাই আন্দাজযোগ্য।
      // তাই আসল স্ক্রিনশট (নম্বর+TrxID দেখা যায়) পুরো বাদ, মোবাইল ও TrxID আংশিক ঢাকা।
      delete row.proof_url;
      if (row.whatsapp !== undefined) row.whatsapp = maskMobile_(row.whatsapp);
      if (row.txn_id !== undefined) row.txn_id = maskTxn_(row.txn_id);

      return jsonOut_(row);
    }
  }
  return jsonOut_({ error: "not found" });
}
/** Run once from the editor (dropdown → authorizeDriveOnce → Run) to grant Drive read. */
function authorizeDriveOnce() {
  var folder = DriveApp.getRootFolder();
  Logger.log("Drive OK: " + folder.getName());
}

/** Run once from the editor (dropdown → authorizeDriveWrite → Run) to grant Drive write.
 *  Accept the Google permission popup (Allow). Creates folder if missing. */
function authorizeDriveWrite() {
  var name = "PrimeBoost Payment Proofs";
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  var folder = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
  var file = folder.createFile("drive_write_test.txt", "ok", MimeType.PLAIN_TEXT);
  Logger.log("WRITE OK: " + folder.getId() + " file=" + file.getId());
}


// =====================================================================
//  TELEGRAM ADMIN PANEL
//  এডমিন প্যানেলের কাজ টেলিগ্রাম বট থেকেই: অর্ডার অ্যাপ্রুভ/রিজেক্ট, টপ-আপ, টিকেট,
//  ওয়ালেট, প্রোভাইডার ব্যালেন্স, অ্যালার্ট, ডিসকাউন্ট।
//  সব কাজ আপনার বর্তমান handleAdmin*_ ফাংশন দিয়েই হয় — আলাদা লজিক নেই।
//
//  ONE-TIME SETUP (এডিটর থেকে):
//   1) Deploy → Manage deployments → New version (Anyone access) করুন
//   2) setupTelegramAdmin() একবার Run করুন → webhook সেট হবে
//   3) বটে /menu পাঠান
//  Script Properties: TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID (আগে থেকেই আছে),
//  TG_WEBHOOK_SECRET (অটো তৈরি হয়), TELEGRAM_ADMIN_IDS (ঐচ্ছিক, কমা দিয়ে আরও অ্যাডমিন)।
// =====================================================================

var TGA_PAGE = 6;

function tgaProp_(k) { return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(k) || ""; }

/** Run once from the editor. */
function setupTelegramAdmin() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var secret = props.getProperty("TG_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  if (!secret) { secret = randomToken_(24); props.setProperty("TG_WEBHOOK_SECRET", secret); }
  var base = props.getProperty("WEBAPP_URL") || ScriptApp.getService().getUrl();
  if (!base) throw new Error("Web app URL পাওয়া যায়নি — আগে Deploy করুন, অথবা Script Property WEBAPP_URL এ /exec লিংক বসান।");
  var url = base + (base.indexOf("?") === -1 ? "?" : "&") + "k=" + secret;
  var r = tgaApi_("setWebhook", { url: url, allowed_updates: ["message", "callback_query"], drop_pending_updates: true });
  Logger.log("setWebhook: " + JSON.stringify(r));
  var c = tgaApi_("setMyCommands", { commands: tgaCommands_() });
  props.setProperty("TG_CMD_VER", TG_CMD_VERSION_);
  Logger.log("setMyCommands: " + JSON.stringify(c));
  notifyTelegram_("✅ Telegram অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল চালু। /menu পাঠান।");
}
/** Remove the webhook (turn the Telegram panel off). */
function removeTelegramAdmin() { Logger.log(JSON.stringify(tgaApi_("deleteWebhook", {}))); }

// ============================================================
// Telegram: সার্ভিস ম্যানেজার (কাস্টমাইজ / রিমুভ) + ডেলিভারি-টাইম রেকর্ড
//   /svc CODE | /svc কিওয়ার্ড   → সাইটে থাকা যেকোনো সার্ভিস খুলে ম্যানেজ করুন
//        দাম · ডিসকাউন্ট · স্পিড ট্যাগ · সময়-সীমা · ক্যাটাগরি · exempt · রিমুভ/ফেরানো
//   /speed                      → কোন সার্ভিস কত সময়ে সম্পন্ন হচ্ছে (SpeedLog শিটের রেকর্ড)
//   /speed CODE                 → ওই সার্ভিসের শেষ অর্ডারগুলোর সময়
//   /speedcfg 5 24 40           → min রেকর্ড · সর্বোচ্চ ঘণ্টা · সর্বোচ্চ ফেইল %
// সব পরিবর্তন আগের handleAdminServiceMeta_ দিয়েই হয় (SERVICE_META) — সাইট/অ্যাডমিন প্যানেল/SMM অ্যাপ একই ডেটা দেখে।
// ============================================================
var SM_PER_ = 8;

/** { code: pkg } — মূল ক্যাটালগ + কাস্টম সার্ভিস (কাস্টম অগ্রাধিকার পায়)। */
function tgaSmPkgs_() {
  var map = {};
  try {
    parsePkgs_(UrlFetchApp.fetch(PACKAGES_JSON_URL, { muteHttpExceptions: true }).getContentText()).forEach(function (p) {
      if (p && p.code != null) map[String(p.code)] = p;
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  customServices_().forEach(function (p) { if (p && p.code != null) map[String(p.code)] = p; });
  return map;
}
function tgaSmCall_(payload) {
  payload.token = tgaTok_();
  return tgaLocked_(function () { return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminServiceMeta_(payload); }); });
}
function tgaSmDur_(sec) { return sec > 0 ? formatDurationShortBn_(sec * 1000) : "—"; }
function tgaSmShort_(s, n) { return String(s || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().substring(0, n || 50); }

// ---------- সার্ভিস খোঁজা (সাইটের ক্যাটালগে) ----------
function tgaSmPrompt_(ctx) {
  tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "smq" });
  tgaShow_(ctx, "🛠 সার্ভিস ম্যানেজ\nসাইটে থাকা সার্ভিসের কোড বা কিওয়ার্ড লিখে পাঠান।\n\nউদাহরণ:\n• 7469\n• facebook followers\n• instagram likes smmrx\n\n(প্রোভাইডার থেকে নতুন সার্ভিস আনতে: ➕ সার্ভিস যোগ)", [[tgaBtn_("➕ সার্ভিস যোগ", "sv"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}
function tgaSmSearch_(ctx, q) {
  q = String(q || "").trim();
  if (!q) return tgaSmPrompt_(ctx);
  var pkgs = tgaSmPkgs_(), meta = getServiceMeta_();
  var one = q.replace(/^#/, "");
  if (/^\d+$/.test(one) && (pkgs[one] || meta.removed[one])) return tgaSmCard_(ctx, one);
  var words = q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(String), codes = [];
  Object.keys(pkgs).forEach(function (c) {
    var p = pkgs[c], hay = (c + " " + p.name + " " + p.platform + " " + p.category + " " + (p.provider || "")).toLowerCase();
    for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) if (hay.indexOf(words[i]) < 0) return;
    codes.push(c);
  });
  codes.sort(function (a, b) { return topNum_(pkgs[a]) - topNum_(pkgs[b]); });
  svcPut_(ctx.chat, "sm_res_", { q: q, codes: codes.slice(0, 80), total: codes.length });
  tgaSmList_(ctx, 0);
}
function tgaSmList_(ctx, page) {
  var d = svcGet_(ctx.chat, "sm_res_");
  if (!d) { ctx.toast = "ফলাফল পুরনো — আবার সার্চ করুন"; return tgaSmPrompt_(ctx); }
  if (!d.codes.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, "😕 \"" + d.q + "\" — সাইটে কিছু পাওয়া যায়নি।", [[tgaBtn_("🔍 আবার সার্চ", "smq"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var pkgs = tgaSmPkgs_(), meta = getServiceMeta_(), pages = Math.ceil(d.codes.length / SM_PER_);
  page = Math.min(Math.max(0, page || 0), pages - 1);
  var lines = ["🛠 \"" + d.q + "\" — " + d.total + "টি সার্ভিস (সস্তা আগে)", ""], kb = [];
  d.codes.slice(page * SM_PER_, page * SM_PER_ + SM_PER_).forEach(function (c, j) {
    var p = pkgs[c]; if (!p) return;
    var hid = meta.hidden.indexOf(c) >= 0 ? "🙈 " : "";
    lines.push((page * SM_PER_ + j + 1) + ". " + hid + "#" + c + " · " + p.platform + " · $" + topNum_(p) + "\n    " + tgaSmShort_(p.name, 60));
    kb.push([tgaBtn_((page * SM_PER_ + j + 1) + ". " + hid + "#" + c + " · " + tgaSmShort_(p.name, 28), "sm:" + c)]);
  });
  var nav = [];
  if (page > 0) nav.push(tgaBtn_("⬅️", "smp:" + (page - 1)));
  if (page < pages - 1) nav.push(tgaBtn_("➡️", "smp:" + (page + 1)));
  if (nav.length) kb.push(nav);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🔍 নতুন সার্চ", "smq"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}

// ---------- সার্ভিস কার্ড ----------
function tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, note) {
  code = String(code || "").trim();
  var meta = getServiceMeta_(), p = tgaSmPkgs_()[code], rm = meta.removed[code];
  if (!p && !rm) return tgaShow_(ctx, "😕 #" + code + " সাইটে পাওয়া যায়নি।", [[tgaBtn_("🔍 সার্চ", "smq"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var hidden = meta.hidden.indexOf(code) >= 0 || !!rm;
  var name = p ? p.name : (rm.name || ("Service " + code));
  var plat = p ? String(p.platform || "").toLowerCase() : (rm.platform || "");
  var cat = p ? String(p.category || "") : (rm.category || "");
  var catKey = meta.catmove[code];
  if (catKey && catKey.indexOf(plat + "|") === 0) cat = catKey.substring(plat.length + 1) + " (সরানো)";
  var usd = p ? topNum_(p) : 0;
  var cp = customPriceFor_(code), baseSell = cp || (usd ? getSellingPrice_(usd) : 0);
  var fin = usd || cp ? getFinalUnitPrice_(code, usd) : 0;
  var disc = activeDiscounts_(meta)[code];
  var spd = meta.speed[code] || "normal";
  var cfg = getSpeedConfig_(), limit = Number(meta.limits[code]) || 0;
  var st = getSpeedStats_()[code], bad = getBadRecordMap_()[code], dq = getDisqualifiedMap_()[code];
  var exempt = meta.exempt.indexOf(code) >= 0;

  var L = ["⚙️ #" + code + (p && p.provider ? " · " + p.provider : ""), tgaSmShort_(name, 110), "",
    "📂 " + catPlatName_(plat) + " › " + (cat || "?"),
    "💵 প্রোভাইডার $" + usd + "/1K → সাইটে ৳" + Math.round(baseSell * 100) / 100 + "/1K" + (cp ? " (কাস্টম দাম)" : ""),
    disc ? "🏷 ডিসকাউন্ট: " + (disc.type === "fixed" ? "৳" + disc.value + "/1K" : disc.value + "%") + (disc.label ? " · " + disc.label : "") + (disc.until ? " · " + String(disc.until).substring(0, 10) + " পর্যন্ত" : "") + " → ৳" + Math.round(fin * 100) / 100 + "/1K" : "🏷 ডিসকাউন্ট: নেই",
    "Min " + (p ? p.min : "?") + " · Max " + (p ? p.max : "?") + (p && p.refill === "Yes" ? " · ♻️ Refill" : ""),
    "⚡ স্পিড ট্যাগ: " + ({ instant: "Instant", slow: "Slow", normal: "Normal" }[spd] || spd) + " · ⏱ সময়-সীমা: " + (limit || cfg.max_hours) + " ঘণ্টা" + (limit ? "" : " (ডিফল্ট)"),
    "অবস্থা: " + (hidden ? "🙈 সাইটে লুকানো/সরানো" : "✅ সাইটে চালু") + (exempt ? " · 🛡 exempt" : "") + (dq ? " · 🚫 বন্ধ নিশ্চিত" : ""), ""];
  if (st && (st.n || st.fail || st.partial)) {
    L.push("⏱ ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড (শেষ " + st.total + "টি অর্ডার)",
      "মিডিয়ান " + tgaSmDur_(st.med_sec) + " · গড় " + tgaSmDur_(st.avg_sec) + " · সর্বশেষ " + tgaSmDur_(st.last_sec),
      "✅ " + st.n + " সম্পন্ন · ⚠️ " + st.partial + " partial · ❌ " + st.fail + " ব্যর্থ");
    if (bad) L.push("🚩 রেকর্ড খারাপ — " + bad);
  } else {
    L.push("⏱ ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড: এখনও কোনো সম্পন্ন অর্ডার নেই", "(প্রোভাইডারে অর্ডার যোগ + লাইভ ট্র্যাকিং চালু থাকলে সময় গোনা শুরু হয়)");
  }
  svcHistLines_(code).forEach(function (l) { L.push(l); });
  if (note) L.push("", note);

  var c = code, kb = [
    [tgaBtn_("💵 দাম" + (cp ? " ✎" : ""), "sma:" + c + ":pr"), tgaBtn_("🏷 ডিসকাউন্ট" + (disc ? " ✎" : ""), "sma:" + c + ":ds")],
    [tgaBtn_("⚡ স্পিড: " + spd, "sma:" + c + ":sp"), tgaBtn_("⏱ সময়-সীমা", "sma:" + c + ":lm")],
    [tgaBtn_("📂 ক্যাটাগরি সরান", "smk:" + c + ":0"), tgaBtn_("📜 অর্ডার রেকর্ড", "sma:" + c + ":h")]
  ];
  var r4 = [tgaBtn_(exempt ? "🛡 exempt তুলুন" : "🛡 exempt করুন", "sma:" + c + ":ex")];
  if (dq) r4.push(tgaBtn_("↩️ বন্ধ তুলুন", "sma:" + c + ":undq"));
  else if (bad) r4.push(tgaBtn_("🚫 বন্ধ নিশ্চিত করুন", "sma:" + c + ":dq"));
  kb.push(r4);
  kb.push([hidden ? tgaBtn_("♻️ সাইটে ফেরান", "sma:" + c + ":rs") : tgaBtn_("🗑 সাইট থেকে রিমুভ", "sma:" + c + ":rm")]);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "sm:" + c), tgaBtn_("🔍 সার্চ", "smq"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, L.join("\n"), kb);
}

// ---------- কার্ডের অ্যাকশন ----------
function tgaSmAct_(ctx, code, act) {
  var meta = getServiceMeta_(), r;
  var back = [[tgaBtn_("✖ বাতিল", "sm:" + code)]];
  switch (act) {
    case "pr":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "smpr", code: code });
      var kbp = back.slice(); if (meta.prices[code]) kbp.unshift([tgaBtn_("↩️ ডিফল্ট দামে ফিরুন", "sma:" + code + ":prx")]);
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "💵 #" + code + " — নতুন সেলিং প্রাইস লিখুন (৳ প্রতি 1K)\nউদাহরণ: 45.5\nএই দামের ওপরেই ডিসকাউন্ট কাটা হবে।", kbp);
    case "prx":
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "remove_price", code: code });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ ডিফল্ট দামে ফেরানো হয়েছে" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "ds":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "smds", code: code });
      var kbd = back.slice(); if (activeDiscounts_(meta)[code]) kbd.unshift([tgaBtn_("🗑 ডিসকাউন্ট তুলুন", "sma:" + code + ":dsx")]);
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "🏷 #" + code + " — ডিসকাউন্ট লিখুন\n• 10 → ১০%\n• 5tk → ৳৫ প্রতি 1K\n• 10 2026-10-31 Eid → শেষ তারিখ + লেবেল সহ", kbd);
    case "dsx":
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "remove_discount", code: code });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ ডিসকাউন্ট তোলা হয়েছে" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "sp":
      var cur = meta.speed[code] || "normal", nxt = cur === "normal" ? "instant" : (cur === "instant" ? "slow" : "normal");
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "set_speed", code: code, speed: nxt });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ স্পিড ট্যাগ: " + nxt : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "lm":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "smlm", code: code });
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "⏱ #" + code + " — সর্বোচ্চ কত ঘণ্টায় সম্পন্ন হওয়া উচিত? (মিডিয়ান এর বেশি হলে \"রেকর্ড খারাপ\")\nসংখ্যা লিখুন, যেমন: 12\n0 লিখলে ডিফল্ট সীমা (" + getSpeedConfig_().max_hours + " ঘণ্টা)।", back);
    case "ex":
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: meta.exempt.indexOf(code) >= 0 ? "unexempt" : "exempt", code: code });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ আপডেট হয়েছে" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "dq":
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "🚫 #" + code + " — রেকর্ড খারাপ হওয়ায় সাইট থেকে লুকিয়ে অর্ডার বন্ধ করবেন?\n(পরে \"বন্ধ তুলুন\" দিয়ে ফেরানো যাবে)", [[tgaBtn_("✅ হ্যাঁ, বন্ধ করুন", "sma:" + code + ":dqy"), tgaBtn_("✖ না", "sm:" + code)]]);
    case "dqy":
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "dq_confirm", code: code });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "🚫 সার্ভিস বন্ধ নিশ্চিত হয়েছে" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "undq":
      tgaSmCall_({ sub: "dq_clear", code: code });
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "unexempt", code: code });
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "↩️ বন্ধ তোলা হয়েছে (আবার রেকর্ড দেখে বিচার হবে)" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "rm":
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "🗑 #" + code + " সাইট থেকে রিমুভ করবেন?\n(সরানো তালিকায় চিহ্নিত থাকবে — সার্ভিস যোগ করার সময় আবার এড হবে না)", [[tgaBtn_("✅ হ্যাঁ, রিমুভ", "sma:" + code + ":rmy"), tgaBtn_("✖ না", "sm:" + code)]]);
    case "rmy":
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, tgaRemoveCode_(code) ? "🗑 সাইট থেকে সরানো ও চিহ্নিত হয়েছে" : "⚠️ হয়নি");
    case "rs":
      r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "restore_removed", code: code });
      var still = !tgaSmPkgs_()[code];
      return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? (still ? "♻️ চিহ্ন তোলা হয়েছে। এটি কাস্টম সার্ভিস ছিল — সাইটে আনতে ➕ সার্ভিস যোগ থেকে আবার যোগ করুন।" : "♻️ সাইটে ফেরানো হয়েছে") : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
    case "h":
      return tgaSmHistory_(ctx, code);
  }
}

// ---------- ক্যাটাগরি সরানো ----------
function tgaSmCatList_(ctx, code, page) {
  var p = tgaSmPkgs_()[code], meta = getServiceMeta_();
  var plat = p ? String(p.platform || "").toLowerCase() : ((meta.removed[code] || {}).platform || "");
  var list = catEffective_(plat), pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(list.length / SM_PER_));
  page = Math.min(Math.max(0, page || 0), pages - 1);
  var kb = [];
  list.slice(page * SM_PER_, page * SM_PER_ + SM_PER_).forEach(function (c, j) {
    kb.push([tgaBtn_((c.hide ? "🙈 " : "") + tgaCatIcon_(c) + " " + c.name + " (" + c.count + ")", "smc:" + code + ":" + (page * SM_PER_ + j))]);
  });
  var nav = [];
  if (page > 0) nav.push(tgaBtn_("⬅️", "smk:" + code + ":" + (page - 1)));
  if (page < pages - 1) nav.push(tgaBtn_("➡️", "smk:" + code + ":" + (page + 1)));
  if (nav.length) kb.push(nav);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("↩️ মূল ক্যাটাগরিতে ফেরান", "smc:" + code + ":-")]);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("⬅️ ফিরুন", "sm:" + code)]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, "📂 #" + code + " — " + catPlatName_(plat) + " এর কোন ক্যাটাগরিতে দেখাবে?", kb);
}
function tgaSmCatPick_(ctx, code, i) {
  var p = tgaSmPkgs_()[code], plat = p ? String(p.platform || "").toLowerCase() : "";
  var key = "";
  if (i !== "-") { var c = catEffective_(plat)[Number(i)]; if (!c) return tgaSmCatList_(ctx, code, 0); key = c.key; }
  var r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "cat_move", codes: [code], key: key });
  var ok = r.ok && (r.moved || []).length;
  tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, ok ? "✅ ক্যাটাগরি আপডেট হয়েছে" : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি"));
}

// ---------- ইনপুট (টাইপ করা উত্তর) ----------
function tgaSmState_(ctx, st, text) {
  var code = st.code, t = String(text || "").trim(), r;
  if (st.t === "smq") return tgaSmSearch_(ctx, t);
  if (st.t === "smpr") {
    if (/^(0|reset|default|ডিফল্ট)$/i.test(t)) r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "remove_price", code: code });
    else r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "set_price", code: code, price: t });
    return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ দাম আপডেট হয়েছে" : "⚠️ দাম বোঝা যায়নি — শুধু সংখ্যা লিখুন (যেমন 45.5)");
  }
  if (st.t === "smds") {
    if (/^(0|off|no)$/i.test(t)) { r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "remove_discount", code: code }); return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ ডিসকাউন্ট তোলা হয়েছে" : "⚠️ হয়নি"); }
    var a = t.split(/\s+/), v = String(a[0] || "").toLowerCase(), rest = a.slice(1), until = "";
    var type = /tk$/.test(v) ? "fixed" : "percent", value = Number(v.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ""));
    if (rest[0] && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rest[0])) { until = rest[0] + "T23:59:59+06:00"; rest = rest.slice(1); }
    r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "set_discount", code: code, type: type, value: value, until: until, label: rest.join(" ") });
    return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? "✅ ডিসকাউন্ট চালু" : "⚠️ হয়নি — যেমন: 10 অথবা 5tk" + (type === "percent" ? " (% সর্বোচ্চ 90)" : ""));
  }
  if (st.t === "smlm") {
    var h = Number(t.replace(/[^\d.]/g, ""));
    if (isNaN(h)) return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, "⚠️ সংখ্যা লিখুন (যেমন 12)");
    r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "set_limit", code: code, hours: h });
    return tgaSmCard_(ctx, code, r.ok ? (h > 0 ? "✅ সময়-সীমা " + h + " ঘণ্টা" : "✅ ডিফল্ট সীমায় ফেরানো হয়েছে") : "⚠️ হয়নি");
  }
}

// ---------- ডেলিভারি-টাইম রেকর্ড ----------
/** শেষ কয়েকটি অর্ডারের রেকর্ড (SpeedLog শিট থেকে)। */
function tgaSmHistory_(ctx, code) {
  var rows = [];
  try {
    var log = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName(SPEEDLOG_SHEET);
    if (log && log.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var last = log.getLastRow(), cnt = Math.min(last - 1, SPEED_SCAN_ROWS), vals = log.getRange(last - cnt + 1, 1, cnt, 8).getValues();
      for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0 && rows.length < 12; i--) if (String(vals[i][2]).trim() === code) rows.push(vals[i]);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  var L = ["📜 #" + code + " — শেষ " + rows.length + "টি অর্ডারের রেকর্ড", ""];
  if (!rows.length) L.push("এখনও কোনো রেকর্ড নেই।");
  rows.forEach(function (x) {
    var res = String(x[6]), icon = res === "completed" ? "✅" : (res === "partial" ? "⚠️" : "❌");
    L.push(icon + " " + String(tgaStr_(x[0])).substring(0, 16).replace("T", " ") + " · " + x[1] + " · " + (x[4] || "?") + "টি · " + (res === "failed" ? "ব্যর্থ" : tgaSmDur_(Number(x[5]) || 0)) + (x[3] ? " · " + x[3] : ""));
  });
  tgaShow_(ctx, L.join("\n"), [[tgaBtn_("⬅️ সার্ভিস", "sm:" + code), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

/** কোন সার্ভিস কত সময়ে সম্পন্ন হচ্ছে — mode: s = ধীর আগে · f = দ্রুত আগে · b = খারাপ রেকর্ড। */
function tgaSmSpeed_(ctx, mode, page) {
  mode = /^[sfb]$/.test(mode) ? mode : "s";
  var rows = buildSpeedReport_(), cfg = getSpeedConfig_(), pkgs = tgaSmPkgs_();
  var titles = { s: "ধীর আগে", f: "দ্রুত আগে", b: "🚩 খারাপ রেকর্ড" };
  if (mode === "b") rows = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.disqualified || r.confirmed; });
  else if (mode === "f") rows = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.n > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.med_sec - b.med_sec; });
  else rows = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.n > 0; });
  var head = "⏱ ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড — " + titles[mode] + "\n🎯 সীমা " + cfg.max_hours + " ঘণ্টা · কমপক্ষে " + cfg.min_samples + " রেকর্ড · ফেইল > " + cfg.max_fail_pct + "%\n";
  var nav0 = [tgaBtn_((mode === "s" ? "• " : "") + "ধীর", "sr:s:0"), tgaBtn_((mode === "f" ? "• " : "") + "দ্রুত", "sr:f:0"), tgaBtn_((mode === "b" ? "• " : "") + "🚩 খারাপ", "sr:b:0")];
  if (!rows.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, head + "\nকোনো রেকর্ড নেই।\n(প্রোভাইডারে অর্ডার যোগ + লাইভ ট্র্যাকিং চালু থাকা অর্ডার সম্পন্ন হলে এখানে জমা হবে)", [nav0, [tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var pages = Math.ceil(rows.length / SM_PER_);
  page = Math.min(Math.max(0, page || 0), pages - 1);
  var L = [head + "পৃষ্ঠা " + (page + 1) + "/" + pages + " (" + rows.length + "টি সার্ভিস)", ""], kb = [];
  rows.slice(page * SM_PER_, page * SM_PER_ + SM_PER_).forEach(function (r, j) {
    var p = pkgs[r.code];
    L.push((page * SM_PER_ + j + 1) + ". #" + r.code + (p ? " · " + tgaSmShort_(p.name, 34) : "") + "\n    মিডিয়ান " + (r.med_sec ? r.med_label : "—") + " · ✅" + r.n + " ⚠️" + r.partial + " ❌" + r.fail + (r.confirmed ? " · 🚫 বন্ধ" : (r.disqualified ? " · 🚩" : "")));
    kb.push([tgaBtn_("#" + r.code + " · " + (r.med_sec ? r.med_label : "—") + (r.disqualified || r.confirmed ? " 🚩" : ""), "sm:" + r.code)]);
  });
  var nav = [];
  if (page > 0) nav.push(tgaBtn_("⬅️", "sr:" + mode + ":" + (page - 1)));
  if (page < pages - 1) nav.push(tgaBtn_("➡️", "sr:" + mode + ":" + (page + 1)));
  if (nav.length) kb.push(nav);
  kb.push(nav0);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "sr:" + mode + ":" + page), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, L.join("\n"), kb);
}

/** /speedcfg [min_রেকর্ড] [সর্বোচ্চ_ঘণ্টা] [সর্বোচ্চ_ফেইল_%] */
function tgaSpeedCfg_(ctx, args) {
  if (args.length) {
    var r = tgaSmCall_({ sub: "set_config", min_samples: args[0], max_hours: args[1], max_fail_pct: args[2] });
    if (!r.ok) return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "⚠️ হয়নি: " + (r.error || ""));
  }
  var c = getSpeedConfig_();
  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "🎯 ডেলিভারি রেকর্ডের নিয়ম\n• কমপক্ষে " + c.min_samples + "টি রেকর্ড না হলে বিচার হয় না\n• মিডিয়ান সময় > " + c.max_hours + " ঘণ্টা হলে \"রেকর্ড খারাপ\"\n• ফেইল/ক্যান্সেল > " + c.max_fail_pct + "% হলে \"রেকর্ড খারাপ\"\n\nবদলাতে: /speedcfg 5 24 40", [[tgaBtn_("⏱ রেকর্ড দেখুন", "sr:s:0"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

function tgaSmRoute_(ctx, p) {
  switch (p[0]) {
    case "sm": return tgaSmCard_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "sma": return tgaSmAct_(ctx, p[1], p[2]);
    case "smk": return tgaSmCatList_(ctx, p[1], Number(p[2]) || 0);
    case "smc": return tgaSmCatPick_(ctx, p[1], p[2]);
    case "smq": return tgaSmPrompt_(ctx);
    case "smp": return tgaSmList_(ctx, Number(p[1]) || 0);
    case "sr": return tgaSmSpeed_(ctx, p[1], Number(p[2]) || 0);
  }
}

/** কমান্ড-মেনু বদলালে (নতুন ভার্সন নম্বর দিলে) /start বা /menu এ নিজে থেকেই আপডেট হয় — আলাদা Run লাগে না। */
var TG_CMD_VERSION_ = "4";
function tgaCommands_() {
  return [
    { command: "menu", description: "অ্যাডমিন মেনু" },
    { command: "wa", description: "বাছা কার্ডগুলো একসাথে WhatsApp-এ" },
    { command: "stats", description: "আজকের স্ট্যাটস" },
    { command: "pending", description: "Pending অর্ডার" },
    { command: "topups", description: "টপ-আপ রিকোয়েস্ট" },
    { command: "tickets", description: "টিকেট" },
    { command: "addsvc", description: "প্রোভাইডার থেকে সার্ভিস যোগ" },
    { command: "svc", description: "সার্ভিস ম্যানেজ (দাম/ডিসকাউন্ট/রিমুভ)" },
    { command: "speed", description: "ডেলিভারি টাইম রেকর্ড" },
    { command: "cats", description: "ক্যাটাগরি কাস্টমাইজ" },
    { command: "removed", description: "সরানো সার্ভিস" },
    { command: "top", description: "Top Services" },
    { command: "bonus", description: "টপ-আপ বোনাস অফার" },
    { command: "help", description: "সব কমান্ড" }
  ];
}
function tgaEnsureCommands_() {
  try {
    var pr = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    if (pr.getProperty("TG_CMD_VER") === TG_CMD_VERSION_) return;
    tgaApi_("setMyCommands", { commands: tgaCommands_() });
    pr.setProperty("TG_CMD_VER", TG_CMD_VERSION_);
  } catch (e) {}
}

// ---------- Telegram API helpers ----------
function tgaApi_(method, payload) {
  var token = getTelegramBotToken_();
  if (!token) return { ok: false, description: "no_token" };
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://api.telegram.org/bot" + token + "/" + method, {
      method: "post", contentType: "application/json",
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload || {}), muteHttpExceptions: true
    });
    return JSON.parse(resp.getContentText());
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, description: String(e.message || e) }; }
}
function tgaSend_(chatId, text, kb) {
  var p = { chat_id: chatId, text: String(text || "").substring(0, 3900), disable_web_page_preview: true };
  if (kb && kb.length) p.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: kb };
  return tgaApi_("sendMessage", p);
}
/** Edit the tapped message in place (callback) or send a new one (typed message). */
function tgaShow_(ctx, text, kb) {
  if (ctx.msgId) {
    var p = { chat_id: ctx.chat, message_id: ctx.msgId, text: String(text || "").substring(0, 3900), disable_web_page_preview: true };
    if (kb && kb.length) p.reply_markup = { inline_keyboard: kb };
    var r = tgaApi_("editMessageText", p);
    if (r && (r.ok || /not modified/i.test(String(r.description || "")))) return r;
  }
  return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, text, kb);
}
function tgaBtn_(text, data) { return { text: text, callback_data: data }; }

// ---------- internal call helpers ----------
function tgaCall_(fn) {
  try {
    var out = fn();
    var s = (out && out.getContent) ? out.getContent() : JSON.stringify(out);
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: String(e.message || e) }; }
}
function tgaTok_() { return getAdminToken_(); }
function tgaLocked_(fn) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(15000)) return { ok: false, error: "busy" };
  try { return fn(); } finally { try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {} }
}
function tgaStr_(v) {
  if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v.getTime())) {
    try { return Utilities.formatDate(v, "Asia/Dhaka", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"); } catch (e) {}
  }
  return String(v == null ? "" : v);
}
function tgaMoney_(n) { return String(Math.round((Number(n) || 0) * 100) / 100); }

// ---------- conversation state (for "type a number / reply" steps) ----------
function tgaSetState_(chat, st) { CacheService.getScriptCache().put("tga_st_" + chat, JSON.stringify(st), 600); }
function tgaGetState_(chat) {
  var s = CacheService.getScriptCache().get("tga_st_" + chat);
  if (!s) return null;
  try { return JSON.parse(s); } catch (e) { return null; }
}
function tgaClearState_(chat) { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("tga_st_" + chat); }

// ---------- entry point (called from doPost) ----------
function tgaOk_() { return ContentService.createTextOutput("ok"); }

function tgaHandleUpdate_(e, upd) {
  var secret = tgaProp_("TG_WEBHOOK_SECRET");
  if (!secret || !e || !e.parameter || String(e.parameter.k || "") !== secret) return tgaOk_();

  // Telegram may re-deliver an update (Apps Script answers with a redirect) — run each once.
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var dk = "tga_u_" + upd.update_id;
  if (cache.get(dk)) return tgaOk_();
  cache.put(dk, "1", 21600);

  var ctx = { chat: null, from: null, msgId: null, cbId: null, toast: "" };
  try {
    var data = "", text = "";
    if (upd.callback_query) {
      var cb = upd.callback_query;
      ctx.cbId = cb.id; ctx.from = cb.from && cb.from.id;
      ctx.chat = cb.message && cb.message.chat && cb.message.chat.id;
      ctx.msgId = cb.message && cb.message.message_id;
      ctx.cbMsg = cb.message || null;
      data = String(cb.data || "");
    } else if (upd.message) {
      ctx.from = upd.message.from && upd.message.from.id;
      ctx.chat = upd.message.chat && upd.message.chat.id;
      text = String(upd.message.text || "").trim();
    } else { return tgaOk_(); }

    if (!tgaIsAdmin_(ctx.from)) {
      Logger.log("TG admin: unauthorized id " + ctx.from);
      if (ctx.cbId) tgaApi_("answerCallbackQuery", { callback_query_id: ctx.cbId, text: "⛔ অনুমতি নেই", show_alert: true });
      else if (ctx.chat) tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "⛔ এই বট শুধু অ্যাডমিনের জন্য।");
      return tgaOk_();
    }

    if (ctx.cbId) tgaRoute_(ctx, data); else tgaText_(ctx, text);
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log("TG admin error: " + (err && err.message));
    try { if (ctx.chat) tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "⚠️ ত্রুটি: " + String(err && err.message || err).substring(0, 200)); } catch (e2) {}
  } finally {
    if (ctx.cbId) tgaApi_("answerCallbackQuery", ctx.toast ? { callback_query_id: ctx.cbId, text: ctx.toast.substring(0, 190) } : { callback_query_id: ctx.cbId });
  }
  return tgaOk_();
}

function tgaIsAdmin_(fromId) {
  if (!fromId) return false;
  var ids = (tgaProp_("TELEGRAM_ADMIN_IDS") + "," + getTelegramChatId_()).split(",");
  for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) if (ids[i].trim() && ids[i].trim() === String(fromId)) return true;
  return false;
}

// ---------- typed messages ----------
function tgaText_(ctx, text) {
  if (!text) return;
  if (text.charAt(0) === "/") {
    var parts = text.split(/\s+/);
    var cmd = parts[0].toLowerCase().replace(/@.*$/, "");
    var args = parts.slice(1);
    tgaClearState_(ctx.chat);
    return tgaCommand_(ctx, cmd, args, text);
  }
  var st = tgaGetState_(ctx.chat);
  if (st) return tgaState_(ctx, st, text);
  var digits = text.replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
  if (digits.length === 11 && digits.indexOf("01") === 0) return tgaWalletView_(ctx, digits);
  if (/^(PB|WT)-/i.test(text)) return tgaOrderView_(ctx, text.toUpperCase());
  return tgaMenu_(ctx);
}

function tgaCommand_(ctx, cmd, args, full) {
  switch (cmd) {
    case "/start": case "/menu": case "/admin": tgaEnsureCommands_(); return tgaMenu_(ctx);
    case "/help": return tgaHelp_(ctx);
    case "/wa": return tgaWaSel_(ctx, "g");
    case "/cancel": return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "বাতিল।", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
    case "/stats": return tgaStats_(ctx);
    case "/pending": return tgaOrderList_(ctx, "Pending", "o", 0);
    case "/processing": return tgaOrderList_(ctx, "Processing", "o", 0);
    case "/topups": return tgaOrderList_(ctx, "Pending", "t", 0);
    case "/tickets": return tgaTicketList_(ctx, "Open", 0);
    case "/balances": return tgaBalances_(ctx);
    case "/alerts": return tgaAlerts_(ctx);
    case "/wallet":
      if (args[0]) return tgaWalletView_(ctx, args[0]);
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "wallet" });
      return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "👛 কাস্টমারের মোবাইল নম্বর পাঠান (01XXXXXXXXX):");
    case "/order":
      if (args[0]) return tgaOrderView_(ctx, args[0].toUpperCase());
      return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "ব্যবহার: /order 63284");
    case "/find":
      if (args.length) return tgaFind_(ctx, args.join(" "));
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "find" });
      return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "🔍 সার্চ করুন — অর্ডার আইডি / মোবাইল / TrxID / সার্ভিস কোড / লিংক:");
    case "/add": case "/deduct": return tgaAdjustCmd_(ctx, cmd === "/add" ? 1 : -1, args);
    case "/addsvc": case "/add_service": return args.length ? tgaSvcSearch_(ctx, args.join(" ")) : tgaSvcPrompt_(ctx);
    case "/top": return tgaTops_(ctx);
    case "/remove": return tgaRemoveCmd_(ctx, args);
    case "/removed": return tgaRemovedList_(ctx, 0);
    case "/cats": case "/categories": return tgaCats_(ctx);
    case "/svc": case "/manage": case "/service": return args.length ? tgaSmSearch_(ctx, args.join(" ")) : tgaSmPrompt_(ctx);
    case "/speed": case "/delivery": return args.length && /^\d+$/.test(args[0]) ? tgaSmCard_(ctx, args[0]) : tgaSmSpeed_(ctx, "s", 0);
    case "/speedcfg": return tgaSpeedCfg_(ctx, args);
    case "/bonus": return tgaBonusCmd_(ctx, args);
    case "/discounts": return tgaDiscounts_(ctx);
    case "/discount": return tgaDiscountSet_(ctx, args);
    case "/nodiscount": return tgaDiscountRemove_(ctx, args);
    default: return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "অচেনা কমান্ড। /help দেখুন।", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  }
}

  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, [
    "🛠 কমান্ড তালিকা",
    "/menu — মেইন মেনু",

    "/order 63284 — একটা অর্ডার খুলুন (আইডি সরাসরি পাঠালেও হয়)",
    "/find কিওয়ার্ড — সার্চ",
    "/tickets — সাপোর্ট টিকেট",
    "/wallet 01XXXXXXXXX — ওয়ালেট (নম্বর সরাসরি পাঠালেও হয়)",
    "/add 01XXXXXXXXX 50 কারণ — ব্যালেন্স যোগ",
    "/deduct 01XXXXXXXXX 50 কারণ — ব্যালেন্স কাটা",
    "/balances — প্রোভাইডার ব্যালেন্স",
    "/alerts — অ্যালার্ট",
    "/bonus — টপ-আপ বোনাস দেখুন · /bonus 500 5 1000 10 (৳500+ এ ৫%, ৳1000+ এ ১০%) · /bonus off · /bonus on",
    "/discounts — চালু ডিসকাউন্ট",
    "/discount CODE 10 [2026-10-31] [লেবেল] — % ডিসকাউন্ট (৳ হলে 5tk)",
    "/nodiscount CODE — ডিসকাউন্ট তুলুন",
    "/addsvc কিওয়ার্ড — প্রোভাইডারে সার্চ করে সাইটে সার্ভিস যোগ (যেমন: /addsvc facebook followers @smmrx)",
    "/top — প্রতিটি প্ল্যাটফর্মের Top Services সাজান",
    "/remove CODE — সার্ভিস সাইট থেকে সরান ও চিহ্নিত রাখুন (আবার এড হবে না)",
    "/removed — সরানো সার্ভিসের তালিকা (চাইলে ফেরানো যায়)",
    "/cats — ক্যাটাগরি কাস্টমাইজ (নাম বদল, যোগ, রিমুভ, আইকন, ক্রম)",
    "/svc CODE বা কিওয়ার্ড — সাইটের সার্ভিস ম্যানেজ: দাম, ডিসকাউন্ট, স্পিড ট্যাগ, সময়-সীমা, ক্যাটাগরি, রিমুভ/ফেরানো",
    "/speed — কোন সার্ভিস কত সময়ে সম্পন্ন হচ্ছে (ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড) · /speed CODE — একটা সার্ভিসের রেকর্ড",
    "/speedcfg 5 24 40 — রেকর্ড-খারাপ নিয়ম (min রেকর্ড · সর্বোচ্চ ঘণ্টা · ফেইল %)",
    "/cancel — চলমান ধাপ বাতিল"
  ].join("\n"), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

function tgaState_(ctx, st, text) {
  tgaClearState_(ctx.chat);
  if (st.t === "find") return tgaFind_(ctx, text);
  if (st.t === "wallet") return tgaWalletView_(ctx, text);
  if (st.t === "adj") return tgaAdjustCmd_(ctx, st.sign, [st.mobile].concat(text.split(/\s+/)));
  if (st.t === "treply") return tgaTicketReplySend_(ctx, st.id, text);
  if (st.t === "svq" || st.t === "svcat") return tgaSvcState_(ctx, st, text);
  if (/^sm(q|pr|ds|lm)$/.test(st.t)) return tgaSmState_(ctx, st, text);
  if (st.t === "tpadd") return tgaTopState_(ctx, st, text);
  if (/^cg(name|ico|move|add)$/.test(st.t)) return tgaCatState_(ctx, st, text);
}

// ---------- main menu ----------
function tgaMenuKb_() {
  return [
    [tgaBtn_("📊 স্ট্যাটস", "st"), tgaBtn_("🔔 অ্যালার্ট", "al")],
    [tgaBtn_("🕐 Pending অর্ডার", "ol:Pending:o:0"), tgaBtn_("💰 টপ-আপ রিকোয়েস্ট", "ol:Pending:t:0")],
    [tgaBtn_("⚙️ Processing", "ol:Processing:o:0"), tgaBtn_("⚠️ Partial", "ol:Partial:o:0")],
    [tgaBtn_("🎫 টিকেট", "tl:Open:0"), tgaBtn_("👛 ওয়ালেট", "w")],
    [tgaBtn_("🏦 প্রোভাইডার ব্যালেন্স", "bal"), tgaBtn_("🔍 সার্চ", "fs")],
    [tgaBtn_("🗂 ক্যাটাগরি কাস্টমাইজ", "cg"), tgaBtn_("🚫 সরানো সার্ভিস", "rv:0")],
    [tgaBtn_("🏆 Top Services", "tp"), tgaBtn_("➕ সার্ভিস যোগ (সার্চ)", "sv")],
    [tgaBtn_("🛠 সার্ভিস ম্যানেজ", "smq"), tgaBtn_("⏱ ডেলিভারি রেকর্ড", "sr:s:0")],
    [tgaBtn_("📲 বাছা কার্ড WhatsApp-এ পাঠান", "ws:g")]
  ];

function tgaMenu_(ctx) { tgaShow_(ctx, "🛠 boostplus Admin — কী করবেন?", tgaMenuKb_()); }

// ---------- callback router ----------
// ---------- একসাথে একাধিক কার্ড বেছে WhatsApp-এ পাঠানো ----------
// প্রতিটা অর্ডার কার্ডে "☐ বাছুন" → বাছা কার্ডের লেখা ক্যাশে জমে (৬ ঘণ্টা) → /wa বা মেনুর বাটনে সব মিলিয়ে WhatsApp বাটন আসে।
// wa.me লিংকের দৈর্ঘ্যের সীমার কারণে বেশি হলে ভাগ ভাগ করে (১/২, ২/২ …) দেওয়া হয়।
var WA_SEL_MAX_ = 30, WA_URL_LIM_ = 1800, WA_SEP_ = "\n\n━━━━━━━━━━━━━━\n\n";
function waSelGet_(chat) { try { var v = CacheService.getScriptCache().get("wa_sel_" + chat); return v ? JSON.parse(v) : []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
function waSelPut_(chat, a) { try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put("wa_sel_" + chat, JSON.stringify(a), 21600); } catch (e) {} }
/** কার্ডের মূল লেখা: মেসেজের "WhatsApp এ ফরোয়ার্ড" বাটনের লিংক থেকেই বের করা হয় (টেইল/প্রোভাইডার তথ্য বাদ থাকে)। */
function waCardFromMsg_(msg) {
  try {
    var kb = (msg && msg.reply_markup && msg.reply_markup.inline_keyboard) || [];
    for (var r = 0; r < kb.length; r++) for (var c = 0; c < kb[r].length; c++) {
      var u = kb[r][c].url;
      if (u && u.indexOf("https://wa.me/?text=") === 0) return decodeURIComponent(u.substring(u.indexOf("?text=") + 6));
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return "";
}
function tgaWaSel_(ctx, op) {
  if (op === "c") {
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().remove("wa_sel_" + ctx.chat); } catch (e) {}
    ctx.toast = "🗑 বাছাই মুছে গেছে";
    return tgaShow_(ctx, "🗑 বাছাই মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  }
  if (op === "g") return tgaWaSend_(ctx);
  // op === "t": এই কার্ড বাছা/বাদ
  var msg = ctx.cbMsg, text = waCardFromMsg_(msg);
  // আগে পাঠানো (✔ চিহ্ন বসা) কার্ড আর বাছা যাবে না
  try {
    var kb0 = (msg && msg.reply_markup && msg.reply_markup.inline_keyboard) || [];
    for (var r0 = 0; r0 < kb0.length; r0++) for (var c0 = 0; c0 < kb0[r0].length; c0++) {
      var b0 = kb0[r0][c0];
      if (b0.callback_data === "ws:t" && String(b0.text || "").indexOf("✔") === 0) { ctx.toast = "✔ এটা আগেই পাঠানো হয়েছে — আবার বাছা যাবে না"; return; }
    }
  } catch (eS0) {}
  if (!text) { ctx.toast = "⚠️ কার্ড পড়া যায়নি (খুব পুরনো মেসেজ?)"; return; }
  var sel = waSelGet_(ctx.chat), idx = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < sel.length; i++) if (String(sel[i].m) === String(ctx.msgId)) { idx = i; break; }
  var on;
  if (idx >= 0) { sel.splice(idx, 1); on = false; }
  else {
    if (sel.length >= WA_SEL_MAX_) { ctx.toast = "সর্বোচ্চ " + WA_SEL_MAX_ + "টি বাছা যায় — আগে /wa দিয়ে পাঠান"; return; }
    sel.push({ m: ctx.msgId, t: text, o: waOidFromMsg_(msg) }); on = true;
  }
  waSelPut_(ctx.chat, sel);
  // এই মেসেজের কিবোর্ড আপডেট: বাটনের চিহ্ন + (বাছা থাকলে) পাঠানোর বাটন
  try {
    var kb = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(msg.reply_markup.inline_keyboard)), out = [];
    kb.forEach(function (row) {
      if (row.length === 1 && row[0].callback_data === "ws:g") return;
      row.forEach(function (b) { if (b.callback_data === "ws:t") b.text = waSelBtn_(on).text; });
      out.push(row);
    });
    if (on) out.push([tgaBtn_("📲 বাছা সবগুলো WhatsApp-এ পাঠান", "ws:g")]);
    tgaApi_("editMessageReplyMarkup", { chat_id: ctx.chat, message_id: ctx.msgId, reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: out } });
  } catch (e) {}
  ctx.toast = (on ? "☑ বাছা হয়েছে" : "☐ বাদ দেওয়া হয়েছে") + " — মোট " + sel.length + "টি। পাঠাতে /wa";
}
function tgaWaSend_(ctx) {
  var sel = waSelGet_(ctx.chat);
  if (!sel.length) {
    ctx.toast = "কিছু বাছা নেই — কার্ডের নিচে ☐ বাছুন চাপুন";
    if (!ctx.cbId) tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "কিছু বাছা নেই। অর্ডার কার্ডের নিচের ☐ বাছুন বাটন চেপে কার্ড বাছুন, তারপর /wa দিন।", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
    return;
  }
  var sepLen = encodeURIComponent(WA_SEP_).length, batches = [], cur = [], curLen = 0;
  sel.forEach(function (x) {
    var t = String(x.t || ""), el = encodeURIComponent(t).length;
    if (cur.length && curLen + sepLen + el > WA_URL_LIM_) { batches.push(cur); cur = []; curLen = 0; }
    curLen += (cur.length ? sepLen : 0) + el; cur.push(t);
  });
  if (cur.length) batches.push(cur);
  var kb = [], tooLong = false;
  batches.forEach(function (b, i) {
    var url = "https://wa.me/?text=" + encodeURIComponent(b.join(WA_SEP_));
    if (url.length > 2000) { tooLong = true; return; }   // টেলিগ্রামের URL সীমা পার — নিচের কপি-মেসেজ থেকে নিন
    kb.push([{ text: "📲 WhatsApp এ পাঠান" + (batches.length > 1 ? " (" + (i + 1) + "/" + batches.length + ")" : "") + " · " + b.length + "টি", url: url }]);
  });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🗑 বাছাই মুছুন", "ws:c"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  var head = "📲 " + sel.length + "টি কার্ড বাছা আছে" + (batches.length > 1 ? " — লিংকের সীমার কারণে " + batches.length + " ভাগে। প্রতিটা বাটন চেপে WhatsApp-এ পাঠান।" : " — নিচের বাটন চাপলে সবগুলো একসাথে WhatsApp-এ যাবে।") + (tooLong ? "\n⚠️ কিছু কার্ড বড় — নিচের লেখা কপি করে WhatsApp-এ পেস্ট করুন।" : "");
  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, head, kb);
  // কপি করার জন্য পুরো লেখা (বাটন কাজ না করলেও চলবে)
  var all = batches.map(function (b) { return b.join(WA_SEP_); });
  var joined = all.join(WA_SEP_);
  if (joined.length <= 3800) tgaSend_(ctx.chat, joined);
  else all.forEach(function (t) { tgaSend_(ctx.chat, t); });
  // পাঠানো-চিহ্ন: বাছা কার্ডগুলোর বাটন "✔ পাঠানো হয়েছে" হয়; বাছাই খালি হয় (পরের নতুন কার্ড আবার অটো-সিলেক্টেড হবে)
  sel.forEach(function (x) {
    try { tgaApi_("editMessageReplyMarkup", { chat_id: ctx.chat, message_id: x.m, reply_markup: { inline_keyboard: waKb_(x.t, x.o, "sent") } }); Utilities.sleep(60); } catch (eS) {}
  });
  waSelPut_(ctx.chat, []);
  ctx.toast = "✔ " + sel.length + "টি কার্ড তৈরি — কার্ডে ✔ পাঠানো হয়েছে চিহ্ন বসেছে";
}

function tgaRoute_(ctx, d) {
  var p = d.split(":");
  var k = p[0];
  switch (k) {
    case "m": tgaClearState_(ctx.chat); return tgaMenu_(ctx);
    case "ws": return tgaWaSel_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "st": return tgaStats_(ctx);
    case "al": return tgaAlerts_(ctx);
    case "alc": return tgaAlertsClear_(ctx);
    case "bal": return tgaBalances_(ctx);
    case "ol": return tgaOrderList_(ctx, p[1], p[2], Number(p[3]) || 0);
    case "o": return tgaOrderView_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "os": return tgaOrderAskStatus_(ctx, p[1], p[2]);
    case "osy": return tgaOrderSetStatus_(ctx, p[1], p[2]);
    case "of": return tgaOrderRetry_(ctx, p[1], false);
    case "ofy": return tgaOrderRetry_(ctx, p[1], true);
    case "fs":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "find" });
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "🔍 সার্চ করুন — অর্ডার আইডি / মোবাইল / TrxID / সার্ভিস কোড / লিংক লিখে পাঠান:", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
    case "tl": return tgaTicketList_(ctx, p[1], Number(p[2]) || 0);
    case "t": return tgaTicketView_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "tr":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "treply", id: p[1] });
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "✉️ " + p[1] + " এর রিপ্লাই লিখে পাঠান (কাস্টমার সাইটে দেখবে):", [[tgaBtn_("✖ বাতিল", "t:" + p[1])]]);
    case "ts": return tgaTicketStatus_(ctx, p[1], p[2]);
    case "w": return p[1] ? tgaWalletView_(ctx, p[1]) : tgaWalletMenu_(ctx);
    case "wf":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "wallet" });
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "👛 কাস্টমারের মোবাইল নম্বর পাঠান (01XXXXXXXXX):", [[tgaBtn_("✖ বাতিল", "w")]]);
    case "wu": return tgaWalletUsers_(ctx);
    case "wp": return tgaPinRequests_(ctx);
    case "wd": return tgaPinDismiss_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "wh": return tgaWalletHistory_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "wa":
      tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "adj", mobile: p[1], sign: p[2] === "-" ? -1 : 1 });
      return tgaShow_(ctx, (p[2] === "-" ? "➖ কাটা" : "➕ যোগ") + " — " + p[1] + "\nলিখুন: পরিমাণ + কারণ\nউদাহরণ: 50 bonus", [[tgaBtn_("✖ বাতিল", "w:" + p[1])]]);
    case "wyc": return tgaAdjustConfirm_(ctx);
    case "wr":
      return tgaShow_(ctx, "🔑 " + p[1] + " এর PIN রিসেট করবেন? নতুন র‍্যান্ডম PIN এখানে দেখানো হবে।", [[tgaBtn_("✅ হ্যাঁ, রিসেট", "wry:" + p[1]), tgaBtn_("✖ না", "w:" + p[1])]]);
    case "wry": return tgaPinReset_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "dr": return tgaDiscountRemove_(ctx, [p[1]]);
    case "sv": case "svp": case "sva": case "sva2": case "svy": case "svf": case "svr": case "svt": case "svl": case "svs": case "svk": case "svc": case "svn":
      return tgaSvcRoute_(ctx, p);
    case "sm": case "sma": case "smk": case "smc": case "smq": case "smp": case "sr":
      return tgaSmRoute_(ctx, p);
    case "tp": case "tpp": case "tps": case "tpu": case "tpd": case "tpr": case "tpa": return tgaTopRoute_(ctx, p);
    case "rv": return tgaRemovedList_(ctx, Number(p[1]) || 0);
    case "rvy": return tgaRemovedRestore_(ctx, p[1]);
    case "cg": case "cgp": case "cgc": case "cgn": case "cgi": case "cgis": case "cgir": case "cgie": case "cgv": case "cgu": case "cgd":
    case "cgx": case "cgxy": case "cgr": case "cgm": case "cga":
      return tgaCatRoute_(ctx, p);
    default: ctx.toast = "অচেনা বাটন"; return;
  }
}

// ---------- stats ----------
function tgaStats_(ctx) {
  var sh = sheet_(); ensureHeader_(sh);
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminStats_(sh, { token: tgaTok_() }); });
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ স্ট্যাটস আনা যায়নি: " + (r.error || ""), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var c = r.counts, t = r.today, tp = r.topup_today || {};
  var txt = "📊 আজ (" + t.date + ")\n" +
    "🛒 অর্ডার: " + t.orders + " (accepted " + t.accepted + " · completed " + t.completed + ")\n" +
    "👤 কাস্টমার বিল: ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.customer) + "\n" +
    "🏭 প্রোভাইডার খরচ: ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.cost) + "\n" +
    "📈 লাভ: ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.profit) + (t.cost_missing ? "  ⚠️ " + t.cost_missing + "টির দাম পাওয়া যায়নি" : "") + "\n" +
    "✅ শুধু Completed: ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.completed_customer) + " − ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.completed_cost) + " = ৳" + tgaMoney_(t.completed_profit) + "\n" +
    "━━━━━━━━\n" +
    "💰 টপ-আপ (আলাদা): ক্রেডিট ৳" + tgaMoney_(tp.credited_amount || 0) + " · Pending " + (tp.pending || 0) + " (৳" + tgaMoney_(tp.pending_amount || 0) + ")\n" +
    "━━━━━━━━\n" +
    "🕐 Pending " + c.Pending + " · ⚙️ Processing " + c.Processing + "\n" +
    "⚠️ Partial " + c.Partial + " · ✅ Completed " + c.Completed + "\n" +
    "❌ Rejected " + c.Rejected + " · মোট অর্ডার " + c.total;
  tgaShow_(ctx, txt, [
    [tgaBtn_("🕐 Pending (" + c.Pending + ")", "ol:Pending:o:0"), tgaBtn_("⚙️ Processing (" + c.Processing + ")", "ol:Processing:o:0")],
    [tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "st"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]
  ]);
}

// ---------- orders ----------
function tgaOrderLabel_(o) {
  var isTop = String(o.service_code || "").toUpperCase() === "TOPUP";
  var s = (isTop ? "💰 ৳" + tgaMoney_(o.amount) + " টপ-আপ" : "৳" + tgaMoney_(o.amount) + " " + String(o.service_code || "")) + " · " + String(o.whatsapp || "").slice(-11);
  return s.substring(0, 60);
}
function tgaFetchOrders_(status, q) {
  var sh = sheet_(); ensureHeader_(sh);
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminList_(sh, tgaTok_(), status, q ? { q: q } : {}); });
  return r;
}
function tgaOrderList_(ctx, status, kind, page) {
  var r = tgaFetchOrders_(status);
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ লিস্ট আনা যায়নি: " + (r.error || ""), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var list = (r.orders || []).filter(function (o) {
    var isTop = String(o.service_code || "").toUpperCase() === "TOPUP";
    return kind === "t" ? isTop : !isTop;
  });
  var title = (kind === "t" ? "💰 টপ-আপ" : "🛒 অর্ডার") + " · " + status;
  if (!list.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, title + "\n\nকিছু নেই ✅", [[tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "ol:" + status + ":" + kind + ":0"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var pages = Math.ceil(list.length / TGA_PAGE);
  if (page >= pages) page = pages - 1;
  var slice = list.slice(page * TGA_PAGE, page * TGA_PAGE + TGA_PAGE);
  var lines = [title + " (" + list.length + ") — পৃষ্ঠা " + (page + 1) + "/" + pages, ""];
  var kb = [];
  slice.forEach(function (o, i) {
    lines.push((page * TGA_PAGE + i + 1) + ". " + o.order_id + "\n   " + tgaOrderLabel_(o) + (o.paid_from ? " · " + o.paid_from : ""));
    kb.push([tgaBtn_((page * TGA_PAGE + i + 1) + ". " + tgaOrderLabel_(o), "o:" + o.order_id)]);
  });
  var nav = [];
  if (page > 0) nav.push(tgaBtn_("◀️", "ol:" + status + ":" + kind + ":" + (page - 1)));
  nav.push(tgaBtn_("🔄", "ol:" + status + ":" + kind + ":" + page));
  if (page < pages - 1) nav.push(tgaBtn_("▶️", "ol:" + status + ":" + kind + ":" + (page + 1)));
  kb.push(nav);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
function tgaFind_(ctx, q) {
  var r = tgaFetchOrders_("All", q);
  if (!r.ok) return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "⚠️ সার্চ ব্যর্থ: " + (r.error || ""));
  var list = (r.orders || []).slice(0, 10);
  if (!list.length) return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "🔍 \"" + q + "\" — কিছু পাওয়া যায়নি।", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var kb = list.map(function (o) { return [tgaBtn_(o.status + " · " + tgaOrderLabel_(o), "o:" + o.order_id)]; });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "🔍 \"" + q + "\" — " + list.length + "টি ফলাফল:", kb);
}
function tgaFindOne_(orderId) {
  var sh = sheet_(); ensureHeader_(sh);
  var row = findRowByOrderId_(sh, orderId);
  if (row === -1) return null;
  return { sh: sh, row: row, o: rowToObject_(sh, row) };
}
function tgaOrderView_(ctx, orderId, prefix) {
  var f = tgaFindOne_(orderId);
  if (!f) return tgaShow_(ctx, "❌ অর্ডার পাওয়া যায়নি: " + orderId, [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var o = f.o;
  var isTop = String(o.service_code || "").toUpperCase() === "TOPUP";
  var st = String(o.status || "").trim();
  var lines = [];
  if (prefix) lines.push(prefix, "");
  lines.push((isTop ? "💰 টপ-আপ" : "🛒 অর্ডার") + " · " + st);
  lines.push("ID: " + o.order_id);
  if (!isTop) {
    lines.push("সার্ভিস: " + o.service_code + (o.service_name ? " — " + String(o.service_name).substring(0, 60) : ""));
    if (o.link) lines.push("লিংক: " + String(o.link).substring(0, 150));
    lines.push("পরিমাণ: " + o.quantity);
  }
  lines.push("টাকা: ৳" + tgaMoney_(o.amount));
  lines.push("পেমেন্ট: " + (o.paid_from || "—") + (o.txn_id ? " · " + o.txn_id : ""));
  lines.push("মোবাইল: " + o.whatsapp);
  lines.push("সময়: " + tgaStr_(o.created_at));
  if (o.provider_order_id) lines.push("প্রোভাইডার অর্ডার: " + o.provider_order_id);
  var note = String(o.note || "").trim();
  if (note) lines.push("নোট: " + note.substring(Math.max(0, note.length - 220)));
  var kb = [];
  if (st === "Pending") {
    kb.push([tgaBtn_(isTop ? "✅ ওয়ালেটে ক্রেডিট" : "✅ অ্যাপ্রুভ", "os:" + orderId + ":P"), tgaBtn_("❌ রিজেক্ট", "os:" + orderId + ":R")]);
  } else if (st === "Processing" || st === "Partial") {
    kb.push([tgaBtn_("✔️ Completed", "os:" + orderId + ":C"), tgaBtn_("❌ রিজেক্ট", "os:" + orderId + ":R")]);
    if (!isTop) kb.push([tgaBtn_("🔁 প্রোভাইডারে রিট্রাই", "of:" + orderId)]);
  }
  var m = normalizeMobile_(o.whatsapp);   // 11-digit mobile or a User name
  var row3 = [tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "o:" + orderId)];
  if (m && (m.length === 11 || (/[^0-9]/.test(m) && m.length <= 20))) row3.unshift(tgaBtn_("👛 ওয়ালেট", "w:" + m));
  kb.push(row3);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
var TGA_ST = { P: "Processing", R: "Rejected", C: "Completed" };
function tgaOrderAskStatus_(ctx, orderId, code) {
  if (code === "P") return tgaOrderSetStatus_(ctx, orderId, code);
  var what = code === "R" ? "❌ রিজেক্ট (ওয়ালেট-পেইড হলে অটো-রিফান্ড)" : "✔️ Completed";
  tgaShow_(ctx, what + " করবেন?\n" + orderId, [[tgaBtn_("✅ হ্যাঁ", "osy:" + orderId + ":" + code), tgaBtn_("✖ না", "o:" + orderId)]]);
}
function tgaOrderSetStatus_(ctx, orderId, code) {
  var status = TGA_ST[code];
  if (!status) { ctx.toast = "ভুল স্ট্যাটাস"; return; }
  var sh = sheet_(); ensureHeader_(sh);
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () {
    return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminUpdate_(sh, { token: tgaTok_(), order_id: orderId, status: status, note: "via Telegram" }); });
  });
  var msg;
  if (r.ok) {
    msg = "✅ " + (r.status || status);
    if (r.refunded) msg += " · ৳" + tgaMoney_(r.refunded) + " ওয়ালেটে রিফান্ড";
    if (r.topup && r.topup.balance != null) msg += " · নতুন ব্যালেন্স ৳" + tgaMoney_(r.topup.balance);
    if (r.refund_error) msg += " · ⚠️ রিফান্ড ত্রুটি: " + r.refund_error;
  } else {
    msg = "⚠️ হয়নি: " + (r.error || "unknown");
  }
  ctx.toast = msg;
  tgaOrderView_(ctx, orderId, msg);
}
function tgaOrderRetry_(ctx, orderId, force) {
  var sh = sheet_(); ensureHeader_(sh);
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () {
    return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminRetryForward_(sh, { token: tgaTok_(), order_id: orderId, force: force }); });
  });
  if (!r.ok && r.error === "already_forwarded") {
    return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ ইতিমধ্যে প্রোভাইডার অর্ডার আছে: " + (r.provider_order_id || "") + "\nজোর করে আবার পাঠালে ডুপ্লিকেট অর্ডার হতে পারে।",
      [[tgaBtn_("⚠️ তবুও পাঠান", "ofy:" + orderId), tgaBtn_("⬅️ ফিরুন", "o:" + orderId)]]);
  }
  var msg = r.ok ? ("🔁 প্রোভাইডারে পাঠানো হয়েছে" + (r.provider_order_id ? " · PO " + r.provider_order_id : "")) : ("⚠️ হয়নি: " + (r.error || r.message || "unknown"));
  ctx.toast = msg;
  tgaOrderView_(ctx, orderId, msg);
}

// ---------- tickets ----------
function tgaTicketList_(ctx, status, page) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminTicketList_({ token: tgaTok_(), status: status }); });
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ টিকেট আনা যায়নি: " + (r.error || ""), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var list = r.tickets || [];
  var tabs = [tgaBtn_(status === "Open" ? "• Open" : "Open", "tl:Open:0"), tgaBtn_(status === "In Progress" ? "• In Progress" : "In Progress", "tl:In Progress:0"), tgaBtn_(status === "Resolved" ? "• Resolved" : "Resolved", "tl:Resolved:0")];
  if (!list.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, "🎫 টিকেট · " + status + "\n\nকিছু নেই ✅", [tabs, [tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var pages = Math.ceil(list.length / TGA_PAGE);
  if (page >= pages) page = pages - 1;
  var slice = list.slice(page * TGA_PAGE, page * TGA_PAGE + TGA_PAGE);
  var lines = ["🎫 টিকেট · " + status + " (" + list.length + ") — পৃষ্ঠা " + (page + 1) + "/" + pages, ""];
  var kb = [tabs];
  slice.forEach(function (t) {
    lines.push(t.ticket_id + " · " + t.category + (t.subcategory ? "/" + t.subcategory : "") + "\n   " + String(t.message || "").substring(0, 70).replace(/\n/g, " "));
    kb.push([tgaBtn_(t.ticket_id + " · " + (t.subcategory || t.category) + " · " + String(t.mobile || "").slice(-11), "t:" + t.ticket_id)]);
  });
  var nav = [];
  if (page > 0) nav.push(tgaBtn_("◀️", "tl:" + status + ":" + (page - 1)));
  if (page < pages - 1) nav.push(tgaBtn_("▶️", "tl:" + status + ":" + (page + 1)));
  if (nav.length) kb.push(nav);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
function tgaTicketGet_(id) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminTicketList_({ token: tgaTok_(), status: "All" }); });
  var list = (r && r.tickets) || [];
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (String(list[i].ticket_id).toUpperCase() === String(id).toUpperCase()) return list[i];
  return null;
}
function tgaTicketView_(ctx, id, prefix) {
  var t = tgaTicketGet_(id);
  if (!t) return tgaShow_(ctx, "❌ টিকেট পাওয়া যায়নি: " + id, [[tgaBtn_("🎫 টিকেট লিস্ট", "tl:Open:0")]]);
  var lines = [];
  if (prefix) lines.push(prefix, "");
  lines.push("🎫 " + t.ticket_id + " · " + t.status);
  lines.push(t.category + (t.subcategory ? " / " + t.subcategory : ""));
  if (t.order_id) lines.push("অর্ডার: " + t.order_id);
  lines.push("মোবাইল: " + t.mobile);
  lines.push("সময়: " + String(t.created_at).substring(0, 16));
  lines.push("", String(t.message || "").substring(0, 700));
  (t.attachments || []).forEach(function (a, i) { if (a) lines.push("📎 " + a); });
  if (t.admin_reply) lines.push("", "↩️ আপনার রিপ্লাই: " + String(t.admin_reply).substring(0, 400));
  var kb = [[tgaBtn_("✉️ রিপ্লাই", "tr:" + id)],
    [tgaBtn_("🔧 In Progress", "ts:" + id + ":I"), tgaBtn_("✅ Resolved", "ts:" + id + ":R"), tgaBtn_("🔒 Closed", "ts:" + id + ":C")]];
  if (t.order_id) kb.push([tgaBtn_("🛒 অর্ডার খুলুন", "o:" + t.order_id)]);
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🎫 লিস্ট", "tl:Open:0"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
var TGA_TST = { I: "In Progress", R: "Resolved", C: "Closed" };
function tgaTicketStatus_(ctx, id, code) {
  var st = TGA_TST[code];
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () { return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminTicketReply_({ token: tgaTok_(), ticket_id: id, status: st }); }); });
  ctx.toast = r.ok ? "✅ " + st : "⚠️ " + (r.error || "হয়নি");
  tgaTicketView_(ctx, id, ctx.toast);
}
function tgaTicketReplySend_(ctx, id, text) {
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () { return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminTicketReply_({ token: tgaTok_(), ticket_id: id, reply: text }); }); });
  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, r.ok ? "✅ রিপ্লাই সেভ হয়েছে (" + id + ")" : "⚠️ রিপ্লাই হয়নি: " + (r.error || ""),
    [[tgaBtn_("🎫 টিকেট খুলুন", "t:" + id), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

// ---------- wallet ----------
function tgaWalletMenu_(ctx) {
  tgaShow_(ctx, "👛 ওয়ালেট", [
    [tgaBtn_("🔍 নম্বর দিয়ে খুঁজুন", "wf")],
    [tgaBtn_("🔑 PIN রিকভারি রিকোয়েস্ট", "wp"), tgaBtn_("👥 ইউজার লিস্ট", "wu")],
    [tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]
  ]);
}
function tgaWalletView_(ctx, mobileRaw, prefix) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(mobileRaw);
  if (!m) return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "⚠️ User name বা মোবাইল নম্বর দিন।");
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "get", mobile: m }); });
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ " + (r.error || "ত্রুটি"), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var lines = [];
  if (prefix) lines.push(prefix, "");
  lines.push("👛 " + m, r.exists ? "ব্যালেন্স: ৳" + tgaMoney_(r.balance) : "এই নম্বরে ওয়ালেট অ্যাকাউন্ট নেই (ব্যালেন্স ৳0)");
  var led = r.ledger || [];
  if (led.length) {
    lines.push("", "সর্বশেষ লেনদেন:");
    led.slice(0, 5).forEach(function (l) {
      var a = Number(l.amount) || 0;
      lines.push((a >= 0 ? "+" : "") + tgaMoney_(a) + " → ৳" + tgaMoney_(l.balance_after) + " · " + l.type + (l.reason ? " · " + String(l.reason).substring(0, 30) : ""));
    });
  }
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), [
    [tgaBtn_("➕ যোগ", "wa:" + m + ":+"), tgaBtn_("➖ কাটুন", "wa:" + m + ":-")],
    [tgaBtn_("🔑 PIN রিসেট", "wr:" + m), tgaBtn_("📜 হিস্ট্রি", "wh:" + m)],
    [tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "w:" + m), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]
  ]);
}
function tgaWalletHistory_(ctx, m) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "customer_history", mobile: m, limit: 8 }); });
  var lines = ["📜 " + m + " — হিস্ট্রি", ""];
  var led = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "list_ledger", mobile: m, limit: 12 }); }).ledger || [];
  led.forEach(function (l) {
    var a = Number(l.amount) || 0;
    lines.push(String(l.created_at).substring(5, 16) + " · " + (a >= 0 ? "+" : "") + tgaMoney_(a) + " → ৳" + tgaMoney_(l.balance_after) + " · " + l.type + (l.reason ? " · " + String(l.reason).substring(0, 28) : ""));
  });
  if (!led.length) lines.push("লেনদেন নেই।");
  var kb = [];
  var orders = (r && (r.orders || r.history)) || [];
  orders.slice(0, 5).forEach(function (o) { if (o && o.order_id) kb.push([tgaBtn_((o.status || "") + " · " + o.order_id, "o:" + o.order_id)]); });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w:" + m), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
function tgaWalletUsers_(ctx) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "list_users", limit: 12 }); });
  var users = r.users || [];
  var kb = users.map(function (u) { return [tgaBtn_(u.mobile + " · ৳" + tgaMoney_(u.balance), "w:" + u.mobile)]; });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, "👥 সাম্প্রতিক ইউজার (" + users.length + ")", kb);
}
function tgaPinRequests_(ctx) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "pin_requests" }); });
  var reqs = r.requests || [];
  if (!reqs.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, "🔑 কোনো PIN রিকভারি রিকোয়েস্ট নেই।", [[tgaBtn_("?? রিফ্রেশ", "wp"), tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w")]]);
  var lines = ["🔑 PIN রিকভারি রিকোয়েস্ট (কাস্টমারকে কোড জানান)", ""];
  var kb = [];
  reqs.forEach(function (q) {
    lines.push(q.mobile + " — কোড " + q.code + " (" + Math.ceil(q.expires_in / 60) + " মিনিট বাকি)");
    kb.push([tgaBtn_("✖ " + q.mobile + " সরান", "wd:" + q.mobile), tgaBtn_("👛 ওয়ালেট", "w:" + q.mobile)]);
  });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "wp"), tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
function tgaPinDismiss_(ctx, m) {
  tgaLocked_(function () { return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "pin_request_dismiss", mobile: m }); }); });
  ctx.toast = "সরানো হয়েছে";
  tgaPinRequests_(ctx);
}
function tgaPinReset_(ctx, m) {
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () { return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "reset_pin", mobile: m }); }); });
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ " + (r.message || r.error || "হয়নি"), [[tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w:" + m)]]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, "🔑 " + m + " এর নতুন PIN: " + r.new_pin + "\n\nকাস্টমারকে জানিয়ে এই মেসেজ ডিলিট করে দিন।", [[tgaBtn_("⬅️ ওয়ালেট", "w:" + m), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

// /add, /deduct (and the typed step after ➕/➖) — always asks for a confirm tap first
function tgaAdjustCmd_(ctx, sign, args) {
  var m = normalizeMobile_(args[0] || "");
  var amt = Number(args[1]);
  var reason = args.slice(2).join(" ").trim();
  if (!m || !(amt > 0) || !reason) {
    return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "ব্যবহার: " + (sign > 0 ? "/add" : "/deduct") + " 01XXXXXXXXX 50 কারণ\n(কারণ দেওয়া বাধ্যতামূলক)");
  }
  tgaSetState_(ctx.chat, { t: "adjc", mobile: m, delta: sign * amt, reason: reason });
  tgaSend_(ctx.chat, (sign > 0 ? "➕ যোগ" : "➖ কাটা") + " ৳" + tgaMoney_(amt) + "\n" + m + "\nকারণ: " + reason + "\n\nনিশ্চিত?",
    [[tgaBtn_("✅ নিশ্চিত", "wyc"), tgaBtn_("✖ বাতিল", "w:" + m)]]);
}
function tgaAdjustConfirm_(ctx) {
  var st = tgaGetState_(ctx.chat);
  if (!st || st.t !== "adjc") { ctx.toast = "মেয়াদ শেষ — আবার করুন"; return tgaMenu_(ctx); }
  tgaClearState_(ctx.chat);
  var r = tgaLocked_(function () {
    return tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminWallet_({ token: tgaTok_(), sub: "adjust", mobile: st.mobile, delta: st.delta, reason: st.reason, type: "adjust", ref: "telegram" }); });
  });
  var msg = r.ok ? ("✅ " + (st.delta > 0 ? "+" : "") + tgaMoney_(st.delta) + " → ব্যালেন্স ৳" + tgaMoney_(r.balance)) :
    ("⚠️ হয়নি: " + (r.error === "insufficient_balance" ? "ব্যালেন্স কম (৳" + tgaMoney_(r.balance) + ")" : (r.error || "unknown")));
  ctx.toast = msg;
  tgaWalletView_(ctx, st.mobile, msg);
}

// ---------- provider balances ----------
function tgaBalances_(ctx) {
  var r = tgaCall_(function () { return handleAdminBalances_({ token: tgaTok_(), auto_retry: false }); });
  if (!r.ok) return tgaShow_(ctx, "⚠️ ব্যালেন্স আনা যায়নি: " + (r.error || ""), [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var lines = ["🏦 প্রোভাইডার ব্যালেন্স", ""];
  var total = 0;
  (r.balances || []).forEach(function (b) {
    if (b.ok) { total += Number(b.balance) || 0; lines.push((Number(b.balance) < 1 ? "🔴 " : "🟢 ") + b.provider + ": " + (b.currency === "USD" || !b.currency ? "$" : b.currency + " ") + (Math.round(Number(b.balance) * 100) / 100)); }
    else lines.push("⚠️ " + b.provider + ": " + String(b.error || "error").substring(0, 40));
  });
  lines.push("", "মোট (USD): $" + (Math.round(total * 100) / 100));
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), [[tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "bal"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
}

// ---------- alerts ----------
function tgaAlerts_(ctx) {
  var arr = listAdminAlerts_();
  if (!arr.length) return tgaShow_(ctx, "🔔 কোনো অ্যালার্ট নেই ✅", [[tgaBtn_("🔄 রিফ্রেশ", "al"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var lines = ["🔔 অ্যালার্ট (" + arr.length + ")", ""];
  var kb = [];
  arr.slice(0, 8).forEach(function (a) {
    var bits = [a.type || "alert"];
    if (a.provider) bits.push(a.provider);
    if (a.order_id) bits.push(a.order_id);
    if (a.topup_bdt) bits.push("টপ-আপ দরকার ≈৳" + a.topup_bdt);
    if (a.message) bits.push(String(a.message).substring(0, 80));
    lines.push((a.severity === "high" ? "🔴 " : "🟡 ") + bits.join(" · ") + "\n   " + String(a.created_at || "").substring(5, 16));
    if (a.order_id) kb.push([tgaBtn_("🛒 " + a.order_id, "o:" + a.order_id)]);
  });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🗑 সব ক্লিয়ার", "alc"), tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
  tgaShow_(ctx, lines.join("\n"), kb);
}
function tgaAlertsClear_(ctx) {
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("ADMIN_ALERTS", "[]");
  ctx.toast = "ক্লিয়ার হয়েছে";
  tgaAlerts_(ctx);
}

// ---------- discounts ----------
function tgaDiscounts_(ctx) {
  var all = activeDiscounts_(getServiceMeta_());
  var codes = Object.keys(all);
  if (!codes.length) return tgaSend_(ctx.chat, "🏷 কোনো চালু ডিসকাউন্ট নেই।\nসেট করতে: /discount CODE 10", [[tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]]);
  var kb = [];
  var lines = ["🏷 চালু ডিসকাউন্ট", ""];
  codes.forEach(function (c) {
    var d = all[c];
    lines.push(c + ": " + (d.type === "fixed" ? "৳" + d.value + "/1K" : d.value + "%") + (d.label ? " · " + d.label : "") + (d.until ? " · " + String(d.until).substring(0, 10) + " পর্যন্ত" : ""));
    kb.push([tgaBtn_("🗑 " + c + " তুলুন", "dr:" + c)]);
  });
  kb.push([tgaBtn_("🏠 মেনু", "m")]);
